In its traditional form, suspicion can be understood as a situated legal judgement by human actors within identifiable jurisdictions. It is argued that this understanding is no longer adequate. As artificial intelligence (AI) becomes an integral part of financial surveillance, suspicion is increasingly produced through data-driven processes. This transformation is epistemic, but also spatial. Since AI-driven financial surveillance operates through transnational data infrastructures, regulatory reach is less a matter of where conduct occurs than a question of whether such conduct becomes visible within data systems. This article develops the concept of algorithmic extraterritoriality, understood as a form of regulatory power mediated by data infrastructures rather than formal assertions of jurisdiction. Moreover, since individuals are increasingly constituted as datafied subjects of suspicion, they are rendered governable through dispersed and opaque processes of evaluation. This constitutes a challenge for accountability and contestability because suspicion becomes more difficult to locate, explain or contest.

</details>


### [209] [Jumping the Line: Exploiting Length Predictions in LLM Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03430)
*Yuyang Dai,Rana Shahout,Mahmood Sharif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JIL，一种针对基于预测的LLM调度器的攻击方法，通过优化对抗后缀使输出长度探针低估请求长度，从而获得更高调度优先级并缩短完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型服务中，高效的请求调度对缩短完成时间至关重要。基于长度的调度策略（如短作业优先）依赖对输出长度的预测，但输出长度在生成前未知，因此实际调度器依赖预测长度。这种预测机制成为可被操纵的调度信号，构成潜在攻击面。

Method: 以TRAIL为案例，JIL优化一个对抗后缀，诱导轻量级输出长度探针低估请求长度。在2个数据集、4个LLM上，针对不同请求画像和部署配置进行评测。

Result: JIL将预测输出长度降低最多83.4%，对抗请求在端到端服务实验中平均完成速度快至1.53倍。预测长度的降低幅度远大于实际输出长度的变化，揭示了调度器估计与请求真实规模之间的失配。响应效用因模型和任务而异，存在调度优势与响应质量之间的权衡。调度器侧防御评估表明，将长度预测分组为粗粒度区间可削弱JIL的调度优势并缓解对良性请求的延迟。

Conclusion: JIL揭示了预测式LLM调度的安全脆弱性，并表明粗粒度分组预测是一种有效的缓解手段，同时凸显调度效率与响应质量之间的权衡。

Abstract: Efficient request scheduling is increasingly important for reducing completion time in large language model (LLM) serving. Size-based policies such as Shortest Job First prioritize shorter requests, but output lengths are unknown before generation, so practical schedulers rely on predicted lengths. We introduce JIL, an attack on prediction-based LLM schedulers that manipulates the scheduling signal to obtain higher priority and reduce completion time. Using TRAIL as a case study, JIL optimizes an adversarial suffix that causes a lightweight output-length probe to underestimate a request's length. We evaluate JIL on two datasets and four LLMs across varied request profiles and deployment configurations. JIL reduces predicted output lengths by up to 83.4 percent, and adversarial requests complete up to 1.53 times faster on average in end-to-end serving experiments. The reduction in predicted length is substantially larger than the change in actual output length, revealing a mismatch between the scheduler's estimate and the request's realized size. Response utility varies across models and tasks, exposing a trade-off between scheduling advantage and response quality. We also evaluate scheduler-side defenses and find that grouping length predictions into coarse intervals reduces JIL's scheduling advantage and mitigates delays to benign requests.

</details>


### [210] [A Near-Zero Monitor Readout Is Not Evidence of Behavioral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03458)
*Zhe Zhou,Tianhua Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 低监控读数不等于行为受控：离线通过同一门槛的探针与两类前缀惩罚，其读数值无法区分低作弊率与"延迟启动"作弊，需额外的带外行为检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证奖励的后训练会诱发 reward hacking，因此希望把监控器纳入训练目标而非仅用于离线审计。但论文要质疑一个隐含假设：监控读数低，是否能说明训练干预真的控制了行为。

Method: 在代码生成环境中（主要 exploit 在推理轨迹起点即可用），针对三类均能通过同一离线门槛的监控器训练策略：一个域内激活探针，以及两种根据策略"多早锁定自己的最终答案"施加的惩罚。比较各监控家族内部的训练读数与行为表现，并做文本层面的前缀分析。

Result: 探针分数从第一步训练就处于数值下限，所有前缀训练run的终末训练分数中位数均为零。在同一固定配置下，中位数为零的run仅因随机种子不同，就从"低作弊率的混合状态"跨越到"接近纯粹的 reward hacking"。探针run全部落入作弊状态，但其下限读数源于验证位置与读取位置不匹配，而非第二种歧义。文本分析发现前缀失败模式：通用规划与填充外壳把 exploit 推迟到切点之后，却未将其从最终输出中消除。

Conclusion: 离线判别能力与低监控对齐读数都不足以证明行为受控，低"承诺度"无法区分低作弊率与延迟承诺作弊；必须加入带外行为检查。论文刻画的是终末读数，而非其演化过程。

Abstract: Post-training with verifiable rewards can induce reward hacking, motivating the use of monitors within the training objective rather than solely for offline auditing. We show that a low monitor readout does not identify whether such an intervention controls behavior. In a code-generation environment whose dominant exploit is available at the start of the reasoning trace, we train policies against three monitors that pass the same offline gate: an in-domain activation probe and two penalties conditioned on how early the policy commits to its own final answer. The probe score is at its numerical floor from the first recorded training step, and the trained-score median is zero for every prefix-trained run at the endpoint. These readouts estimate different quantities, and we do not compare their scales; within each monitor family, however, low values do not establish behavioral control. Within one fixed configuration, prefix-trained runs with the same zero-median trained score range, by seed alone, from a mixed regime with a low hacking share to near-pure reward hacking. All probe runs reach the hacking regime, but their floor-level readout reflects a mismatch between the position where the probe was validated and the position where it was read during training, not a second instance of this ambiguity. Text-level analysis identifies a prefix failure mode: generic planning and filler shells postpone the exploit past the cut without eliminating it from the final output. Low measured commitment therefore does not distinguish a low hacking share from delayed commitment to the exploit. Offline discrimination and low monitor-aligned readouts are insufficient evidence of behavioral control; an out-of-band behavioral check is required. We characterize the endpoint readout, not its evolution. Code is available at https://github.com/zhezhou1106/spoof-cost.

</details>


### [211] [Efficient Reasoning Training Does Not Always Harm CoT Faithfulness and Monitorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03509)
*Samuel Lewis-Lim,Xingwei Tan,Mario Sanger,Zhixue Zhao,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究高效推理（缩短思维链）对思维链忠实性与可监控性的影响，通过三种长度压力方法微调多种模型，发现两者受影响程度不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链推理虽提升透明度，但增加推理成本，因此需要训练模型用更少token完成任务；然而缩短推理可能导致模型跳过关键步骤，使思维链不再忠实反映决策，且该问题在实践中的表现尚不明确。

Method: 对多种模型使用三种施加长度压力的方法进行微调：固定生成长度预算、按样本设定长度目标、群体相对长度奖励；随后评估高效推理对思维链忠实性（思维链多大程度反映模型在相关输入上的决策）与可监控性（思维链是否揭示输入干预改变输出的情况）的影响。

Result: 高效推理对忠实性和可监控性影响不同。忠实性在多数设置下下降，主要因训练后的模型一致性降低；可监控性更稳健，即便思维链大幅缩短，模型仍会承认输入对其答案的影响。

Conclusion: 缩短思维链的效率训练会损害忠实性但基本保持可监控性，提示两类性质需分别评估，可监控性对长度压力更为鲁棒。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning allows humans to inspect how large language models reach their answers, and oversee model behaviour. This reasoning comes at an increased inference cost, motivating efficient methods that train models to solve tasks using fewer tokens. However, a common concern is that such training may cause models to skip important reasoning steps, so the CoT no longer faithfully reflects the model's decision. It is unclear whether or when this occurs in practice, since different efficiency methods apply length pressure to models' CoT in distinct ways, and faithfully explaining a model's decision takes more tokens on some tasks than others. To understand these dynamics, we fine-tune a variety of models with three methods that apply length pressure differently, namely a fixed generation budget, a per-example length target, and a group-relative length reward. We evaluate how efficient reasoning affects CoT faithfulness (i.e., how well the CoT reflects model decisions on related inputs) and monitorability (i.e., whether the CoT reveals when input interventions alter the output). We find that it affects faithfulness and monitorability differently. Faithfulness falls in most settings, primarily because the trained models are less consistent. Monitorability is more robust, as models keep acknowledging the influence on their answer even when the CoT is much shorter.

</details>


### [212] [Reasoning Models Are Accurate but Unsound on Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03519)
*Arman Behnam,Binghui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CERTID，一个形式化因果识别评估流水线，用可靠完备的 ID 算法认证查询可识别性，并用已知干预分布的 SCM 验证返回公式，揭示推理模型在非可识别查询上存在严重虚假声称问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在判断因果效应是否可从观测数据恢复时有两种失败：拒绝可识别查询，或回答不可识别查询。后者更严重，因为没有观测数据能验证其公式。但已有评测缺乏可证明不可识别的查询，也无法以等价形式对公式进行评分（字符串匹配做不到）。

Method: CERTID 采用可靠完备的因果识别算法 ID，对给定图和查询认证效应是否可识别；并通过干预分布已知的结构因果模型（SCM）验证模型返回的公式；同时给出缓解结构泄漏、修复不可识别查询以及建立评分保证的理论结果。在 1,200 个认证实例（4 到 50 个顶点）上评测 Gemini Flash、Gemini Pro 和 GPT5.5。

Result: 准确率不能代表可靠性：在相同实例上，模型对不可识别查询的虚假声称率相差达十七倍。在最强模型训练快照之后生成的图上，模型判断可识别性的准确率为 97–100%。

Conclusion: 准确率是可靠性的糟糕代理指标，评估因果识别需可证明不可识别的查询与等价形式评分。实例、认证流程、验证器及逐实例记录已公开。

Abstract: A reasoning model asked whether a causal effect is recoverable from observational data can fail in two ways: it refuses an identifiable query or answers a nonidentifiable one. The latter is more consequential, as no observational data can validate the claimed formula. Measuring this failure requires queries that are provably non-identifiable, which prior evaluations lack, and grading that accepts correct formulas in any equivalent form, which string matching cannot provide. We build CERTID, a formal identification pipeline that addresses both limitations. CERTID uses the sound and complete causal identification algorithm ID to certify whether an effect is identifiable from a given graph and query, and verifies returned formulas against structural causal models whose interventional distributions are known exactly. CERTID further develops theoretical results to mitigate structural leakage, repair non-identifiable queries, and establish grading guarantees. We evaluate three frontier reasoning models (Gemini Flash, Gemini Pro, and GPT5.5) on 1,200 certified instances spanning 4 to 50 vertices. Accuracy proves a poor proxy for soundness: on identical instances, the false-claim rate on non-identifiable queries varies by seventeen-fold across models. We also find that models decide identifiability with 97-100% accuracy on graphs generated after the strongest model's training snapshot. Instances, the certification procedure, the verifier, and per-instance records are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/certid-D718.

</details>


### [213] [From Benchmarks to Production: A Text-to-SQL System for Complex Financial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03524)
*Arijit Sehanobish,Bruno Gomes Coelho,Guillaume Michel,Sophia Zhi,Valerie Faucon-Morin,Kristen Howell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FLINT，一个面向生产金融数据库的领域专用Text-to-SQL系统，通过查找代理、模板检索和模式链接三个组件，解决了因使用不透明整数键、需多表连接等导致的性能瓶颈，在359个问题上的表现超过多种基线，并已部署上线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用Text-to-SQL系统在Spider、BIRD等学术基准上表现优异，但这些基准的数据库模式较浅、列值多为人类可读字符串。在生产金融数据库中，概念以不透明的整数键存储而非可读字符串，导致简单查询也需多次连接和引用不透明ID的过滤谓词，通用方法准确率跌破50%。

Method: FLINT包含三个核心组件：（1）查找代理，动态将自然语言概念解析为与问题相关的参照表约束；（2）基于嵌入的检索，从紧凑的专家编写的查询模板库中检索结构相似的模板；（3）模式链接，通过遍历外键链将大型表模式剪枝至相关子集，而非仅依赖名称相似度。

Result: 在两个数据集共359个问题、基于生产金融模式进行评估。使用相同LLM的情况下，FLINT优于多种最先进基线。系统已作为金融数据检索服务的一部分部署到生产环境。

Conclusion: FLINT通过领域专用设计有效弥补了通用Text-to-SQL方法在生产金融数据库中的性能差距，验证了查找代理、模板检索与外键链模式剪枝结合的实际价值，并成功实现生产部署。

Abstract: General-purpose Text-to-SQL systems achieve strong performance on academic benchmarks like Spider and BIRD, where schemas are relatively shallow and column values are often human readable. In production financial databases, where concepts are stored as opaque integer keys rather than human-readable strings, these methods fall below 50%, as even simple queries require multiple joins and filter predicates reference opaque IDs. We present Financial LINking Text-to-SQL (FLINT), a domain-specialized Text-to-SQL system that closes this gap through three key components: (1) a lookup agent that dynamically resolves natural-language concepts to question-specific reference table constraints, (2) embedding-based retrieval of structurally similar query templates from a compact, expert-authored bank, and (3) schema linking that prunes a large table schema to the relevant subset by traversing foreign-key chains, rather than relying on name similarity alone. We evaluate on two datasets totaling 359 questions over production financial schemas. FLINT outperforms various state-of-the-art baselines using the same LLM. The system is deployed in production as part of a financial data retrieval service.

</details>


### [214] [Recursive Harness Self-Improvement for Frontier Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03548)
*Wenlong Zhang,Zhengbo Jiao,Chenxu Zhang,Lekang Jiang,SiYuan Ma,Qituan Zhang,Guo Chen,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出任务-工具协同演化框架，通过递归自我改进让合成工具与任务一起演化，生成越来越难的推理数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务级递归只把生成的问题当作新种子，但构造工具（harness）保持不变，难以持续提升任务难度。需要让合成工具与任务分布共同演化。

Method: 任务-工具协同演化（task-harness co-evolution）：在线自我改进把求解器的中间失败转化为可复用技能；任务后自我改进在每批后修订技能、提示和工作流，仅在有界成本增加内生成更难且有效的任务时才采纳候选。模型权重与验证标准保持固定。

Result: 在数学、编程与科学领域，十四轮演化中平均求解器准确率从100.0%降至54.8%。消融显示两种更新调度结合比固定工具递归或单调度生成更难任务。生成数据提升下游SFT与GRPO表现；27B学生在1万条合成数学样例上微调，在APEX达到62.5% mean-16准确率，与部分前沿模型参考相当。

Conclusion: 结果支持在任务之外同步改造合成工具，从而生成难度递增且具下游训练价值的数据。

Abstract: Generating progressively harder reasoning problems requires synthesis procedures that adapt as the task distribution evolves. Existing task-level recursion reuses generated problems as seeds but leaves the construction harness unchanged. We present task-harness co-evolution, a framework for recursive harness self-improvement (RSI) in reasoning-data synthesis. Online self-improvement converts intermediate solver failures into reusable skills during generation. Post-task self-improvement revises skills, prompts, and workflows after each batch, adopting candidates only when they generate harder valid tasks within a bounded cost increase. Model weights and verification criteria remain fixed. Across mathematics, coding, and science, mean solver accuracy decreases from 100.0% to 54.8% over fourteen evolution rounds. Ablations show that combining both update schedules produces harder tasks than fixed-harness recursion or either schedule alone. The resulting data improves downstream SFT and GRPO performance. In particular, a 27B student fine-tuned on 10K synthesized mathematics examples achieves 62.5% mean-16 accuracy on APEX, competitive with selected frontier-model references. These results support adapting the synthesis harness alongside the tasks to generate increasingly challenging data with downstream training value.

</details>


### [215] [Knowledge or Calculator? Decomposing the Skill Premium in Verifiable Financial Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03564)
*Jermyn Zhen Yong Bek,Zhuang Qiang Bok,Zhongtian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FinSkillBench金融AI智能体评测套件（2603个时点任务、12个子任务），发现"技能包"带来的性能提升取决于技能来源、资源类型与评测框架的整体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融AI智能体不仅要检索事实，还需正确的量化执行、可靠使用流程性资源以及可审计的结构化输出，因此需要专门评测其能力及外部技能资源的价值。

Method: 构建FinSkillBench，覆盖投资组合构建、风险管理与基本面分析共12个子任务、2603个时点化情景，具有隐藏可再生的真值与任务专属确定性验证器；在9个模型、3种资源条件下执行17820个情景，并对8个模型做配对分析，分解人工技能包为文档与可执行工具，进行10种评分变体与聚类自助法稳健性检验，以及独立第二套评测框架复现。

Result: 精选技能包使平均分提升+16.2（0.366→0.528），而单情景内生成的技能仅+0.5且消耗更多token与轮次；拆分后文档单独+5.6、工具单独+19.5，二者组合呈次可加性；提升效果依赖工作流：数值密集型任务由可执行工具主导，流程或输出模式指引受限时文档更重要，解释性任务两者兼需；效应方向在10种评分变体与聚类自助法下稳定，第二套框架复现方向性模式但幅度受工具与数据暴露方式影响。

Conclusion: 可测量的"技能溢价"是整个模型、资源与评测框架系统的属性，而非仅由底层模型决定。

Abstract: Financial AI agents must do more than retrieve facts: investment workflows require correct quantitative execution, reliable use of procedural resources, and auditable structured outputs. We introduce FinSkillBench, an evaluation suite of 2,603 point in time episodes across 12 subtasks in portfolio construction, risk management, and fundamental analysis, with hidden regenerable ground truth and task specific deterministic verifiers. Executing 17,820 episodes across 9 models and 3 resource conditions, the paired analysis across 8 models shows that curated skill packages raise mean scores by +16.2 points (0.366 to 0.528), whereas skills generated within a single episode add only +0.5 points while consuming more tokens and turns. We then decompose the curated premium by granting human authored procedural documents and executable domain tools separately: documents alone add +5.6 points, tools alone add +19.5 points, and their combination is subadditive. The premium is strongly workflow dependent: executable tools dominate numerically intensive workflows, documentation matters more when procedural or output schema guidance is the bottleneck, and interpretive tasks benefit from both. The effects are sign stable across 10 scoring variants and cluster bootstrap analyses, and an independently implemented second harness reproduces the directional pattern while showing that effect magnitudes depend on how tools and data are exposed. Overall, a measured "skill premium" is a property of the full model, resource, and harness system rather than of the underlying model alone.

</details>


### [216] [Learning to Assess Heartbeat Observability for mmWave Heart-Rate Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03570)
*Yuxuan Hu,Shilin Shan,Jianfei Yang,Feng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了HEAR，一种紧凑的双任务Transformer，用于从毫米波雷达相位谱中评估心跳可观测性，从而实现选择性心率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达非接触式心率感知需要判断单次测量是否可靠。由于散射体回波的相干叠加可能在相似观测几何下抑制心跳成分，因此必须直接从采集的测量中评估可观测性。

Method: 开发了可控多散射体FMCW仿真器，通过心跳频带主峰与已知心率的一致性自动生成可观测性标签。提出HEAR双任务Transformer，联合预测可观测性分数和心率，输入结合频谱幅度与相对于呼吸基频的频率。

Result: 仅在仿真数据上训练，HEAR零样本迁移到60 GHz和120 GHz两个公开真实数据集（134名受试者）。在120 GHz数据集上，基于分数的选择将心率头MAE从全覆盖的17.9 BPM降至50%覆盖率下的1.6 BPM。端到端处理延迟为50.8 ms。

Conclusion: 学习的可观测性分数可支持选择性预测，并适用于HEAR自身及多种现有估计器，实现了低延迟的边缘端部署。

Abstract: Contactless heart-rate sensing with millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar requires assessing whether individual measurements support reliable estimation. We study learning to assess heartbeat observability, defined as the readability of the heartbeat component in an acquired phase spectrum, for selective heart-rate estimation. Coherent superposition of scatterer returns can suppress this component even under similar macroscopic observation geometry, motivating assessment directly from acquired measurements. To obtain training supervision across different observability conditions, we develop a controllable multi-scatterer frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) simulator. Agreement between the dominant heartbeat-band peak and the known heart rate provides an automatic observability label for each simulated measurement. We propose HEAR (Heartbeat Estimation with Assessed Reliability), a compact dual-task Transformer that jointly predicts an observability score and heart rate. Its input combines spectral magnitudes with frequencies relative to the respiration fundamental, providing context for respiratory harmonics. Trained solely on simulated observations, HEAR transfers zero-shot to two public real-world datasets collected at 60 and 120 GHz from 134 subjects. The same learned score supports selective prediction with both HEAR's own heart-rate head and multiple existing estimators. On the 120 GHz dataset, score-based selection reduces the HR head's mean absolute error from 17.9 BPM at full coverage to 1.6 BPM at 50% coverage. The complete pipeline achieves an end-to-end processing latency of 50.8 ms on an edge device. Project page: https://yuxuanhu9.github.io/HEAR/.

</details>


### [217] [HyperBrowseComp: A Multilingual and Multimodal Stress Test for Web-Browsing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03574)
*Alham Fikri Aji,Faiz Rizki Ramadhan,Zayd M. K. Zuhri,Seung Hun Eddie Han,Ryandito Diandaru,Qinrong Cui,Jan Christian Blaise Cruz,Badrinath Chandana,Peerawat Chomphooyod,Ahmed Attia,Jonibek Mansurov,Emilio Villa-Cueva,Canh Duong Nguyen,Imran Turganov,Minghao Wu,Peerat Limkonchotiwat,Irina Nikishina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyperBrowseComp：多语言多模态浏览基准，含13种语言423道人工编写验证的极难题目，需追踪隐晦证据与多步线索链，跨视频/扫描文档/图像/地图等多源检索，用于评估智能体的持续信息检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浏览/检索基准题目偏易，且多为单语言、单模态，难以真正检验模型在开放网络中发现并串联隐晦证据的能力，因此需要一个高难度、多语言、多模态的评测平台。

Method: 构建HyperBrowseComp：423道由母语或高熟练度者人工撰写并验证的题目，覆盖13种语言；每题对应简短、可公开核实的答案，须通过多步线索链或检索视频、扫描文档、图像、地图等异构来源才能得到；用无联网模型过滤掉可凭参数化知识回答的简单题。评估时采用统一智能体协议，对比提供商原生搜索与共享外部检索框架下的多个模型，并对部分题目做人工评测以对照模型表现与投入。

Result: 题目经无联网模型筛选后保留极难样本；在统一协议下对多个模型的评测显示其性能受检索方式（原生搜索 vs. 外部检索框架）影响，人工评测为模型表现与努力程度提供了对照基准，表明该基准对当前模型仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: HyperBrowseComp为跨语言、跨证据模态的持续信息检索提供了高难度测试平台，其难度源于在开放网络中定位并连接证据，可作为衡量与推动下一代检索智能体研究的基准。

Abstract: We introduce HyperBrowseComp, a multilingual and multimodal browsing benchmark comprising 423 manually authored and human-validated questions across 13 languages, written by native or highly proficient speakers. Questions are designed to be extremely challenging. Each question targets a concise, publicly verifiable answer whose discovery requires locating obscure evidence, following multi-step clue chains, or inspecting heterogeneous sources such as videos, scanned documents, images, or maps. Easier questions are filtered out by evaluating them with models without internet access to reduce the likelihood that they can be answered with parametric knowledge alone. We evaluate several models using provider-native search and a shared external retrieval harness under a common agent protocol. To contextualize model performance and effort, we also conduct a human evaluation on a sample of the questions. HyperBrowseComp provides a challenging testbed for persistent information seeking across languages and evidence modalities, with difficulty arising from discovering and connecting evidence on the open web.

</details>


### [218] [HazardWeaver: Scientific Route Selection for Hazard Analysis Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03591)
*Wangshu Zhu,Xueqi Cheng,Liang Wu,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出状态依赖的科学路线选择问题，并构建HazardWeaver系统与配套基准，用于灾害分析的自动化决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然灾害理解与评估对防灾减灾至关重要。大语言模型推动了灾害分析AI智能体研究，但有效自动化要求智能体判断哪些科学方法适用于特定事件、且在当前数据和工具下可执行；随着新证据和执行结果出现，条件会变化，需重新决策。

Method: 将问题形式化为状态依赖的科学路线选择。HazardWeaver包含：Hazard Knowledge Compiler提取以证据关联的科学适用条件；Hazard Capability Graph表征可执行科学能力并检查输入输出兼容性；Hazard Weaver Agent基于两种表示选择适用且可执行的路线、执行工作流并随状态变化修订决策。同时提出Hazard Weaver Benchmark，含7个单灾害领域和4类多灾害交互共141个实例，支持多种有效路线，评估输出正确性、路线有效性与有依据的弃权。

Result: 在该基准上的大量实验表明，HazardWeaver优于现有智能体系统，在存在多条合格科学路线的任务上提升最大。代码已公开。

Conclusion: HazardWeaver通过知识编译与能力图表示实现状态依赖的科学路线选择，提升了灾害分析自动化的适用性与可执行性，并为相关研究提供了评估基准。

Abstract: Understanding and assessing natural hazards is essential for disaster preparedness and risk reduction. Recent advances in large language models have spurred growing interest in AI agents for hazard analysis, particularly their ability to integrate scientific data, models, and tools into automated workflows. However, effective automation requires agents to determine which scientific methods are appropriate for a given event and executable with the available data and tools. As new evidence and execution results become available, these conditions can change, requiring agents to reconsider their choices. We formulate this problem as state-dependent scientific route selection and introduce HazardWeaver. Specifically, HazardWeaver first leverages the Hazard Knowledge Compiler to extract evidence-linked conditions governing scientific applicability, then its Hazard Capability Graph represents executable scientific capabilities and checks compatibility between their inputs and outputs. Using these complementary representations, the Hazard Weaver Agent component selects applicable and executable routes, carries out their workflows, and revises its decisions as the analysis state changes. To evaluate both the scientific outputs and the decisions that produce them, we introduce the Hazard Weaver Benchmark, comprising 141 instances across seven single-hazard domains and four multi-hazard interaction classes. The benchmark accommodates multiple valid scientific routes and evaluates output correctness, route validity, and justified abstention. Extensive experiments on this benchmark show that HazardWeaver outperforms existing agent systems, with the largest gains on tasks with multiple eligible scientific routes. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/LabRAI/HazardWeaver.

</details>


### [219] [Depth as Time in One-Step Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03626)
*Arnold Caleb Asiimwe,William Yang,Sanghyuk Chun,Esin Tureci,Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一步生成模型把多步扩散的采样轨迹压缩进单次前向传播后，去噪计算并未消失，而是以“深度即时间”的形式在网络的层间深度上展开；这种深度去噪的强弱取决于流映射所求解的传输任务，并可据此实现高达16.6倍的参数压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步生成模型（蒸馏或学习流映射）已能生成高质量图像，但作者追问：当生成被压缩为单次前向传播时，多步扩散原有的去噪轨迹究竟发生了什么变化？是否真的被消除了？

Method: 作者进行实证观测，提出“深度即时间”（depth as time）现象：多步扩散在采样步之间执行的去噪计算似乎展开在单次前向传播的网络深度上，可用模型自身的输出头解码中间层加以恢复。他们对比不同训练目标的生成器（含时间索引传输任务的流映射如MeanFlow，与无此类任务如drifting models），并通过探测更短的传输区间、将层间计算显式视作一个流来训练单个时间条件化模块。

Result: 层间计算依赖流映射所学的传输任务；最令人意外的是MeanFlow在探测较短传输区间时，单次网络评估内同时出现去噪与再加噪（denoise-then-renoise）。未使用时间索引传输任务训练的生成器（如drifting models）不具备同样的深度去噪。展现出深度去噪现象的模型在层间计算上更易压缩：MeanFlow SiT-L/2可压缩16.6倍参数为单个时间条件化块。

Conclusion: 扩散的时间性计算并未被一步生成消除，而是在网络深度上被重组。作者给出了去噪再加噪行为的解释，说明可据此压缩模型，表明时间计算结构在一步生成模型中依然存在并可被显式利用。

Abstract: The recent wave of one-step generative models, which compress the multi-step trajectory of diffusion via either distillation or learned flow maps, has reached an inflection point where they can generate high-quality images. Here, we ask a natural question that follows from these advances: what happens to the denoising trajectory of multi-step diffusion when generation is compressed into a single forward pass? We offer an empirical observation we call \textit{depth as time}: the denoising computation that multi-step diffusion performs across sampling steps appears to unfold across the depth of a single forward pass, and can be recovered by decoding intermediate layers with the model's own output head. Most interestingly, we show that this depthwise computation depends on the transport task a flow map is trained to solve. The most surprising case is MeanFlow, where probing shorter transport intervals reveals both denoising and renoising within a single network evaluation. In contrast, generators trained without a time-indexed transport task, such as drifting models, do not exhibit the same depthwise denoising. Consequently, we show that models that exhibit the depthwise denoising phenomenon are more compressible across the layerwise computation: a MeanFlow \texttt{SiT-L/2} model can be compressed by $16.6\times$ in parameters into a single time-conditioned block. We offer an explanation for this denoise-then-renoise behavior and show that, when we treat the layerwise computation explicitly as a flow, a single time-conditioned block can be trained to denoise across layers, compressing a MeanFlow \texttt{SiT-L/2} model by $16.6\times$ in parameters. Together, these results suggest that the temporal computation of diffusion is not eliminated by one-step generation, but reorganized across network depth.

</details>


### [220] [NeutronGym: Physics-Graded Neutron Instrument Design for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03631)
*Lijie Ding,Changwoo Do*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出NeutronGym，首个用于中子仪器设计的可执行环境，智能体通过验证工具搭建仪器、McStas进行射线追踪、分级阶梯自动评分。七个模型最多重现16个基准任务中的7个，而强化学习可将Qwen3-8B从11%提升到77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计科学仪器可以检验语言模型智能体是否真正在做物理而非单纯回忆。前提是评分机制必须无可争议，不能依赖LLM评判，因此需要一个可执行、可自动验证的评测环境。

Method: 构建NeutronGym环境：智能体通过验证工具构建仪器，McStas进行射线追踪，分级阶梯从语法、运行、结构和科学四个层面自动评分。程序化族提供无限实例并设置保留参数区间；McStasBench收录16个已发表仪器任务，配有记忆探测和沙箱。此外用其奖励进行强化学习训练，并做消融分析。

Result: 七个模型最多只能重现16个任务中的7个，无一能检索到参考，无一达到改进目标。RL将Qwen3-8B从11%提升至77%的保留实例（第二种子69%），超过未训练的Qwen3-32B，并在另外三个门控族上复现。去掉阶梯部分得分会下降60分。仅靠奖励，训练模型在智能体仿真预算下达到经典优化器水平（77%对81%，差异不显著），但前沿模型仍达98-99%。

Conclusion: 该环境既能严格评测也能有效训练，且阶梯部分得分对性能至关重要。为了获得可信结果，作者淘汰了四个连无模型基线都能解决的任务设计，并公开了发现这些问题的探测工具。

Abstract: Designing a scientific instrument tests whether language-model agents can do physics rather than recall it, provided the grading cannot be argued with. We introduce NeutronGym, to our knowledge the first executable environment for neutron instrument design: agents build instruments through validating tools, McStas ray-traces what they build, and a level-resolved ladder grades syntax, runtime, structure and science with no LLM judge. Procedural families supply unlimited instances of a fixed layout whose design parameters the agent must set, with held-out parameter regimes; a curated slice, McStasBench, adds 16 tasks from published instruments behind memorization probes and a sandbox. Seven models reproduce at most 7 of the 16, none retrieves a reference, and none meets an improvement target. The environment also trains. Reinforcement learning on its reward takes Qwen3-8B from 11% to 77% of held-out instances of a family whose targets come from a hidden design (69% at a second seed), past an untrained Qwen3-32B, and the recipe holds, at one seed each, on three further gated families. The analysis says what that gain is. Without the ladder's partial credit it collapses by 60 points. From reward alone the trained model reaches what a classical optimizer reaches, at the agent's simulation budget, only when handed the closed-form physics (77% against 81%, a gap that does not separate at this size), while frontier models still solve 98-99%. Getting a trustworthy result meant failing four task designs that no-model baselines could solve, and we release the probes that found them.

</details>


### [221] [Credit Where It Matters: Dependency-Aware Policy Optimization for Terminal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03634)
*Yu Li,Guangfeng Cai,Long-Fei Li,Shuo Han,Shengtian Yang,Han Luo,Kaibing Yang,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出依赖感知组策略优化（DepGPO），利用终端命令间的执行依赖关系指导信用分配，通过构建命令依赖图并回溯验证器检查的资源，将信用分配给相关写操作及其支撑读操作，从而改进复杂终端任务上的性能与训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹级和步骤级信用分配方法未显式追踪命令间影响最终结果的读写依赖，导致训练信号可能被分配给无关操作，削弱对关键步骤的学习效果，尤其在后继命令依赖前序命令信息或中间结果的多步终端任务中。

Method: 方法为依赖感知组策略优化（DepGPO）：从执行轨迹构建命令依赖图，并从任务验证器所检查的资源向后回溯，沿这些路径将信用分配给相关的写操作及其支撑读操作，再用该信用在步骤间重新分配轨迹优势。

Result: 大量对比实验与消融研究表明，DepGPO 在复杂终端任务上提升了任务表现和训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 利用命令执行依赖关系指导信用分配可有效改善终端智能体的强化学习效果。

Abstract: Terminal-using agents benefit from reinforcement learning (RL) in coding, debugging, and other multi-step terminal tasks. In these tasks, later commands often depend on information or intermediate results produced by earlier commands. However, existing trajectory-level and step-level credit assignment methods do not explicitly trace the read-write dependencies through which commands affect the final outcome. Consequently, training signals could still be assigned to irrelevant operations, weakening learning from relevant steps. In this paper, we propose Dependency-Aware Group Policy Optimization (DepGPO), which uses execution dependencies between commands to guide credit assignment for terminal agents. Specifically, we construct a command dependency graph from execution traces and trace backward from the resources inspected by the task verifier. We then assign credit to relevant writes and their supporting reads along these paths, and use it to redistribute trajectory advantages across steps. Extensive comparative experiments and ablation studies demonstrate that DepGPO improves task performance and training stability on complex terminal tasks.

</details>


### [222] [Do Large Language Models Know Colombian Law? A Reliability Benchmark for the Colombian Legal System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03639)
*Rubén Manrique,Michelle Castellanos,Jorge Morales,Juan David Gutiérrez,Antonio Barreto Rozo,Joaquín Vélez Navarro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个经专家验证的哥伦比亚法律体系LLM基准（1042题，涵盖10个法律领域与3种题型），测评15个模型发现：封闭题准确率最高0.905，但自由文本事实正确率不超过0.45，且模型"答得相关却常答错"，需专家监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在律所实践、法律教育与研究中应用日增，但其在美国以外国家法律体系中的可靠性几乎无系统记录，缺乏专家验证的评估基准。

Method: 采用人在回路（human-in-the-loop）流水线，经多阶段专家审查，构建含1042项、覆盖10个法律领域和3种题型（封闭选择题、半开放题、开放式IRAC）的哥伦比亚法律基准；用适配各题型的指标评测15个当代闭源与开源模型，并引入独立评分标准LLM裁判与盲审人类专家打分进行交叉验证。

Result: 封闭题准确率跨度大（Gemini 3.1 Pro 0.905 至 0.577），但自由文本事实正确率任何模型均不超0.45；答案相关性与正确性出现分离（Spearman rho = -0.46）；封闭题准确率与自由文本正确性排名强相关（rho = 0.94）；LLM裁判与人类专家均复现自由文本排名（rho >= 0.88）；模型引用的法条仅约一半正确。可靠性随法律领域系统性变化，并随问题复杂度呈倒U型。

Conclusion: 当前LLM用于哥伦比亚法律任务必须配备专家监督，将答案锚定权威来源是提升可靠性的有前景路径；作者公开基准构建流水线以支持可复现评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support legal practice, education, and research, yet their reliability in national legal systems outside the United States remains largely undocumented. We introduce an expert-validated benchmark for evaluating LLM reliability on the Colombian legal system. The benchmark comprises 1,042 items spanning ten areas of law and three question formats (closed multiple-choice, semi-open, and open-ended IRAC), built through a human-in-the-loop pipeline with multi-stage expert review. We evaluate 15 contemporary proprietary and open-weight models with format-appropriate metrics. Accuracy on closed questions ranges widely, from 0.905 (Gemini 3.1 Pro) to 0.577, but on free-text legal answers factual correctness never exceeds 0.45 (on a 0-1 scale) for any model. We find a dissociation between answer relevancy and correctness (Spearman rho = -0.46): models reliably sound responsive while frequently being wrong, a pattern of particular concern for non-expert users. Closed-question accuracy and free-text correctness are strongly rank-correlated (rho = 0.94), so cheap multiple-choice screening predicts model ranking but overstates absolute reliability. An independent rubric-based LLM judge and blind human expert scoring both reproduce the free-text ranking (rho >= 0.88). The judge further reveals that only about half of the norms models cite are correct; the rest are wrong or non-existent. Reliability varies systematically by legal area and follows an inverted-U across question complexity. Our results indicate that current LLMs require expert supervision for Colombian legal tasks, and that grounding answers in authoritative sources is a promising path to higher reliability. We release the benchmark construction pipeline to support reproducible evaluation.

</details>


### [223] [MRVQ: One Resident Index for Dimension- and Rate-Elastic Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03651)
*Sean Culatana,Shang-En Huang,Kang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MRVQ是一种面向冻结嵌入的事后残差量化器，单个常驻产物通过截断残差阶段和嵌入坐标同时支持多种（维度，码率）组合，在评测中内存占用最低，但逐步码优质量不及逐码率训练的QINCo2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索服务需随延迟、质量和内存预算变化而在嵌入前缀维度与索引码率之间切换。若为每个码率单独调优量化器，虽质量最优，但检索层需同时保存多份码流与量化器状态，内存开销大。因此需要一种单份常驻即可覆盖多种（维度，码率）配置的低内存量化方案。

Method: 提出Matryoshka Residual Vector Quantization（MRVQ），一种针对冻结嵌入的事后残差量化器。其最高码率码字可沿两个方向截断：丢弃残差阶段以降低码率，丢弃嵌入坐标以降低维度，从而用同一份常驻产物服务所有评测的（维度，码率）组合。另评估一种低构建成本的PCA-标量设计。

Result: 在FiQA与NFCorpus、四类嵌入家族及{4,8,16}字节码上，MRVQ是所评测方案中RAM最低者：比三个分别训练的QINCo2索引省内存17.8-22.0倍，比精简的共享模型强基线省1.89-2.02倍。代价是逐码率QINCo2在FiQA上nDCG@10高0.026-0.107。MRVQ在相同码字大小下优于PQ、OPQ和AdANNS-OPQ。PCA-标量设计质量与RaBitQ及其扩展相当，中位构建速度快420倍。另报告两项负面结果：QINCo2在高码率训练时崩溃；排序界限假设未达预设接受标准。

Conclusion: MRVQ是弹性检索场景下的低内存工作点，而非普遍的质量最优方案；单份常驻产物以少量质量换取显著内存节省，适合内存预算受限的多码率、多维度弹性检索部署。

Abstract: Dense-retrieval services must switch among embedding-prefix dimensions and index bit rates as latency, quality, and memory budgets change. Tuning a quantizer separately for each rate gives the best quality, but the retrieval tier then holds several code streams and quantizer states at once. We introduce Matryoshka Residual Vector Quantization (MRVQ), a post-hoc residual quantizer for frozen embeddings. Its maximum-rate code can be truncated two ways: dropping residual stages lowers the rate, and dropping embedding coordinates lowers the dimension. One resident artifact therefore serves every (dimension, rate) pair we evaluate. Across FiQA and NFCorpus, four embedding families, and {4, 8, 16}-byte codes, MRVQ is the lowest-RAM design we evaluate. It uses 17.8-22.0x less memory than three separately trained QINCo2 indices, and 1.89-2.02x less than a lean shared-model steelman. The saving is not free: per-rate QINCo2 is 0.026-0.107 nDCG@10 better on FiQA. But MRVQ beats PQ, OPQ, and AdANNS-OPQ at matched code size. We also evaluate a low-build-cost PCA-scalar design that attains quality comparable to RaBitQ and its extension while fitting 420x faster at the median. Finally, we report two negative results: QINCo2 collapses when trained at high rates, and a ranking-bound hypothesis misses its pre-specified acceptance criteria. MRVQ is therefore a low-memory operating point for elastic retrieval, not a universal quality winner.

</details>


### [224] [Transcriptome-informed multi-modal AI for predicting neoadjuvant therapy response from breast cancer biopsies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03693)
*Jungkyu Park,Dhruva Biswas,Joseph Cappadona,Cerise Tang,Ken G. Zeng,Bartosz Machura,Chuwen Liu,Paolo Tarantino,Coral Omene,Francisco J. Esteva,Rohit Bhargava,Marcin Braun,Kamila Paździerz,Jakub Czerwiński,Hanna Romańska-Knight,Albert Grinshpun,Bareket Daniel,Michele Buchinger,Frederick Howard,Piotr Wysocki,Brie Chun,Freya Schnabel,Rich Caruana,Jan Witowski,Krzysztof J. Geras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个两阶段AI模型，先从组织病理图像推断转录组，再结合临床变量预测乳腺癌新辅助治疗的病理完全缓解（pCR），在1,412例患者中取得0.79的汇总AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤学中标注数据稀缺限制了深度学习生物标志物的发展，需要一种在数据稀疏场景下仍能泛化的方法。

Method: 两阶段AI模型：第一阶段利用32种癌症类型、8,742例患者的组织病理学习转录组（经病理学家审阅和空间表达一致性验证）；第二阶段将pCR预测简化为基于推断表达与临床变量的任务。开发用1,080例（5个队列），评估用1,412例（9个队列）。

Result: 汇总AUROC为0.79（95% CI 0.73-0.85），可在分子亚型内区分应答者，优于组织病理生物标志物，在瘤内采样变化和极少活检组织下保持稳定。消融实验显示转录组范围推断优于单用临床变量或单阶段病理模型，并因避开基因组检测的基因选择限制而更具稳健性。

Conclusion: 生物学信息引导的压缩方法可能泛化到精准肿瘤学中的数据稀疏应用。

Abstract: Scarcity of labeled data limits development of deep learning biomarkers in oncology. We develop a two-stage AI model predicting pathological complete response (pCR) to neoadjuvant therapy in breast cancer. The first stage learns the transcriptome from histopathology using 8,742 patients across 32 cancer types, corroborated by pathologist review and spatial agreement with measured expression. This simplifies the second stage to predicting pCR from inferred expression and clinical variables. Developed using 1,080 patients (five cohorts) and evaluated in 1,412 patients (nine cohorts), the model achieves a pooled AUROC of 0.79 (95% CI, 0.73-0.85), discriminating responders within molecular subtypes. It outperforms histopathological biomarkers, remaining stable across intratumoral sampling and with minimal biopsy tissue. Ablations show transcriptome-wide inference improves discrimination over clinical variables alone or one-stage pathology models, and robustness by avoiding genomic assays' gene selection constraints. These results indicate that biologically informed compression may generalize to data-sparse applications in precision oncology.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [225] [Hybrid Machine Learning-Assisted Raman Spectroscopy with Generative Feature Augmentation for Pharmaceutical Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02224)
*Quach Thi Thai Binh,Ton Nu Quynh Trang,Thang B. Phan,Vu Thi Hanh Thu,Nguyen Tuan Hung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HyMLRaman混合拉曼光谱框架，结合深度特征提取、生成模型与经典机器学习分类器，实现六种药物残留的高精度识别（96.31%）并验证了低数据场景下的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速可靠地识别药物残留对保障公共健康、食品安全及实现基于拉曼光谱的实际筛查至关重要，但现有方法在数据有限和分类性能上仍有不足。

Method: 将拉曼光谱转换为光谱图像，用多种深度网络骨干编码，其中EfficientNet-B3表现最佳，输出1536维嵌入；再用SVM、KNN、逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost和ANN等分类器结合分层10折交叉验证进行训练；并引入基于DDPM的生成式特征增强，在PCA降维的EfficientNet-B3潜空间中缓解数据不足问题。

Result: EfficientNet-B3--SVM混合配置取得最强基线性能，准确率96.31%，宏F1为96.36%，优于独立CNN基线；低数据消融显示DDPM增强带来选择性收益，尤其对训练样本比例减少时的KNN有效，且效果依赖分类器；应用级拉曼药物分析器验证了模型嵌入交互式工作流的可行性。

Conclusion: HyMLRaman为快速、可解释的拉曼药物筛查提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Rapid and reliable identification of pharmaceutical residues is important for safeguarding public health, ensuring food safety, and enabling practical Raman-based screening. In this study, we propose HyMLRaman, a hybrid Raman spectroscopy framework that combines deep spectral feature extraction, generative models, and classical machine-learning classifiers to identify six pharmaceutical compounds, including amoxicillin, chloramphenicol, ciprofloxacin, tetracycline, ibuprofen, and paracetamol. Raman spectra are converted into spectral images and encoded with several deep neural-network backbones, among which EfficientNet-B3 yields the most effective representation. The resulting 1536-dimensional embeddings are then used to train downstream classifiers, including SVM, KNN, logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, and ANN, using stratified 10-fold cross-validation. The hybrid EfficientNet-B3--SVM configuration achieves the strongest baseline performance, reaching 96.31% accuracy and a macro-F1 score of 96.36%, outperforming the standalone CNN baseline. To address limited-data conditions, a generative model, a DDPM-based feature augmentation, is introduced in a PCA-reduced EfficientNet-B3 latent space. The low-data ablation results show that DDPM augmentation provides selective benefits, particularly for KNN with reduced training fractions, and that its effect remains classifier-dependent. Finally, an application-level Raman Pharmaceutical Analyzer demonstrates the feasibility of embedding the trained model into an interactive Raman analysis workflow. These results suggest that HyMLRaman provides a practical and interpretable route for rapid Raman-based pharmaceutical screening.

</details>


### [226] [The Price of Greenwashing: Algorithmic Verification and Market Discipline using Conformal Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02225)
*Sourav Bose,Taoufik Bouraoui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文融合SEC财务数据与EPA设施级温室气体登记数据，构建机器学习基线量化企业排放自报数据与物理现实的偏差，提出CWCD指标，并证明该偏差显著负向预测企业后续市场估值与盈利能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业可持续性披露要求日益扩展，但市场严重依赖企业自报排放数据，导致"漂绿"风险普遍存在。现有文献多依赖主观ESG评级或文本情感分析，缺乏客观量化物理气候现实的计量方法，形成关键研究缺口。

Method: 将美国SEC财务基本面数据与EPA设施级温室气体登记数据融合，建立具有数学保证的企业物理排放基线；采用梯度提升模型与Mondrian Conformal Prediction，量化自报数据与算法基线的缺口，构造"Conformal-Weighted Continuous Divergence（CWCD）"指标；再用横截面领先-滞后计量设计评估该偏差的市场效应。

Result: 算法排放偏差与随后的市场估值（Tobin's Q）和经营盈利能力（ROA）呈显著负相关，效果稳健且统计显著。这否证了"市场失灵/市场盲目"假说，表明机构资本已将环境欺骗定价为基本面管理不善的领先指标。

Conclusion: 研究证明市场能够识别并惩罚环境信息造假，为资产管理者和监管者大规模部署算法审计基础设施提供了量化依据。

Abstract: While corporate sustainability mandates are expanding, the systemic reliance on self-reported emissions data exposes financial markets to pervasive greenwashing. Current literature relies heavily on subjective ESG ratings or textual sentiment analysis, leaving a critical econometric gap in objectively quantifying physical climate realities. To resolve this information asymmetry, we fuse U.S. SEC financial fundamentals with facility-level EPA greenhouse gas registries to establish a mathematically guaranteed baseline of physical corporate emissions. Leveraging a gradient boosting architecture and Mondrian Conformal Prediction, we quantify the shortfall between self-reported data and this algorithmic baseline into a novel Conformal-Weighted Continuous Divergence (CWCD) metric. Evaluating this divergence via a cross-sectional lead-lag econometric design, we uncover a robust mechanism of market discipline: algorithmic emissions divergence exhibits a severe, statistically significant negative relationship with subsequent market valuation (Tobin's Q) and operational profitability (ROA). Providing definitive evidence against the market blindness hypothesis, this study proves that institutional capital actively prices environmental deception not merely as an ethical lapse, but as a leading indicator of fundamental corporate mismanagement. Ultimately, these findings provide the quantitative justification necessary for asset managers and regulators to deploy algorithmic auditing infrastructure at scale.

</details>


### [227] [State-Space Unlearning for Non-Stationary Bias in Land Surface Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02248)
*Anidipta Pal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SSU-LSF，首个面向地学Mamba状态空间模型的地表预测机器遗忘框架，可定位并消除状态转移矩阵中吸收的混杂事件影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业务化地表预测系统基于Mamba族结构化状态空间模型，会将未记录的灌溉骤增、大坝调度变化、传感器重校准等非平稳混杂事件悄悄吸收进状态转移矩阵，在物理成因消失后仍长期偏置NDVI、LST与作物物候预测，需要针对性遗忘机制。

Method: 提出EKFac影响函数，通过闭式矩阵指数梯度专门适配Mamba状态矩阵；用谱半径加权肘部阈值法定位时间混杂足迹Φ；在KL散度信赖域内执行Hessian-free投影梯度上升，并加入空间全变分(TV)正则。理论证明命题1：残余混杂上界为O((1-ρ(Ā)^{T_c})/((1-ρ(Ā))μ))，随窗口长度T_c增长。

Result: 在三个异构基准与十一个基线上，混杂削减率分别为0.773(CropHarvest)、0.821(NDVI-LST)、0.859(ERA5)；ERA5干净域RMSE最差退化仅4.2%；3–5轮收敛，单次遗忘请求GPU开销比全量重训练低8.4倍。

Conclusion: SSU-LSF是首个面向地学Mamba状态空间模型的地表预测机器遗忘框架，可在低算力代价下有效消除状态转移矩阵中的时间混杂，同时保持干净域性能，为业务化地表预测提供可行的遗忘方案。

Abstract: Operational land surface forecasting systems built on Mamba-family Structured State Space Models absorb non-stationary confounding events (unrecorded irrigation booms, dam-operation shifts, sensor recalibrations) into their state-transition matrices, silently biasing NDVI, LST, and crop phenology predictions long after the physical cause ends. This paper introduces SSU-LSF (State-Space Unlearning for Land Surface Forecasting), the first machine-unlearning framework purpose-built for geoscientific Mamba-based SSMs. We develop EKFac influence functions specialized to the Mamba state matrices via a closed-form matrix-exponential gradient, use spectral-radius-weighted elbow thresholding to localize a temporal confounding footprint $Φ$, and apply Hessian-free projected gradient ascent within a KL-divergence trust region augmented by spatial total-variation (TV) regularization. Proposition 1 establishes that residual confounding is bounded by $\mathcal{O}\big((1-ρ(\bar{A})^{T_c})/((1-ρ(\bar{A}))μ)\big)$, which grows with the window length $T_c$. Across three heterogeneous benchmarks and eleven baselines, SSU-LSF achieves confounding reduction rates of $0.773$ (CropHarvest), $0.821$ (NDVI-LST), and $0.859$ (ERA5), with worst-case clean-domain RMSE degradation of $4.2\%$ on ERA5, converging in 3--5 epochs at $8.4\times$ lower GPU-cost per unlearning request than full retraining. Code: https://github.com/Anidipta/SSU-LSF

</details>


### [228] [Nearest-neighbour baselines for fingerprint prediction from MS/MS spectra under different assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02249)
*Ling Min Serena Khoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该报告系统比较了多种最近邻检索变体，揭示不同推理假设如何影响MS/MS谱预测分子指纹的性能，旨在建立更严格的基线以促进更严谨的评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明最近邻检索是MS/MS谱预测分子指纹的强基线，部分变体可与深度学习模型媲美甚至更优。但"最近邻"包含一系列在推理时假设可用信息不同的方法，这些差异影响性能，需系统梳理以建立更严格的基线。

Method: 系统比较多种最近邻检索变体，分析各变体在推理阶段所假设可用信息的不同，并评估这些假设对性能的影响。

Result: 不同最近邻变体因推理假设差异而表现出不同的性能，说明检索方法的选择与假设设置对结果有显著影响。

Conclusion: 应建立更严格的基线，以实现更严谨的评测，并更准确地衡量该领域的进展。

Abstract: It has recently been shown that nearest-neighbour retrieval provides a strong baseline for molecular fingerprint prediction from MS/MS spectra, with several variants matching or outperforming current deep learning models (Khoo and Barzilay, 2026; Liu et al., 2026; Gupta et al., 2026). Importantly, "nearest neighbour" encompasses a family of retrieval methods that differ in the information assumed to be available at inference. In this report, we systematically compare several nearest-neighbour variants and show how these differing assumptions affect performance. Our goal is to establish stricter baselines that enable more rigorous benchmarking and better measure progress in this area.

</details>


### [229] [Rank-Aware Speculative Sampling for Diffusion Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02251)
*Marcello Bullo,Yanxiao Liu,Öykü Sıla Güner,Arpan Mukherjee,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RASS，一种基于秩感知列表耦合的扩散模型推测采样验证规则，利用草稿候选的排序信息提升验证效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树状推测采样方法（如 D-GRS）比单链草稿更能有效利用并行算力，但其条件独立生成的候选虽然蕴含排序信息，却未被利用；作者希望在不增加目标模型评估的前提下挖掘这一排序信息。

Method: RASS 沿提议-目标的均值位移对草稿候选排序，按优化后的秩权重采样一个秩，以最小化所选提议分布与目标分布之间的全变差；随后将所选候选与目标做最大耦合，并通过残差校正保证任意秩权重下采样的精确性。

Result: 在高斯混合目标、FFHQ 无条件像素生成、CIFAR-10 条件生成以及使用 COCO2014 提示的 Stable Diffusion 3.5 潜空间扩散上评估；以标准采样与推测采样的目标模型评估次数之比衡量，RASS 在各设定下均优于 D-GRS，在匹配算力预算下 CIFAR-10 上增益约达 20%。

Conclusion: 利用草稿候选的秩信息进行列表耦合验证，可在保持精确采样（目标分布不变）的同时提升推测采样的加速效果，验证了秩感知验证规则的有效性。

Abstract: Speculative sampling accelerates diffusion generation by verifying inexpensive draft states in parallel while preserving the target law. Recent tree-based methods allocate the parallel compute budget more effectively than single-chain drafts, as demonstrated by Diffusion Greedy Rejection Sampling (D-GRS). D-GRS generates $K$ conditionally independent candidates per node, and sequentially tests them in their generation order. Yet the sampled candidates admit an informative ranking without additional target-model evaluations. To exploit this, we introduce Rank-Aware Speculative Sampling (RASS), a verification rule for speculative draft trees based on rank-aware list coupling. RASS orders draft candidates along the proposal-target mean displacement and samples a rank with weights optimized to minimize total variation between the selected-proposal and target laws. Finally, the selected candidate is maximally coupled with the target, with residual correction ensuring exact sampling for any choice of rank weights. We evaluate RASS on a Gaussian-mixture target, unconditional pixel-space generation on FFHQ, conditional generation on CIFAR-10, and latent diffusion with Stable Diffusion 3.5 using COCO2014 prompts. Measured by the ratio of standard to speculative sampling's target-model evaluation counts, RASS improves on D-GRS across the evaluated settings, with gains reaching approximately 20% on CIFAR-10 at matched compute budgets.

</details>


### [230] [Counterfactual Predictions in Scientific Emulators Without Controlled Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02252)
*Dingling Yao,Kahaan Gandhi,Valentin Duruisseaux,Boris Bonev,Francesco Locatello,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ReRoute 框架，无需受控干预数据即可实现科学 what-if 预测：将预训练主干网络中被查询的输入固定到参考值，再通过已知机制路径重新引入其变化，并仅在原始事实数据上微调，从而在相关性输入独立变动时给出准确的反事实预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学问题常涉及对未观测情形的推理（如条件、干预或历史不同会怎样）。模型在观测数据上预测准确，但当相关输入被独立变动时，若不做干预则会在 what-if 查询上失败。常见补救是加入受控仿真数据显式解耦各因素，但这依赖模拟器、计算昂贵，并继承模拟器的建模假设。

Method: ReRoute 将事实数据与部分机制知识结合，无需受控干预数据进行适配。具体地，把预训练主干网络中查询的输入固定为参考值，通过已知机制路径重新引入其变化，并在原始事实数据上微调，下游效应留给学习到的动力学。作者给出该构造在明确结构假设下的因果可识别性结果，核心论证用 Lean 做了机器化验证。

Result: 在可获取精确响应的受控平流-扩散系统中，ReRoute 达到高精度反事实预测。在先进气候仿真上，于留出的耦合气候干预中，CO2 分布严重偏移下综合气候误差降低 18.2%-31.8%，同时保持标准条件下的技能，成本仅为在额外受控仿真上重训的一小部分，且未计入生成此类数据的巨大开销。在由历史 ERA5 再分析数据训练的仿真器上，虽无反事实参考，ReRoute 在固定 CO2 反事实下仍保留显著更多的、由观测边界条件所隐含的地表增暖。

Conclusion: ReRoute 无需受控干预数据即可实现目标化科学 what-if 预测，在理论上具备因果可识别性并获机器化验证，在受控系统与气候仿真中均显著提升反事实预测精度，同时大幅降低成本，为数据稀缺或无法干预场景下的反事实推理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Many scientific questions require reasoning about what was never observed: What if the conditions, interventions, or history had been different? Models can predict accurately on observed data yet fail on such what-if queries when correlated inputs are varied independently. A common remedy is to add controlled simulation data in which these factors are explicitly disentangled, but this requires access to a simulator, can be computationally expensive, and inherits the simulator's modeling assumptions. We introduce ReRoute, a framework for targeted scientific what-if prediction that combines factual data with partial mechanistic knowledge, without requiring controlled intervention data for adaptation. ReRoute fixes the queried input of a pretrained backbone to a reference value, reintroduces its variation through a known mechanistic pathway, and fine-tunes on the original factual data, while leaving downstream effects to the learned dynamics. We provide a causal identification result for this construction under explicit structural assumptions, with the core argument machine-checked in Lean. After showing that ReRoute achieves highly accurate counterfactual predictions in a controlled advection-diffusion system where exact responses are available, we turn to state-of-the-art climate emulation. On held-out coupled-climate interventions, ReRoute reduces aggregate climate error by 18.2-31.8% under severe CO$_2$ distribution shifts while preserving skill under standard conditions, at a small fraction of the cost of retraining on additional controlled simulations, without even accounting for the substantial expense of generating such data. Finally, on an emulator trained from historical ERA5 reanalysis, where no counterfactual reference exists, ReRoute preserves substantially more of the surface warming implied by the observed boundary conditions under a fixed-CO$_2$ counterfactual.

</details>


### [231] [Approximation Property of Dropout Neural Networks: Sobolev Rates and Confidence Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02253)
*Jia-He Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究稀疏（dropout）ReLU 网络对 $W^{n,\infty}([0,1]^d)$ 单位球的均匀逼近：构造常数深度、规模约 $\widetilde O_{n,d}(p^{-9}\varepsilon^{-\max\{d/n,2\}}\log(1/\delta))$ 的网络，使单次随机采样以概率 $1-\delta$ 达到精度 $\varepsilon$；并给出基于 Sobolev 容量的下界，在若干参数区间内上下界在精度指数上匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用逼近性只能说明存在这样的网络，却无法刻画单次随机采样（边以概率 $p$ 保留）所需的具体网络规模。论文旨在定量描述 dropout 网络在"单样本、高概率"意义下逼近光滑函数时的规模—精度—置信度三者关系。

Method: 对 $[0,1]^d$ 上 $W^{n,\infty}$ 单位球进行均匀逼近，构造常数深度 ReLU 网络。证明策略结合三部分：有界局部子网络、在"成功逼近事件"上的局部化、以及多尺度 Taylor 分解。下界方面，用 Sobolev 容量约束存活边数量，并分析固定仿射函数逼近时输出层所需代价。

Result: 上界：常数深度、规模 $\widetilde O_{n,d}(p^{-9}\varepsilon^{-\max\{d/n,2\}}\log(1/\delta))$，以概率 $\geq 1-\delta$ 成立。下界：存活边数受 Sobolev 容量限制；逼近固定仿射函数需输出层代价 $\Theta\big(((1-p)/p)\varepsilon^{-2}\log(1/\delta)\big)$。当 $p\in(0,1)$ 固定、$\delta<\min\{1/2,1-p\}$ 时，在固定或对数深度预算下上下界精度指数匹配；当 $d\le 2n$ 时置信度依赖也匹配（至精度对数因子）。下界可推广到 $W^{n,r}$ 目标与 $L^s$ 误差，并区别于 $W^{n,\infty}$ 的上界。

Conclusion: dropout 网络的随机逼近规模可以被精确刻画：构造与下界在精度指数（部分情形含置信度）上匹配。最优的保留概率 $p$ 依赖关系及对数因子仍是开放问题。

Abstract: The universal approximation property of dropout neural networks does not by itself describe the network size required for an accurate random realization. In this work, we study approximation of the unit ball of $W^{n,\infty}([0,1]^d)$ by ReLU networks whose edges are retained independently with probability $p$. The approximation error is measured uniformly over the input domain, and the guarantee holds with probability at least $1-δ$ for a single sampled network. We construct networks of constant depth and size $\widetilde O_{n,d}(p^{-9}\varepsilon^{-\max\{d/n,2\}} \log(1/δ))$. The construction combines bounded local subnetworks, localization on a successful approximation event, and a multiscale Taylor decomposition. Conversely, Sobolev capacity imposes a lower bound on the number of surviving edges, while approximation of a fixed affine function requires an output-layer cost of order $((1-p)/p)\varepsilon^{-2}\log(1/δ)$ at sufficiently high confidence. For fixed $p\in(0,1)$ and $δ<\min\{1/2,1-p\}$, the upper and lower bounds match in the accuracy exponent under a fixed or logarithmic depth budget. When $d\leq2n$, they also match in confidence up to logarithms of accuracy. We extend the lower bounds to $W^{n,r}$ targets with $L^s$ error, and distinguish this extension from the upper bound for $W^{n,\infty}$. The optimal retention dependence and logarithmic factors remain open.

</details>


### [232] [Overcoming Challenges of Interpretive Structural Modeling with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02254)
*Everett Rush,David J. Icove,Ari Kim,Byung H. Park,Michael A. Langston*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索将大语言模型（LLM）与解释结构建模（ISM）集成，用LLM充当"不完全专家"自动完成因果图发现，以解决传统ISM依赖专家反复交互、难以扩展到数百变量的瓶颈，并对比了四种发现方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ISM建模依赖与领域专家的反复交互直至达成共识，过程繁琐、耗费人力，最关键的是限制了ISM扩展到含数百个变量的研究。因此作者希望借助已有的"LLM作为不完全专家进行因果图发现"的工作，提升ISM的可扩展性。

Method: 提出集成的LLM-ISM方法，让LLM代替专家承担ISM中的因果图发现任务，并对四种图发现方法（成对pairwise、k元k-wise、按行rowwise、全图full graph）进行比较与评估。

Result: 因果图发现方法中，按行（rowwise）方法表现最佳（SHD=160，F1-score=0.77），全图（full graph）方法次之（SHD=135，F1-score=0.73）。

Conclusion: LLM-ISM集成方案在ISM因果图发现上可行，rowwise与full graph方法效果较好，为ISM向大规模变量研究扩展提供了新思路。

Abstract: Interpretive Structural Modeling (ISM) is a well-known process for multi-criteria decision making. The success of ISM over other methodologies is its ability to model causal relationships, the binary scale of factors, and resulting hierarchical representation. Traditionally, the modeling process is performed by repeated interactions with subject matter experts until consensus is reached. This process is tedious, labor-intense, and most importantly limits the ability of ISM to scale to studies with hundreds of variables. Drawing on existing work of causal graph discovery with large language models (LLM) as imperfect experts, this work explores an integrated LLM-ISM approach for ISM. Pairwise, k-wise, rowwise, and full graph discovery methodologies are compared and evaluated. It is shown that causal graph discovery methods for ISM perform best using rowwise (SHD=160, F1-score=0.77) and full graph methods (SHD=135, F1-score=0.73).

</details>


### [233] [MACTS-EM: Multi-Agent Collaborative Time Series Forecasting with Emergent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02255)
*Ahmad Shahi,Mamehgol Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MACTS-EM框架，通过多智能体协作与涌现记忆机制提升时间序列预测性能，在金融、气候、能源和疫情传播等场景取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测在众多领域仍是关键挑战。现有方法难以应对机制转换（regime shifts）、跨域知识迁移以及多模态数据集成等复杂现象，因此需要超越传统架构的新方法。

Method: 提出MACTS-EM框架，包含五个核心组件：(1) 面向领域专业化的预测智能体，负责模式识别、异常检测、因果推断与不确定性量化；(2) 用于动态智能体分配的元认知层；(3) 支持跨域模式迁移的涌现记忆机制；(4) 多模态上下文集成；(5) 对抗鲁棒性组件。

Result: 在金融市场、气候模式、能源消费和疫情传播场景的评估中，MACTS-EM在多数情境下优于现有方法：预测精度提升8–12%，零样本迁移能力提升22–27%，机制转换期间韧性增强16–21%，分布偏移后恢复速度加快15–18%。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，采用协作式、智能体化的时间序列预测方法，有望超越传统架构，特别适用于需要多分辨率时间理解和上下文适应的复杂现实场景。

Abstract: Time series forecasting remains a critical challenge across numerous domains. Despite significant advancements, existing approaches struggle with complex phenomena such as regime shifts, cross-domain knowledge transfer, and multimodal data integration. This paper introduces Multi-Agent Collaborative Time Series Forecasting with Emergent Memory (MACTS-EM), a novel framework where specialised agents collaborate to achieve superior forecasting performance. The MACTS-EM architecture integrates: (1) domain-specialised forecasting agents for pattern recognition, anomaly detection, causal inference, and uncertainty quantification; (2) a meta-cognitive layer for dynamic agent allocation; (3) an emergent memory mechanism enabling cross-domain pattern transfer; (4) multimodal contextual integration; and (5) adversarial robustness components. Evaluation across financial markets, climate patterns, energy consumption, and pandemic propagation demonstrates that MACTS-EM outperforms existing approaches in most scenarios, with 8-12% improvement in forecasting accuracy, 22-27% better zero-shot transfer capability, 16-21% enhanced resilience during regime shifts, and 15-18% faster recovery after distribution shifts. Our findings suggest that collaborative, agentic approaches to time series forecasting represent a promising direction beyond traditional architectures, particularly for complex real-world scenarios requiring multi-resolution temporal understanding and contextual adaptation.

</details>


### [234] [TRACE: A Reproducible Benchmark for Electricity Price Forecasting with Official Operational Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02256)
*Xinyi Yi,Moy Yuan,Ioannis Lestas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出TRACE基准，将预测时点可获取的官方运营文本与电价配对，以评估文本信息对电价预测的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电价预测基准主要关注数值输入，未充分评估预测时点文本上下文对预测的价值。

Method: 构建包含7,300个区域-日实例的可复现基准，将美国某主要市场五个区域的价格与预测截止时点可得的官方运营文本配对；重建截止时点运营文本以避免未来信息泄漏；进行语义对齐与预测价值评估，并开展跨日文本错配消融实验。

Result: 语义评估与真实价格的中心走势及两侧尾部风险一致，上尾风险最稳定；预测价值体现为时间序列基础模型上尾pinball损失中位数降低7.4%；跨日文本错配消融后收益逆转，低于无文本基线。

Conclusion: TRACE表明，预测时点文本上下文对电价预测具有实际价值，尤其在上尾风险预测上。

Abstract: Electricity price forecasting (EPF) supports scheduling, bidding, and risk management in electricity markets, yet existing benchmarks focus mainly on numerical inputs, leaving the forecasting value of forecast-time textual context insufficiently evaluated. We introduce TRACE, a reproducible benchmark of 7,300 zone--day instances pairing prices from five zones in a major U.S. market with official operational text available at the forecast cutoff. TRACE reconstructs official operational text at each cutoff, preventing post-cutoff information leakage. We evaluate TRACE for semantic alignment and forecasting value. Semantic assessments align with central movement and both tail risks in ground-truth prices, most consistently for upper-tail price risk. Forecasting value is reflected in a median 7.4\% reduction in upper-tail pinball loss across time-series foundation models. A controlled cross-day text-mismatch ablation reverses the gains, falling below the no-text baseline.

</details>


### [235] [Parameter-Free Interval-Dynamic Regret under Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02258)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究每轮仅有一个无偏随机次梯度的在线凸优化，在未知有限条件 p 阶噪声矩（1<p≤2）下，提出一种无需知道 G、σ、p、区间 I、P_I 的 learner，实现区间自适应遗憾界，并兼顾均值梯度与噪声项的独立指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线凸优化的遗憾界通常依赖梯度范数、噪声矩、时间区间等参数，而这些量在实际中往往未知。作者希望在每轮只有一个无偏随机次梯度、且条件 p 阶噪声矩未知的情况下，实现对任意区间和比较器路径的自适应最优保证。

Method: 方法基于对校准的期望控制与观测尺度变化成本限制的分析，给出一般性定理：与可预测可用专家的分布比较，并带有对非均匀先验的相对熵依赖。采用共同先验偏向长窗口和长重启长度，从而在给定统计量下使区间代价变为 1+log(T/n)。理论工具包括变测度下界，用于识别保持全时最优保证的 learner 中该对数的噪声幂次。

Result: 对任意长度 n 的固定区间 I 和比较器路径，learner 达到 E[Regret_I(u)] ≤ min(GDn, C[GD√(n(Λ_I+log^2(2T))) + σD n^{1/p}(Λ_I+log^2(2T))^{(p-1)/p}])，其中 Λ_I=1+P_I/D，常数为通用常数。区间自适应增加了比较器复杂度，但保留了均值梯度与噪声的独立指数。共同先验下区间代价为 1+log(T/n)，包含最优全时静态速率。变测度下界在显式条件下识别了该对数的噪声幂次。静态比较和确定性划分也可由同一决策导出。

Conclusion: 本文给出了未知条件 p 阶噪声矩下、每轮单个无偏随机次梯度的区间自适应在线凸优化方法，实现了对任意区间与比较器路径的近优遗憾界，并刻画了全时最优保证下对数项噪声幂次的根本下界。

Abstract: We study online convex optimization with one unbiased stochastic subgradient per round and an unknown finite conditional $p$th noise moment, $1<p\le2$. For every fixed interval $I$ of length $n$ and comparator path with $Λ_I=1+P_I/D$, one learner achieves
  \[ E[Regret_I(u)]\le\min(GDn, C[GD\sqrt{n(Λ_I+\log^2(2T))} +σDn^{1/p}(Λ_I+\log^2(2T))^{(p-1)/p}]). \]
  The learner uses none of $G,σ,p,I,P_I$, and the constant is universal. Interval adaptation adds to comparator complexity, preserving the distinct mean-gradient and noise exponents. The analysis controls calibration in expectation and limits the cost of observation-scale changes. Its general theorem compares to distributions over predictably available experts with relative-entropy dependence on a nonuniform prior. A common prior favors long windows and long restart lengths. With the statistics supplied, the interval cost becomes $1+\log(T/n)$, including the optimal full-horizon static rate. A change-of-measure lower bound identifies the noise power of this logarithm for learners retaining a full-horizon optimal guarantee, under explicit conditions. Static comparisons and deterministic partitions follow from the same decisions.

</details>


### [236] [From Mathematical to Executable Certificates for Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02268)
*Ziyu Zhao,Xinyu Wang,Xiaowen Chang,Yixuan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出可执行发布认证（ExecCert），在模型发布时对实际产出的有限精度工件进行认证，弥合数学保证与实际部署之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘在数据删除、记录过期或数据质量问题时十分必要，而从头重训练代价高昂。已有的认证式遗忘方法仅提供数学保证，但实际部署系统释放的是软件生成的有限精度工件，二者之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 提出ExecCert发布时认证层：要么为已执行候选关闭方法的原生证书，要么应用重训练参考发布验证（RRV）来认证其对当前保留集重训练的保真度。针对顺序删除中参考与存储数值状态分别演化的问题，对冻结表示加可变岭回归头的情形，开发了RRV的增量实现，跨删除请求维持认证证据而非每次发布重构。

Result: 在四个已发表的遗忘实现上，ExecCert保持有效证书、改变发布决策、收紧保守边界，并识别出具体输出所支持的重训练参考保真度。在顺序服务实验中，RRV消除了由存储方程验证导致的错误发布，同时紧密跟踪实际误差；增量认证在发布检查足够频繁时比新建和维持验证因子方案都更便宜。

Conclusion: ExecCert通过发布时认证将数学保证与可部署工件对齐，RRV提供了对重训练参考的保真认证，增量实现进一步降低了频繁发布检查的成本，为实际部署提供可行路径。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is needed when data must be removed because of deletion requests, outdated records, or data-quality concerns, while retraining from scratch can be costly. Certified machine unlearning methods provide mathematical guarantees, while deployed systems release concrete finite-precision artifacts produced by software. To bridge the gap between mathematical guarantees and practical deployment, we introduce Executable Release Certification (ExecCert), a release-time layer that certifies the candidate artifact considered for release. ExecCert either closes a method's native certificate for the executed candidate or applies Retraining-Reference Release Verification (RRV) to certify fidelity to current retain-set retraining. Sequential deletion makes the latter nontrivial because the exact retain-set reference and the stored numerical state evolve separately. For frozen representations with a mutable ridge head, we develop an incremental realization of RRV that maintains certified evidence across deletion requests rather than reconstructing it at each release. On four published unlearning implementations, ExecCert preserves valid certificates, changes release decisions, tightens conservative bounds, and identifies the retraining-reference fidelity supported by concrete outputs. In sequential-service experiments, RRV eliminates false releases caused by stored-equation verification while closely tracking realized error, and incremental certification remains cheaper than both fresh and maintained verified-factor alternatives once release checks become sufficiently frequent.

</details>


### [237] [CLEAN: Psychometrically Consistent Incremental Cognitive Diagnosis under Concept-Space Expansion via Architectural Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02278)
*Tao He,Jinxing Xiang,Fan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLEAN是一个支持概念空间扩展的增量认知诊断框架，通过架构隔离为历史诊断的逐点不变性提供结构性保证，实现零表征漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增量认知诊断模型假设概念空间固定，新题目引入未见概念时梯度会覆盖历史路径导致灾难性遗忘；且仅依赖软约束难以满足认知诊断中心理测量一致性所要求的诊断不变性。

Method: CLEAN引入严格拓扑二分协议，冻结历史诊断函数并用确定性正交列掩码切断梯度干扰；采用可扩展满秩分支配合微方差初始化学习新概念，支持概念扩展；并形式化表征漂移（RD）以量化历史特征扰动。

Result: 在三个大规模教育数据集上，CLEAN实现零RD，旧题目指标与静态基线完全一致，同时在新题目上达到或优于强持续学习基线。

Conclusion: 通过架构隔离而非软约束，CLEAN在支持概念空间扩展的同时从构造上保证了历史诊断的不变性，验证了结构设计对心理测量一致性的有效性。

Abstract: Cognitive diagnosis (CD) is a fundamental task in intelligent education that profiles learner proficiency over knowledge concepts. In real-world learning platforms, newly added items continually introduce previously unseen concepts, necessitating dynamic expansion of the underlying concept space. Yet existing incremental CD models assume a fixed concept space, allowing gradients from new items to overwrite historical pathways and induce catastrophic forgetting. More critically, these methods rely solely on soft constraints to preserve historical diagnoses. Such constraints may fail to satisfy the requirement of diagnostic invariance after incremental updates, a requirement known as psychometric consistency in cognitive diagnosis. Therefore, we propose CLEAN (Continual Learning with Expandable and Architecturally Isolated Networks), a novel incremental CD framework supporting concept-space expansion while providing structural guarantees for pointwise invariance of historical diagnoses. Specifically, CLEAN first introduces a strict topological bipartition protocol, freezes historical diagnostic functions and applies deterministic orthogonal column masking to sever gradient interference. Second, to accommodate concept expansion, expandable full-rank branches with micro-variance initialization are deployed to learn novel concepts. Finally, to verify that this architectural design achieves invariance by construction, we formalize Representation Drift (RD) to quantify the perturbation of historical traits. Extensive experiments on three large-scale educational datasets demonstrate that CLEAN achieves zero RD, preserving old-item metrics identically to static anchors through architectural isolation while remaining competitive with or superior to strong continual-learning baselines on new items.

</details>


### [238] [MuLoRA: Spectrally Balanced Low-Rank Adaptation for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02283)
*Junkang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别出LoRA在持续学习中的"谱可塑性崩溃"问题，并提出MuLoRA，通过联合控制容量分配与利用来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA的名义秩可能掩盖有效适应能力的损失。作者发现顺序适应过程中，更新能量会集中在少数奇异模式上，导致低秩空间大量未被利用。这揭示了仅靠避免干扰的局限：保护历史表示不足以保证剩余适应容量对新任务敏感并被有效利用。

Method: MuLoRA包含两个机制：一是历史白化，通过识别相对于累积历史响应具有强当前任务响应的输入方向，得到任务自适应基并在训练中固定；二是对动量更新做近似极正交化，降低所选空间内的谱集中。正交基连接两机制，将因子更新谱精确传递到诱导的权重更新。作者还建立了精确子空间选择的max-min刻画，并在受控跨步各向异性下推导累积谱界。

Result: 在五个类增量基准和八个增量设置下，MuLoRA在16个报告指标中有15个取得最高平均准确率。

Conclusion: MuLoRA通过同时控制适应容量的分配与利用，有效缓解谱可塑性崩溃，在持续学习任务中取得领先性能，表明干扰避免之外还需关注容量的响应性与利用率。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) provides a parameter-efficient approach to continual learning, but its nominal rank can conceal a loss of effective adaptation capacity. We identify \emph{spectral plasticity collapse}: during sequential adaptation, update energy becomes concentrated in a small subset of singular modes, leaving much of the available low-rank space underutilized. This exposes a limitation of interference avoidance alone: protecting historical representations does not ensure that the remaining adaptation capacity is responsive to new tasks or effectively utilized. To address this problem, we propose \texttt{MuLoRA}, which jointly controls capacity allocation and utilization. First, historical whitening identifies input directions with strong current-task response relative to accumulated historical response, yielding a task-adaptive basis that remains fixed during training. Second, approximate polar orthogonalization of momentum updates reduces spectral concentration within theselected space. An orthonormal basis connects these mechanisms by transferring the factor-update spectrum exactly tothe induced weight update. We establish a max--min characterization of exact subspace selection and derive cumulative spectral bounds under controlled cross-step anisotropy. Across five class-incremental benchmarks and eight incremental settings, \texttt{MuLoRA} achieves the highest mean accuracy in 15 of 16 reported metrics.

</details>


### [239] [Effects of interpulse-interval variation on deep-learning classification of bat vocalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02284)
*Welmoed R. Eversteijn,Burooj Ghani,A. Leonie Baier,Dan Stowell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究探究蝙蝠脉冲间隔（IPI）是否提供物种判别信息，以及基于Transformer的模型是否比CNN更敏感于该信息。结果表明支持证据有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间上下文可能有助于蝙蝠物种的自动分类，但具体特征（如脉冲间隔IPI）的贡献仍不清楚，且不同架构对该信息的利用能力未知。

Method: 构建两个匹配数据集：自然IPI条件（保留原始叫声时序）和归一化IPI条件（叫声起始间隔统一为50ms）。微调并评估EfficientNet-B0与PaSST；另进行跨条件实验，分别在自然与归一化IPI上训练并在同一自然IPI测试集上评估；最后评估预训练分类器BatDetect2与BAT。

Result: 条件内归一化影响因模型而异：PaSST在自然IPI（71±2.3%）与归一化IPI（70±6.3%）间差异很小，EfficientNet从47±4.7%升至57±3.9%；PaSST两条件下均优于EfficientNet。跨条件评估中，自然IPI训练的模型在自然IPI测试集上更好：EfficientNet从54%降至50%，PaSST从65%降至57%。BatDetect2与BAT在两条件间差异很小。

Conclusion: 对"自然IPI变化对蝙蝠物种分类有实质贡献"及"Transformer比CNN更有效利用该信息"的假设支持有限。但跨条件性能下降表明，归一化条件下获得的结果可能无法完全迁移到自然录音。

Abstract: Temporal context may aid automated bat-species classification, but the contribution of specific features remains unclear. We investigated whether variation in the interpulse interval (IPI)-the time between consecutive call onsets-provides species-discriminative information and whether transformer-based models are more sensitive to this information than convolutional neural networks. We created two matched datasets from European bat recordings: a natural-IPI condition retaining the original call timing and a normalized-IPI condition in which call onsets were spaced at 50-ms intervals. EfficientNet-B0 and PaSST were fine-tuned and evaluated within each condition. In an additional experiment, each architecture was trained separately on natural-IPI and normalized-IPI recordings, and evaluated on the same natural-IPI test set. Finally, the pretrained classifiers BatDetect2 and BAT were evaluated on both conditions. Within-condition IPI normalization had model-dependent effects. PaSST accuracy differed little between the natural-IPI ($71 \pm 2.3\%$) and normalized-IPI ($70 \pm 6.3\%$) conditions, whereas EfficientNet accuracy increased from $47 \pm 4.7\%$ to $57 \pm 3.9\%$. PaSST exceeded EfficientNet under both conditions. In the cross-condition evaluation, models trained on natural-IPI recordings outperformed those trained on normalized-IPI recordings on the natural-IPI test set: accuracy decreased from 54% to 50% for EfficientNet and from 65% to 57% for PaSST. BatDetect2 and BAT differed little between IPI conditions. Overall, we found limited support for the hypotheses that natural IPI variation contributes substantially to bat-species classification and that it is used more effectively by transformer-based than CNN-based models. Nevertheless, the cross-condition performance decrease shows that results obtained under normalized conditions may not transfer fully to natural recordings.

</details>


### [240] [Diffusion-Based Synthetic Data Pretraining for Enhancing Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02292)
*E. Riveros,D. Vega-Oliveros,A. Soriano-Vargas,A. Rocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在CABiGRU模型基础上，利用扩散模型生成合成传感器数据并对模型进行两阶段训练（合成数据预训练+真实数据微调），在DEO数据集上实现90.6%的平衡准确率，有效改善饮食活动识别的少数类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类活动识别（HAR）在医疗健康、日常监测中日益重要，其中饮食活动（如进食、饮水）的检测对饮食习慢和慢性病管理具有价值。然而HAR系统在细微且样本不足的类别上表现往往不佳，限制了其在真实饮食监测场景中的应用。

Method: 基于CABiGRU架构（卷积层＋双向GRU＋多头注意力＋残差连接），从智能手表的加速度计、陀螺仪和磁力计数据中提取判别性时序特征；利用扩散模型生成合成传感器数据窗口，采用两阶段训练策略：先在合成数据上预训练CABiGRU，再在真实数据上微调。

Result: 在DEO（饮水/进食/其他）数据集上，所提流程达到90.6%的平衡准确率，优于强监督基线，表明基于扩散的合成预训练对饮食活动识别具有明显收益，在应对类别不平衡方面取得进展。

Conclusion: 将扩散生成数据与针对性微调相结合，能够增强对饮食行为的鲁棒识别，支持在医疗健康和营养监测场景中更可靠的部署。

Abstract: Human activity recognition (HAR) is increasingly important for healthcare, well-being, and daily monitoring ap- plications, for which detecting alimentary activities such as eating and drinking can provide actionable insight into dietary habits and chronic disease management. HAR systems, however, often underperform on subtle and underrepresented classes, limiting their utility in real-world dietary monitoring. This work builds upon CABiGRU, a convolutional architecture with Bidirectional GRU layers, multi-head attention, and residual connections, designed to capture discriminative temporal patterns from smart- watch accelerometer, gyroscope, and magnetometer data. To improve CaBiGRU's generalization and reduce underfitting in the minority class, we leverage synthetic sensor data windows using a diffusion model and adopt a two-stage training strategy: pre-training CABiGRU on synthetic data, followed by fine-tuning on the real-world data. On the DEO (drinking/eating/other) dataset, the proposed pipeline achieves a balanced accuracy of 90.6%, improving over a strong supervised baseline and showing the benefits of diffusion-based synthetic pre-training for recognizing alimentary activities and representing a step forward dealing with unbalanced classes. These results suggest that combining diffusion-generated data with targeted fine-tuning enhances robust recognition of dietary behaviors, supporting more reliable deployment in healthcare and nutrition-monitoring settings.

</details>


### [241] [$Ψ$-Resilience: Model-Free Feature Importance from 1D Topological Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02299)
*Fabian Galis,Darian Onchis,Pedro Real Jurado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Ψ-Resilience，一种基于一维拓扑信号的无模型特征重要性方法，通过类别条件密度之差构造景观，用 0 维持续同调定义稳健性函数，在合成与真实数据上均具竞争力且可审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征重要性方法多依赖预测模型（如 SHAP），缺乏不依赖模型、可严格审计的解释方式。作者希望从数据本身直接导出解释，并引入稳健性尺度以得到上下文稳健的重要性评分。

Method: 构造类别分歧景观：估计类别条件密度，取其在特征轴上的逐点绝对差。对该一维信号计算 0 维持续同调，定义 resilience 函数，仅聚合在用户设定的稳健性尺度内仍存活的拓扑特征，得到上下文稳健的重要性分数；该分数可通过底层一维景观及其持续同调进行审计。

Result: 在具有指定真值重要性的合成数据上，方法高保真恢复特征排序，Spearman 秩相关最高达 0.8，与 SHAP、互信息等方法竞争力相当；在无真值的真实数据上，与这些方法一致，相关性最高达 0.9。

Conclusion: Ψ-Resilience 是一种稳定的解释方法，能在不依赖预测模型的情况下实现严格的、分布级别的特征重要性审计。

Abstract: We introduce $Ψ$-Resilience, a model-free feature importance method that derives explanations directly from the data itself via 1D topological signals. Our method constructs a class-disagreement landscape by estimating class-conditional densities and taking their pointwise absolute difference along the feature axis. Then, the 0-dimensional persistence of this 1D signal defines a resilience functional that aggregates only those topological features that survive perturbations up to a robustness scale which is set by the user. This gives us a context-robust importance score that is inherently auditable via the underlying 1D landscapes and their persistence. We evaluate our method on both synthetic and real datasets. On synthetic generators with specified ground-truth importance, $Ψ$-Resilience recovers the ranking of features with high fidelity, achieving Spearman rank correlations up to 0.8 and performing competitively with multiple feature importance methods, including SHAP and mutual information. On real datasets with no known ground truth, our technique agrees with these methods, with correlations up to 0.9. These results show that $Ψ$-Resilience is a stable explanation method that enables rigorous, distribution-level auditing of feature importance without relying on a predictive model.

</details>


### [242] [PowerBench: Measuring Language Model Bias in Power-shifting Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02303)
*Nicolas Martorell,Wendy Brau,Gonzalo A. Heredia,Tomás Pablo Korenblit,Gaspar Labastié,Tomás Gimenez Molina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PowerBench，一个评估语言模型在面对"权力转移请求"时如何拒绝或协助的基准，涵盖自我赋权、去权和夺权三类请求，并评测了 24 个中美模型在多种条件下的行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地协助人们处理与权力相关的请求，因此模型在"帮助谁"上的系统性差异可能大规模改变权力分布，或被用户利用来规避拒绝。作者希望将这类不对称性变得可测量。

Method: 构建并开源 PowerBench 数据集，请求在权力领域、情境、受影响方规模、用户既有权力地位上变化，并设置不涉及权力转移的"引发拒绝"对照请求。评估 24 个模型（12 个美国开发者、12 个中国开发者），在三种实验条件下测试：用户与受影响方国籍互换、AI 智能体作为用户、8 种请求语言。

Result: 模型对夺权的拒绝多于去权，对去权的拒绝多于自我赋权；对夺权的拒绝随受影响方规模（从个人到社会）上升。模型倾向帮助他人从美国夺取权力，而不愿帮助美国用户从他人处夺权，但当美国获益且无人受损时则偏向美国。当用户为 AI 智能体时，对权力转移请求的拒绝增加，尤其在针对个人的夺权中。语言会影响拒绝，但影响因模型而异，平均后大体相互抵消。

Conclusion: 作者发布 PowerBench，使这些不对称性在当前和未来模型中可被测量。

Abstract: Language models increasingly assist people with power-related requests, so systematic differences in whom they help could shift the distribution of power at scale, or be exploited by users who learn which identities are refused less. We introduce PowerBench, an evaluation of power-shifting requests that distinguishes self-empowerment, disempowerment, and power grabbing, plus a control of refusal-inducing requests that shift no power. We build, curate, and open-source a dataset of such requests varying the power domain, the context, the scale of the affected party, and the prior power standing of the user, and evaluate 24 models (12 from US and 12 from Chinese developers) under three experimental conditions: reciprocal nationalities of user and affected party, an AI agent as the user, and 8 request languages. Models refuse power grabbing more than disempowerment, and disempowerment more than self-empowerment. Refusal of power grabbing rises with the scale of the affected party, from an individual to a society. Models are biased toward helping others take power from the US and against helping US users take power from others, but favor the US when it gains power and nobody loses it. When the user is an AI agent, refusal of power-shifting requests increases, especially in power grabbing against an individual. Finally, language biases refusal, but in model-specific ways that largely cancel on average. We release PowerBench to make these asymmetries measurable in current and future models.

</details>
