### [243] [Slow-Fast Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Capability Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02324)
*Xiaofei Yin,Tong Chu,Jiyuan Fu,Jun Lan,Shuheng Zhou,Huijia Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SF-MOPD，用快慢双模型解耦学习信号，缓解多教师在线策略蒸馏中的能力干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础多模态大模型需覆盖广泛能力，而多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）可将各领域专家能力整合进单一学生模型。但 MOPD 训练会使学生逐渐偏离初始化模型，偏离越大通用能力退化越严重，产生能力干扰；直接约束学生靠近初始化又会抑制领域专长的获取，因此需要一种既能吸收专家知识又能保留通用能力的方案。

Method: SF-MOPD 将当前被各教师直接更新的学生作为“快模型”，其指数滑动平均作为“慢模型”。慢模型逐步吸收学习信号，充当融合通用基础与已确认领域专长的动态能力参考。对每个教师，在 log 概率空间中计算教师引起的更新，并仅剔除使快模型进一步偏离慢模型的分量，保留方向一致与正交的分量。

Result: 在多个模型规模上的实验表明，SF-MOPD 有效缓解能力干扰，提升多模态专长能力，同时降低通用能力基准上的平均退化，整体一致优于原始 MOPD。

Conclusion: 通过快慢模型耦合与选择性更新剔除，SF-MOPD 在多教师在线策略蒸馏中实现了专长获取与通用能力保持的更好平衡。

Abstract: Foundation multimodal large language models are designed to support a broad spectrum of capabilities across diverse domains. Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) provides an effective framework for consolidating domain-specific expertise into a single student model. However, MOPD training gradually drives the student away from its initialization model, and general capabilities decline as the displacement grows, resulting in capability interference. A direct remedy is constraining the student toward its initialization, but this suppresses the acquisition of domain expertise as well. We propose Slow-Fast Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (SF-MOPD), which couples a fast model, the current student updated directly by each teacher, with a slow model, an exponential moving average of the student. The slow model absorbs the learning signal gradually, serving as a moving capability reference that fuses the general foundation with confirmed domain expertise. For each teacher, SF-MOPD computes the teacher-induced update in log-probability space and removes only the component that pushes the fast model further away from the slow model, while retaining aligned and orthogonal components. Experiments across multiple model scales demonstrate that SF-MOPD effectively mitigates capability interference, enhances specialized multimodal capabilities, and reduces the average degradation on general-capability benchmarks, consistently outperforming vanilla MOPD.

</details>


### [244] [Drive vs. Decay: On the Training Dynamics of Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02344)
*José Lucas De Melo Costa,Seong Woo Ahn,Fabrice Popineau,Arpad Rimmel,Bich-Liên Doan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出JEPA早期训练稳定性理论，将表征坍缩的经验性启发式统一为驱动效应与衰减效应的竞争，并据此设计出ResidualPred预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA容易发生表征坍缩，现有缓解手段多依赖经验性启发式，缺乏对坍缩机制的理论解释，也缺少理论指导的稳定化设计。

Method: 在平凡不动点附近对耦合JEPA梯度流做线性化，揭示驱动效应γ与衰减效应σ两种竞争作用；在近似谱解耦下，将逐模态稳定性比μ_i=γ_i/σ_i分解为数据侧与预测器侧独立项，并预测相边界；据此提出ResidualPred——一种在初始化时注意力偏向恒等映射的Transformer预测器。

Result: 在超过800组Tabular-JEPA配置中验证了相边界；统一了预测器缩放、掩码比例与EMA作为调节μ的不同机制；ResidualPred在表格基准以及CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、STL-10、ImageNet上的I-JEPA预训练中提升了有效秩和下游准确率。

Conclusion: 该框架将经验性的坍缩规避启发式与显式动力学图景联系起来，提供了理论驱动的稳定化方法。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) are prone to representation collapse, typically mitigated through empirical heuristics. We develop an early-training stability theory that unifies these heuristics. Linearising the coupled JEPA gradient flow around the trivial fixed point reveals two competing effects: a driving force ($γ$) and a decay effect ($σ$). Under approximate spectral decoupling, a per-mode stability ratio $μ_i = γ_i / σ_i$ factorises into independent data-side and predictor-side terms and the count of unstable modes tracks the rank of representations that can emerge. The framework predicts a phase boundary, which we confirm empirically across more than 800 Tabular-JEPA configurations. It also unifies predictor scaling, masking ratio, and EMA as distinct mechanisms for shifting $μ$. Guided by this analysis, we introduce ResidualPred, a transformer predictor whose attention is biased toward the identity at initialisation; it improves both effective rank and downstream accuracy on tabular benchmarks and in I-JEPA pretraining on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, STL-10, and ImageNet. Our framework connects empirical collapse-avoidance heuristics to an explicit dynamical picture, yielding theory-driven stabilizers. Code is available at https://github.com/jose-melo/drive-vs-decay.

</details>


### [245] [Mitigating Convergence Collapse in Fixed-Target Anomaly Detectors via Kernel-Anchored Locality Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02345)
*José Lucas De Melo Costa,Fabrice Popineau,Arpad Rimmel,Bich-Liên Doan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了固定目标表格异常检测器的"收敛崩溃"现象，提出核锚定正则化(KAR)以缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定目标表格异常检测器（收缩匹配、一步整流流、重构自编码器）通过测试时残差打分，但在优化收敛后反而变差，原因是学习到的映射在分布外也跟踪目标，使残差信号在异常与正常数据上同时消失。作者认为这一现象是结构性的，有效异常检测需要阻止无约束外推的局部性约束。

Method: 作者将固定目标检测器的核回归对应物形式化为有限带宽的Nadaraya-Watson平滑器（Kernel Contraction Matching, KCM），闭式、免训练且CPU高效。基于此桥梁，提出核锚定正则化(KAR)，惩罚神经预测与训练目标核加权平均之间的偏差。

Result: KCM在ADBench上匹配已建立的神经基线；在易崩溃的ADBench数据集和三种骨干网络上，KAR缓解崩溃并在延长训练下提升AUROC。

Conclusion: 有效异常检测需要局部性约束，经典检测器显式强制局部性，而固定目标神经检测器则没有。KCM建立了核方法与神经方法的桥梁，KAR则是将这一局部性先验注入神经检测器的实用方案。

Abstract: A family of tabular anomaly detectors trains a neural map toward a fixed target under squared-error loss and scores anomalies by the test-time residual; contraction matching, one-step rectified flow, and reconstruction autoencoders all fit this template. We characterize a convergence collapse: better optimization makes the detector worse. At convergence, the learned map tracks the target even off-distribution, so the residual signal vanishes on anomalies as well as on normal data. These detectors therefore rely on implicit non-convergence (early stopping, capacity caps) to retain signal. We argue this is structural: effective anomaly detection requires a locality constraint that blocks unconstrained extrapolation. Classical detectors (kNN, KDE, isolation forests, LOF) enforce locality explicitly; fixed-target neural detectors do not. We formalize the connection by showing that the kernel-regression analog of a fixed-target detector is a finite-bandwidth Nadaraya-Watson smoother, which we call Kernel Contraction Matching (KCM). KCM is closed-form, training-free, and CPU-efficient, yet matches established neural baselines on ADBench. Building on this bridge, we introduce the Kernel-Anchored Regularizer (KAR), which penalizes deviation of the neural prediction from a kernel-weighted average of training targets. Across collapse-prone ADBench datasets and three backbones, KAR mitigates collapse and improves AUROC under prolonged training.

</details>


### [246] [From Behavior to Provenance: Attributing Tabular Foundation Models to Synthetic Pretraining Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02347)
*Mohamed Bouadi,Nassim Bouarour,Shivam Dubey,Aditya Tanna,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出用可控合成预训练数据（O'PRIOR）来使训练数据归因可实验验证，通过反事实重训与溯源干预检验归因的忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据归因旨在识别哪些训练样本塑造了模型行为，但因果性训练影响难以观测，导致归因主张难以验证。作者认为受控的合成预训练可以让归因变得可实验检验。

Method: 构建O'PRIOR——一个面向表格基础模型的溯源丰富合成任务生成器，每个预训练任务都带有关于结构机制、缺失性、混杂、捷径和分布偏移的显式谱系。结合行为条件归因、反事实重训与溯源感知干预，检验任务级忠实性和机制级一致性。

Result: 在留出的真实任务上，移除归因排名前5%的合成任务使平均ROC-AUC下降0.013，而随机移除仅下降0.002±0.004；移除归因最低的任务性能反而提升0.003。在捷径溯源任务中，定向移除效应为0.043，匹配随机移除为0.016。溯源判别能力较弱（排序AUROC为0.55-0.62），尽管top-k富集显著，说明溯源关联与干预忠实性未必一致。

Conclusion: 合成溯源为可验证的贡献性归因提供了受控实验环境，并揭示了溯源关联性与干预忠实性之间的分离。

Abstract: Training-data attribution aims to identify which training examples shape model behavior, yet validating such claims is difficult because causal training influence is rarely observable. We argue that controlled synthetic pretraining makes attribution experimentally testable. Using O'PRIOR, a provenance-rich synthetic task generator for tabular foundation models, we construct a testbed in which every pretraining task carries explicit lineage over structural mechanisms, missingness, confounding, shortcuts, and distribution shift. We combine behavior-conditioned attribution with counterfactual retraining and provenance-aware interventions to test both task-level faithfulness and mechanism-level consistency. On held-out real tasks, removing the top-attributed 5% of synthetic tasks decreases mean ROC-AUC by 0.013, compared with 0.002$\pm$0.004 under random removal, while removing bottom-attributed tasks improves performance by 0.003. Within shortcut-provenance tasks, targeted removal yields an effect of 0.043 versus 0.016 for matched random removal. Provenance discrimination is more modest by ranking AUROC (0.55-0.62), despite substantial top-k enrichment, revealing that provenance association and interventional faithfulness need not coincide. Our results establish synthetic provenance as a controlled setting for verifiable contributive attribution

</details>


### [247] [Does Every User Need a Private LoRA? Decoupling Personalization from Per-User Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02353)
*Songyuan Sui,Srikanth Malla,Chiho Choi,Joon Hee Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 LINEUP，通过学习可复用的低秩个性化因子库，并以用户条件召回和查询依赖校准进行组合，将每个目标用户的适配限制为仅 8 个标量的用户代码，从而在个性化分类、预测和生成任务上全面领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化大语言模型通常需要为每个用户维护完整的适配状态，这种范式随用户规模增长而扩展性差。论文从"个性化容量分配"角度重新审视该设计，探究多少适配容量可跨用户共享、共享容量应如何组合、以及多少必须保留为特定用户专属。

Method: 通过三项互补的实证分析验证：独立用户适配器包含大量跨用户可复用结构；可复用方向的价值同时取决于用户相关性和查询间的变化；用户历史提供可迁移信号用于紧凑的个体校正。据此提出 LINEUP：学习可复用低秩个性化因子库，通过用户条件召回和查询依赖校准进行组合，并将目标用户适配限制为共享校正空间上的极小用户代码。

Result: 每个目标用户仅优化 8 个标量，所有共享组件保持固定，而对比的 private-LoRA 配置每用户需 419 万参数。理论分析给出了有限步、有限历史的风险界及用户代码细化优于历史初始化的充分条件。在涵盖个性化分类、预测与生成的六项任务、共 12 个指标上（各取三次独立运行均值）全面领先，例如相对最强基线将 LaMP-3 RMSE 降低 11.4%，并在历史数据有限时仍保持优势。

Conclusion: 丰富的个性化能力主要可由可复用、条件化组合的共享容量支撑，而独立用户适配可被限制在极小的校正状态中，从而在保持个性化效果的同时大幅降低每用户可训练状态规模。

Abstract: Personalized large language models often require a complete adaptation state for each user. However, this paradigm scales poorly as the user population grows. We revisit this design through the lens of personalization capacity allocation: how much adaptation capacity can be shared across users, how the shared capacity should be composed, and how much must remain user-specific. We answer them through three complementary empirical analyses. We find that independent user adapters contain substantial cross-user reusable structure, that the utility of reusable directions reflects both user relevance and variation across queries, and that user histories provide transferable signals for compact individual correction. Motivated by these findings, we propose LINEUP. It learns a bank of reusable low-rank personalization factors, composes them through user-conditioned recall and query-dependent calibration, and restricts target-user adaptation to a tiny user code over a shared correction space. This design decouples expressive personalization capacity from per-user trainable state. Each target user optimizes only eight scalars, while all shared components remain fixed. By comparison, the evaluated private-LoRA configuration uses 4.19 million per-user parameters. Our theoretical analysis gives a finite-step, finite-history risk bound and sufficient conditions for user-code refinement to improve on history initialization. Across six tasks spanning personalized classification, prediction, and generation, LINEUP leads on all 12 metrics, each averaged over three independent runs (e.g., reducing LaMP-3 RMSE by 11.4% relative to the strongest baseline). It maintains advantages under limited history. These results show that rich personalization can be supported primarily by reusable, conditionally composed shared capacity, while independent user adaptation remains confined to a tiny correction state.

</details>


### [248] [Why Does Adaptive Batching Help LLM Pretraining? A Perspective from Unbounded Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02355)
*Arda Fazla,Antesh Upadhyay,Ege C. Kaya,M. Berk Sahin,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型预训练中增大批次的成功机制，提出广义BG-a噪声模型，给出增长相关复杂度下界与匹配上界，并提出自适应批次调度器，在OLMo2预训练上以更少迭代取得更低验证损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增大批次是LLM预训练的常见做法，但其理论依据不清。随机优化分析常假设随机梯度方差一致有界，而近期证据表明该假设在许多实际非凸问题中不成立。BG-0噪声模型虽放松了该假设（方差可随距初始化距离二次增长），但其增长在实践中可能过于保守。

Method: 作者先实证考察LLM预训练中的方差增长，发现带可调增长指数的广义BG模型能更紧地刻画实际噪声行为。据此提出广义BG-a噪声模型，在有限方差（a=0）与BG-0噪声（a=2）之间插值。在L-光滑条件下，推导出增长相关的oracle复杂度下界Ω(ε^{-(4+a)})，并在ε依赖上建立匹配上界（通过在迭代远离初始化时增大批次实现）。最后提出自适应批次调度器，通过动态调整批次大小控制方差增长。

Result: 在C4上预训练最大1B参数的OLMo2模型，所提调度器在相同token预算下取得比小批次和大批次训练更低的验证损失，同时迭代次数不到小批次训练的10%。

Conclusion: 广义BG-a噪声模型更好地描述了实际噪声行为，其增长相关复杂度分析为批次调度提供了理论依据；自适应批次调度器能有效控制方差增长，在实践中兼具更少迭代与更低损失的优势。

Abstract: Increasing the batch size during training is a common practice in large language model (LLM) pretraining, yet the theoretical justification behind its success is not well understood. Analyses of stochastic optimization often assume uniformly bounded stochastic gradient variance, yet recent evidence suggests that this assumption fails in many practical nonconvex problems. The Blum--Gladyshev (BG-$0$) noise model relaxes this assumption by allowing the variance to grow quadratically with the distance from initialization, suggesting that batch size schedulers can help by controlling the variance growth during training. However, this growth can be overly conservative in practice. We empirically investigate variance growth in LLM pretraining and observe that a generalized BG model with a tunable growth exponent provides a tighter description of practical noise behavior. Motivated by this observation, we introduce the generalized BG-$a$ noise model, which interpolates between bounded variance ($a=0$) and BG-$0$ noise ($a=2$). Under $L$-smoothness, we derive an information-theoretic lower bound with growth-dependent oracle complexity $Ω(ε^{-(4+a)})$ and establish a matching upper bound in $ε$-dependence by increasing the batch size as the iterates move away from initialization. Finally, we propose an adaptive batch scheduler that controls variance growth through dynamic batch size adjustments during training. In pretraining OLMo2 models of up to 1B parameters on C4, our scheduler achieves a lower validation loss than both small and large batch training under matched token budgets, while using less than 10\% of the iterations of small batch training.

</details>


### [249] [Lexicographic Multi-Objective On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02359)
*Doseok Jang,Jon Ander Campos,Youran Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LMOPD——一种在显式优先级下整合多奖励专家的词典型多目标在线蒸馏方法，能更好保留高优先级能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 通常只优化答案正确性，但高质量推理与简洁回答同样重要。现有多奖励后训练方法多采用奖励标量化或专家拼接，未显式保护奖励优先级；而某些权衡是非对称的，例如简洁性不应以牺牲正确性为代价。

Method: LMOPD 为多教师方法：对每个学生 rollout，选择第一个被门控判定为欠缺的目标所对应的专家，再将其中心化 log-policy 修正做局部投影，去除与更高优先级专家相冲突的分量。

Result: 在 30B-A3B MoE 模型、三个数学基准上评估。两专家设置下，点估计完全保留准确率与推理质量增益，获得 46.9% 的简洁性增益；四专家设置下，保留约 90% 的准确率增益与推理正确性增益，次优基线仅约 57%。消融显示词典型路由优于随机路由，投影进一步增强最高优先级能力。

Conclusion: LMOPD 在两种规模下都比现有基线更有效保留最高优先级能力，说明显式优先级对专家整合具有重要价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) usually optimizes answer correctness, yet useful language-model behavior also requires high-quality reasoning and concise responses. Existing multi-reward post-training methods typically scalarize rewards or combine specialists without explicitly protecting a reward priority order. This is problematic when trade-offs are asymmetric: conciseness, for example, should not improve at the cost of correctness. We introduce Lexicographic Multi-Objective On-Policy Distillation (LMOPD), a multi-teacher method for integrating reward-specialized policies under explicit priorities. For each student rollout, LMOPD selects the specialist for the first objective whose gate detects a deficiency, then locally projects its centered log-policy correction to remove components that oppose higher-priority specialists. We evaluate 30B-A3B mixture-of-experts transformer models in two- and four-expert settings on three math benchmarks, measuring retained specialist gains. With two experts, LMOPD's point estimates fully retain the accuracy and reasoning-quality gains while acquiring $46.9\%$ of the conciseness gain. With four experts, it retains $\approx90\%$ of both the accuracy gain and reasoning-correctness gain, compared to only $\approx57\%$ by the next best evaluated baseline. Matched four-expertablations show that lexicographic routing outperforms random routing and that projection further strengthens both top-priority capabilities. Across both scales, LMOPD preserves the highest-priority capabilities more effectively than the existing baselines we evaluate, demonstrating the value of explicit priorities for specialist integration.

</details>


### [250] [ArrivalBench: Agent-Generated Data Pipelines Are Correct Once and Wrong Under Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02363)
*Pranay Kothari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ArrivalBench 通过对抗性重放投递调度评估智能体生成的数据流水线，揭示单次评测大幅高估了正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅用固定快照单次运行流水线来评分，无法暴露真实投递条件下（延迟、重复、乱序、重试）的静默错误。

Method: 让智能体遗留的流水线在可重放的对抗性投递调度下重新执行，并要求最终状态等于完整日志的批处理重算结果；因判定标准是重算而非分类，可区分"错误结果"与"崩溃"两类结论。在40个自建任务上对比十一种模型。

Result: 单次执行评测判定 86-100% 的流水线通过，重放后发现其中 7.0-79.2% 实为静默错误；同模型同任务下，修复循环并非差距来源，经快照测试修复的流水线重放失败率与首轮通过者相当；各模型中幂等性风险比顺序风险更易失败。加入风险提示使某模型静默失败从 48.2% 降至 10.5%，但崩溃率从 9.0% 升至 37.0%，总体失败仅由 51.0% 降到 44.0%。全部十一个实验臂独立重跑，比率波动不超过 5.9 个百分点。

Conclusion: 仅凭单次快照评测会严重高估智能体数据流水线的可靠性；区分静默错误与崩溃对评测与干预解读至关重要，且风险提示未必能降低总体失败率。

Abstract: Benchmarks for agent-generated data work grade a pipeline by running it once against a fixed snapshot. ArrivalBench instead re-executes the pipeline an agent leaves behind under adversarial but replayable delivery schedules (late, duplicated, out-of-order and retried records) and requires its final state to equal a batch recomputation of the complete log. Because the oracle recomputes rather than classifies, a wrong table and a crash are distinct verdicts: a crash is visible to monitoring a team already runs, and a wrong table is not. On 40 tasks we built, our reimplementation of single-execution grading certifies 86-100% of the pipelines eleven models produce; re-executing the same artifacts finds 7.0-79.2% of the certified ones silently wrong. The gap is not produced by the repair loop: within the same model and task, pipelines repaired against the snapshot test fail replay about as often as those that passed it first time. In every model, idempotency hazards fail more often than ordering hazards. Separating a wrong answer from a crash also changes how interventions read: a hazard warning cuts one model's silent failure from 48.2% to 10.5% while raising its crash rate from 9.0% to 37.0%, so all-in failure moves only from 51.0% to 44.0%. All eleven arms were independently re-run, and rates moved by at most 5.9 points.

</details>


### [251] [Co-design Gym: A Unified Benchmark for Embodiment-Policy Co-optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02366)
*Aviraj Newatia,Yordan Tsvetkov,Leonard Pleiss,Andrew Spielberg,Rika Antonova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Co-Design Gym，一套用于联合优化智能体"具身设计"与"控制策略"的基准环境套件，涵盖20个领域、85+预设，并系统评估代表性协同设计算法以刻画当前技术水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习/决策基准大多假设智能体具身（设计）固定，只关注策略学习。但具身与策略相互塑造：具身决定可发现的策略，最优具身又由其所允许的策略定义。分离优化二者高度次优，因此需要专门的基准来系统研究这类协同设计问题。

Method: 构建 Co-Design Gym 基准环境套件，用于联合优化具身与策略；覆盖机器人操作与运动、多机器人协作、可变形与软体动力学、电子游戏、电网、无线网络、F1赛车、多智能体仓储及最优控制等领域，共20个环境族、85+协同设计预设；并对代表性协同设计算法进行系统评估。

Result: 提供覆盖广泛领域的20个环境族与85+协同设计预设，并给出代表性协同设计算法的系统性评测，刻画了当前协同设计领域的最新技术水平。

Conclusion: Co-Design Gym 为具身与策略协同设计研究提供了统一、可比的基准与评测，为该方向累积性、可比较的进展奠定基础。

Abstract: Finding an optimal behaviour policy within a given environment is a widely studied problem in domains as diverse as games, robotics, energy infrastructure, communication networks, and multi-agent systems. Numerous benchmarks have been developed to support such research, but the vast majority assume that the agent's embodiment (design) is fixed, focusing instead on policy learning alone. Lifting this assumption gives rise to a broader class of problems in which optimizing embodiment and policy separately is highly suboptimal. An agent's embodiment strongly shapes which control policies can be discovered, while the optimal embodiment is in turn defined by the policies it admits. To help the research community study this class of problems explicitly and systematically, we introduce Co-Design Gym - a suite of benchmark environments for jointly optimizing embodiment and policy. Our environments span domains such as robotic manipulation and locomotion, multi-robot cooperation, deformable and soft dynamics, video games, electricity grids, wireless networks, F1 racing, multi-agent warehouses, and optimal control, offering 20 environment families (domains), with over 85 distinct co-design presets in total. We further contribute a systematic evaluation of representative co-design algorithms, characterizing the current state of the art. Together, these contributions lay the groundwork for cumulative, comparable progress in co-design.

</details>


### [252] [Latent-MOPD: Latent Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02381)
*Zhengyu Fang,Seoyeon Hong,Jie Yang,Muyang Li,Koyoshi Shindo,Brandon Joseph Lwowski,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Latent-MOPD，首个针对 LLM 的表征级多教师在线策略蒸馏（OPD）方法，通过同时利用教师的预测分布与隐藏状态来训练学生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 多教师 OPD 仅通过教师的输出分布传递专家能力，未利用产生这些预测的内部隐藏状态信息，丢失了丰富的表征监督信号。

Method: Latent-MOPD 无需额外教师训练，同时整合教师的预测与隐藏状态；通过依据师生关系选择后层目标、用共享投影弥合隐藏宽度差异、按领域分组更新来协调多教师表征监督；每个教师的监督从隐藏状态逐渐转向 token 预测，两通道共用同一路由专家。

Result: 在同家族设置下，Latent-MOPD 在数学、代码、逻辑共九个基准上均超越仅 token、仅表征与均匀平均基线；在参数量与各教师相同时，学生在多数基准上超过逐基准最优教师。跨家族大模型教师下，方法在所有基准上超越单通道基线。同家族全层仅表征对照在领域纯净更新时稳定，教师领域交错时崩溃。

Conclusion: 单个学生模型可通过教师的输出分布与内部表征，同时整合多个专家的能力；表征级多教师蒸馏是有效的 LLM 能力集成途径。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on the responses it generates. Existing LLM multi-teacher OPD transfers what specialists predict through their output distributions. We introduce Latent-MOPD, to our knowledge the first representation-level multi-teacher OPD method for LLMs. It integrates existing specialists through both their predictions and the hidden states used to compute them, without additional teacher training. To coordinate representation supervision from multiple specialists, we select late-layer targets according to the teacher-student relationship, bridge unequal hidden widths with a shared projection, and group updates by domain. Each teacher's supervision gradually shifts from hidden states to token predictions, with both channels using the same routed specialist. In our main same-family setting, Latent-MOPD outperforms the token-only, representation-only and uniform-averaging baselines on all nine benchmarks across math, code and logic. With the same parameter count as each teacher, the student also surpasses the per-benchmark best teacher on a majority of these benchmarks. With larger, separately developed cross-family teachers, Latent-MOPD outperforms both single-channel baselines on all benchmarks. A same-family all-layer representation-only control remains stable with domain-pure updates but collapses when teacher domains are interleaved within an update. Our results show that a single student can integrate capabilities from several specialists through both their output distributions and internal representations.

</details>


### [253] [The Surprising Effectiveness of Shared Memory in Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02383)
*Giovanni Monea,Keshav Ramji,Yousef El-Kurdi,Luis A. Lastras,Yoav Artzi,Nathan Godey,Ramón Fernandez Astudillo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出循环预测Transformer（LPT），通过让循环Transformer共享KV缓存（仅首次递归写入，后续递归读取共享缓存并保留短窗口局部缓存），在不增加参数的情况下降低推理内存，同时提升质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer对每个token多次复用同一层以增加计算量、提升质量而不增加参数，但每次递归都写入各自的KV缓存，导致内存随计算量增长；现有推理阶段压缩缓存的方法会损失质量。

Method: 预训练循环语言模型以共享内存：仅第一次递归写入KV缓存，后续递归读取该共享缓存，同时保留自身的短窗口缓存。模型规模150M-1B，包括LPT及其混合变体。并通过分析研究共享内存为何有效。

Result: 共享内存不仅不损失质量反而提升质量。五轮递归下，混合变体在FineWeb-Edu上的验证困惑度较同规模标准Transformer降低1.12-1.82，且上下文内存减少76-79%。分析显示共享内存与局部内存形成不同表征，后续递归主要关注共享内存，后者还充当通往首次递归的梯度高速通道。

Conclusion: 循环模型中共享KV缓存可同时改善质量与内存效率，为循环模型建立了新的质量-内存前沿，其机理在于共享与局部记忆的分化表征及梯度传播优势。

Abstract: Looped Transformers apply the same layers several times per token, adding compute to improve quality without more parameters. Each recursion, however, writes its own key-value cache, so memory still grows with compute. Inference-time techniques can shrink this cache at a cost in quality. We pretrain looped language models to share memory: only the first recursion writes a cache, and later recursions read it while keeping a short window of their own. Surprisingly, we find that sharing memory does not cost quality and instead improves it. At 150M-1B parameters, our Looped Prediction Transformer (LPT) and its hybrid variant set a new quality-memory frontier for looped models: with five recursions, the hybrid lowers validation perplexity on FineWeb-Edu by 1.12-1.82 relative to a same-size standard Transformer while using 76-79% less context memory. Through an extensive analysis, we investigate why memory sharing helps. Shared and local memory develop different representations, and later recursions attend mostly to the shared memory, which also acts as a gradient highway to the first recursion.

</details>


### [254] [Hesitation Has a Geometry: Entropy-Trained Hyperbolic Probes for Sparse Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02391)
*Zeyong Zhang,Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Tengfei Ma,Mengjia Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双曲熵引导（HEST），利用模型自身下一词熵作为监督信号，在庞加莱球内对高熵分支token进行测地线方向激活引导，在多个数学推理基准上提升了贪心解码准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM数学推理呈层级树状结构，解常在少数高熵分支token处分叉；这样的树状结构在双曲空间中嵌入的失真远低于欧氏空间。然而现有激活引导方法通常在每一个token处加同一个固定的欧氏向量，忽视了大多数token已由上下文确定这一事实，因此效率低且可能带来负面影响。

Method: HEST用一个轻量探针把隐藏状态嵌入庞加莱球，探针的唯一标签是模型自身的下一词熵。当熵超过阈值时，HEST将嵌入状态沿探针读出函数的最速下降测地线移动，再把变化映射回隐藏状态。针对学习到的理想点的Busemann读出，作者证明固定长度的一步在任意状态下都能使该读出下降相同的量。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math和Llama-3.1家族的三个指令微调模型上，使用Busemann读出的HEST在六种设置中的五种提升了MATH-500与GSM8K的贪心准确率，最高提升1.8个百分点；而在每个token都加对比引导向量的方法反而降低准确率。使用同样方式训练的欧氏探针在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct上，该增益消失。增益在模型频繁犹豫的问题上最大，其余问题的准确率几乎不变。

Conclusion: 结果表明显式建模推理的树状/双曲几何结构并只在关键高熵token处进行引导是有效的，双曲空间中的引导优于整个序列使用固定欧氏向量或欧氏探针的做法。

Abstract: When a large language model solves a mathematical problem, its reasoning is largely hierarchical, and the solution often branches at a few tokens where the next-token entropy is high. Such tree-like structure embeds in hyperbolic space with far lower distortion than in Euclidean space. Activation steering, however, usually edits the hidden states of a pretrained model by adding one fixed Euclidean vector at every token, even though most tokens of a solution are already determined by the context. We propose Hyperbolic Entropy Steering (HEST), which embeds the hidden states in the Poincaré ball with a lightweight probe whose only label is the model's own next-token entropy. Where this entropy exceeds a threshold, HEST moves the embedded state along the geodesic of steepest descent of a readout of the probe and maps the change back to the hidden state. For the Busemann readout of a learned ideal point, we prove that a step of fixed length lowers it by the same amount at every state. On three instruction-tuned models from the Qwen2.5-Math and Llama-3.1 families, HEST with the Busemann readout improves greedy accuracy on MATH-500 and GSM8K in five of six settings, by up to 1.8 points, whereas a contrastive steering vector added at every token lowers accuracy. With a Euclidean probe trained in the same way, this gain disappears on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct. The gains are largest on problems where the model hesitates often, and accuracy on the remaining problems is almost unchanged.

</details>


### [255] [Inherit-MAS: Test-Time Evolution of Multi-Agent Systems through Workflow and Execution Inheritance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02396)
*Songtao Wei,Yi Li,Zhichun Guo,Bingzhe Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Inherit-MAS，通过显式的"工作流继承"与"执行继承"进行测试时演化，在提升多智能体系统性能的同时显著降低重复计算与 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统需要协作完成复杂任务，但有效的工作流难以预先设计。测试时演化虽可利用执行反馈改进工作流，但大范围修改会破坏有用的组件，且对未变请求的重复执行会带来冗余计算。受生物演化中"遗传与选择"相互作用的启发，作者希望将"继承"机制显式化。

Method: 提出 Inherit-MAS，在工作流与执行两个层面显式实现继承。元模型首先合成由 worker 智能体组成的工作流，显式声明角色、通信输入和工具权限；单独的判别模型对每次执行的候选工作流打分并诊断缺陷。常规精化轮次中，工作流继承从最新完成的候选出发，可丢弃判定为无用且可移除的节点，并施加经过验证的编辑以解决诊断出的缺陷。执行继承则在完整解析后的请求与执行上下文完全匹配时，复用合格的历史结果，避免冗余的模型与工具调用。

Result: 使用 GPT-4o-mini 作为 worker 时，Inherit-MAS 在 WorkBench 上达到 55.4% 完成率、在 HotpotQA FullWiki 上达到 49.7% 联合 F1，优于 EvoAgent、EvoMAS 和 TacoMAS。使用 Qwen3-32B 作为 worker 时，在两个基准上同样超过这些演化式 MAS 基线。相比禁用执行继承的相同控制器，执行继承在 WorkBench 与 HotpotQA 上分别减少 29.1% 和 34.6% 的 worker token 消耗，总 token 消耗减少 5.3% 和 18.1%。

Conclusion: 显式化工作流与执行层面的继承机制，能够在测试时有效提升多智能体工作流的性能，同时避免冗余计算、显著降低 token 成本，优于现有演化式 MAS 方法。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built from large language models coordinate specialized agents to tackle complex tasks, but effective workflows are difficult to design in advance. Test-time evolution refines workflows using execution feedback, yet broad revisions can disturb useful components, while re-executing unchanged requests can incur redundant computation. Inspired by the interplay of inheritance and selection in biological evolution, we introduce Inherit-MAS, which makes inheritance explicit at the workflow and execution levels. A meta-model first synthesizes a workflow of worker agents with declared roles, communication inputs, and tool permissions, and a separately prompted judge scores each executed candidate and diagnoses its deficiencies. In ordinary refinement rounds, \emph{workflow inheritance} starts from the latest completed candidate, may discard removable nodes judged unhelpful, and applies a validated edit to address the diagnosed deficiency. When the new candidate executes, \emph{execution inheritance} inherits eligible stored results only if the complete resolved request and execution context match, avoiding redundant model and tool calls. With GPT-4o-mini workers, Inherit-MAS achieves 55.4\% completion on WorkBench and 49.7\% joint F1 on HotpotQA FullWiki, outperforming EvoAgent, EvoMAS, and TacoMAS. With Qwen3-32B workers, it also exceeds these evolving-MAS baselines on both benchmarks. Compared with rerunning the same controller with execution inheritance disabled, execution inheritance reduces worker-token usage by 29.1\% on WorkBench and 34.6\% on HotpotQA, and total token usage by 5.3\% and 18.1\%.

</details>


### [256] [Validated Data Onboarding for AI Demand Forecasting on U.S. Building Meter Data: Design, Controlled Evaluation, and a Corrected Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02397)
*Yixuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该报告提出一个数据入模前的缺陷检测与修复流水线，仅使用预测时点可得信息修复电表数据缺陷，并通过受控实验证明其能保护24小时前电力负荷预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网运营方日益依赖机器学习模型预测次日用电需求，但训练所用表计数据普遍存在缺失、传感器冻结、读数为零、单位错位等缺陷，需要在下游建模前进行数据清洗。

Method: 基于Building Data Genome 2公开数据集（12栋美国建筑小时级数据，210,528行，2016-2017）构建数据onboarding流水线，检测并修复缺陷；采用带种子、哈希记录的缺陷注入方式（覆盖0.10%训练期），进行受控实验；对梯度提升、岭回归、随机森林及1-24小时预测跨度进行验证；并用仅含历史信息的"past-only"修复策略。

Result: 缺陷使梯度提升预测误差上升86%；检测修复后误差回到干净水平（MASE 0.760干净、1.415污染、0.729修复），保留93%训练目标。按已发表现场研究的1.6%缺陷率校准后，未保护模型误差达季节性朴素规则的4.4倍，修复后重回干净基线，该模式在岭回归、随机森林及1-24小时跨度下均成立。流水线首版对自然数据过度清洗致预测变差25%，原因已被追溯并以带日期的修订文档记录校准方法。

Conclusion: 仅用预测时点可得信息的数据清洗流水线可有效抵御表计缺陷对次日负荷预测的损害，且全部数字可通过固定公开输入、SHA-256校验、84项自动化测试与持续集成复现。

Abstract: Electric utilities and grid operators increasingly rely on machine-learning models to forecast next-day demand, and those models learn from meter data that is routinely defective: readings go missing, sensors freeze, buildings read zero for hours, and units change by a factor of 100. This report presents a data-onboarding pipeline that detects and repairs such defects before a model is trained, using only information available at forecast time, and a controlled experiment that measures whether the pipeline protects a 24-hour-ahead forecast. On hourly electricity data for twelve U.S. buildings from the public Building Data Genome 2 dataset (210,528 rows, 2016-2017), seeded, hash-logged defects touching 0.10% of the training period raised the error of a gradient-boosting forecaster by 86%; after detection and past-only repair the error returned to the clean-data level (mean absolute scaled error 0.760 clean, 1.415 corrupted, 0.729 repaired) while 93% of training targets were retained. At a defect prevalence calibrated to published field studies (1.6% of training rows) the unprotected forecaster's error reached 4.4 times that of a seasonal-naive rule, and the repaired forecaster again matched the clean baseline. The same pattern held for ridge regression and a random forest and across horizons of 1 to 24 hours. A first version of the pipeline over-cleaned natural data and made forecasts 25% worse; that result is retained, its cause is traced in the published artifacts, and the per-building calibration that corrects it is documented as a dated amendment. Every number is reproducible from pinned public inputs with SHA-256 verification, 84 automated tests and continuous integration.

</details>


### [257] [VisAudit: Evaluating Multimodal Agents for Visual Diagnosis and Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02399)
*Shicheng Liu,Adam Kahirov,Qi Zhang,Zhimin Hu,Song Wang,Junhong Lin,Julian Shun,Yada Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VisAudit基准，用于评测多模态智能体在可视化自主审查（诊断、修复、验证）方面的能力，实验显示最强模型在自主修复设置下仅能完全恢复47.4%的缺陷图表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体被广泛用于数据可视化任务，但其自主审查能力有限：难以识别可视化错误、确定修改内容、在不破坏正确内容的前提下修复，并验证干预是否成功。现有基准多评测预定义的单一能力（如图表生成、指令引导编辑或缺陷检测），无法刻画这一自主审查缺口。

Method: 提出VisAudit基准，评测可视化的诊断、修复与验证。给定渲染后的图表及可配置的辅助证据（源数据表、预期文本摘要、可视化代码），智能体需迭代诊断潜在缺陷、修改并执行可视化代码、检查执行与视觉反馈，并判断何时无需进一步干预。VisAudit定义了三条评测轨道：诊断式修复、自主修复和开放世界验证，包含跨21种图表类型、10类缺陷的1,900个缺陷实例，以及300个初始正确的图表。基准通过已验证源可视化的受控扰动构建，并采用系统化验证与人机对齐的质量控制。

Result: 领先多模态模型的实验显示出与可靠自主审查之间存在显著差距：在自主修复设置下，最强被评测模型仅能完全恢复47.4%的缺陷图表。

Conclusion: 现有模型在可视化的自主审查（诊断、修复、验证闭环）上仍远未达到可靠水平，VisAudit为衡量和推动该方向提供了系统化基准。

Abstract: Multimodal agents are increasingly used for data visualization tasks but remain limited in autonomous review. Unlike humans, they may fail to recognize when a visualization is incorrect, determine what to change, repair it without disrupting correct content, and verify whether the intervention succeeded. Existing benchmarks largely evaluate predefined individual capabilities such as chart generation, instruction-guided editing, or defect detection, and therefore do not capture this gap in autonomous review. We introduce VisAudit, a benchmark for evaluating visualization diagnosis, repair, and verification. Given a rendered chart and configurable auxiliary evidence, including its source data table, intended text summary, and visualization code, an agent iteratively diagnoses potential defects, modifies and executes visualization code, inspects execution and visual feedback, and determines when no further intervention is needed. VisAudit defines three tracks spanning diagnosed repair, autonomous repair, and open-world verification, and contains 1,900 flawed instances across 21 chart types and 10 flaw categories, together with 300 initially correct charts. We construct the benchmark through controlled perturbations of validated source visualizations, with systematic verification and human-aligned quality control to ensure that injected defects are well-defined and recoverable from the available evidence. Experiments with leading multimodal models reveal a substantial gap from reliable autonomous review: the strongest evaluated model fully recovers only $47.4\%$ of flawed charts in the autonomous-repair setting.

</details>


### [258] [Trained Agentic Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02404)
*Bryce Sandlund*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 训练模型使用最简单的工具（自我调用提示与读取上下文片段），仅8K上下文即可在长文本基准上媲美1M上下文的GPT-5.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文语言模型通常依赖原生长上下文训练或复杂的长上下文框架（harness），成本高且复杂，因此作者探索用最简工具框架替代。

Method: 不使用原生长上下文训练，也不设计长上下文框架，而是训练模型使用最简单的框架：一个用任意提示调用自身的工具，以及一个从输入上下文中读取指定范围token的工具。在多样化合成数据集上微调 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B。

Result: 在 OOLONG-synth 基准上，当文档长度超过40K tokens 时，仅用8,000 tokens 上下文的该小模型与拥有1M tokens 上下文的 GPT-5.4 表现相当。

Conclusion: 通过最简工具框架微调小模型，可用极短上下文实现与超长上下文大模型相当的长文本能力，表明原生长上下文训练或复杂框架并非必要。

Abstract: We study long context language models. Instead of training long context natively, or designing a long context harness, we train a model over the simplest possible harness: a tool to call itself with any specified prompt and a tool to read tokens in a range from the input context. We finetune Qwen3.6-35B-A3B on a diverse synthetic dataset using this harness. With only 8,000 tokens of context, our small model is as strong as GPT-5.4 with 1M tokens of context on the OOLONG-synth benchmark when document length exceeds 40K tokens.

</details>


### [259] [Efficient Neural Field Learning via Adaptive Coverage and Focused Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02410)
*Guang Zhao,Xihaier Luo,Huan-Hsin Tseng,Seungjun Lee,Shinjae Yoo,Yihui Ren,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ACES，一种解耦覆盖与重要性的结构化采样框架，用于提升隐式神经表示（INR）的采样与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: INR训练因均匀子采样忽略空间异质性而效率低下；现有自适应采样方法虽优先采样高误差点，但在点级别操作，易在局部区域产生冗余采样且对定义域覆盖不足。

Method: ACES构建自适应空间划分以保证域覆盖、减少冗余，并应用区域级重要性权重优先训练信息量高的区域；理论分析表明自适应划分通过提高区域内同质性降低梯度方差，且区域级加权的受控偏差可相对标准无偏估计提升优化效率。

Result: 在科学场学习任务上，ACES相比均匀采样和点级自适应采样基线实现更快收敛和更低误差，在复杂度高度局部化的场中收益最大。

Conclusion: 通过解耦覆盖与重要性的结构化采样，ACES能有效提升INR训练效率与精度，尤其适用于局部复杂度高的场学习场景。

Abstract: Implicit neural representations (INRs) provide a flexible framework for modeling high-dimensional continuous fields, but their training is often inefficient due to uniform subsampling that ignores spatial heterogeneity. Existing adaptive sampling methods partially address this issue by prioritizing high-error samples, but typically operate at the point level, often leading to redundant sampling in localized regions and insufficient coverage of the domain. We propose ACES (Adaptive Coverage-aware Efficient Sampling), a structured sampling framework that improves training efficiency by decoupling coverage and importance. ACES constructs adaptive spatial partitions to ensure domain coverage and reduce redundancy, and applies region-level importance weighting to prioritize informative regions during training. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that adaptive partitioning reduces gradient variance by increasing within-region homogeneity, and that controlled bias in region-level weighting may improve optimization efficiency relative to standard unbiased estimators. Experiments on scientific field learning tasks demonstrate that ACES achieves faster convergence and lower error than uniform and pointwise adaptive sampling baselines, with the largest gains in fields with highly localized complexity.

</details>


### [260] [Prompted to Discriminate: Generalizing Malicious-Input Probes in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02413)
*Elad David,Max Fomin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在严格LODO评估下，附在用户输入后的分类指令后缀能稳定提升激活探针的分布外检测性能（最高约4个AUC点），增益主要来自"分类格式"本身而非具体标准措辞，且该收益可迁移到生产级多位置池化探针，通过KV-cache fork低成本部署，但最优后缀依模型与读出方式而定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地将激活探针用作运行时监控器，用于在行动前拦截提示注入、越狱和不安全请求。业界流行一种从LLM-as-judge借鉴来的廉价做法：在用户回合后附加简短分类指令，以锐化探针信号。但该后缀的措辞是否重要、其收益在分布外攻击（部署中真正面临的场景）上是否成立，尚不清楚，本文正是要检验这一点。

Method: 构建受控的后缀阶梯（含分类式、无内容标签、离题、仅注意力等多种后缀），在严格留一数据集（LODO）设定下跨13个安全基准（越狱、注入、良性对话）和三个开源权重模型族（Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen3.5-9B、Gemma-4-12B）进行评测；除单位置探针外，还检验注意力、multi-max、MLP等多位置池化探针，并测试KV-cache fork的服务化部署。

Result: 单位置探针上，分类后缀在分布外检测上持续优于无后缀（最高约4个AUC点）；提示模型对输入进行分类的后缀（即便是无内容标签）稳定胜出，离题或仅注意力后缀帮助甚微；无内容后缀即可匹敌真实恶意/良性后缀，具体标准只在高严格阈值下带来精度增益。该收益并非单位置读取的假象，可迁移到生产用多位置池化探针，但最优后缀依赖于读出方式。

Conclusion: 分类格式而非命名标准是增益来源；该技巧可作为任意激活探针监控器的低成本即插即用方案，但并非自动获胜——哪个后缀有效、提升多少取决于模型与读出方式。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on activation probes as runtime monitors for prompt injection, jailbreaks, and unsafe requests, reading the model's own hidden state to catch a harmful input before the agent acts on it. A cheap, increasingly common move, borrowed from LLM-as-judge prompting, is to append a short classification instruction after the user's turn and read the probe at that point, to sharpen it: the instruction asks the model to represent the incoming request as a class, concentrating the signal the probe must separate, at negligible serving cost. But does the wording of that suffix matter, and does its benefit hold in the wild, on attack types the probe never saw in training, the regime a deployed monitor faces? We test this with a controlled ladder of post-user suffixes under strict leave-one-dataset-out (LODO) evaluation across 13 safety benchmarks (jailbreak, injection, and benign chat) and three open-weight model families (Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen3.5-9B, Gemma-4-12B). On a single-position probe, a classification suffix consistently improves out-of-distribution detection over no suffix (up to ~4 AUC points); yet which suffix matters: prompting the model to classify the input, even into content-free labels, reliably wins; an off-topic or merely-attentive suffix helps little. The gain comes from the classification format, not the named criterion: a content-free suffix matches the real malicious/benign one, with the criterion adding precision only at strict thresholds. This is not an artifact of the single-position read: the benefit carries to the multi-position pooling probes used in production (attention, multi-max, MLP), though the best-performing suffix there is readout-dependent. Served through a KV-cache fork, it is a cheap drop-in for any activation-probe monitor, though not an automatic win: which suffix helps, and by how much, depends on the model and the readout.

</details>


### [261] [The AI Theorist reveals excitonic structure in $α$-RuCl$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02417)
*Hongjian Zhou,Xianfan Nie,Sean Wu,Tarun Patel,Jinge Wu,Andrew Liu,Adam Wei Tsen,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 AI Theorist——一个由 AI 智能体组成的系统，能自主完成假设生成、第一性原理计算与证据驱动修正，用于发现物理模型；并在 α-RuCl₃ 的光学谱研究中给出新解释，首次展示 AI 系统自主构建物理模型解释未发表的量子材料实验观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验仪器与自动化带来日益丰富的数据，但将实验观测转化为微观物理理解仍是科学发现的瓶颈。作者希望加速这一过程，实现物理模型的自主发现。

Method: 构建 AI Theorist 系统：多 AI 智能体框架，进行假设生成、第一性原理电子结构与多体计算、以及证据驱动的模型修正。将其应用于 Kitaev 量子自旋液体候选材料 α-RuCl₃，通过光学谱研究其电子结构。

Result: AI Theorist 对光学与光电流观测给出了新解释，识别出具有相反光学选择定则和不同实空间分布的多种激子态。

Conclusion: 这是首个 AI 系统自主构建物理模型以解释量子材料中未发表实验观测的展示，开辟了材料科学中自主理论发现的路径——AI 智能体利用第一性原理计算将实验观测转化为物理模型和可检验预言。

Abstract: Advances in experimental instrumentation and automation generate increasingly rich datasets, but turning experimental observations into microscopic understanding remains a bottleneck in scientific discovery. To accelerate this process, we introduce AI Theorist, a system of artificial intelligence (AI) agents for autonomous discovery of physical models through hypothesis generation, first-principles calculations and evidence-driven refinement. We apply the framework to $α$-RuCl$_3$, a leading candidate material for realizing a Kitaev quantum spin liquid, to investigate its electronic structure through optical spectra. AI Theorist develops a new interpretation of the optical and photocurrent observations, identifying distinct excitonic states with contrasting optical selection rules and real-space distributions. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of an AI system autonomously developing a physical model to explain previously unpublished experimental observations in a quantum material, utilizing first-principles electronic-structure and many-body calculations. Our results establish a route to autonomous theoretical discovery in materials science, in which AI agents use first-principles calculations to turn experimental observations into physical models and testable predictions.

</details>


### [262] [Geometry-Aware Time Reparameterization for Flow-Map Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02427)
*Félix Dedek,Makoto Yamada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何感知的时间重参数化方法，用于流映射蒸馏，通过将更多学生时间分配给法向加速度较大的轨迹区域，提升一至几步生成的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流映射蒸馏通过学习预训练生成ODE的有限时间转移，实现一步或少步生成。作者假设法向加速度较大的轨迹段更难蒸馏，因此希望通过改变教师模型的时间参数化，使这些转移更易学习。

Method: 提出几何感知的时间重参数化：在保持教师几何路径与终端分布的前提下，推导出一个共享时钟，使总体法向加速度统计量均衡，并从教师轨迹上的稳健正则化估计构造实用近似。将该时钟融入拉格朗日流映射蒸馏，用变换后的时间坐标条件化学生模型。该时钟在蒸馏前一次性估计，无需重训教师、增加学生参数或推理时额外网络评估。

Result: 在合成数据、CIFAR-10和CelebA-64上的实验表明，在相同推理预算下，相比恒等时间蒸馏，样本质量有所提升，包括一步和两步图像生成的改进。一步生成中无法调整中间采样时间，其增益凸显了蒸馏期间时间重参数化的优势。

Conclusion: 改变教师的时间参数化可让流映射蒸馏的转移更易学习，所提几何感知时钟在多种数据集上提升了少步生成质量，且实现代价低。

Abstract: Flow-map distillation enables one- and few-step generation by learning finite-time transitions of a pretrained generative ODE. We investigate whether changing the teacher's time parameterization can make these transitions easier to learn. Motivated by the hypothesis that trajectory segments with large normal acceleration are harder to distill, we propose a geometry-aware time reparameterization that allocates more student time to these regions while preserving the teacher's geometric paths and terminal distribution. We derive a shared clock that equalizes a population normal-acceleration statistic under suitable assumptions, and construct a practical approximation from robust, regularized estimates across teacher trajectories. We incorporate this clock into Lagrangian flow-map distillation, using the transformed time coordinate to condition the student. The clock is estimated once before distillation and requires neither teacher retraining nor additional student parameters or inference-time network evaluations. Experiments on synthetic data, CIFAR-10, and CelebA-64 show improved sample quality over identity-time distillation at matched inference budgets, including improvements in one- and two-step image generation. The gains in one-step generation, where no intermediate sampling times can be adjusted, highlight the benefits of time reparameterization during distillation.

</details>


### [263] [CRISP: A Framework for Clause-Reconstructed Interpretable NeuroSymbolic Propositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02431)
*Alex Chan,Shafi Muhtasim Chowdhury,Ekin Can Erkuş,Ole-Christoffer Granmo,Alex Yakovlev,Rishad Shafik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRISP框架将二值神经网络教师模型的最后一层激活向量重构为Tsetlin Machine子句，实现从输入阈值到教师神经元的符号化追溯，兼顾重构保真度与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络决策证据隐藏在隐层激活中而非显式规则，导致其决策难以审计。论文旨在为二值神经教师模型的最终隐藏表示提供一条子句级别的可解释性检查路径。

Method: CRISP对教师模型倒数第二层（logit前）激活进行符号二值化，为每个LLAV神经元分配一个Individual TM（ITM），用基于布尔化输入特征的命题子句表示重构的隐藏位。实验在MNIST、KMNIST、FMNIST、SVHN、CIFAR10上使用BCCNN和全二值BNN教师进行评测，并额外研究二值阈值化、温度计编码、四分位分箱在多种位深下的表现。

Result: LLAV符号二值化在测试的BNN设定下未降低教师头部准确率，ITM重构误差是主要限制因素。四分位一位布尔化在SVHN上取得最佳重构保真度87.52%，在CIFAR10上同样具有竞争力；重构的LLAV在FMNIST上保留了78.41%的教师头部准确率。汇总子句证据可视化显示，学习到的ITM文字集中于居中基准数据的物体区域。

Conclusion: CRISP为检查二值神经教师模型的最终隐藏表示提供了一条子句级途径，在保持教师头部准确率的同时实现了符号化追溯，其中ITM重构误差是关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Deep neural networks achieve high accuracy through layered numerical transformations, yet their decisions remain difficult to audit because decision evidence is encoded in hidden activations rather than explicit rules. This paper introduces CRISP, a framework that reconstructs the last-layer activation vector (LLAV) of binary neural teachers as Tsetlin Machine (TM) clauses. CRISP sign-binarizes the teacher's penultimate pre-logit activations, and assigns one Individual TM (ITM) to each LLAV neuron. Each reconstructed hidden bit is represented by propositional clauses over Booleanized input features, which gives a direct symbolic trace from named input thresholds to a named teacher neuron. CRISP is evaluated on MNIST, KMNIST, FashionMNIST (FMNIST), SVHN, and CIFAR10 using a BinaryConnect convolutional neural network (BCCNN) teacher and a fully binary neural network (BNN) teacher, with an additional study on binary thresholding, thermometer encoding, and quartile binning at multiple bit depths. The results show that LLAV sign-binarization does not reduce teacher-head accuracy in the tested BNN setting, while ITM reconstruction error is the main limiting factor. Quartile one-bit Booleanization gives the strongest reconstruction fidelity on SVHN at 87.52% test fidelity and is competitive on CIFAR10, and the reconstructed LLAV preserves 78.41% teacher-head accuracy on FMNIST. Pooled clause-evidence visualizations show that the learned ITM literals concentrate on the object region in centered benchmarks. CRISP therefore provides a clause-level route for inspecting the final hidden representation of binary neural teachers.

</details>


### [264] [Are you Synthesizing or Recalling? Evaluating LLMs on Algorithmic Code Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02438)
*Nickil Maveli,Antonio Vergari,Shay B. Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AlgoREval 基准，将知名算法的代码生成视为"参数化代码检索"，评估 15 个模型在 77 类算法、7 种语言、4 种图输入表示下的检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 代码生成流程不透明，未能显式区分"回忆算法知识"与"推理如何应用"这两个环节；对于预训练语料中广泛存在的经典算法，代码生成更适合被衡量为从内部知识中复现算法，而非合成新算法。

Method: 构建 AlgoREval 基准，包含 599 道题，覆盖 14 个领域的 77 种经典算法、7 种编程语言和 4 种图输入表示；在零样本设置下评估 15 个模型（7B–34B）；并对比提示增强（检索代码片段、结构化算法提示）、SFT 与 GRPO 等改进手段。

Result: 即使是文档充分的算法，检索准确率在不同语言和图输入表示间仍存在显著差异；检索代码片段或结构化算法提示可提升复杂算法的准确率；SFT 带来更广泛的语言增益，GRPO 在特定语言上获得更大的单语言增益。

Conclusion: 参数化代码检索应被视为一种独立且可测量的能力；在缺乏系统验证的情况下，不应直接部署 AI 生成的算法代码。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance in code generation, where success depends on both recalling relevant algorithmic knowledge and reasoning about how to apply it. However, existing LLM pipelines are opaque, with no explicit separation between these two components. We argue that for well-known algorithms whose canonical implementations are widely accessible in pretraining corpora, code generation is better measured as \textit{parametric code retrieval}: reproducing a named algorithm from internalised knowledge rather than synthesizing a novel one. We introduce AlgoREval, a benchmark of 599 problems spanning classical 77 algorithms across 14 domains, 7 programming languages, and 4 graph-input representations to evaluate this capability in isolation, and assess 15 models (7B--34B parameters) in a zero-shot setting. We find substantial variation in retrieval accuracy across languages and input representations, even for widely documented algorithms and show that prompt augmentation with retrieved code snippets or structured algorithmic hints improve accuracy on complex algorithms, while SFT achieves broader language gains and GRPO achieves larger per-language gains on specific languages. Together, our results establish parametric code retrieval as a distinct, measurable capability and caution against deploying AI-generated algorithmic code without systematic validation.\footnote{Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/Nickil21/AlgoREval

</details>


### [265] [A Generative Model of Complex Networks Using Graphons and Neural Inverse Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02439)
*Wooseong Choi,Italo'Ivo Lima Dias Pinto,Chen Sun,Gaurav Gupta,Dong Song,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在函数空间中统一生成图模型的机制性与深度范式，用多重分形阶梯图子（multifractal step graphon）紧凑参数化复杂网络，并训练神经逆算子从图中恢复其参数，从而兼具可解释性与摊销推断能力，且可泛化到未见过的图规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成图模型存在互补的优缺点：机制模型可解释但依赖针对单个实例的估计方法；深度生成模型可摊销推断但牺牲可解释性，且受限于训练时见过的图规模。科学应用需要一个能同时保留两种范式优点的框架。

Method: 在函数空间中形式化生成模型与参数恢复。提出多重分形阶梯图子，通过递归构造扩展标准阶梯图子，紧凑参数化复杂网络。该表述允许训练一个神经逆算子来恢复其参数，从而支持对未见图规模的推断。模型仅在合成的多重分形阶梯图子实现上训练。

Result: 在零样本图生成基准上，与在真实网络预训练的图基础模型相比，本方法在四个指标中的三个上取得最佳平均表现，说明可迁移到真实图。在单观测网络上（深度模型因需训练语料而难以处理的场景），表现与逐图优化的实例特定方法相当。在多被试EEG案例中，推断参数比传统网络统计量更敏感地追踪脑状态的可逆变化。

Conclusion: 结果表明，机制可解释性与摊销推断可在生成图模型中有效统一，从而增强对复杂网络的理解。

Abstract: Generative graph models are central to understanding and simulating complex networks. However, existing approaches have complementary strengths and limitations. Mechanistic models offer interpretability but rely on instance-specific estimation methods. Deep generative models, on the other hand, offer amortized inference at the cost of interpretability and are largely limited to graph sizes seen during training. Scientific applications motivate a framework that retains the strengths of both paradigms. We bridge them by formulating both the generative model and parameter recovery in function space. A multifractal step graphon extends standard step graphons with a recursive construction that compactly parameterizes complex networks. This formulation admits a neural inverse operator to recover its parameters, enabling inference on unseen graph sizes. We evaluate our model, trained only on synthetic multifractal step graphon realizations, against both paradigms. Against a graph foundation model pretrained on empirical networks, our method achieves the best average performance on three of four metrics in a zero-shot graph-generation benchmark, indicating that the model transfers to real-world graphs. We also apply our method to single-observation networks, a regime largely inaccessible to deep models that require training corpora, where it performs comparably to an instance-specific method that optimizes on each graph. In a multi-subject EEG case study, the inferred parameters track a reversible change in brain state more sensitively than traditional network statistics. Together, these results indicate that mechanistic interpretability and amortized inference can be effectively unified in a generative graph model to enhance our understanding of complex networks.

</details>


### [266] [Bandits via Additive Quantized Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02440)
*Ami Tavory,Noam Touitou,Tal Sarig,Frank Cheng,Ido Guy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出残差量化（RQ）作为表示层，将连续上下文映射为多层级离散质心分配，使线性/加性上下文赌博机算法在严格有限内存下获得非线性表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文赌博机需在非线性奖励建模与在线效率间权衡：树集成和神经网络能捕捉非线性，但需周期性重训练和大型回放缓冲区；线性模型可逐观测高效更新且内存为O(1)，但受限于线性奖励结构。作者希望弥合二者差距。

Method: 提出残差量化（RQ）作为表示层：离线训练RQ码本，通过阴影机制动态设定，将连续上下文映射为多层级离散质心分配；在此基础上构建一系列加性赌博机算法，实现非线性表达力且严格有界内存。

Result: 在13个数据集上，RQ变体在11个数据集上优于其非RQ对应版本，且常优势明显；同时能以最多少1000倍的内存匹配需要周期性重训练的XGBoost和神经网络基线。

Conclusion: RQ作为表示层有效桥接了非线性建模与在线效率，使加性赌博机算法在有限内存下达到与强基线相当的性能，验证了该方法的实用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Contextual bandits require balancing nonlinear reward modeling with online efficiency. Tree ensembles and neural methods capture nonlinearities but require periodic retraining and large replay buffers. Linear models update efficiently per observation with O(1) memory, but are fundamentally restricted to linear reward structures. We propose Residual Quantization (RQ) as a representation layer to bridge this gap. An offline-trained RQ codebook maps continuous contexts into discrete centroid assignments across multiple levels, set dynamically through a shadow mechanism. This enables a spectrum of additive bandit algorithms that achieve nonlinear expressivity with strictly bounded memory. Across 13 datasets, RQ variants beat their non-RQ counterparts on 11 of 13 datasets, often by wide margins, while matching doubling-retrain XGBoost and neural baselines using up to 1000 times less memory.

</details>


### [267] [AI-driven Thermal-aware Data Center Capacity Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02442)
*Yixing Li,Mark Fenton,Matthew Kaufeler,Ka Ming Leung,Xin Ai,Zhiyu Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种AI驱动框架，利用嵌入的AI模型从机架功率、服务器功率、服务器布局、HVAC设置等关键参数中学习，可在毫秒级预测温度，实现秒级数据中心热感知容量规划，对未见设计达高精度并有10000倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型带来密集GPU计算，导致数据中心局部热点，训练与推理突发时热负荷剧增，使实时冷却响应难以预测与控制。热感知容量规划依赖大量昂贵的高保真CFD仿真，而现有AI方法要么预测误差大，要么对数据中心运行假设过度简化。

Method: 构建AI驱动框架：嵌入AI模型学习机架功率、服务器功率、服务器放置、HVAC设置等多关键参数，并以高保真CFD仿真进行测试验证；基于该AI模型设计热感知容量规划框架。

Result: AI模型可在毫秒内给出温度预测；对未见数据中心设计达到高精度，相比高保真CFD实现10000倍加速；框架支持数据中心设计者与运维者即时优化工作负载分布与HVAC冷却效率。

Conclusion: 该AI驱动框架可在数秒内完成真实数据中心的热感知容量规划，兼顾高精度与实时性，为应对LLM带来的热管理挑战提供高效工具。

Abstract: The emerging of large language models (LLMs) has posed significant challenges to the thermal management of data center. Intense GPU computation for LLMs results in localized hotspots. Moreover, spiking thermal loads during training and inference bursts make real-time cooling response more difficult to predict and control. Thermal-aware capacity planning of data center requires massive expensive high-fidelity CFD simulations. AI models can perform real-time prediction for unseen designs. However, existing works either have large prediction error, or have over-simplified assumptions for data center operations. This work presents an AI-driven framework that can perform thermal-aware capacity planning for a real-world data center in seconds. The embedded AI model learns from numerous key parameters (rack power, server power, server placement, HVAC settings etc.), and provides temperature prediction within milliseconds. This AI model is tested against high-fidelity CFD simulations, and results show that for unseen data center designs, model can achieve high accuracy with 10000X speedup. Driven by the AI model, the authors design the thermal-aware capacity planning framework. This framework can help data center designers and operators instantaneously optimize both workload distribution and HVAC cooling efficiency.

</details>


### [268] [A Composable AI-Accelerated Iterative Solver for 3D-IC Thermal Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02461)
*Yixing Li,Jiahang Zhou,Zhiyu Zeng,Xin Ai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DAIST，将全局封装热仿真分解为块级子域并用神经算子求解，通过界面温度与热流迭代耦合，实现跨拓扑复用与可控精度-速度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构2.5D/3D-IC封装热分析精确但计算代价高：单次全封装FEM耗时数小时，而现有AI代理将整个堆叠视为单一预测目标，存在"拓扑锁定"，芯片数量或结构变化即需重新训练。

Method: DAIST采用域分解的AI加速迭代求解：将全局封装仿真拆解为块级子域问题，用神经算子替换子域求解器，并通过子域间界面温度与热流的迭代交换进行耦合，形成"局部到全局"架构。

Result: 在多芯粒系统与先进封装系统上评估，相比传统FEM最高加速178倍，平均温度误差分别为0.068%和0.323%，并实现了块级模型在结构差异明显的封装组件间的跨拓扑复用。

Conclusion: DAIST通过块级神经算子与迭代耦合消除了单体模型的拓扑锁定，支持无需重训练的跨拓扑复用，并提供可通过迭代预算调节的精度-运行时间权衡。

Abstract: Accurate thermal analysis of heterogeneous 2.5D/3D-IC packages is essential yet computationally prohibitive. A single full-package FEM simulation can take hours, while AI-based surrogates treat the entire stack as a monolithic prediction target and must be retrained whenever the die count or topology changes. To address this limitation, this work proposes Domain-Decomposed AI-Accelerated Iterative Solver for Thermal Analysis (DAIST), a composable thermal solver that decomposes the global package simulation into block-level subdomain problems, replaces subdomain solvers with neural operators, and couples them through iterative exchanges of interfacial temperature and heat flux. This local-to-global architecture eliminates the topology lock-in of monolithic models: block-level neural operators can be directly reused in unseen package assemblies without retraining. The iterative coupling strategy further provides a controllable accuracy-runtime tradeoff, where the iteration budget can be adjusted to trade accuracy for runtime. Evaluated on a multi-chiplet system and an advanced packaging system, DAIST achieves up to $178\times$ speedup over traditional FEM solvers with mean temperature errors of 0.068% and 0.323%, respectively, while demonstrating cross-topology reuse of block-level models across structurally distinct package assemblies.

</details>


### [269] [Capability Scaling-Down Laws for LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02462)
*Xueqi Cheng,Liang Wu,Kelly Wan,Liangjie Hong,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究LLM压缩（剪枝、量化、蒸馏）的能力下降缩放律，提出紧凑预测关系，量化压缩配置选择的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM压缩能降低推理成本与内存，但方法选择与配置设定长期依赖经验：相同资源削减可能带来截然不同的能力损失，缺乏可预测的规律指导选型。

Method: 构建能力下降缩放律框架，在数学、代码生成、问答任务上测量能力损失，并将其与模型规模、训练阶段、压缩设置、数据可用性及训练曝光度相关联；提出简单预测关系，评估其精度、测量效率及对未见配置与模型状态的泛化能力。开展受控蒸馏实验与跨两模型族的独立评估，比较数值选择、配置中位数与固定方法优先级的决策价值。

Result: 跨剪枝层级共享密度响应可将拟合剪枝预测器所需的配置测量量减半；在新Pythia状态、预注册OLMo-2测试状态及Wanda剪枝下，紧凑关系在数学与代码上以每token 0.020 nats以内的误差匹配全量测量回归（系数按设置重新拟合）。蒸馏实验中重度数据复用的代价在多个问答分布上重现，净收益取决于评估分布。方法选择在问答上捕获了大部分跨方法收益，固定方法优先级达到相同regret，而数学与代码机会较小。

Conclusion: 研究澄清了能力下降缩放律的预测范围及其在压缩方法选择中的实用价值，代码已公开。

Abstract: LLM compression reduces inference costs and memory requirements, but selecting a method and configuration remains largely empirical because comparable resource reductions can produce different capability losses. We systematically investigate capability scaling-down laws for LLM compression across pruning, quantization, and distillation. Our framework measures capability loss in mathematics, code generation, and question answering, and relates these measurements to model size, training stage, compression settings, data availability, and training exposure. We develop simple predictive relations and evaluate their accuracy, measurement efficiency, and generalization to unseen configurations and model states. Sharing the density response across pruning levels halves the configuration measurements needed to fit a pruning predictor: on new Pythia states, on pre-registered OLMo-2 test states and under Wanda pruning, the compact relation matches a regression fitted with all measurements on math and code to within 0.020 nats per token, with coefficients refitted for each setting. Controlled distillation experiments show that the cost of heavy data reuse recurs across question-answering distributions, while the net benefit depends on the evaluation distribution. We further evaluate the decision value of these predictions by comparing numerical selection with configuration medians and fixed method priorities. Independent evaluations across two model families show that selection captures most of the available cross-method benefit for question answering within the tested candidate sets, where a fixed method priority attains the same regret, with smaller opportunities for mathematics and code. These results clarify the predictive scope of capability scaling-down laws and their use in compression method selection. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/LabRAI/scaling_down_law.

</details>


### [270] [Threshold-Aware Conformal Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02487)
*Shiwei Tan,Huzefa Rangwala,Danielle C. Maddix*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出阈值感知保形路由（TACR），通过让预测区间在决策边界附近更紧，减少不必要的模拟器调用，同时保持分布无关的边缘覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真模拟在科学与工程设计中至关重要，但反复运行成本高昂。学习型代理模型虽快，但误差较大可能改变下游决策，由此产生准确率与速度的权衡：需要判断何时可用代理模型、何时仍需完整模拟器。标准保形预测构建区间时未考虑下游决策阈值，导致即使很窄的区间若靠近阈值也会跨越阈值而触发模拟，而远离阈值的较宽区间反而无需模拟。

Method: 研究由标量关注量是否高于/低于固定阈值所决定的决策问题。对每个输入：当代理模型的保形区间完全位于阈值一侧时使用代理；当区间与阈值相交时路由到模拟器。提出TACR，学习依赖输入的尺度，采用阈值感知目标，使区间紧致性集中在决策边界附近。在留出数据上进行精确的分割保形校准，保持分布无关的边缘覆盖。

Result: 在多个科学与工程数据集上，相同覆盖目标下，TACR相比标准保形预测将模拟器延迟减少14-75%。与预测精度相近但无阈值局部加权的变体相比，TACR在四个数据集上进一步将延迟减少10-24%。

Conclusion: 为路由优化区间分配可以在不削弱标准保形保证的前提下减少模拟器调用次数。分布无关的边缘覆盖同时也上界了未被路由的错误阈值决策概率。

Abstract: High-fidelity simulations are essential to scientific and engineering design, but can be expensive to run repeatedly. Learned surrogates offer a faster alternative, yet their higher errors may alter downstream decisions. This accuracy-speed tradeoff creates a need to determine whether a surrogate can be used or the full simulator remains necessary. We study decisions determined by whether a scalar quantity of interest lies above or below a fixed threshold. For each input, we use the surrogate when its conformal interval lies entirely on one side of the threshold and route the input to simulation when the interval intersects it. Standard conformal prediction constructs intervals without reference to the downstream decision threshold: even a narrow interval near the threshold can cross it and trigger simulation, whereas a wider interval farther away can remain entirely on one side and require no simulation. We introduce Threshold-Aware Conformal Routing (TACR), which learns an input-dependent scale using a threshold-aware objective that concentrates interval tightness near the decision boundary. Exact split-conformal calibration on held-out data preserves distribution-free marginal coverage, which also upper-bounds the probability of an incorrect threshold decision that is not routed. Across various scientific and engineering datasets, TACR reduces simulator deferrals by 14-75% relative to standard conformal prediction at the same coverage target. Against a variant without threshold-local weighting but with similar predictor accuracy, TACR further reduces deferrals by 10-24% on four datasets. These results show that optimizing interval allocation for routing can reduce simulator calls without weakening the standard conformal guarantee.

</details>


### [271] [Harnessing LLMs as Agents: What Does It Cost?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02488)
*Zelin Zhao,Xinyu Guo,Jingyuan Zhang,Yuxuan Zhang,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"语言模型智能体机器"(LAM),一个资源受限的抽象框架,在固定底层语义模型的同时,显式计入智能体外围机制(harness)所消耗的计算资源,并给出通信、访问、重计算与可靠性四类理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体日益依赖管理有界上下文、持久记忆、工具、验证与重复执行的外围机制(harness),但现有模型能力概念并未量化这些机制所消耗的计算资源。作者希望建立一套资源理论,刻画智能体外围机制的计算成本。

Method: 作者定义LAM(语言模型智能体机器)作为资源受限抽象:固定底层语义模型,显式对harness层级资源计费。在此框架下分别研究通信(将LAM执行等价为带调用-传输预算的红蓝卵石模型,迁移经典I/O下界)、访问(内存接口导致渐近分离,如指针追逐中随机与非投机顺序访问的Θ(n)差距)、重计算(位反转DAG在上下文容量C与持久记忆容量S下的模型调用次数Θ(n²/(C+S)+n))以及可靠性(紧的阶段局部采样界、精确的不完美验证成本、带闭式最优验证区间的Young-Daly型检查点定律)。并在GPT-6 Astra上进行受控与留出实验。

Result: 实验验证了通信与可靠性的预测,包括检查点最优值、程序化检查下的策略选择,以及链式MATH任务中调用粒度、逻辑输入流量与可靠性之间的权衡。位反转DAG结果量化了何时存储中间状态可避免重复的语义计算。

Conclusion: 这些结果共同构成了一套关于语言模型智能体外围机制计算成本的资源理论。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly rely on harnesses that manage bounded context, persistent memory, tools, verification, and repeated execution, yet existing notions of model capability do not quantify the computational resources these mechanisms consume. We introduce the Language Model Agent Machine (LAM), a resource-bounded abstraction that fixes the underlying semantic model while explicitly charging harness-level resources. We establish four classes of results. Communication: LAM execution is instancewise equivalent to red--blue pebbling under simultaneous call--transfer budgets, transferring classical I/O lower bounds to context--memory traffic. Access: memory interfaces induce asymptotic separations, including a $Θ(n)$ gap between random and non-speculative sequential access on pointer chasing. Recomputation: bit-reversal DAGs require $Θ(n^2/(C+S)+n)$ model calls with context capacity $C$ and persistent-memory capacity $S$, quantifying when stored intermediate state avoids repeated semantic computation. Reliability: we derive tight stage-local sampling bounds, exact imperfect-verification costs, and a Young--Daly-type checkpoint law with a closed-form optimal verification interval. Controlled and held-out experiments on GPT-6 Astra test communication and reliability predictions, including checkpoint optima, policy selection under programmatic checking, and tradeoffs among call granularity, logical input traffic, and reliability on chained MATH tasks. Together, these results provide a resource theory for the computational cost of language-model agent harnesses.

</details>


### [272] [LiteEMG-FM: An Efficient and Deployable Foundation Model for Robust EMG Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02497)
*Tianhao Wu,Xu Wu,Amirmohammad Radmehr,Jiawei Yu,Yi Wu,Phuc Nguyen,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LiteEMG-FM，一个高效的混合 CNN-Transformer 肌电基础模型，结合分层唤醒架构，实现跨用户、跨数据集的泛化与资源受限可穿戴部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMG 信号在个体、身体部位、记录会话和传感硬件间差异大，限制了辅助设备与交互模型的泛化；现有时间序列基础模型计算开销大，难以实时可穿戴部署，且未很好捕捉 EMG 特有的时频特征。

Method: 构建混合 CNN-Transformer 基础模型，在 16 个上肢与下肢 EMG 数据集上预训练；采用分层唤醒架构，由常开轻量 1D-CNN 过滤静息与非目标活动，仅在有效手势时激活主模型；评估全推理卸载、拆分推理与全端侧处理三种方案。

Result: 在多种评测设置下优于现有时序基础模型和监督基线，尤其在零校准跨参与者和数据稀缺条件下表现突出；并刻画了不同部署方案在时延、功耗与内存占用上的权衡。

Conclusion: LiteEMG-FM 是一个有效、高效且可部署的 EMG 基础模型，适用于实际肌电应用。

Abstract: Electromyography (EMG) signals vary substantially across individuals, body regions, recording sessions, and sensing hardware, limiting the generalization of models for assistive devices and human-computer interaction. Existing time-series foundation models are also computationally expensive for real-time wearable deployment and often fail to capture EMG-specific time-frequency characteristics. We present LiteEMG-FM, an efficient hybrid CNN-Transformer foundation model for practical EMG sensing. Pretrained on 16 diverse upper- and lower-limb EMG datasets, LiteEMG-FM learns representations that generalize across users and datasets. For resource-constrained deployment, we implement a hierarchical wake-up architecture in which a lightweight, always-on 1D-CNN filters rest and non-target activity and activates LiteEMG-FM only for valid gestures. We evaluate full inference offloading, split inference, and full on-device processing, characterizing their trade-offs in latency, power consumption, and memory footprint. Across diverse evaluation settings, LiteEMG-FM outperforms state-of-the-art time-series foundation models and supervised baselines, particularly under zero-calibration cross-participant and data-scarce conditions. These results demonstrate that LiteEMG-FM is an effective, efficient, and deployable foundation model for EMG applications.

</details>


### [273] [Multi-Fidelity Policy Gradients Stabilize Data-Scarce Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02505)
*Xinjie Liu,Ruihan Zhao,Anirban Chaudhuri,Cyrus Neary,Ufuk Topcu,David Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MFPG-PPO，将多保真策略梯度框架扩展到现代actor-critic学习，通过重新设计采样、优势估计与控制变量构造来保持跨保真相关性并监控估计不确定性，在GPU并行仿真与真实Franka机械臂上取得稳定且高效的学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在on-policy强化学习中，目标域高保真数据昂贵稀缺，导致策略梯度估计噪声大、训练不稳定。虽然可用廉价但有偏的低保真数据，但直接用其优化会引入偏差。MFPG框架本可仅用低保真数据构造控制变量来降方差且不引入偏差，然而已有工作仅限于小规模仿真上的REINFORCE，缺乏面向现代actor-critic方法的应用。

Method: 作者将MFPG扩展到现代actor-critic学习（GPU并行仿真与真实机器人）。分析并发现朴素地将MFPG扩展到PPO会丢失跨保真相关性或放大方差。为此提出MFPG-PPO：重新设计采样、优势估计和控制变量构造以保持跨保真相关性，并通过监控估计器不确定性来防止方差膨胀；此外引入预算感知的MFPG-PPO，在高低保真数据源间分配固定采样预算。

Result: 在难度与HF预算各异的仿真机器人运动任务中，MFPG-PPO在几乎所有设置下优于仅用HF数据训练的PPO；在最难任务的最小HF预算下，其性能持续匹配使用了16倍HF数据的PPO。相比之下，大多数使用LF数据的基线仅在直接LF-to-HF迁移成功的场景表现良好。MFPG-PPO还能在真实Franka机械臂上稳定学习，每次更新仅用4个真实机器人回合且无需人类演示。

Conclusion: MFPG-PPO成功将多保真策略梯度方法推广到现代actor-critic强化学习，在仿真与真实机器人任务中显著提升高保真数据效率并保持训练稳定性，为降低真实机器人学习成本提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Policy gradient methods for on-policy reinforcement learning (RL) can become unstable when expensive, scarce target-domain data yield noisy gradient estimates. We address this challenge by complementing limited high-fidelity (HF) target-domain data with abundant, cheap, but biased low-fidelity (LF) data, e.g., from a simplified simulator. Most existing methods directly optimize biased objectives based on LF data. In contrast, the recently introduced multi-fidelity policy gradient (MFPG) framework uses LF data solely to construct a control variate that reduces variance and improves HF data efficiency without biasing the policy gradient estimator. However, published work on MFPG is limited to REINFORCE on small-scale simulation tasks. We develop MFPG for modern actor-critic learning in GPU-parallel simulation and on a physical robot. Our analysis and experiments show that naive extensions to proximal policy optimization (PPO) can lose cross-fidelity correlation or inflate variance. Our MFPG-PPO addresses these failures by redesigning the sampling, advantage estimation, and control variate construction to preserve cross-fidelity correlation, and by monitoring estimator uncertainty to prevent variance inflation. We also introduce a budget-aware MFPG-PPO to divide a fixed sampling budget among high- and low-fidelity data sources. Across simulated robot locomotion tasks of varying LF-to-HF transfer difficulty and HF data budgets, MFPG-PPO improves upon PPO trained on HF data alone in nearly all settings, and consistently matches the performance of PPO trained with 16x more HF data on the hardest task at the smallest HF budgets. In contrast, most baselines that use LF data perform well only where direct LF-to-HF transfer succeeds. MFPG-PPO enables stable learning on a physical Franka arm using only 4 real-robot episodes per update and no human demonstrations.

</details>


### [274] [Post-Training Quantization of Autoregressive Weather Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02511)
*Ananyo Bhattacharya,Swastik Bhattacharya,Christiane Jablonowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统评估了训练后量化(PTQ)在预训练全球天气预报AI仿真器(DLWP与FourCastNet)上的效果，为地球物理流体动力学应用提供了量化基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率数值天气预报(NWP)和深度学习天气仿真器虽能显著降低计算成本，但在GPU上仍依赖硬件加速的矩阵乘法，规模化到高分辨率时计算与资源开销依然巨大。训练后量化(PTQ)可在多类DL架构中加速推理并降低功耗，但尚未在自回归天气仿真器上进行系统性验证。

Method: 在预训练的DLWP和FourCastNet两个全球尺度天气仿真器上实现PTQ算法，作为地球物理流体动力学应用的概念验证；使用模拟量化(simulated quantization)系统考察不同PTQ配置对短程预报时段自回归推理的影响。

Result: 基于模拟量化的PTQ配置评估显示，模型在短时预报时段内仍能给出定性上有意义的预报结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为自回归天气仿真器提供了PTQ的首个基准，并为面向动力系统的DL模型量化优化奠定了基础。

Abstract: Advancements in high-resolution numerical weather prediction (NWP) and data assimilation (DA) have shaped the developments in deep learning (DL) architectures emulating atmospheric dynamics. Emulators for weather forecasting exhibit forecast quality comparable to physics based models at forecast horizon scaling from few days to subseasonal time scales. The emulators are driven by hardware-accelerated matrix multiplication in autoregressive inferences, significantly reducing the computation time and resources required for NWP. Optimization of the matrix multiplication processes in GPU architectures provides opportunities to scale towards high-resolution domain, and offers implementation of out of the box solutions. Post-training quantization (PTQ) has been demonstrated across multiple DL architectures to accelerate and increase the number of computations in unit time while consuming less power, enabling applications on edge hardware. In this study, we investigate the effect of PTQ on pre-trained AI emulators for global-scale weather forecasting. We implement PTQ algorithms in Deep Learning Weather Prediction (DLWP) and FourCastNet (FCN) models as a proof of concept for geophysical fluid dynamics applications. We systematically investigate the effect of PTQ on emulator inferences over short-range forecast horizons. Evaluation of PTQ configurations using simulated quantization hints at qualitatively meaningful forecasts over short-time horizons. These results provide a first benchmark of PTQ for autoregressive weather emulators and a basis for quantization-based optimization of DL models for dynamical systems.

</details>


### [275] [Student-Guided Teacher Distillation for Efficient LLM Task Routing: Positioning Against Jev-Style System-1 Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02516)
*Haifeng Wu,Srinivasan Manoharan,Jian Wan,Fangbo Tu,Junhua Zhao,Xin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 面向60类LLM任务分类，提出学生引导的教师蒸馏流水线：紧凑学生模型一次前向预测全类别分布并召回top-k候选，再由大模型零样本NLI教师仅重排候选，迭代提升学生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本分类器将用户请求路由到专门LLM任务时，需对每个标签逐一评估前提-假设对，成本随分类体系规模线性增长，因此需要降低大规模候选集打分开销。

Method: 固定60类LLM任务体系下构建学生引导的教师蒸馏：ModernBERT学生单次前向预测全类别分布并检索小规模top-k候选；DeBERTa-v3零样本NLI教师仅重排这些候选而非全部60类；教师标签迭代改进学生，生成更锐利的候选。候选生成器在目标体系上端到端训练，并与生产服务模型一致。

Result: 最佳学生检查点在200例评测集上达到77.5%教师一致性；初步覆盖度测量显示Coverage@16为91-100%。同时发现截断的top-k教师分数不能当作完整60类软目标用于KL蒸馏，将未截断类置零会破坏软标签蒸馏依赖的暗知识，引入系统性偏差。

Conclusion: top-k候选集可保留大部分教师决策相关信息，学生引导蒸馏具备降低路由成本的潜力；但完整评测（多k覆盖度、嵌入检索基线、更大人审测试集）仍在进行中。

Abstract: Zero-shot classifiers are useful for routing user requests to specialized LLM tasks, but scoring every request against a large candidate set is expensive: a zero-shot NLI classifier must evaluate one premise-hypothesis pair per label, so cost scales linearly with taxonomy size. We study a student-guided teacher distillation pipeline for a fixed taxonomy of 60 LLM task categories: a compact ModernBERT classifier predicts the full category distribution in one forward pass and retrieves a small top-k candidate set, and a larger DeBERTa-v3 zero-shot NLI classifier reranks only those candidates rather than all 60 labels; the resulting teacher labels iteratively improve the student, which produces sharper candidates for the next round. Unlike generic embedding retrieval or clustering-derived shortlists used in extreme multi-label classification, our candidate generator is trained end-to-end on the target taxonomy and is the same model serving production traffic, distinguishing it from LLM-routing work that routes between candidate models, and from concurrent System-1 encoder-classifier proposals (e.g. TypeSafe AI's Jev and the open-source Laya project) whose training methodology is undocumented or RL-based. Our best student checkpoint reaches 77.5% teacher agreement on a 200-example evaluation set, and preliminary coverage measurements show Coverage@16 of 91-100%, suggesting top-k sets retain most of the teacher's decision-relevant information. We further show truncated top-k teacher scores should not be treated as full 60-class soft targets for KL distillation: zeroing untruncated classes destroys the dark knowledge soft-label distillation depends on, introducing systematic bias rather than a harmless sparse approximation. A complete evaluation, including coverage at multiple k on a held-out set, an embedding-retrieval baseline, and a larger human-reviewed test set, remains in progress.

</details>


### [276] [Learning the Latent Structure: A Feature-Centric Approach to Graph Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02517)
*Yu Song,Zhigang Hua,Yan Xie,Bingheng Li,Jingzhe Liu,Bo Long,Jiliang Tang,Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自监督的、以特征为中心（feature-centric）的图数据增强框架 SelfAug，绕过显式结构建模，直接在嵌入空间通过 inverse masking 恢复未观测结构信号，并引入消息正则器与 bootstrap 策略提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实图数据常因采集与观测限制而不完整，严重制约图学习效果。现有图数据增强（GDA）方法存在三大缺陷：依赖标签、计算开销大、本质为直推式（transductive），难以适用于实际场景。

Method: 提出以特征为中心的图数据增强框架，直接在嵌入空间中操作，绕过显式结构建模；通过自监督的 inverse masking 过程捕捉观测图与完整图之间的潜在关联；并引入 message regularizer 与 bootstrap 策略以应对噪声与稀疏监督。

Result: 在覆盖多个领域的十个图数据集上评估，SelfAug 在归纳（inductive）与冷启动（cold-start）设置下，于准确率与效率两方面均持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: SelfAug 无需标签、计算高效且非直推式，具备良好的可扩展性与泛化能力，有望成为现实图学习场景中的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Graph-structured data plays a pivotal role in modeling complex relationships. However, real-world graphs are often incomplete due to data collection and observational constraints, severely limiting the effectiveness of modern graph learning pipelines. While existing Graph Data Augmentation (GDA) methods attempt to refine graph structures for improved downstream performance, they are typically label-dependent, computationally expensive, and inherently transductive, limiting their applicability in practical scenarios. In this work, we present a novel feature-centric graph data augmentation framework that bypasses explicit structure modeling by operating directly in the embedding space. Through a self-supervised inverse masking process, our method captures latent ties between observed and complete graphs, enabling recovery of unobserved structural signals through refined node representations. To enhance robustness under noisy and sparse supervision, we introduce a message regularizer and a bootstrap strategy for effective training and generalization. Evaluated on ten graph datasets spanning multiple domains, our approach, SelfAug, consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both accuracy and efficiency across inductive and cold-start settings, highlighting its potential as a scalable and generalizable solution for real-world graph learning scenarios.

</details>


### [277] [Instance-Dependent Regret for CMDPs with Step-Wise Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02520)
*Qian Zuo,Francesco Emanuele Stradi,Leyang Xue,Sattar Vakili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带逐步安全约束的情景式表格约束MDP在线学习，提出SVAE算法，通过学习候选安全子图并在其中进行方差自适应乐观规划，同时控制约束违反，达到近最优的遗憾与违反界，并给出下界证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
