Existing studies have made substantial progress on how to construct and optimize graphs, but rarely consider a more fundamental question: whether additional relational structures should be introduced for a given dataset and task. Through empirical studies across diverse datasets, tasks, and graph constructors, we reveal that graphification is not consistently beneficial: introducing relational structures can provide substantial improvements in some cases, while offering limited or even negative gains. This observation motivates a new perspective that graph construction should be treated as a selective decision based on its expected utility rather than a default preprocessing step. To address this issue, we propose MAG-SCOUT, a pre-construction graph assessment framework that estimates whether introducing graph structures is beneficial before generating the complete topology. MAG-SCOUT collects limited relational evidence, analyzes its potential taskspecific contribution, and estimates the expected utility of graphification together with construction cost to make a build-or-skip decision. Extensive experiments across six multimodal datasets, three downstream tasks, and diverse graph constructors demonstrate that MAG-SCOUT effectively identifies when graph structures should be introduced, saving 33.1% of task-macro graph work while retaining 96.7% of held-out positive-gain mass under the pre-registered floor.

</details>


### [314] [Nearly Optimal Fixed-Confidence Best-Arm Identification with 1-Bit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02771)
*Khang Luong,Dinh Thai Son,Hoang Ta,Hung The Tran,Tuan Quang Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究严格1比特反馈下的固定置信度最优臂识别问题，提出基于随机阈值查询与截断尾积分恒等式的时间一致均值估计原语，并嵌入候选-挑战者算法，给出固定截断与分阶段自适应截断两种算法及最坏情况信息论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格1比特反馈约束下（每轮仅收到奖励是否属于查询集合的单比特信息），直接经验均值估计不可用，截断不可避免，需要新的估计原语与算法来在有限方差、无分布假设下实现最优臂识别。

Method: 首先构建基于随机阈值查询和截断尾积分恒等式的时间一致1比特均值估计原语；然后将其嵌入候选-挑战者最优臂识别算法：一是固定截断算法，提供随时可用的(ε,δ)-PAC保证；二是分阶段自适应截断算法，将截断水平与当前分辨率匹配，得到间隙自适应样本复杂度。此外证明了K臂最坏情况信息论下界。

Result: 固定截断算法给出简单的随时(ε,δ)-PAC保证；分阶段自适应截断算法实现间隙自适应样本复杂度；下界表明有限方差1比特反馈导致的对数惩罚是内在的，且该下界与分阶段算法的主导依赖在低阶log log因子内匹配。

Conclusion: 在严格1比特反馈与有限方差设定下，所提原语与分阶段自适应截断算法可达到与信息论下界匹配的样本复杂度（至低阶log log因子），说明由1比特反馈引起的对数惩罚不可避免。

Abstract: We study fixed-confidence best-arm identification under strict 1-bit feedback constraints. At each round, the learner selects an arm and a query set, and receives only a single bit indicating whether the sampled reward belongs to that set. We consider a distribution-free finite-variance setting with arm-wise localization, where direct empirical mean estimation is no longer available and clipping becomes unavoidable. We first formulate a time-uniform 1-bit mean-estimation primitive based on randomized threshold queries and a clipped tail-integral identity. We then embed this primitive into candidate-challenger best-arm identification algorithms. A fixed-clipping algorithm gives a simple anytime $(ε,δ)$-PAC guarantee, while a phased adaptive-clipping algorithm matches the clipping level to the current resolution and yields a gap-adaptive sample complexity. We also prove a $K$-arm worst-case information-theoretic lower bound showing that the logarithmic penalty caused by finite-variance 1-bit feedback is intrinsic. This bound matches the leading dependence of the phased algorithm up to lower-order $\log\log$ factors.

</details>


### [315] [LatticeSMC: Where to Spend Inference-Time Compute in Chunked Sequence Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02774)
*Xuanchen Wang,Heng Wang,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出预算匹配的分块引导采样框架与LatticeSMC采样器，通过同时在"分块索引"和"去噪步"两个维度上重采样，在同等计算量下显著提升长序列生成的奖励对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列生成器（音乐、运动、视频）以分块方式产生序列，奖励却定义在整个序列上。现有推理期引导方法通常只作用于单一维度：末端best-of-N、跨去噪步的Feynman-Kac引导、或跨块流式剪枝，且常在不匹配的计算量或不同回报规则下比较，缺乏公平评估与统一设计原则。

Method: 提出预算匹配的分块引导（budget-matched chunked steering）设定；构建定义在"分块索引 × 去噪步"二维格点上的Feynman-Kac模型，由此导出LatticeSMC采样器。两个望远镜式（telescoping）结论支撑其精确性：对分块可加奖励，两个维度导出相同权重，故应在前瞻成本最低处重采样；对终端奖励，任意前缀得分可定义精确中间势函数，从而得到无需估计、无需额外去噪调用的前缀可评估奖励扭转。LatticeSMC在分块边界处基于这些势函数重采样，评分免费时还在块内重采样，返回加权样本或最优粒子。

Result: 在同等计算量下：音乐到舞蹈扩散任务中拍点对齐从0.234提升至0.441（best-of-N为0.354）；40秒文本到音乐生成中提示遵循度在32粒子时从0.470升至0.560，同时保持留出质量。在长程奖励上仍保持优势，人类评估者在60-77%的成对比较中偏好该方法。此外发现：承诺强度应跟随当前势函数所含信息，而前瞻的价值可由未来奖励的组内可预测性来预测。

Conclusion: 将分块引导统一到二维格点上的Feynman-Kac框架可导出精确且预算匹配的采样规则；LatticeSMC在多项长序列生成任务上以相同计算量取得更优奖励对齐与人类偏好，并给出承诺强度与前瞻价值的设计判据。

Abstract: Long-form generators for music, motion and video produce sequences chunk by chunk, with each chunk generated by iterative denoising while rewards are defined over the full sequence. Existing inference-time steering methods typically act on one axis at a time: best-of-N at the end, Feynman-Kac steering across denoising steps, or streaming pruning across chunks, and are often compared under unmatched compute or different return rules. We introduce budget-matched chunked steering and propose LatticeSMC, a sampler derived from a Feynman-Kac model on the two-dimensional lattice of chunk index and denoising step. Two telescoping results make its design exact: for chunk-additive rewards, the two axes induce identical weights, so resampling should occur where lookahead is cheapest; for terminal rewards, any prefix score defines an exact intermediate potential, making prefix-evaluable rewards twists with no estimation or extra denoiser calls. LatticeSMC resamples on these potentials at chunk boundaries and, when scoring is free, within chunks, returning either a weighted draw or the best particle. Under matched compute, on music-to-dance diffusion and 40-second text-to-music generation, it raises beat alignment from 0.234 to 0.441 (best-of-N: 0.354) and prompt adherence from 0.470 to 0.560 at 32 particles, while preserving held-out quality. It also retains its advantage on long-range rewards and is preferred by human raters in 60-77 percent of pairwise comparisons. Finally, we show that commitment strength should follow the information in the current potential, while the value of lookahead is predicted by the within-set predictability of future reward.

</details>


### [316] [Controlling Polar Exposure to Delay Memorization in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02780)
*Xuanchen Wang,Heng Wang,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出质量门控去白化（QGD）控制器与复制预算选择（CBS），通过保留快速极坐标更新前缀并逐步恢复固定增益动量，延迟样本特定记忆化，从而扩大扩散模型的泛化窗口、改善质量-复制权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在开始复制训练样本前即可达到可用样本质量，但快速优化会加速样本特定拟合，从而压缩这一泛化窗口。作者希望通过更新几何视角研究该效应，找到延迟复制同时保持质量提升的控制方法。

Method: 提出QGD控制器：保留快速极坐标更新前缀，逐步恢复固定增益动量，实现机制包括确认的质量门、有界衰减包络和因果复制反馈。随机特征分析将记忆化时钟分为协方差控制、曲率均衡和幅度控制三类，并在对齐谱假设下给出有限曝光条件。配套CBS对不同检查点族进行同时二项校准，并对发布检查点做独立评估。

Result: 在2,000张图像的CIFAR-10子集上，QGD保持极坐标基线的质量到达时间，将有效区间扩大8.32倍，并将共同检查点复制降低75.9%。相同校准和独立质量评估下，QGD的FID为75.56，优于同选择器SGD的79.37。曝光匹配对照、独立检测器审计以及在流匹配和舞蹈生成上的迁移结果均支持该方法。

Conclusion: 自适应曝光控制是一种改善质量-复制权衡的实用方法，QGD通过延迟复制、扩大泛化窗口，在不损失质量到达速度的前提下显著提升样本质量并减少复制。

Abstract: Diffusion models can reach useful sample quality before copying training examples, but fast optimization can compress this generalization window by accelerating sample-specific fitting. We investigate this effect through update geometry and propose Quality-Gated De-whitening (QGD), a controller that retains a fast polar-update prefix and progressively restores fixed-gain momentum. Our random-feature analysis separates covariance-controlled, curvature-equalized and amplitude-controlled memorization clocks. Under aligned spectral assumptions, it establishes a finite-exposure condition under which a fixed-gain tail recovers a delay proportional to dataset size. QGD implements this principle with a confirmed quality gate, a bounded decay envelope and causal copy feedback. Immediate switching is the conservative limit; gradual control balances delayed copying against continued quality improvement. We pair QGD with Copy-Budgeted Selection (CBS), which applies simultaneous binomial calibration to a frozen checkpoint family, followed by a fresh evaluation of the released checkpoint. On 2,000-image CIFAR-10 subsets, QGD preserves the polar baseline's quality-arrival time while expanding its useful interval by 8.32x and reducing common-checkpoint copying by 75.9%. With identical calibration and independent quality evaluation, QGD achieves FID 75.56 versus 79.37 for SGD with the same selector. Exposure-matched controls, independent detector audits and transfer to flow matching and dance generation support adaptive exposure control as a practical way to improve the quality-copying tradeoff.

</details>


### [317] [OPD Before RL: Warm-Starting Rubric-Based RL with On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02781)
*Xinpeng Wang,Wei Shi,Yu-Chia Chen,Maria Zontak,Yun He,Richard Yuanzhe Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种两阶段训练框架：先用评分标准（rubrics）作为特权教师上下文进行on-policy蒸馏（RP-OPD），再用评分标准奖励进行强化学习（RL），在健康与科学任务上取得最优效果并减少reward hacking。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多语言模型任务难以用精确的结果验证来评估，基于评分标准的强化学习（RL）通过显式准则为开放式回答打分来解决这一问题。但奖励是在完整回答之后才给出的，训练信号无法直接指出哪些具体决策导致了最终得分，因此需要一种能提供更细粒度监督的方法。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段为“评分标准特权on-policy蒸馏”（RP-OPD），让无法访问评分标准的学生模型在自身生成的prefix上匹配具备评分标准感知的教师的next-token分布，从而获得稠密的token级监督；第二阶段用RL直接优化评分标准奖励，突破蒸馏平台期。在HealthBench、ResearchQA和RubricHub Science上，使用开放权重模型比较多种后训练方法，并改变RL前的SFT或RP-OPD训练量。

Result: 在所有评估的方法中，两阶段框架（RP-OPD + RL）取得最高分数。在RubricHub Science上，RP-OPD + RL显示出有限的reward hacking迹象，而SFT + RL基线则越来越多地因声称符合评分标准却未提供所需内容而获得高奖励。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持在应用基于评分标准的RL之前，先使用评分标准指导on-policy蒸馏，从而提供更细粒度的监督并缓解reward hacking问题。

Abstract: Many useful language-model tasks cannot be evaluated by exact outcome verification. Rubric-based reinforcement learning (RL) addresses this issue by scoring open-ended responses against explicit criteria. However, because the reward is assigned after the complete response, the training signal does not directly identify which individual decisions contributed to the final score. We propose a two-stage training framework that uses rubrics first as privileged teacher context for dense token-level supervision, then as rewards for further RL. In the first stage, rubric-privileged on-policy distillation (RP-OPD), a student without access to the rubric matches a rubric-aware teacher's next-token distributions at student-generated prefixes. In the second stage, RL directly optimizes the rubric reward and improves beyond the observed distillation plateau. We evaluate the framework on health and science tasks using open-weight models. Across HealthBench, ResearchQA, and RubricHub Science, we compare post-training methods and vary the amount of SFT or RP-OPD training before RL, finding that our two-stage framework achieves the highest scores among the methods evaluated. RP-OPD + RL shows limited signs of reward hacking on RubricHub Science, whereas the SFT + RL baseline increasingly receives high rewards for claims of rubric compliance without providing the required content. These findings support using rubrics to guide on-policy distillation before applying rubric-based RL.

</details>


### [318] [Efficient Memory Crystallization for Graph Learning under Non-Stationary Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02795)
*Yue Hou,Ruomei Liu,Yingke Su,Junran Wu,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练测试时框架EMC，通过闭式解蒸馏记忆实现高效持续图适应，性能优越且大幅降低开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖辅助生成模块合成记忆图进行跨域适应，计算开销大且难以应对长期分布漂移，因此需要更经济的路径。

Method: EMC无需训练，通过闭式求解面向记忆的分布匹配目标，将每个到来的图域蒸馏为紧凑且语义忠实的记忆，并通过状态演化记忆建模域间依赖，给出更紧的泛化误差界。

Result: 在非平稳分布漂移的图上优于现有最优基线，平均运行时间降低87.4%，GPU内存消耗降低92.4%。

Conclusion: EMC使大规模持续图适应变得实用，兼顾泛化性与适应性。

Abstract: Deep graph learning models deployed in real-world systems often need to cope with non-stationary environments, where the underlying graph distribution drifts continually over time. Prevailing solutions rely on training auxiliary generative modules to synthesize memory graphs for cross-domain adaptation, which incurs substantial computational overhead and scales poorly under prolonged distribution shifts. We argue that a more economical path exists: rather than generating memory, one can crystallize it. To this end, we propose Efficient Memory Crystallization (EMC), a training-free test-time framework that distills each incoming graph domain into a compact, semantically faithful memory through a closed-form solution to a memory-oriented distribution-matching objective, thereby eliminating redundant domain information under continual covariate shifts. To preserve both generalizability and adaptability as the model traverses a long sequence of target domains, EMC further models inter-domain dependencies through state-evolving memories and admits a theoretically grounded, tighter generalization error bound than direct adaptation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance of EMC over state-of-the-art baselines on graphs under non-stationary distribution shifts, while reducing average runtime by 87.4% and GPU memory consumption by 92.4% relative to the recent competitor, making continual graph adaptation practical at scale.

</details>


### [319] [Muon Learns Facts Better: Understanding the Role of Spectral Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02798)
*Xuheng Li,Qiwei Di,Yuan Cao,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过一个可处理的"事实回忆"模型，从机理层面分析 Muon 优化器（谱正交化）如何改变特征学习动力学，证明谱梯度流相比普通梯度流能显著缩小主体特征与关系特征的学习时间差异，并加快误差收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 优化器对矩阵值更新做谱正交化，在大规模神经网络训练中表现优异，但这种变换在特征学习中的机制尚不清楚。作者希望借助一个可解析的模型，揭示谱正交化究竟如何重塑特征学习动力学。

Method: 采用可处理的"事实回忆"模型：一个事实将每个"主体-关系"对映射到一个答案，用线性 transformer 学习恢复该映射所需的主体相关与关系相关信息。分别用梯度流（GF）、谱梯度流（Spectral GF）和 Sign 梯度流（Sign GF）优化，它们分别是梯度下降、Muon 和 Adam 的连续时间极限。以主体/关系预测分量达到目标精度所需的学习时间来刻画特征分离过程。

Result: 当主体数 S 超过关系数 R 时，GF 先学关系相关信息、后学主体相关信息，形成特征分离阶段，其学习时间比为 Θ̃(√(S/R))；而 Spectral GF 将该比值降至 Θ̃(1)。对固定的 S、R，GF 下主体与关系误差随训练时间 T 以 1/(T log T) 衰减，而 Spectral GF 下以 exp(-poly(T)) 指数衰减。此外，GF 与 Spectral GF 在 token 嵌入的正交变换下等变，Sign GF 则不等变：不同的正交嵌入可能导致无特征分离、很大的分离阶段，甚至反转的学习顺序。

Conclusion: 研究给出了谱正交化如何从根本上重塑特征学习动力学的机理视角：Muon 类方法能消除由主体数与关系数不平衡导致的学习时间差异并加速收敛，同时其正交等变性使学习行为对嵌入的正交变换保持不变，而 Adam 类方法不具备这一性质。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer applies spectral orthogonalization to matrix-valued updates and has shown strong performance in large-scale neural network training, yet the mechanisms of this transformation in feature learning remain poorly understood. In this work, we investigate this question through a tractable factual-recall model, where a fact maps each subject-relation pair to an answer, and a linear transformer learns the subject- and relation-dependent information required to recover this mapping. The transformer is optimized with gradient flow (GF), spectral GF, or Sign GF, which are continuous-time limits of gradient descent, Muon, and Adam, respectively. Prior studies (Nichani et al., 2025) have shown that when the number of subjects exceeds the number of relations, GF learns relation-dependent information before subject-dependent information, producing a feature-separation phase during training. We characterize this separation with the learning times when the subject- and relation-dependent components of the prediction reach a target accuracy. With $S$ subjects and $R$ relations, GF has a learning-time ratio of $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{S/R})$, whereas Spectral GF reduces this ratio to $\widetildeΘ(1)$. In addition, for fixed $S$ and $R$, the subject- and relation-dependent errors decay as $1/(T\log T)$ in training time $T$ under GF, but as $\exp(-\mathrm{poly}(T))$ under spectral GF. Finally, we show that GF and spectral GF are equivariant under orthogonal transformations of the token embeddings, whereas Sign GF is not: Different orthonormal embeddings can potentially produce no feature separation, a large feature-separation phase, or even a reversed learning order. These results provide a mechanistic view of how spectral orthogonalization can fundamentally reshape feature-learning dynamics.

</details>


### [320] [Gated Slot Attention-2: Two-Sided Associative Memory Correction in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02816)
*Ruijie Li,Shengnan Ding,Weimin Zhang,Derick Tang,Zhanpeng Zeng,Qinsong Zeng,Ming Chen,Jiaxi Hu,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GSA2，将键侧修正的Gated Oja Rule-2与值侧修正的Gated Delta Rule-2通过共享latent slots结合，并给出硬件高效的分块并行算法，在保持线性时间与常数内存解码的同时持续优于强线性注意力基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力模型的固定大小循环记忆难以有效管理。已有两类改进方向：用于精确修正键关联值的delta-rule变体，以及如Gated Slot Attention的两阶段slot架构。作者观察到这两者互补：delta rule提供有效的记忆修正，两阶段结构天然支持在关联的两侧进行操作，但此前工作未将其结合。

Method: 1) 提出新的Gated Oja Rule用于键侧修正，并扩展为带解耦擦除与写入控制的Gated Oja Rule-2；2) 提出Gated Slot Attention-2（GSA2），通过共享latent slots将键侧修正的Gated Oja Rule-2与值侧修正的Gated Delta Rule-2相结合；3) 推导了硬件高效的分块（chunkwise）算法以支持并行训练。

Result: 实验表明，GSA2在多个基准上一致优于强线性注意力基线，同时保持线性时间序列建模与常数内存的循环解码。

Conclusion: 将键侧与值侧修正机制通过共享latent slots统一起来是有效的，GSA2在效率与性能上均取得提升，为线性注意力记忆管理提供了新的结合范式。

Abstract: Linear attention models have emerged as efficient alternatives to standard attention, but effectively managing their fixed-size recurrent memory remains challenging. To improve memory, recent work has explored two distinct directions: delta-rule variants for precise correction of values associated with keys, and slot-based architectures such as Gated Slot Attention for modeling key and value memories in two stages. We observe that these directions are complementary--the delta rule provides effective memory correction, while the two-stage structure provides a natural way to operate on both sides of an association. Building on this insight, we introduce a new Gated Oja Rule for key-side correction and extend it with decoupled erase and write control to obtain Gated Oja Rule-2. We then introduce Gated Slot Attention-2 (GSA2), which combines Gated Oja Rule-2 for key-side correction with Gated Delta Rule-2 for value-side correction through shared latent slots. We further derive a hardware-efficient chunkwise algorithm for parallel training. Experiments demonstrate that GSA2 consistently improves over strong linear-attention baselines across benchmarks while retaining linear-time sequence modeling and constant-memory recurrent decoding.

</details>


### [321] [Adaptive Spectral-Koopman Dynamics Modeling for Temporal Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02822)
*Tengxue Zhang,Yu Ke,Yang Shu,Chenchen Sun,Yisheng An,Chenjuan Guo,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaSpecK，一个结合谱-Koopman动力学建模与自适应上下文提取的时序域泛化框架，通过潜空间谱正则滤波去噪并利用Koopman算子线性化建模系统动态，同时设计上下文感知的异质模式提取机制，在八个分类与回归基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实流式数据存在随时间变化的分布偏移，现有时序域泛化方法存在两类问题：一是在数据空间容易过拟合域特定的噪声，二是在参数空间变得过于复杂且可解释性差。作者希望弥合这两方面差距。

Method: 提出AdaSpecK框架，包含两部分：（1）谱正则化Koopman动力学建模，在潜空间进行谱感知滤波，提取去噪的低频轨迹，并学习Koopman算子在线性化空间建模系统动态，以缓解不规则采样域上的噪声拟合；（2）上下文感知的异质模式提取机制，使用目标条件注意力模块关注不同历史窗口，生成动态的、目标特定的历史摘要，并通过学习路由器从当前演化模式构建环境签名，自适应感知对预测最有信息量的历史上下文。

Result: 在八个多样的分类与回归基准上的大量实验表明，AdaSpecK取得了最先进的性能。代码与数据集已公开。

Conclusion: AdaSpecK通过谱-Koopman建模与自适应上下文提取，有效解决时序域泛化中的噪声过拟合与模型复杂性问题，实现最优性能，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Temporal Domain Generalization (TDG) has emerged to address real-world streaming data with distribution shifts over time. However, existing methods are either prone to overfitting to domain-specific noise in the data space or become overly complex and less interpretable in the parameter space. To bridge these gaps, we propose \textbf{AdaSpecK}, a spectral-Koopman framework with adaptive context extraction for TDG. To mitigate noise fitting to irregularly sampled domains, we introduce spectral-regularized Koopman dynamics modeling, which applies spectral-aware filtering in the latent space to extract denoised low-frequency trajectories and learn a Koopman operator to model the system dynamics in a linearized space. To model complex historical environments under non-stationarity, we design a context-informed heterogeneous pattern extraction mechanism. Specifically, we employ a target-conditioned attention module to attend to distinct past windows, producing a dynamic, target-specific historical summary. By constructing an environmental signature from the current evolutionary pattern, our model adaptively perceives which aspects of the past context are most informative for future prediction via a learned router. Extensive experiments on eight diverse classification and regression benchmarks demonstrate that AdaSpecK achieves state-of-the-art performance. The code and datasets are available at \href{}{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Ada-Spec-K}.

</details>


### [322] [All Work And No Play Makes Jack a Dull Boy: Understanding and Preventing Catastrophic Strategy Collapse in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02835)
*Qiyuan Huang,Tianshi Xu,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究RLVR训练中GRPO类算法的后期崩溃现象，提出策略容量收缩假说，推导MEI预警指标，并提出Mesh Learning方法防止崩溃，在多基准和多模型上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO类算法在大语言模型RLVR后训练中会出现严重的后期崩溃。作者想揭示其本质：这不是良性的策略剪枝，而是有效策略容量的有害收缩，使不同推理策略逐渐不可及，从而需要机制性解释与解决方案。

Method: 通过轨迹级策略更新交互定义策略；建立结合优化动力学与信息论的统一理论框架；证明主流RLVR目标会逐步将概率质量集中到单一策略，而维持任务准确率需要最低策略容量；据此推导Mirrored Entanglement Index（MEI）作为轻量在线预警信号；提出Mesh Learning，暴露多种推理策略并防止单一策略主导优化。

Result: 在AIME26、AIME25、MATH-500、GPQA、LiveCodeBench上，Mesh Learning在Qwen和Phi模型族中一致优于强基线，增益分别最高达13.4个百分点和11.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 策略保持是稳定RLVR的关键原则；论文从理论与实证两方面说明策略容量收缩导致崩溃，并用MEI预警和Mesh Learning缓解该问题。

Abstract: During post-training of large language models (LLMs) with Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), GRPO-style algorithms can exhibit severe late-stage collapse. Prompt-based probing reveals that this is not benign strategic pruning, but a harmful contraction of effective strategy capacity that makes distinct reasoning strategies increasingly inaccessible. To characterize this phenomenon, we define strategies through trajectory-level policy-update interactions and develop a unified theoretical framework combining optimization dynamics and information theory. We prove that major RLVR objectives progressively concentrate probability mass onto a single strategy, while sustaining nontrivial task accuracy requires a minimum strategy capacity. The conflict between these two results provides a mechanistic explanation for catastrophic collapse. We further derive the {Mirrored Entanglement Index (MEI)} as a lightweight online warning signal. To prevent collapse, we propose \textbf{Mesh Learning}, which exposes multiple reasoning strategies and prevents any single strategy from dominating optimization. Across AIME26, AIME25, MATH-500, GPQA, and LiveCodeBench, Mesh Learning consistently outperforms strong baselines across Qwen and Phi model families, with gains of up to 13.4 pp and 11.5 pp, respectively. These results establish strategy preservation as a key principle for stable RLVR. Code is available at https://github.com/Ayanami-0123/Open-Mesh-Learning.

</details>


### [323] [To Explore The Strange New World Beyond Data Distribution: System Behavior, Causality Tax, and Non-causal Base Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02839)
*Xianzhi Zeng,Jiangneng Li,Gao Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出SBD框架，将系统行为$S$作为贝叶斯第一性原理特征纳入ELBO，揭示"因果税"现象，并构造非因果变分族Green Shell(GSH)降低该税。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型建模中，因果性被视作默认基础，但近期研究表明其与模型实际表现存在持续的不匹配和矛盾，这些问题主要源于系统行为$S$（数据空间之外的额外主导因素、耦合效应）而非数据分布本身。因此作者质疑因果性对LM是否必要且最优。

Method: 提出SBD框架，把系统行为$S$作为证据下界(ELBO)中不可约的组成部分；通过隐式测量、理论边界引导的对照实验以及神经正切核(NTK)评估来验证SBD所预测的$S$的影响；构造Green Shell(GSH)——一种非因果变分族，用分而治之的划分替代$S$成分的序列依赖链。

Result: 理论上SBD揭示反直觉的"因果税(Causality Tax)"现象：由于忽略了$S$，因果性表现为带额外结构误差的次优近似。NTK谱在惰性训练机制下证实GSH的误差上界始终显著紧于因果性，信噪比提升7dB以上；在惰性训练较后阶段，GSH进一步带来更优泛化能力（多尺度拟合能力提升最高达20%）。

Conclusion: SBD将系统行为确立为除因果性与分布拟合之外的补充性理论抽象，为设计与优化LM基础模型开辟了新研究方向。

Abstract: We show that the causality of language models (LMs) may not be necessary nor optimal. This is the case when system behavior (denoted as $S$) is incorporated as a first-principle Bayesian feature. Here, $S$ refers to extra dominant factors beyond the data space, and they involve coupled effects. Despite being the de facto foundation of modern architecture, recent studies indicate persistent mismatches and contradictions with causality. These issues largely stem from system behavior rather than the data distribution. We therefore propose the SBD framework, which incorporates $S$ as an irreducible component of the evidence lower bound (ELBO). SBD theoretically reveals a counter-intuitive Causality Tax phenomenon, where causality emerges as a suboptimal approximation with an additional structural error, due to the obliviousness to $S$. To address the challenge of latent variable analysis, we validate the SBD-predicted impact of $S$ via implicit measurements, theoretical-bound-guided controls, and Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) evaluations. In particular, we construct Green Shell (GSH) to show the possibility of reducing Causality Tax. GSH is a non-causal variational family, and it replaces the sequential dependency chain of $S$ components with a divide-and-conquer partition. NTK spectra in the lazy-training regime confirm that GSH always achieves significantly tighter error bounds than causality, with $7dB+$ improvement in signal-to-noise ratio. In the relatively later stage of lazy-training, GSH further leads to superior generalization (up to $20\%$ richer multi-scale fitting capabilities). Taken together, SBD establishes system behavior as a complementary theoretical abstraction besides causality and distribution fitting, opening new research avenues such as designing and optimizing LM base models.

</details>


### [324] [Understanding Enrichment in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02846)
*Jinwoo Kim,Shraddha Barke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从数学上揭示了RLVR中对"增强rollout"不做或截断重要性权重修正等价于隐式重加权奖励，将梯度误差分解为尺度、旋转和方差三部分，并提出基于顺序蒙特卡洛(SMC)的权重修正机制把修正方差的指数级累积降为加性累积，最后用Qwen3-1.7B在稀疏数据上的微调验证了增强与修正的作用机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在奖励稀疏时，RLVR常借助提示或中间指导来生成更多成功rollout，但这种增强会使策略梯度更新产生偏差，需要通过重要性权重修正。现有方法要么忽略修正，要么为避免高方差而截断权重，因此人们并不清楚在RLVR中对增强rollout进行修正究竟带来什么收益与损失。作者希望一般性地理解增强与修正如何影响训练，而非断言某种模式优于未增强的RL。

Method: 1) 数学证明：忽略或截断修正会隐式地重新加权所定义的奖励，并将由此产生的梯度误差分解为尺度(scale)、旋转(rotation)与方差(variance)三类，用以解释其不同学习效应。2) 提出一种新的顺序蒙特卡洛(SMC)权重修正机制，在稳定性与粒子排序假设下，将标准修正方差随样本长度的指数级复合降为加性累积。

Result: 通过将增强分析应用于Qwen3-1.7B在OpenMathReasoning稀疏片段上的微调结果进行解释，建立了增强(无论是否修正)如何帮助避免稀疏域坍塌的具体机制。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献在于一般性地理解增强与修正如何影响训练，而非宣称修正或未修正的某一种操作模式优于未增强的强化学习。

Abstract: When rewards are sparse, reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) often uses hints or intermediate guidance to generate more successful rollouts. This enrichment biases policy-gradient updates unless corrected via importance weights, but existing methods omit correction or truncate importance weights in order to avoid the high variance of correction. It thus remains unclear what exactly is gained or lost in RLVR by correcting enriched rollouts. We show, mathematically, that omitted or truncated correction implicitly reweights the defined reward, and we decompose the resulting gradient error into scale, rotation, and variance to explain their distinct effects on learning. To make correction practical, we develop a novel sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) weight correction mechanism that, under stability and particle-order assumptions, tempers the exponential compounding of standard correction variance over the length of a sample to an additive accumulation. We then apply our analysis of enrichment to interpreting the results of a fine-tune of Qwen3-1.7B on a sparse band of OpenMathReasoning, establishing a concrete mechanism of how enrichment, both corrected and uncorrected, helps avoid collapse in sparse domains. Our main contribution is to understand, in general, how enrichment and correction can affect training, as opposed to claiming that either mode of operation is superior to unenriched RL.

</details>


### [325] [Counterfactual Action Evaluation, Observation Bottlenecks, and Representation Geometry in Joint-Embedding Predictive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02860)
*Arjun Subramanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 低潜空间预测误差不能证明世界模型能区分自身动作的后果。本文提出通过追踪同一干预在模拟器状态、栅格观测、目标嵌入与预测器输出中的传递来审计世界模型，揭示多个独立瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低潜空间预测误差常被当作世界模型学好的证据，但这一指标无法保证模型真正区分不同动作导致的物理后果。作者希望区分"预测误差低"与"因果关系被正确建模"之间的差距，并定位失败发生在哪个环节。

Method: 提出一套评测协议，在受控可形变物理测试环境中对同一干预做精确的模拟器状态分叉，逐层追踪其在模拟器状态、栅格观测、目标嵌入和预测器输出中的响应；并对比等向性状态扰动与目标反事实嵌入位移对预测器的影响，比较 MSE-only 与 VICReg 目标编码器，以及在满分辨率栅格与机械状态下解码刚度与特权材料参数。

Result: 改变指令虽会改变粒子运动，但 41.5% 的单步栅格对完全相同。在 579 个高可见度反事实中，预测器对目标的中位响应仅为 0.0051 和 0.0217（两个随机种子），方差归一化后降至 0.0027 和 0.0116。等向性状态扰动在相同可见对上引发的预测器变化比目标反事实大 190x 和 53x，说明是动作路径未被充分使用，而非预测器死亡或全局收缩。MSE-only 训练在匹配种子上 10 步潜误差低 8.36x，但处于谱高度集中的空间；一个 VICReg 目标编码器同样强集中，因此误差或秩都无法单独证明物理状态。刚度即使从满分辨率栅格和机械状态仍接近随机水平，而特权材料参数可完美解码。

Conclusion: 结果支持将物理效应、观测可见性、表示几何与动作依赖性分开审计，而非依赖单一的低潜预测误差或表示秩来判断世界模型质量；刚度识别弱源于该激励下可辨识性不足，而非编码器丢弃信息。

Abstract: Low latent prediction error does not establish that a world model distinguishes the consequences of its actions. We introduce an evaluation protocol that traces the same intervention through simulator state, raster observations, target embeddings, and predictor outputs. Exact simulator-state forks in a controlled deformable-physics testbed reveal distinct bottlenecks. Changed commands alter particle motion, yet 41.5% of one-step raster pairs are identical. Observation loss is not the whole explanation: among 579 high-visibility counterfactuals, median predictor-to-target response is 0.0051 and 0.0217 across two seeds, falling to 0.0027 and 0.0116 after variance normalization. An isotropic state perturbation matched to the target counterfactual embedding shift produces 190x and 53x larger predictor changes on the same visible pairs, isolating action-path under-use rather than a dead or globally shrunk predictor. MSE-only training gives 8.36x lower 10-step latent error in matched seeds, but in spectrally concentrated spaces; one VICReg target encoder is also strongly concentrated, so neither error nor rank alone certifies physical state. Finally, stiffness remains near chance even from full-resolution rasters and mechanical state while privileged material parameters decode perfectly, indicating weak identifiability under this excitation rather than encoder discard. These results motivate auditing physical effect, observation visibility, representation geometry, and action dependence separately.

</details>


### [326] [NeuroLens: Learning Latent Embeddings of Neural Semantics from Chronic Recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02864)
*Hanrui Lyu,Baiyuan Chen,Tianshu Tan,Matthew R. Whiteway,Maxwell D. Melin,Ji Xia,Linyang He,Bradly C. Stadie,Anne Churchland,Liam Paninski,Yizi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 NeuroLens，一种基于 JEPA 的自监督模型，从慢性神经记录中学习去噪的语义隐表征，可区分表征可塑性与记录不稳定，提升解码稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究神经活动如何表征高阶认知及其随时间演化是神经科学核心问题，但现有分析工具难以在慢性记录中区分表征可塑性与记录不稳定。

Method: 基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的自监督模型，包含自适应编码器（将变化的神经群体映射到共同隐空间）与时间预测器（学习可预测未来隐状态的结构），在隐空间进行预测。

Result: 在小鼠与人类慢性皮层内数据上，所学表征提升了对决策与语义任务变量的解码；多天预训练可泛化到未来session、快速少样本适应未见神经群体，且解码稳定性优于现有基线。

Conclusion: NeuroLens 为研究学习过程及长时间尺度下神经表征如何变化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Understanding how neural activity represents higher-order cognition and how these representations evolve over time has long been a central pursuit in neuroscience. However, current analytical tools cannot easily distinguish representational plasticity from recording instability in chronic neural recordings. Here, we introduce NeuroLens (Latent Embeddings of Neural Semantics), a self-supervised model based on the Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) framework that learns denoised, semantically informative latents from chronic neural recordings. An adaptive encoder maps changing neural populations into a common latent space, while a temporal predictor learns structure that supports prediction of future latent states. By predicting in latent space, NeuroLens captures temporally predictable structure and reduces sensitivity to transient, recording-specific variability. Across chronic intracortical data in mice and humans, the learned representations improve decoding of decision-making and semantic task variables. Multi-day pretraining enables generalization to future sessions, rapid few-shot adaptation to unseen neural populations, and more stable decoding over time than state-of-the-art baselines. Together, these results establish NeuroLens as a new paradigm for studying how neural representations change during learning and over long timescales.

</details>


### [327] [Adaptive Second-Order Solvers for Fast Stochastic Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03034)
*Ella Kemperman,Luca Ambrogioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将比例-积分(PI)步长控制引入扩散模型采样，提出扩散噪声归一化误差估计器，实现更平滑的自适应步长；并发现单样本轨迹具有共性结构可聚合为固定调度。在图像与语言数据集上，固定调度与PI自适应求解器在匹配NFE下取得更优或相当的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的数值求解器需时间离散化，直接影响采样成本与质量的权衡。然而逆向过程的计算难度沿采样轨迹及不同数据分布而变化，使离散化选择至关重要；现有自适应方法仅响应当前误差，步长调整不够平滑。

Method: 将比例-积分(PI)步长控制迁移到扩散采样，配合自研的扩散噪声归一化误差估计器；PI求解器除当前误差外还引入历史(先前)误差以实现更平滑的步长自适应。进一步观察到单样本轨迹存在共享结构，可聚合为固定调度，保留自适应采样的多数收益。与常用随机求解器及调度在自然图像和语言数据集上进行对比评估。

Result: 图像上：固定离散化配合随机Heun采样器在样本质量上优于常用EDM调度，配合EDM-churn采样器在低NFE时亦优；PI自适应求解器FID优于多数随机与自适应基线，但低NFE下未超过EDM-churn。语言扩散上：低至中等NFE时困惑度优于EDM与熵调度，代价是token熵更低。自适应收益依赖问题：1D玩具示例中收益显著，图像与语言数据中仅边际，平均调度有时甚至优于PI自适应求解器。

Conclusion: PI步长控制为扩散采样提供更平滑的自适应离散化，且单样本自适应轨迹可聚合为高效固定调度；在图像与语言任务上具竞争力，但逐样本自适应的实际收益强依赖问题类型，对高维真实数据收益有限。

Abstract: Diffusion models rely on numerical solvers requiring time-discretization, which has a large influence on the tradeoff between sampling cost and quality. However, the computational difficulty of the reverse process varies along the sampling trajectory and across data distributions, making the choice of discretization important. We adapt proportional-integral (PI) step-size control to diffusion, using our diffusion noise-normalised error estimator. Unlike existing adaptive methods in diffusion that respond only to the current error, the PI solver also incorporates the previous error, yielding smoother step adaptation. We further show that these per-sample trajectories exhibit shared structure and can be aggregated into a fixed schedule that retains much of the benefit of adaptive sampling. We evaluate both approaches on natural-image and language datasets, in terms of quality, measured by FID at a matched number of neural network evaluations (NFE), comparing them with widely used stochastic solvers and schedules. For images, our fixed discretization outperforms the commonly used EDM schedule in terms of sample quality when used with the stochastic Heun sampler, and with the EDM-churn sampler at low NFE. Additionally, our PI adaptive solver obtains better FID than most stochastic and adaptive baselines, although it does not beat the EDM-churn sampler at low NFE. Moreover, we find our solver outperforms both the EDM and the entropy schedule on language diffusion at low-to-medium NFE in terms of perplexity, with the drawback of lower token entropy. Lastly, we find that the benefit of per-sample adaptivity is problem-dependent. It is highly beneficial in 1D toy examples, while only marginal for image and language data, where the average schedule sometimes even outperforms the PI-adaptive solver. Code is available at https://github.com/ellakemperman/adaptive-second-order-diffusion-solvers

</details>


### [328] [On Unlearning for Time-series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02865)
*Zeyu Shi,Yanhui Luo,Ziming Hong,Chongyang Gao,Kezhen Chen,Shanshan Ye,Lixu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RDTU，一个用于时间序列机器遗忘的残差扩散框架。它先用保留集神经正切核预测器得到与删除兼容的基预测，再量化受影响窗口的全局与局部结构支持，并用扩散模型生成残差校正来估计反事实预测，形成伪标签场以指导轻量级模型更新。实验表明 RDTU 的遗忘模型最接近完全重训练结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测常用于敏感领域，模型在纵向用户或实体记录上训练，这些记录可能因敏感、专有信息或传感器故障而被要求删除。为避免昂贵重训练，机器遗忘被广泛研究。但现有遗忘方法在时间序列预测中面临独特挑战：基于梯度的遗忘不稳定，因为一个被删除的观测会参与多个因果关联的预测窗口，导致参数更新传播超出请求区间并损害保留数据的预测效用；标签引导更新虽更可控，但连续且依赖上下文的预测缺乏合适的替换目标，且遗忘时无法获得精确重训练输出；此外，被删除时间模式在剩余数据中的支持高度不均匀，部分受影响窗口仍有结构相似对应，另一些则变得欠代表或孤立。

Method: RDTU 包含三个关键步骤：第一，使用保留集神经正切核（NTK）预测器获得一个与删除兼容的基预测；第二，通过保留参考数据的体积贡献量化每个受影响窗口的全局和局部结构支持；第三，用扩散模型生成残差校正以估计反事实预测，从而产生伪标签场来指导轻量级模型更新。

Result: 实验表明，RDTU 持续产生与精确重训练最接近的遗忘模型。

Conclusion: RDTU 通过残差扩散框架有效应对时间序列机器遗忘中的梯度传播不稳定、替换目标缺失和结构支持非均匀等挑战，在无需昂贵重训练的情况下实现接近完全重训练的遗忘效果。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting is widely used in sensitive domains. Models in these settings are often trained on longitudinal user- or entity-level records, which may later require removal because they contain sensitive or proprietary information or have been corrupted by sensor failures. To address such deletion requests without costly retraining, machine unlearning has been widely studied as a practical mechanism for privacy protection and data governance. However, the application of machine unlearning to time series prediction has not yet been well realized; this is mainly due to the following unique challenges: Gradient-based unlearning can be unstable because a deleted observation participates in multiple causally connected forecasting windows, causing parameter updates to propagate beyond the requested interval and degrade retained forecasting utility. Label-guided updating offers a more controlled alternative, but continuous and context-dependent forecasts lack a suitable replacement target, while the exact-retrained output is unavailable during unlearning. Moreover, the remaining support for a deleted temporal pattern is highly non-uniform. Some affected windows retain structurally similar counterparts in the retained data, whereas others become underrepresented or isolated. We present RDTU, a Residual Diffusion framework for time-series unlearning. RDTU first uses a retained-set neural tangent kernel predictor to obtain a deletion-compatible base forecast. Then it quantifies the global and local structural support of each affected window using the volume contribution of the retained-reference data. Then a diffusion model generates a residual correction that estimates the counterfactual forecast, yielding a pseudo-label field that guides a lightweight model update. Experiments show that RDTU consistently produces unlearned models that most closely match exact retraining.

</details>


### [329] [WebFovea: When the Model Is Right but the Click Is Wrong -- Reliable Round Trips for Vision-Based Web Agents on Live Websites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03036)
*Jiangang Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 WebFovea——一个在 WebRetriever 2026 挑战赛中获第二名（57.0/100）的视觉网页智能体，其核心贡献是通过加固模型与网页之间的"harness"层，将成绩从 31.0 提升至 57.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实网站上，许多失败并非源于模型推理能力不足，而是发生在模型与浏览器之间的工程环节（代码 harness）。作者希望证明：即使使用同一个多模态大模型，仅通过改进 harness 也能显著提升智能体表现。

Method: WebFovea 将智能体循环拆解为四个必须成功的阶段：1）模型回复被正确解析为预期动作；2）动作在页面上真正生效；3）结果被准确回传；4）模型被展示所需信息。针对每个阶段进行加固，并在循环外围加入保持规则合规与预算的护栏。

Result: 在四次官方提交中使用相同模型的情况下，隐藏集分数从 31.0 上升至 57.0，说明提升主要来自 harness 改进而非模型更换。观察到的问题包括：坐标空间不匹配导致点击落在目标坐标的 3/4 处；原生下拉框、iframe 内及文本框中的操作静默失败；自生成聊天模板 token 污染了 4.9% 的任务回合。

Conclusion: 四阶段分析框架不依赖于具体模型（尽管部分修复依赖）。论文描述了设计、各组件证据（含负面结果）、失败分析、局限性，并提出包括将不同步骤路由到不同模型在内的路线图。最终成绩包含了实时网站上的运行间方差。

Abstract: We present WebFovea, a vision-based web agent that placed 2nd in the WebRetriever Challenge 2026 with a final score of 57.0 out of 100. The challenge evaluates agents end to end on Protocol III of the WebRetriever benchmark (arXiv:2607.06118): starting from an entry URL on a live website, the agent must operate the site's own interface and return a verifiable answer. A capable multimodal large language model (LLM) is necessary for this, but not sufficient. The model's decisions reach the browser through the harness, the code between the model and the page. At every step, four things must go right: the model's reply must be parsed into the intended action, the action must take effect on the page, the result must be reported back accurately, and the model must be shown the information it needs. On real websites, many of the failures we observed occurred at one of these four stages rather than in the model's reasoning. A coordinate-space mismatch placed every click at 3/4 of its intended coordinates; actions on native dropdowns, inside iframes, and in text boxes failed silently; and self-generated chat-template tokens contaminated 4.9% of task episodes. WebFovea hardens each stage and surrounds the loop with guardrails that keep the agent within the rules and its budget. The four-stage view does not depend on the model, although some individual fixes do. Because we used the same model in all four submissions, the rise of our official hidden-set score from 31.0 to 57.0 reflects changes to the harness, up to run-to-run variance on live sites. We describe the design, the evidence for each component (including negative results), a failure analysis, the limitations, and a roadmap that includes routing different steps to different models.

</details>


### [330] [DIVINE: Simple Cross-Market Stock Pretraining via Diverse Indicator Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02866)
*Kuan-Yu Chen,Shu-Cheng Zheng,Yu-Chen Den,Wei-Cheng Liao,Tien-Hao Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DIVINE 跨市场预训练框架，通过从原始 OHLCV 历史重建技术指标来学习可迁移的金融表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融时序预训练目标（掩码观测、对比关系、未来结果）难以同时避免未来监督的不确定性并保持与收益预测的对齐。技术指标由已观测的量价历史计算，定义一致且与收益预测相关，因此可作为理想监督信号。

Method: DIVINE 从原始 OHLCV 历史重建技术指标：基于 16 个标准指标构建 77 个重建目标，在六个股票市场数据集上联合预训练，下游仅迁移学到的编码器用于股票排序。

Result: 在六个市场上，仅用 0.05M 参数的轻量编码器即取得最强平均组合表现，超越预训练基线，并匹配或超过规模更大的金融基础模型，同时具备稳健性与数据高效性。

Conclusion: 指标多样性与市场多样性在迁移中提供互补增益，说明监督设计与跨市场多样性而非模型规模，才是强可迁移金融表征的关键驱动因素。

Abstract: Financial time-series pretraining typically learns from masked observations, contrastive relations, or future outcomes---yet existing objectives struggle to simultaneously avoid future-supervision uncertainty and maintain return-prediction alignment. We propose DIVINE (DIVerse INdicator rEconstruction), a simple cross-market pretraining framework that reconstructs technical indicators from raw OHLCV history. Computed from observed price-volume history, technical indicators provide consistently defined supervision across markets while summarizing diverse market dynamics with established relevance to return prediction. Pretrained jointly on six-equity market datasets, DIVINE reconstructs 77 targets derived from 16 standard indicators and transfers only the learned encoder to downstream stock ranking. Across all six markets, DIVINE achieves the strongest average portfolio performance with a lightweight 0.05M-parameter encoder, outperforming pretraining baselines and matching or exceeding substantially larger financial foundation models, while remaining robust and data-efficient. Systematic analyses show that indicator diversity and market diversity provide complementary gains in transfer. Together, these results suggest that supervision design and cross-market diversity---rather than model scale---are the key drivers of strong, transferable financial representations.

</details>


### [331] [Distributionally Robust Survival Models under Subpopulation Shift and Outlier Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02868)
*Seonghwi Kim,Sung Ho Jo,Minwoo Chae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种分布鲁棒生存分析框架，联合应对潜在子群偏移与离群点污染，在保持整体性能的同时显著提升最差子群表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存模型在分布偏移下鲁棒性不足：异质人群中平均表现好的模型可能在特定子群上表现差，且训练数据受离群点污染时问题更严重。现有方法未能同时处理潜在子群偏移与离群点污染，且生存损失（如Cox负偏对数似然）具有不可分解性和风险集结构，难以直接套用常规鲁棒方法。

Method: 提出分布鲁棒框架，结合外层最小化与内层最大化：外层通过降低污染样本影响来选取精炼的名义分布，内层聚焦最具挑战性的子群。该形式化直接适配不可分解的生存损失，并保留样本间交互（包括Cox负偏对数似然的风险集结构）。开发基于交替梯度的算法，其外层更新由内层最大化的KKT条件推导得出。

Result: 在模拟数据和两个生存分析基准上的实验表明：当子群偏移与离群点污染同时发生时，方法仍保持鲁棒；在污染环境下稳定训练，并在线性与非线性生存模型上大幅提升最差子群性能，同时保持有竞争力、有时更优的整体性能。

Conclusion: 所提分布鲁棒框架能有效联合应对子群偏移与离群点污染，兼顾最差子群鲁棒性与整体性能，适用于不可分解的生存损失。

Abstract: Learning robust survival models under distribution shift is an important but challenging problem in many applications. In heterogeneous populations, a model that performs well on average may still perform poorly on certain subpopulations, and this issue becomes even more severe when the training data are contaminated by outliers. In this paper, we propose a novel distributionally robust framework for survival analysis that jointly addresses latent subpopulation shift and outlier contamination. The proposed method combines an outer minimization that selects a refined nominal distribution by reducing the influence of contaminated samples and an inner maximization that focuses on the most challenging subpopulation. This formulation directly accommodates non-decomposable survival losses while preserving interactions across samples, including the risk-set structure of the Cox negative partial log-likelihood. We develop an alternating gradient-based algorithm with outer updates derived from the KKT conditions of the inner maximization. Experiments on simulated data and two survival benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method remains robust when subpopulation shift and outlier contamination occur simultaneously. It stabilizes training in contaminated settings and substantially improves worst-group performance across both linear and nonlinear survival models, while maintaining competitive and sometimes superior overall performance.

</details>


### [332] [What Should World Models Forget? Stratified Retention for Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03713)
*Nishit Anand,Ramani Duraiswami,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视持续学习中对"遗忘"的判定标准，指出世界模型的预测目标（环境）是非平稳的，因此需要按不变性时间尺度分层保留知识，并提出"差分保留"评测方法来区分正确的知识更新与灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习通常把在旧数据上的性能下降视为失败，这一惯例源自预测目标平稳的设定——即正确标签永远正确。但世界模型并不满足该条件：其预测目标是不断变化的环境，曾经准确的知识可能随后变为错误，丢弃它是必需行为而非缺陷。非平稳真值在概念漂移和语言模型时序事实性研究中已有讨论，但尚未在世界模型中被形式化，而世界模型的独特之处在于它还编码了永远不该被修改的知识。

Method: 作者主张持续世界模型需要"按不变性时间尺度分层的保留"：将诸如物理规律、客体永久性等永远不应修改的不变量（invariants），与一旦环境变化就应立即修改的实例级事实区分开来。并据此提出"差分保留"（differential retention）评测方法，在适应流上联合报告不变量回归测试与修改延迟（revision latency），且不做聚合。

Result: 标准的遗忘度量无法区分"已正确更新过时知识"的世界模型与"遭受灾难性遗忘"的世界模型，因而会把冻结模型（frozen model）排在最前；而现有的物理推理基准也只评估冻结的检查点。

Conclusion: 世界模型的持续学习需要重新定义遗忘与保留：应以不变性时间尺度分层评估，并采用不聚合的差分保留指标（同时报告不变量回归与修改延迟），以避免误将正确的知识修订判定为遗忘。

Abstract: Continual learning treats degradation on previously seen data as evidence of failure, a convention inherited from settings with a stationary prediction target, where a correct label remains correct indefinitely. World models do not satisfy this condition. Their prediction target is the environment, which changes, so knowledge that was accurate when acquired may later become false, and discarding it is required behavior rather than a defect. Non-stationary ground truth is well studied in the concept drift literature and in the temporal factuality of language models, but has not been formulated for world models, which are distinctive in that they also encode knowledge that must never be revised. We argue that continual world models require retention stratified by invariance timescale, separating invariants such as physics and object permanence, which must never be revised, from instance-level facts that should be revised as soon as the environment changes. Standard forgetting metrics cannot distinguish a world model that has correctly revised outdated knowledge from one that has suffered catastrophic forgetting, and consequently rank a frozen model highest, while existing physical-reasoning benchmarks evaluate only frozen checkpoints. We propose differential retention, which reports invariant regression testing across the adaptation stream jointly with revision latency, without aggregation.

</details>


### [333] [Peer Effects in Signed Networks: Separating Influence Through Positive and Negative Ties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02872)
*Xiaojing Du,Jiuyong Li,Lin Liu,Debo Cheng,Jixue Liu,Thuc Duy Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出区分正负关系的网络干预效应框架与SiDE估计器，识别并估计正向、负向及符号重组效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估网络干预需理解处理如何经社会关系影响个体；若仅统计受处理邻居而不区分支持性与对抗性关系，会掩盖方向相反的影响。

Method: 定义正向关系、负向关系、二者交互以及固定总处理量下在两类关系间重新分配的符号组成效应，并给出识别公式；在符号盲分配下展示忽略符号如何混合两类关系效应；提出SiDE（符号暴露双稳健估计器），结合符号特定结果模型与个体处理分配诱导的暴露概率，建立得分双稳健性并评估考虑邻域重叠的近似区间。

Result: 在六个真实符号网络上的半合成实验显示效应估计准确，并检验了区间覆盖的局限；对已发表学校实验数据的探索性再分析得到腕带佩戴经共度时间关系的同伴效应为正，但多重比较调整后四种效应的区间均包含零。

Conclusion: 该框架可通过显示两类关系的影响何时相互增强或抵消，为网络干预设计提供依据。

Abstract: Evaluating network interventions requires understanding how treatment affects people through their social relationships. Counting treated neighbors without distinguishing supportive and antagonistic ties can conceal opposing influences. We define effects through positive and negative ties, their interaction, and a sign-composition effect of reallocating treatment between the two types at a fixed total, and give their identification formulas. Under sign-blind assignment, we show how ignoring signs mixes the effects of the two tie types. We propose SiDE (Signed-exposure Doubly robust Estimator), which combines sign-specific outcome models with exposure probabilities induced by individual treatment assignment. We establish double robustness of its score and assess approximate intervals that account for overlapping neighborhoods. Semi-synthetic experiments on six real signed networks demonstrate accurate effect estimation and examine the limits of interval coverage. An exploratory reanalysis of published school-experiment data yields a positive estimate of the peer effect through spend-time ties on wristband wearing, but the intervals for all four effects include zero after adjustment for multiple comparisons. This framework can inform network intervention design by showing when influences through the two tie types reinforce or offset one another.

</details>


### [334] [Toward Omni Multimodal Graph Foundation Model: A Topology-Driven Binding Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02881)
*Xunkai Li,Chenxi Wan,Yinlin Zhu,Wang Luo,Hongchao Qin,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GraphBind，一种以图拓扑为驱动的多模态图基础模型方法，通过拓扑绑定多模态信息构建统一共享表示空间，在判别与生成任务上均取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实多模态属性图常存在节点属性不完整，限制了预训练语料的规模与多样性；同时现有MGFM多将拓扑仅作为结构上下文，忽视了其在引导多模态绑定和塑造统一表示空间中的作用。

Method: GraphBind基于图拓扑的稳定性，利用拓扑将自身语义与可靠邻域语义组织进一个融合结构与语义的全局共享空间，并通过轻量级接口将该空间适配到判别式和生成式任务。

Result: 与11个代表性基线对比，GraphBind在判别式和生成式任务上均达到领先性能，相对最强基线提升最高达28.1%。

Conclusion: 图拓扑可作为多模态绑定的结构参考与互补语义来源，为构建统一多模态图表示空间提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Multimodal graph foundation models (MGFMs) seek to learn generalizable representations from large-scale graphs with heterogeneous node modalities. However, real-world Multimodal-Attributed Graphs (MAGs) often contain incomplete node attributes, limiting the scale and diversity of available pretraining corpora. Besides, existing MGFMs primarily incorporate graph topology as structural context, overlooking its role in guiding multimodal binding and shaping a unified representation space. To address these challenges, we propose GraphBind, a topology-driven approach that uses graph topology to bind rich modality information into a unified shared space. GraphBind is motivated by the stability of graph topology, which provides structural references and complementary semantic information for multimodal binding. Concretely, GraphBind uses topology to organize self semantics and reliable neighborhood semantics into a global shared space that integrates structure and semantics, and adapts this space to discriminative and generative tasks through lightweight interfaces. Extensive experiments against 11 representative baselines demonstrate that GraphBind achieves leading performance on both discriminative and generative tasks, with relative improvements of up to 28.1% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [335] [DyRA: Dynamic Residual Approximation for Efficient Matrix Multiplication in DNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02882)
*Daewon Chae,Hyunwon Chung,Changwoo Lee,Hun-Seok Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DyRA，一种输入自适应的结构化矩阵乘法近似方法，通过直接优化输出的低秩因子并动态校正输出误差，在相同计算预算下比仅近似权重的方法更准确地逼近全矩阵乘法，在视觉、语音和语言模型上均改善精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模基础模型推理成本高，主要源于稠密矩阵乘法。已有工作用低秩分解等高效结构化形式替换稠密权重矩阵，但这些方法近似的是权重而非决定推理精度的输出激活，导致权重空间的小误差会被输入激活放大，产生较大的输出误差。

Method: 作者提出DyRA，证明直接优化输出的低秩因子能更有效地近似矩阵乘法。基于此，DyRA在推理过程中动态地近似并校正由结构化权重近似所引入的输出误差，将高效结构化计算与输入相关的校正结合起来，在相同计算预算下获得对完整矩阵乘法更忠实的近似。

Result: 在视觉、语音和语言模型上，DyRA相比仅做结构化权重近似的方法一致地提升了精度-效率权衡。特别地，DyRA在DINOv3上实现1.5倍端到端GPU加速，同时将精度下降相比仅权重基线减少3倍以上。

Conclusion: 通过从近似权重转向直接优化并动态校正输出误差，DyRA在保持高效结构化计算的同时显著提升了近似保真度，为降低基础模型推理成本提供了更优的精度-效率权衡方案。

Abstract: Large-scale foundation models achieve strong performance across diverse tasks, but their size makes inference costly, largely due to dense matrix multiplications. Prior work reduces this cost by replacing dense weight matrices with efficient structured forms such as low-rank factorizations. However, these methods approximate weights rather than the output activations that determine inference accuracy. Consequently, small weight-space errors can be amplified by input activations, producing large output errors. In this work, we propose DyRA, an input-adaptive method that improves structured matrix multiplication approximation by correcting residual output errors during inference. We show that matrix multiplication can be approximated more effectively by directly optimizing low-rank factors of the output. DyRA builds on this insight by dynamically approximating and correcting the output error introduced by structured weight approximations. This combines efficient structured computation with input-dependent correction, yielding a more faithful approximation of full matrix multiplication under the same computational budget. Across vision, speech, and language models, DyRA consistently improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off over structured weight approximations alone. Notably, DyRA achieves a 1.5$\times$ end-to-end GPU speedup for DINOv3 while reducing accuracy degradation by more than 3$\times$ relative to weight-only baselines.

</details>


### [336] [When Can We Trust the Matching Principle? Robust Deployment Geometry Under Finite-Sample and Model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02894)
*Vishal Rajput*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出信任比 tau = epsilon / gamma 来量化匹配决策，并通过置信度校准匹配（CCM）将 tau 转化为自适应策略，实验验证了方向性与各向同性两种匹配机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匹配时应仅使用可识别的几何信息，否则应分散惩罚；需要一个量化指标来指导何时进行匹配、何时避免匹配。

Method: 定义信任比 tau = epsilon / gamma（估计不确定性除以谱分离度）；在线性二次匹配响应下分析估计投影器与 oracle 投影器的相对漂移，标度为 tau^2；提出置信度校准匹配（CCM），依据校准阈值实现方向性到各向同性的渐进策略。

Result: 在 Davis-Kahan 分离区域 tau < 1/2 内，漂移为 O(tau^2)，实际有用性取决于常数；实验展示了两种匹配机制，包括 UCI HAR 嵌入中 always-match 在所有单元上均差于 abstain。

Conclusion: 匹配问题处于分离区域，理论提供 tau^2 漂移界限，但阈值与软策略多依赖校准与启发式；CCM 能根据 tau 大小自适应选择方向性或各向同性匹配。

Abstract: Match only geometry you can identify; otherwise spread the penalty. We quantify that decision by the trust ratio tau = epsilon / gamma (estimation uncertainty over spectral separation). Under the linear-quadratic Matching response, oracle-relative drift between estimated and oracle projector matching scales as tau^2 for probes in the chosen top-r deployment subspace -- O(tau^2) in the Davis-Kahan separation region tau < 1/2, with practical usefulness depending on constants. Confidence-Calibrated Matching (CCM) turns tau into a policy -- directional when tau is small, progressively isotropic when not -- with thresholds from calibration, not from the theorem (match sits in the separation region; soft is mostly heuristic). Experiments show both regimes, including UCI HAR embeddings where always-match is worse than abstain on every cell.

</details>


### [337] [Tangent Schrödinger Bridge Matching: Learning Stochastic Transport with Mechanistic Sensitivities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02906)
*Jowaria Khan,Elizabeth Bondi-Kelly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Tangent-SBM，从端点观测与机理敏感性学习可复用的随机传输模型，能更准确预测系统对参数变化的响应并提升决策效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测随机系统对粘度、反应速率或外力变化的响应通常需要昂贵仿真，因此需要可复用的学习模型。但仅匹配观测到的结果分布并不能保证对干预响应的预测准确。

Method: 提出 Tangent Schrödinger Bridge Matching（Tangent-SBM）：在轨迹传播过程中同时传播参数导数，并用给定目标监督这些敏感性。针对平均响应目标，使用两次独立 rollout 匹配均值，避免单次 rollout 平方误差对响应方差的额外惩罚。理论上给出敏感性精度约束有限变化预测误差与决策遗憾的条件。

Result: 在 Gaussian、随机双井、PDEBench 反应扩散和随机 Navier–Stokes 系统上，Tangent-SBM 相比匹配的条件桥基线提升了敏感性与有限变化预测，同时保持相近的端点与分布精度。消融控制考察了目标正确性、响应目标与仿真预算分配。在 Navier–Stokes 的校准粘度选择任务中，Tangent-SBM 相对不采取动作降低了跟踪误差。

Conclusion: 将机理敏感性监督与随机桥匹配相结合，可学习到对参数干预响应更准确、且具决策实用性的随机传输模型。

Abstract: Predicting how stochastic systems respond to changes in viscosity, reaction rates, or external forces requires costly simulations, motivating reusable learned models. Yet matching observed outcome distributions does not ensure accurate intervention responses. We introduce Tangent Schrödinger Bridge Matching (Tangent-SBM), which learns stochastic transports from endpoint observations and mechanistic sensitivities. It propagates parameter derivatives alongside trajectories and supervises them against supplied targets. For average-response targets, single-rollout squared error also penalizes response variability; our objective uses two independent rollouts to match the mean without this additional penalty. We establish conditions under which sensitivity accuracy bounds finite-change prediction error and decision regret. Across Gaussian, stochastic double-well, PDEBench reaction--diffusion, and stochastic Navier--Stokes systems, Tangent-SBM improves sensitivity and finite-change prediction over matched conditional-bridge baselines while maintaining comparable endpoint and distributional accuracy. Controls examine target correctness, response objectives, and simulator-budget allocation. To test decision usefulness, we evaluate calibrated viscosity selection in Navier--Stokes: Tangent-SBM reduces tracking error relative to taking no action on every evaluated task.

</details>


### [338] [Do ResNets Route? Sparse Interaction Experts in Residual Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02907)
*Liang Yan,Siying Chen,Kaijie Chen,Bo Li,Jinghao Zhang,Mu Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将训练好的ResNet表述为关于二值残差分支掩码的集合函数，利用Möbius反演将其输出精确分解为各阶残差修正与高阶交互，揭示了深度残差网络隐含的"软路由"机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ResNet每个输入都执行所有残差块，但各块的功能贡献未必与输入无关。作者希望厘清残差网络中不同阶次交互对输出的实际贡献，并检验网络是否存在类似路由的自适应功能分配现象。

Method: 将训练好的ResNet建模为二值残差分支掩码上的集合函数，通过Möbius反演对其输出进行精确分解，得到单块残差修正与高阶交互项；对平滑残差堆叠证明固定k阶交互在残差缩放下的量级为O(λ^k)；对ImageNet预训练的ResNet-18与ResNet-34进行穷尽式分析。

Result: 交互质量峰值分别出现在五阶（ResNet-18）与十阶（ResNet-34），而非低阶；减小残差缩放使谱向低阶移动但同时改变预测；交互系数幅值集中但非硬稀疏，保预测稀疏性随深度减弱；主导交互随输入与预测类别变化但围绕一个共享全局核心，且整体复杂度随样本难度变化不大。

Conclusion: 稠密ResNet实现了一种隐式软路由：所有块都被执行，但不同输入依赖不同的残差交互"专家"。路由可自发涌现于功能贡献层面，而无需显式路由器或稀疏执行。

Abstract: Residual networks execute every block for every input, yet their functional contributions need not be input independent. We formulate a trained ResNet as a set function over binary residual-branch masks and apply Möbius inversion to decompose its output exactly into individual residual corrections and higher-order interactions. For smooth residual stacks, we show that each fixed $k$-way interaction scales as $\mathcal{O}(λ^k)$ under residual scaling. Exhaustive analysis of ImageNet-pretrained ResNet-18 and ResNet-34 reveals that interaction mass peaks at orders five and ten, respectively, rather than at low orders. Reducing the residual scale shifts both spectra toward lower orders, but also changes model predictions. The interaction coefficients are concentrated in magnitude but not hard sparse, and prediction-preserving sparsity weakens with depth. Crucially, the dominant interactions vary across inputs and predicted classes around a shared global core, while their overall complexity changes little with sample difficulty. These results show that dense ResNets implement an implicit form of soft routing: every block is executed, but different inputs rely on different residual interaction experts. Routing can therefore emerge at the level of functional contribution without an explicit router or sparse execution.

</details>


### [339] [Constraint-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02909)
*Jinwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"约束感知"训练目标，将推理时约束解码所用的程序分析从训练中外部化，证明其能带来更高效率的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准训练已教会模型抑制那些会被程序分析拒绝的token，这与推理时的约束解码存在重复。作者追问：既然推理时反正要用分析过滤token，能否在训练中干脆不教这些分析，从而获得更高效的模型？

Method: 定义了一个满足"外部化"要求的通用约束感知目标，并将外部化收益形式化为三个关于模型规模与数据效率的定理；通过受控合成实验验证理论。

Result: 定理在训练动态下依然成立：在相同参数量和数据量下，约束感知训练相比普通交叉熵训练取得更低的预测损失。

Conclusion: 应鼓励采用融入生成时所用分析的训练目标。

Abstract: When generating programs with language models, constrained decoding can apply program analyses to exclude tokens that violate syntax, scope, or typing rules. However, there is a duplication: standard training already teaches the model to suppress the tokens rejected by these analyses. This duplication leads to the question: if we will perform some analysis to filter a set tokens out during inference anyways, can we avoid teaching the model the said analysis altogether during training, and does this externalization lead to more efficient models? This paper defines a general constraint-aware objective satisfying this externalization desideratum and formalizes the benefits of externalization into three concrete theorems about model size and data efficiency. We show, through a controlled synthetic experiment, that the theorems survive training dynamics: constraint-aware training yields lower prediction loss at a matched parameter count and data compared to ordinary cross-entropy training, motivating training objectives that incorporate the analyses used during generation.

</details>


### [340] [Probe the Harness: Setup Checks for Stale-Data RL Comparisons in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02911)
*Taiheng Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出：用于训练语言模型的过期样本方法，其优劣排序可能被实验框架（harness）的实现细节所逆转；作者提出 PTH 检查清单以暴露这些细节，并在检查后修正了 SAN 与 TIS 的比较结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对过期样本训练方法（如 SAN 与重要性修正基线 TIS）的评判依赖于实验对比，但实验框架中的实现细节可能暗中影响结果，导致方法排序不可靠。作者希望揭示这些未被察觉的框架问题，使比较变得透明可信。

Method: 作者以 SAN（无需行为策略的方法）与截断重要性采样（TIS）在 verl 及单 GPU 训练器上的对比为案例，提出 PTH（Probe The Harness）检查集。他们逐步排查并识别出四个改变比较结果的关键细节：PPO 比率使用学习器自身重算的概率、数据种子未传递到 TIS 分支、回放队列首个批次被重复用于 33 次更新、以及两个损失归一化器与描述不符。

Result: 初始实验中 SAN 在两个平台上均领先。修正上述四个框架细节后：在 verl 上 TIS 与 SAN 表现相当；在单 GPU 训练器中 TIS 能稳定学习，而 SAN 仍保持一定优势。此外，作者还提供了采样器滞后下 TIS 与未修正 GRPO 的参考结果。

Conclusion: 实验框架的细节足以逆转方法比较的结论，且这些细节往往不会在日志中显现异常。作者贡献了每个细节的特征信号及其对比较的影响、采样器滞后下的参考结果以及 PTH 检查清单，呼吁在评估过期样本训练方法时先校验框架。

Abstract: Methods for training language models on stale samples are judged by comparisons against importance-corrected baselines. We show that details of the experimental harness can reverse the observed ranking of methods, and we introduce PTH (Probe The Harness), a set of checks that makes the harness visible. Our case is a comparison between SAN, a behaviour-free method, and truncated importance sampling (TIS) on verl and in a single-GPU trainer, in which SAN first finished ahead in both stacks. Four details of the harness changed this comparison: the PPO ratio was taken against the learner's own recomputed probabilities, the data seed did not reach the TIS arm, the replay queue reused its first batch for 33 updates, and two loss normalisers differed from their description. In each case the logged quantity looked consistent with a working setup, while the quantity that defines the comparison went unchecked. With the harness checked, TIS matches SAN on verl, and in the trainer TIS learns steadily while SAN keeps a margin. We contribute the signature of each detail and its effect on the comparison, reference results for TIS and uncorrected GRPO under sampler lag, and the PTH checklist.

</details>


### [341] [Dynamic Expert Pruning for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02951)
*Jabin Koo,Soheil Abbasloo,Sungjae Lee,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态专家剪枝（DEP），利用agent的system和task prompt在单次前向中生成每个请求专属的专家mask，在多智能体异构负载下比静态剪枝/合并基线取得更高总体精度，且在保留少量专家时优势最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽只激活少量专家以节省计算，但所有专家仍需常驻显存，导致部署受限。现有专家剪枝方法为静态：离线校准一个固定mask应用于所有请求。该假设在异构负载（尤其多智能体系统，一个骨干同时服务多任务多角色）下会失效——分析表明不同任务和角色会调用不同专家，而静态方法给它们分配同一子集。

Method: 基于一个发现：agent的系统提示与任务提示本身已足以识别该agent及其任务所需的专家（因文本已描述agent将做什么）。据此设计轻量预测器，仅在工作流transcript上训练一次，将提示转化为每个请求专属的mask，单次前向完成，无需针对每个配置做校准。

Result: 在多样任务与角色、不同模型规模与MoE架构上，DEP总体精度优于静态剪枝与合并基线；无需重训练即可泛化到训练中未见过的工作流；在保留专家数很少时相对基线的优势最大。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统固有的角色专门化使得可进行比静态剪枝更稀疏的部署，DEP验证了基于提示的动态专家剪枝的有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale language models efficiently by activating only a few experts per token, but the saving is confined to computation: every expert must stay resident on the accelerator, so memory bounds where these models can be deployed. Expert pruning reduces this footprint, yet existing methods are static --- a single mask, calibrated offline, is applied to the model for every subsequent request. This assumption can fail when the workload is heterogeneous, most prominently in multi-agent systems, where one backbone serves many tasks and roles at once: our analysis shows that different tasks and roles recruit different experts, while static methods assign one fixed subset to all of them. We therefore propose Dynamic Expert Pruning (DEP), which rests on a finding we establish here: an agent's system and task prompts are by themselves sufficient to identify the experts that agent and its task require, since that text already describes what the agent will do. A lightweight predictor, trained once on workflow transcripts, turns those prompts into a specialized per-request mask in a single forward pass, with no per-configuration calibration. Across diverse tasks and roles, model scales, and MoE architectures, DEP achieves better overall accuracy than static pruning and merging baselines, and generalizes to workflows unseen in training without retraining. Its margin over those baselines is largest when few experts are retained, suggesting that the role specialization inherent to multi-agent systems permits sparser serving than static pruning allows.

</details>


### [342] [SlimKV: Joint Token-Feature KV Cache Compression with Reconstruction-Free Beacon Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02953)
*Zihan Teng,Jiayu Zhao,Wentao Ren,Minhao Fan,Tianrui Ma,Song Chen,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SlimKV 提出一种问题无关的联合 token-特征 KV-cache 压缩方法，通过低秩感知训练结合 K-RoPE-free 约束与隐空间注意力，在长上下文服务中实现高压缩率、高分保持与显著解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 LLM 服务受限于 KV-cache 内存，尤其在资源受限场景。现有 KV-cache 压缩中：token 级方法通过驱逐或压缩减少缓存状态，但存在信息损失风险；特征级方法降低每 token 的 KV 维度，却往往需要全维重建才能施加位置编码，限制了解码加速。

Method: 提出 SlimKV，一种问题无关的联合 token-特征 KV-cache 压缩方法。使用低秩感知训练，将长上下文压缩为带潜在 KV 表示的 beacon 记忆状态，并进行逐层自适应秩分配。发现位置非对称性：移除 key 侧 RoPE 对 beacon token 与原始 token 影响不同，beacon token 退化小得多。基于此，在 K-RoPE-free 约束下训练 beacon KV 投影，并在解码时启用隐空间注意力以减少重建延迟。

Result: 在 LongBench 上，SlimKV 在 16x/32x 压缩下优于基线，在 4x/8x 下保持领先，保留未压缩模型 96% 以上分数。Needle-in-a-Haystack 验证了跨证据位置的鲁棒性。效率评估显示，在 128K 长度下相较未压缩模型最高获得 7.34x 注意力加速与 3.38x 端到端解码加速。

Conclusion: SlimKV 通过联合 token-特征压缩、低秩感知训练与 K-RoPE-free 隐空间注意力，在保持长上下文性能的同时显著提升压缩率与解码效率，为资源受限下的长上下文 LLM 服务提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Long-context LLM serving is increasingly bottlenecked by KV-cache memory, especially in resource-constrained scenarios. Among existing KV-cache compression strategies, token-wise methods reduce cached states but risk information loss through eviction or condensation, while feature-wise methods reduce per-token KV dimensions but can require full-dimensional reconstruction to apply positional embedding, limiting decoding speedups. We introduce SlimKV, a question-agnostic joint token-feature KV-cache compression method. SlimKV uses low-rank-aware training to compress long contexts into beacon memory states with latent KV representations, together with layer-adaptive rank allocation. We further uncover a positional asymmetry: removing key-side RoPE affects beacon and raw tokens differently, with much smaller degradation for beacon tokens. Exploiting this asymmetry, SlimKV trains beacon KV projections under a K-RoPE-free constraint and enables latent-space attention during decoding, mitigating reconstruction latency. On LongBench, SlimKV outperforms baselines at 16x/32x compression and remains leading at 4x/8x, where it retains over 96% of the uncompressed model's score. Needle-in-a-Haystack confirms robustness across evidence positions, and efficiency evaluation shows up to 7.34x attention speedup and 3.38x end-to-end decoding speedup over the uncompressed model at 128K length.

</details>


### [343] [Hyperparameter selection for equation learning with biologically-informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02954)
*William Lavery,Jodie A. Cochrane,John T. Nardini,Sara Hamis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套用于生物启发神经网络（BINNs）超参数选择的诊断工作流，在未知真实方程的情况下辅助学习偏微分方程项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BINNs适合处理高度非线性、部分未知且数据稀疏嘈杂的生物系统，但其效果严重依赖超参数选择；而超参数常靠经验选取且记录不足，导致结果难以复现、方法难以迁移。

Method: 围绕三个问题构建诊断工作流：(1) 更大网络容量的收益是否值得其代价？(2) 更多训练轮次是否持续降低验证损失？(3) 随网络容量和训练增加，学习到的项是否趋于稳定？并在已知真值的合成系统上验证。

Result: 在扩散与增长右端项、1D+t至2D+t数据等不同复杂度系统中，验证损失总体跟随学习项的真实误差；据此总结出BINN架构超参数选择的实用经验规则。

Conclusion: 通过提供结构化工作流、实践指南和推荐初始值，该工作降低了基于BINN进行方程学习的门槛，并提升结果可复现性与方法可迁移性。

Abstract: Biologically-informed neural networks (BINNs) have emerged as a flexible subclass of physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for learning terms in partial differential equations from data. BINNs are particularly suited for biological systems, where the governing equations are highly nonlinear and only partially known a priori, and where data observations are often sparse, noisy, and incomplete. However, applying BINNs effectively in practice depends critically on hyperparameter selection, which remains a central challenge in equation-learning frameworks. Hyperparameters are often chosen heuristically and only cursorily documented, which limits the reproducibility of results and the transferability of methods. We present a diagnostic workflow for hyperparameter selection that can be used when the ground-truth equations are not known. The workflow is guided by three main questions: (1) Are the benefits of greater network capacity worth the cost? (2) Do more training epochs keep reducing the validation loss? (3) Do the learned terms stop changing as network capacity and training increase? We apply our workflow to synthetic systems of varying complexity with known ground truth, spanning diffusion and growth right-hand side terms and data ranging from 1D+t to 2D+t. We demonstrate that the validation loss generally follows the true error in the learned terms and distil practical rules of thumb for selecting hyperparameters in the BINN architecture. By providing a structured workflow, practical guidelines, and suggested starting values for hyperparameter selection, this work lowers the barrier to BINN-based equation learning.

</details>


### [344] [Understanding Trajectory Heterogeneity in Federated World Model Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02957)
*Yipan Wei,Zhaokun Yan,Ziming Hong,Jiaqi Wu,Lixu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过MIMIC-IV八个疾病队列的逐小时动作条件临床预测，系统评测了联邦学习在跨时间世界模型建模中的时间上下文获取、参与覆盖、优化行为与预测视野问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型需要从轨迹中学习状态演化，因此时间上下文的获取是核心训练需求；联邦学习虽可利用分布式记录，但轨迹内部的所有权边界限制了单个客户端可构建的样本，需要针对这一跨时间场景建立基准。

Method: 在MIMIC-IV八个疾病队列上进行逐小时动作条件临床预测（4087万条转移成员关系）；设定基于严重程度的客户端所有权、按患者分离的构建方式、本地历史与未来窗口规则，以及1至32小时的成对rollout评估；在五轮10%参与率下，用十种联邦算法覆盖32种疾病-分区配置。

Result: 第一，客户端所有权与参与度共同限制长窗口覆盖：平均仅7.55%–21.36%的池化可用32步窗口拥有训练中被访问的本地完整锚点；第二，16组配对比较中有15组显示更细的严重程度分区伴随更高的FedAvg误差，算法增益小且依赖视野，FedProx平均误差仅降低0.56%，32步时无一致改善；第三，算法标签掩盖了不同的更新行为，包括不活跃外推和数量级差异的更新尺度；相同基准协议下，缓存更新性能在不同轨迹分区间也差异显著。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了时间可访问性、参与覆盖、优化行为与视野分辨预测，是评估联邦临床世界模型的四个互补维度。

Abstract: World models learn state evolution from trajectories, making access to temporal context a central training requirement. Federated learning can use distributed records, while ownership boundaries within a trajectory restrict the examples each client can construct. Our study benchmarks this cross-time setting through hourly action-conditioned clinical prediction on eight MIMIC-IV disease cohorts, comprising 40.87 million transition memberships. We specify severity-based client ownership, patient-separated construction, local history and future-window rules, and paired rollout evaluation from one to 32 hours. A matrix of ten federated algorithms covers 32 disease--partition configurations under five rounds of ten-percent participation. Three findings emerge from existing results and training logs. First, client ownership and participation jointly restrict long-window coverage: only 7.55\%--21.36\% of pooled-available 32-step windows have a locally complete anchor visited during training, averaged across diseases. Second, finer severity partitions accompany higher FedAvg error in 15 of 16 paired comparisons, while algorithm gains are small and horizon-dependent: FedProx reduces mean error by 0.56\%, with no consistent improvement at 32 steps. Third, algorithm labels conceal distinct update behavior, including inactive extrapolation and orders-of-magnitude differences in update scale. Cached-update performance also varies strongly across trajectory partitions under the same benchmark protocol. These results establish temporal access, participation coverage, optimization behavior, and horizon-resolved prediction as complementary dimensions for evaluating federated clinical world models.

</details>


### [345] [Dirac-Interconnected Neural Elements: Discovering Modularity in Physical Systems Without Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02960)
*Reiho Li,Razmik Arman Khosrovian,Takaharu Yaguchi,Hiroaki Yoshimura,Takashi Matsubara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dirac-interconnected neural elements (DINEs)，用微分-代数方程（DAE）表示物理系统，其代数约束由 kernel representation 下的 Dirac 结构给出，可同时从数据中辨识子系统间的互联结构并学习子系统特性，支持不降阶、可组合复用及部分可观测场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的成功更多来自基于物理先验（如能量守恒、辛结构）的归纳偏置，而非网络本身的灵活性。但现有方法未充分利用真实物理系统是多个部件互联这一事实：有的要求互联结构已知，有的假设系统可降阶为 ODE，只学习降阶后的 ODE，从而丢弃了互联带来的代数约束。

Method: 提出 DINEs，将物理系统建模为微分-代数方程（DAE），其代数约束由 Dirac 结构的 kernel representation 给出。通过该方法可同时：(1) 从数据中辨识部件间的互联关系（作为 Dirac 结构）；(2) 将部件特性学习为神经网络。此外，DINEs 还能处理部分可观测系统。

Result: 实验在现有方法难以处理的物理系统上验证了 DINEs 的能力，包括保持学习到的子系统为不降阶形式、无需重训练即可隔离或组合子系统、以及处理部分可观测系统。

Conclusion: DINEs 通过将互联建模为 Dirac 结构、子系统建模为神经网络，实现了不降阶的物理系统学习与可组合复用，并能处理部分可观测情况，拓展了数据驱动动力学建模的能力边界。

Abstract: Deep learning has shown remarkable success in the data-driven modeling of dynamical systems. Much of its success is attributed not to the flexibility of neural networks but to inductive biases based on physical prior knowledge, such as energy conservation and symplecticity. However, existing methods do not fully exploit the fact that real-world physical systems are interconnections of components. Some methods require the interconnection to be known a priori, while others assume the system to be reducible to an ordinary differential equation (ODE) and learn only the reduced ODE, discarding the algebraic constraints imposed by the interconnection. Here, we propose Dirac-interconnected neural elements (DINEs), a neural network model that represents a physical system as a differential-algebraic equation (DAE), whose algebraic constraints are given by a Dirac structure in kernel representation. With DINEs, we simultaneously identify from data the interconnection among the components as a Dirac structure and learn the characteristics of the components as neural networks. This allows us to keep the learned subsystems in unreduced form and isolate or compose them to make a new system without retraining. Moreover, DINEs can handle partially observable systems. Experimental results demonstrate these capabilities on physical systems beyond the reach of existing methods.

</details>


### [346] [Differentiable Koopman Operator for Contrastive Learning on Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02990)
*Md Abrar Jahin,Taufikur Rahman Fuad,Md Rizwan Parvez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KAIROS是一个自监督框架，将可微Koopman算子嵌入动态图对比学习，用于动态图表示学习与异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实交互网络是动态的，边随时间形成与消失。多数基于快照的对比方法仅隐式地在编码器权重中编码时序依赖，缺乏对节点表示演化的显式建模，导致分布漂移下表现脆弱。

Method: 在动态图对比学习循环中嵌入可微Koopman算子，在学习嵌入空间中对时序演化进行线性化；采用双视图编码器，将原始节点特征与图扩散结构视图配对，并在多个时间窗口上用多粒度对比目标优化；异常检测结合Koopman预测残差、时序不一致性和局部邻域偏差。

Result: 在九个动态图基准上取得全部数据集的最优异常检测结果，相比已有工作最高提升23.15 ROC-AUC点，同时在无监督节点分类上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 显式动力学建模为时序图表示学习提供了可扩展且有效的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Real-world interaction networks are inherently dynamic: edges form and dissolve as node behavior shifts over time. Most snapshot-based contrastive methods encode temporal dependencies implicitly in encoder weights, without an explicit model of how node representations evolve, making them brittle under distribution shifts. We propose KAIROS (Koopman-Aligned Invariant Representations for Open Dynamic Systems), a self-supervised framework that embeds a differentiable Koopman operator within a dynamic graph contrastive learning loop to linearize temporal evolution in the learned embedding space. A dual-view encoder pairs raw node features with a graph-diffused structural view and is optimized with multi-granularity contrastive objectives across temporal windows. For anomaly detection, KAIROS uses the Koopman prediction residual together with temporal inconsistency and local neighborhood deviation to separate irregular behavior from predictable graph evolution. Evaluated on nine dynamic graph benchmarks, KAIROS achieves state-of-the-art anomaly detection results on all nine datasets, with gains of up to 23.15 ROC-AUC points over prior work, while remaining competitive for unsupervised node classification. These results show that explicit dynamics modeling provides a scalable and effective inductive bias for temporal graph representation learning.

</details>


### [347] [Sentry: Learning to Recover from LLM Agent Failures at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02994)
*Changxiu Ji,Amy Lu,Qizheng Zhang,Kunle Olukotun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出失败知识属于"条件性知识"，应当条件性暴露。为此设计了Sentry——一个与智能体并行运行的失败管理层：检测到失败时从外部手册检索匹配的经验指导恢复，无任务奖励地验证是否恢复成功，仅在成功时存入新经验，且完整手册从不进入智能体上下文。在多个智能体基准上，Sentry平均超越最强运行时干预基线37%，在可比较的两个基准上超越最强上下文演化基线39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体常因无效工具调用、重复操作或推理缺乏依据而中途失败，从失败中学习是通向可靠性的路径。作者发现"失败知识如何到达智能体"与"知识内容本身"同样重要：失败经验具有条件性——留在上下文中会在相应失败缺席时误触发，从演化手册中移除反而提升性能；而运行时干预仅在失败发生时起作用，却不从其修复中学习。

Method: 将失败知识视为条件性知识并应条件性暴露。实现为Sentry，一个与智能体并行的失败管理层：检测到失败时，从外部手册检索匹配经验以指导恢复；在不访问任务奖励的情况下验证智能体是否恢复；仅当验证为已恢复时才存储新经验；完整手册始终不进入智能体上下文。

Result: 在多个智能体基准上，Sentry在每一个基准上都优于最强运行时干预基线，平均提升37%；在两者均被评估的两个基准上，优于最强上下文演化基线39%；与上下文演化结合可带来额外收益。所学经验可迁移到留出任务，对照实验表明，即使相关经验仍可按需获取，将完整手册暴露给智能体也会降低性能。

Conclusion: 失败知识应被视为条件性知识并条件性暴露；将失败管理置于智能体外部、仅在需要时提供匹配经验，比将失败经验常驻或被移除的上下文演化方式更有效，为提升LLM智能体可靠性提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLM agents often fail mid-task due to invalid tool calls, repeated actions, or poorly grounded reasoning, and learning from these failures is a path to reliability. We find that how failure knowledge reaches the agent matters as much as what it contains. Failure lessons are conditional: kept in the agent's context, they misfire when their failure is absent, and removing them from an evolving playbook improves performance. Runtime interventions, in contrast, act only when a failure occurs but do not learn from their repairs. We argue that failure knowledge is conditional knowledge and should be conditionally exposed, and instantiate this principle in Sentry, a failure-management layer that runs alongside the agent. When Sentry detects a failure, it retrieves matching lessons from an external playbook to guide recovery, verifies without access to task rewards whether the agent recovered, and stores a new lesson only if it did; the full playbook never enters the agent's context.
