Across multiple agentic benchmarks, Sentry outperforms the strongest runtime-intervention baseline on every benchmark, by 37\% on average, and the strongest context-evolution baseline by 39\% on the two benchmarks where both are evaluated; combining Sentry with context evolution yields further gains. Learned lessons transfer to held-out tasks, and controlled experiments show that exposing the full playbook to the agent lowers performance even when relevant lessons remain available on demand.

</details>


### [348] [Temporal Geometry of Deep Networks: Hyperbolic Representations of Training Dynamics for Intrinsic Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03000)
*Ambarish Moharil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种几何与时序图结合的元学习框架，用双曲几何（Poincaré模型）表示MLP训练过程中的动态参数图，以捕捉神经网络的自组织与演化几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在可解释性仍是难题，尤其当MLP需要在优化环境中动态重训练时。传统基于度量的可解释性方法仅分析单一训练检查点，忽略了神经网络在训练轨迹中编码的信息。作者希望从几何与时序角度刻画权重拓扑的演化与自组织过程。

Method: 构建时序"参数图"，即在T个训练/优化步骤上对MLP进行快照；借鉴复杂网络可嵌入隐度量空间（距离对应连接概率）的思想，将时序参数图嵌入双曲Poincaré球模型中。学习时保持快照内神经元置换等变性以及历史快照置换不变性，从而维护功能等价性并恢复网络潜在的演化几何。

Result: 在回归和分类任务上，对训练好的MLP进行实验，元网络表现出强劲的性能，并得到双曲时序表示，揭示了网络结构在特定训练环境下随时间的涌现过程。

Conclusion: 该方法通过双曲时序图表示，为理解神经网络在训练中的自组织与结构演化提供了新视角，弥补了传统单检查点可解释性方法的不足。

Abstract: Intrinsic explainability remains a challenging problem, particularly in contexts where multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) require dynamic re-training within an optimization environment. This paper investigates how MLPs and their training dynamics can be represented and studied in non-Euclidean spaces; our representation features the Poincaré model of hyperbolic geometry. We aim to capture the geometric evolution of their weighted topology and self-organization over time. Instead of restricting the analysis to single checkpoints---as per established measure-based explainability methods---we construct temporal \textit{parameter graphs}, i.e., snapshots over time $T$ steps of the optimization/training process for MLPs. This reflects the view that neural networks encode information not only in their weights but also in the trajectory traced during training. Drawing on the idea that many complex networks admit embeddings in hidden metric spaces where distances correspond to connection likelihood, we present a geometric and temporal graph-based metalearning framework for obtaining dynamic hyperbolic representations of the underlying neural parameter graphs. Our model embeds temporal parameter graphs in the Poincaré model ball, and learns from them while maintaining equivariance to within-snapshot neuron permutations and invariance to permutations of past snapshots. In doing so, the approach preserves functional equivalence over time and recovers the latent evolving geometry of the network. Experiments on regression and classification tasks with trained MLPs show strong meta-network performance, accompanied by hyperbolic temporal representations. This reveals how the network structure emerges over time under specific training environments, thus providing insights into the network's self-organization.

</details>


### [349] [Neural Data Needs Semantic Tokenization: Behavioral Events as Boundaries of Session-Transferable Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03001)
*Sangyoon Bae,Jiook Cha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TWS，以群体活动的"状态"为跨会话共享单元进行 token 化，无需神经元或会话嵌入，在留出 IBL 会话上优于逐神经元基础模型，并可从仅用小鼠训练迁移到猕猴与 Utah 阵列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞外电生理每次记录到的神经元集合都不同，神经基础模型把每个神经元和每个会话嵌入到 token 中，因此新会话都是未见输入，难以泛化。需要一个跨会话共享且携带行为信息的单元。

Method: 利用群体活动在低维流形上演化、该流形在神经元更替甚至跨动物（会话对齐后）后仍保持的性质。流形在刺激起始、运动起始等任务事件处切换"状态"（regime），每个状态是两次事件之间流形上的片段并携带各自行为含义。TWS 按这些事件切分每次试次，将每个状态转换为群体几何的 token，不使用神经元或会话嵌入。

Result: 在留出的 IBL 会话上，TWS 甚至能从对目标无信息的 regime 边界解码运动，而预训练于这些会话的逐神经元基础模型仅达随机水平。仅用小鼠训练后冻结，TWS 只用线性探针即可迁移到猕猴和 Utah 阵列。在到达方向上（其边界并不定义该目标），达到 Matthews 相关 0.23，而仅用事件时间只有 0.01。

Conclusion: 跨会话泛化中，token 比其上的模型更重要；以群体几何状态为单元可提供跨会话、跨物种的共享表示。

Abstract: Extracellular electrophysiology records a different set of neurons in every session. Neural foundation models embed each neuron and each session into their tokens, so every new session is an input they have never seen, and they fail to generalize to it. A tokenizer for new sessions needs a unit that every session shares and that carries behavior. Population activity offers such a unit. It evolves on a low-dimensional manifold that persists across neuronal turnover and across animals once sessions are aligned. This manifold changes regime at task events such as stimulus onset and movement onset. Within each regime the population occupies a state, the part of the manifold it spans between two events, and each state carries its own behavioral meaning. We propose Tokenization with States (TWS), which segments each trial, one repetition of the task, at these events and converts every state into tokens of population geometry, with no neuron or session embedding. On held-out International Brain Laboratory (IBL) sessions, TWS decodes movement even from regime boundaries that carry no information about the target, while a per-neuron foundation model pretrained on those sessions decodes at chance. Frozen after training on mice alone, TWS transfers to macaques and Utah arrays with only a linear probe. On reach direction, a target that its boundaries do not define, it achieves a Matthews correlation of 0.23, where the event time alone achieves $0.01$. For cross-session generalization, the token matters more than the model on top.

</details>


### [350] [AvoKV-E: Payload-Aware KV Cache Eviction for Long Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03007)
*Han Yu,Wenhui Zhu,Xiwen Chen,Zhipeng Wang,Hejian Sang,Han Shi,Menglin Zhou,Xuanzhao Dong,Minzhou Huang,Rui Cai,Hao Wang,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练的长推理KV缓存淘汰策略AvoKV-E，先延迟近期状态的可淘汰资格，再用候选归一化读压力、键冗余度、值载荷潜力排序，在紧缓存场景下优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长输出推理使KV缓存瓶颈从固定提示转向生成的推理轨迹。现有推理缓存淘汰方法多把缓存条目当作"路由对象"，只估计旧键是否还会被读取、复现或替换。这种路由视角忽略两个效应：低注意力条目可能携带大值载荷，删除会改变未来预测；新生成的状态在后续查询读取前就可能显得"过时"。

Method: AvoKV-E是一种免训练淘汰策略：第一步对近期状态延迟其淘汰资格（delayed eligibility）；第二步对已具备资格的条目，用候选归一化的读压力、键冗余度、值载荷潜力三项打分排序决定淘汰对象。

Result: 在不同模型和数据集上的实证评估显示，在匹配的活跃KV预算下，AvoKV-E达到或超过冗余感知、基于复现、思维自适应等淘汰基线，且在缓存最紧的区间增益最大。组件与反事实分析将增益归因于延迟观察、载荷感知打分、冗余度与尺度鲁棒归一化。

Conclusion: 长推理KV淘汰不应只保留"可能被读取的键"，还应保留支撑推理轨迹的"值载荷"。

Abstract: Long-output reasoning shifts the KV-cache bottleneck from the fixed prompt to the generated trace. Existing reasoning-cache eviction methods largely treat cached entries as routing objects, estimating whether an old key will still be read, will recur, or can be replaced. This routing-only view overlooks two effects: low-attention entries can carry large value payloads whose removal changes future predictions, and newly generated states can appear stale before later queries have had a chance to read them. We introduce AvoKV-E, a training-free eviction policy that first delays eligibility for recent states and then ranks eligible entries using candidate-normalized read pressure, key redundancy, and value-payload potential. According to empirical evaluation across different models and datasets, AvoKV-E matches or exceeds redundancy-aware, recurrence-based, and thought-adaptive eviction baselines at matched active-KV budgets, with its largest gains in the tightest-cache regime. Component and counterfactual analyses further connect these gains to delayed observation, payload-aware scoring, redundancy, and scale-robust normalization. Together, the results show that long-reasoning KV eviction should preserve not only keys that are likely to be read, but also the value payloads that sustain the reasoning trajectory.

</details>


### [351] [Signal Simplification Is Not Predictive Simplification: Diagnosing Residual Neural Forecasting in Short-Horizon Volatility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03019)
*Bingqi Lian,Linfeng Cheng,Mei Lu,Jerry Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验"统计第一阶段成功后残差更易学习"这一假设，发现残差虽在统计上被大幅简化，但用它做LSTM增强反而使MSE升高，提出"预测器-预处理器不对称"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合统计-神经流水线常假设统计第一阶段能产出更干净、更易学习的残差目标。作者针对短期波动率预测场景，检验这一假设是否成立。

Method: 构建信号-预测-系统诊断框架，在五个流动性较好的美国资产上，比较反波动率对齐的HAR类模型与AR、MA、ARIMA；在扩张训练窗口内，考察用于构建残差LSTM序列的标准化前拟合残差过程，并进行滚动伪样本外HAR误差分析，同时对比纯LSTM与残差混合方法。

Result: 对齐波动率的HAR类模型优于AR、MA、ARIMA。残差过程方差比对应目标低约82%，lag-1自相关近零；滚动伪样本外HAR误差方差降低约74%，lag-1依赖同样微弱。但残差LSTM增强使平均MSE从0.3049升至0.3594，且每个资产均恶化；纯LSTM取得最低的伪样本外MSE 0.2649，残差混合方法还需显著更长的端到端运行时间且未提升精度。

Conclusion: 第一阶段的预测成功与统计残差简化并不必然转化为对下游神经网络的有利预处理，作者将这一现象称为"预测器-预处理器不对称"。

Abstract: Hybrid statistical-neural pipelines often assume that a successful statistical first stage leaves a cleaner and more learnable residual target. We examine that assumption in short-horizon volatility forecasting through a signal-forecast-system diagnostic framework. Across five liquid U.S. assets, a volatility-aligned HAR-style model outperforms AR, MA, and ARIMA. Within expanding training windows, the pre-standardization fitted residual process used to construct residual-LSTM sequences has about 82% lower variance than the corresponding target and near-zero lag-1 autocorrelation; independently, rolling pseudo-out-of-sample HAR errors show about 74% variance reduction and similarly weak lag-1 dependence. Residual-only LSTM augmentation nevertheless raises mean squared error from 0.3049 to 0.3594 on average, with deterioration on every asset. Pure LSTM records the lowest selected pseudo-out-of-sample MSE, 0.2649, while the residual hybrid requires substantially more end-to-end runtime without improving accuracy. We describe this pattern as forecaster-preconditioner asymmetry: first-stage forecasting success and statistical residual simplification need not translate into useful downstream neural preconditioning.

</details>


### [352] [Tailoring the Quantization Space for 1-Bit KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03027)
*Minsoo Cheong,Donghyun Son,Sungjoo Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TaSQ，通过定制 VQ 目标空间（查询引导通道加权、跨头归一化、协方差感知通道分组）实现极致 1-bit KV cache 压缩，性能优于现有基线且保持推理稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 LLM 推理中 KV cache 成为主要内存瓶颈，给内存容量与带宽带来巨大压力。向量量化（VQ）是有前景的激进压缩方法，但现有 VQ 方法在 1-bit 极限压缩下显著退化，因为每个码本需用有限质心表示更大的通道组，容量利用困难。

Method: TaSQ 通过定制 VQ 的目标空间来提升码本容量利用：结合（1）查询引导的通道加权，（2）跨头归一化，（3）协方差感知的通道分组，以更好反映缓存激活的误差敏感性与统计结构。这些变换与 RoPE 兼容，可并入投影权重和码本，保留传统 VQ 查表结构，服务开销可忽略。

Result: 在通用、长链式思维推理和长上下文检索基准上，TaSQ 一致优于现有低比特 KV cache VQ 基线，并保持推理稳定性。在单块 RTX 6000 Ada GPU 上，其 SGLang 实现支持最高 14 倍更大的 batch size，峰值吞吐量比 BF16 基线高 1.87 倍。

Conclusion: TaSQ 通过对 VQ 目标空间进行针对性变换，在 1-bit 极限 KV cache 压缩下显著提升精度与推理稳定性，同时具备良好的部署效率与低服务开销。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache becomes a major memory bottleneck in long-context LLM inference, placing substantial pressure on memory capacity and bandwidth. To mitigate this bottleneck, vector quantization (VQ) has emerged as a promising approach for aggressive KV cache compression. However, existing VQ methods degrade substantially in the 1-bit regime. At such extreme compression, each codebook must represent a larger group of channels with a limited set of centroids, making effective use of its capacity increasingly challenging. To address this, we introduce $\textbf{TaSQ}$, which tailors the VQ target space by combining query-guided channel weighting, cross-head normalization, and covariance-aware channel grouping to better reflect the error sensitivity and statistical structure of cached activations. Since these transforms are RoPE-compatible and can be easily merged into projection weights and codebooks, TaSQ preserves the conventional VQ lookup structure and adds negligible serving overhead. Across general, long-chain-of-thought reasoning, and long-context retrieval benchmarks, TaSQ consistently outperforms existing low-bit KV cache VQ baselines while preserving reasoning stability. On a single RTX 6000 Ada GPU, its SGLang implementation supports up to $14\times$ larger batch sizes and achieves $1.87\times$ higher peak throughput compared to the BF16 baseline.

</details>


### [353] [Balancing Multimodal Learning via Functional Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03035)
*Zhongjing Gu,Fengqiang Wan,Yiming Cui,Yufa Feng,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Function-Space Guided Multimodal Optimization (FGMO)，利用函数空间进度信号评估各模态的优化进度并协调优化，以缓解多模态学习中的模态不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习常受模态不平衡困扰，联合优化过程往往被单一模态主导。现有方法多依赖预测不确定性或优化统计得到的分数差异来估计模态不平衡，但由于不同模态的预测不确定性和学习动态各异，直接比较这些分数可能将内在模态差异误判为进度差距，导致有偏的不平衡估计。

Method: 提出 FGMO，包含：Functional Progress Estimation (FPE)，用于度量各模态更新引起的函数空间响应，并以损失对齐的单模态参考进行校准，得到可比较的进度信号；Functional Response Control (FRC)，基于该信号重新分配模态级函数空间预算，并通过张量级学习率调整实现目标响应。

Result: 理论分析证明了在控制器状态失配有界条件下 FRC 的单步目标收缩性质；大量实验表明 FGMO 在多个多模态基准上有效。

Conclusion: 通过函数空间进度信号协调各模态优化，FGMO 能够更准确地评估模态进展并有效缓解模态不平衡，兼具理论保证与实验有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal learning often suffers from modality imbalance, where the joint optimization process is dominated by a single modality. Existing methods typically estimate modality imbalance from score disparities derived from prediction uncertainty or optimization statistics. However, due to distinct prediction uncertainty and learning dynamics across modalities, direct comparison of such scores may misinterpret intrinsic modality differences as progress gaps, leading to biased imbalance estimation. In this paper, we propose Function-Space Guided Multimodal Optimization (FGMO), which leverages a function-space progress signal to assess modality-wise optimization progress and coordinate optimization across modalities to alleviate modality imbalance. Specifically, we introduce Functional Progress Estimation (FPE) to measure each modality's update-induced function-space response and calibrate it against a loss-aligned unimodal reference, producing a comparable progress signal. Based on this signal, Functional Response Control (FRC) redistributes modality-level function-space budgets and realizes the target responses through tensor-wise learning-rate adjustment. Theoretical analysis establishes a one-step target-contraction property of FRC under bounded controller-state mismatch, and extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of FGMO across multiple multimodal benchmarks.

</details>


### [354] [RIPPLE in Still Water: Zero-Shot Clustering in Federated Learning with Wavelet Scattering Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03054)
*Alessandro Licciardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RIPPLE，一种完全离线确定簇分配的聚类联邦学习框架，通信成本等同FedAvg，并能为训练中未出现的客户端单次前向传播获得个性化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类联邦学习方法在训练循环内通过梯度相似度、损失评估或EM式更新来发现簇结构，导致通信开销增加、梯度暴露于反转攻击，且无法为未参与训练的客户端分配簇。

Method: RIPPLE在联邦开始前，服务器端基于合成客户端群体训练的Gaussian Mixture VAE，对每个客户端本地数据进行频谱刻画：用Wavelet Scattering Transform嵌入方差加权的主成分原型，并离线解码得到簇分配。

Result: 理论证明RIPPLE的代理聚类目标与oracle之间的差距可由一个随客户端样本量衰减且与联邦时长无关的可计算量界定；每簇收敛匹配非凸光滑目标的minimax最优速率。在五个涵盖受控与真实异质性的基准上，RIPPLE持续优于所有基线，且在最真实的数据划分上优势更大。

Conclusion: RIPPLE通过完全离线的簇分配实现了与FedAvg相同的每轮通信成本，无需梯度计算、模型评估或额外通信轮次即可为未见客户端生成个性化模型，在理论与实验上均验证了其有效性与隐私优势。

Abstract: Clustered Federated Learning (FL) partitions a client population into groups of similar local distributions and trains one specialized model per cluster, mitigating client drift that degrades single-model methods under non-IID data. Prior methods discover cluster structure inside the training loop through gradient similarity, loss evaluation, or EM-style updates, thus increasing communication overhead, exposing gradients to inversion attacks, and providing no mechanism to assign clients absent from training. We propose RIPPLE, a clustered FL framework in which cluster assignment is computed entirely offline from a spectral characterization of each client's local data: a variance-weighted principal-component prototype embedded via the Wavelet Scattering Transform and decoded by a Gaussian Mixture VAE trained server-side on synthetic client populations before federation begins. Per-round communication cost matches FedAvg exactly, and a client absent from training obtains a personalized model from a single forward pass, without gradient computation, model evaluation, or extra communication round. We prove that the gap between RIPPLE's surrogate clustered objective and the oracle is bounded by a computable quantity decaying with client sample size and independent of federation duration; per-cluster convergence matches the minimax-optimal rate for non-convex smooth objectives. Across five benchmarks spanning controlled and realistic heterogeneity, RIPPLE consistently outperforms all baselines, with margins growing on the most realistic partitions.

</details>


### [355] [When Does Synthetic Relational Data Teach Models to Use Relations? Tracing Predictive Structure from Pretraining Data to Model Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03057)
*Shivam Dubey,Mohamed Bouadi,Nassim Bouarour,Varun Kulkarni,Aditya Tanna,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过数据归因方法，发现合成预训练数据中"跨表信息对掩码预测必要"这一性质能诱导关系型机制的产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系基础模型越来越多地使用合成数据库预训练，但下游基准无法说明为何某些合成语料能训练出更好的模型。强性能可能仅源于真实的行级统计，而模型未必学会使用关系结构，因此需要研究哪种数据性质能诱导关系计算。

Method: 将问题视为数据归因：用相同架构、初始化、目标和算力，在四个关系数据生成器产生的语料上训练四个 Relational Transformer 检查点，追踪数据中可测量性质到所学计算及下游行为的联系。假设当跨表信息对掩码单元预训练目标具有预测必要性时，关系机制才会涌现。

Result: RelDiff 从外键链接父表中获得最大的预测增益，其模型对未见数据库上的外键干预异常敏感。这种依赖通过随机初始化对照、随损坏链接比例单调增长，并定位于一条串行跨表通路。下游推理中破坏同一机制会消除 RelDiff 在关系任务上的优势，而结构不敏感模型几乎不受影响。

Conclusion: 研究将合成训练数据的一个性质与学到的机制联系起来，并通过干预连接到下游行为，为理解合成数据如何诱导关系计算提供了因果证据。

Abstract: Relational foundation models are increasingly pretrained on synthetic databases, yet downstream benchmarks reveal little about why one synthetic corpus produces a better model than another. In particular, strong performance may arise from realistic row-level statistics without the model ever learning to use relational structure. We study this as a data-attribution problem: which property of synthetic pretraining data induces relational computation? Using four Relational Transformer checkpoints trained with the same architecture, initialization, objective, and compute budget on corpora produced by four relational data generators, we trace a measurable property of the data to learned computation and downstream behavior. We hypothesize that relational mechanisms emerge when cross-table information is predictively necessary for the masked-cell pretraining objective. RelDiff exhibits by far the largest predictive gain from foreign-key-linked parents, and its corresponding model is uniquely sensitive to foreign-key interventions on unseen databases. This dependence survives a random-initialization control, grows monotonically with the fraction of corrupted links, and localizes to a serial cross-table pathway. Finally, disrupting the same mechanism during downstream inference removes RelDiff's advantage on relational tasks while leaving structure-insensitive models nearly unchanged. These results connect a property of synthetic training data to a learned mechanism and, through intervention, to downstream behavior.

</details>


### [356] [Learning Transferable Policies from Action-free Time Series Through Dynamical Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03065)
*Niklas Emonds,Georgia Koppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分层基于模型的强化学习框架，利用相关系统间的共享结构，从无动作记录中学习系统特异性控制策略，实现可迁移控制与机制性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从无动作记录中学习控制十分困难，因为干预效果无法被观测，且策略可能利用重建动力学中的误差。作者希望借助相关系统间的共享结构来解决这一问题。

Method: 构建分层动力学系统重建模型，通过低维嵌入捕获共享动力学与个体差异；复用这些嵌入参数化共享策略与价值网络，将重建动力学差异与控制差异关联。策略完全通过仿真训练，采用显式干预模型并加入加性潜在扰动。使用分段线性循环神经网络支持受控动力学的机制性分析，并通过解码器约束使干预的即时效应在观测空间中可解释，还支持在保护一种模态的同时对另一模态施加干预。

Result: 在Lorenz-63与双摆系统上，分层策略相比独立训练策略提升了迁移性能。在Lorenz-63上，其平均奖励高于使用相同重建模型的重复规划方法，与用受控交互训练的方法表现相当，并且仅经嵌入推断即可泛化到策略训练中未出现的系统。应用于神经-行为记录时，在受限神经扰动下展示了预测运动的抑制。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，共享动力学表征能够支持可迁移控制，并可从无动作记录中生成机制性假设。

Abstract: Learning control from action-free recordings is challenging because intervention effects are unobserved and policies may exploit errors in reconstructed dynamics. We present a hierarchical model-based reinforcement learning framework that uses shared structure across related systems to learn system-specific control policies from action-free recordings. A hierarchical dynamical system reconstruction model captures shared dynamics and individual variation through low-dimensional embeddings. These embeddings are then reused to parameterize shared policy and value networks, linking differences in reconstructed dynamics to differences in control. Policies are trained entirely via simulation under an explicit intervention model with additive latent perturbations. Piecewise-linear recurrent neural networks enable mechanistic analyses of the controlled dynamics, while decoder-based constraints make the immediate effects of interventions interpretable in observation space and permit interventions on one modality while protecting another from direct manipulation. On Lorenz-63 and double-pendulum systems, hierarchical policies improve transfer over independently trained policies. On Lorenz-63, they also achieve a higher mean reward than repeated planning with the same reconstructed models, perform comparably to methods trained with controlled interactions, and generalize to systems absent from policy training after embedding inference alone. Applications to neural-behavioral recordings demonstrate suppression of predicted movement under constrained neural perturbations. Together, these findings show how shared dynamical representations support transferable control and mechanistic hypothesis generation from action-free recordings.

</details>


### [357] [Learn Feasibility Once, Optimize All Objectives: Derivative-Free Diffusion Models for Chance-Constrained Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03071)
*Ziwen Liu,Yan Liu,Congying Han,Tiande Guo,Yao Yan,Weichen Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出D³Opt，一种无导数扩散框架，将机会约束建模与目标优化解耦：先冻结学习约束可行先验，再用退火粒子Feynman-Kac校正优化后指定目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机会约束规划在非凸/非光滑目标优化上困难，且传统与学习方法在固定机会约束下难以适配不同目标，往往需要针对目标重新训练。

Method: 仅用约束筛选后的决策训练风险条件扩散模型，学习与目标无关的机会可行结构并冻结为先验；推理时在冻结反向扩散过程中引入退火、基于粒子的Feynman-Kac校正，仅靠函数评估优化后指定目标，实现无导数优化。

Result: 理论上证明校正在冻结先验满足可行性时保持可行性，并给出分离先验覆盖、有限粒子近似与有限温度效应的优化误差界；实验在线性高斯机会约束、目标迁移任务及机会约束经济调度上验证了对光滑与非光滑目标（含非凸情形）的有效优化，以及固定机会约束下无需重训练的目标泛化。

Conclusion: D³Opt通过将约束建模与目标优化解耦，实现了固定机会约束下对多类后指定目标的无导数、免重训练优化，兼具可行性保证与误差分析。

Abstract: Chance-constrained programs (CCPs) optimize decisions under uncertainty by limiting the probability of constraint violation. Despite advances in traditional and learning-based approaches, optimizing non-convex or non-smooth objectives and adapting to different objectives under fixed chance constraints remain challenging. In this paper, we propose a \textbf{D}erivative-free \textbf{D}iffusion-based framework that \textbf{D}isentangles constraint modeling from objective optimization, termed \textbf{D$^3$Opt}. We learn the chance-feasible structure once, independently of any particular objective, by training a risk-conditioned diffusion model solely on constraint-filtered decisions and freezing it as a reusable prior for post-specified objectives. At inference time, we propose an annealed, particle-based Feynman--Kac correction along the frozen reverse diffusion process to optimize post-specified objectives using only function evaluations. This enables derivative-free optimization of non-convex and non-smooth objectives without objective-specific retraining. We prove that the correction preserves feasibility when this property holds for the frozen prior, and derive an optimization-error bound separating learned-prior coverage, finite-particle approximation, and finite-temperature effects. Experiments on linear Gaussian CCPs, objective-transfer tasks, and chance-constrained economic dispatch demonstrate effective optimization across smooth and non-smooth objectives, including non-convex cases, and objective generalization under fixed chance constraints without retraining.

</details>


### [358] [Smart Sensing for Safer Bridges: From Sensor Signals to AI-Driven Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03082)
*Rahul Jaiswal,Joakim Hellum,Halvor Heiberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对真实桥梁传感器数据，采用信号处理与Isolation Forest机器学习两种互补方法进行异常检测，并在挪威某桥梁iBridge设备采集的实时数据上评估其检测特性与计算需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桥梁对交通连通性和城市发展至关重要，可靠的桥梁监测对保障公共安全和及早发现结构异常十分关键。因此需要研究真实桥梁传感器数据中的异常检测方法。

Method: 使用两种互补方法：基于信号处理的方法和数据驱动的机器学习模型Isolation Forest。数据来自安装在挪威某桥梁上的iBridge传感器设备所采集的实时桥梁传感器数据。评估指标包括异常计数、异常检测时间、处理速率、异常率、可视化以及时间一致性；此外还进行了受控的异常注入分析以评估各方法的敏感性。

Result: 数值结果展示出两种方法各具不同的检测特性和计算需求；机器学习（尤其是数据驱动的Isolation Forest）结合信号处理在识别桥梁传感器测量异常方面具有潜力。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的Isolation Forest与信号处理方法可互补地用于桥梁传感器测量的异常检测，具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Bridges contribute significantly to transportation connectivity and urban development. Therefore, reliable bridge monitoring is crucial for protecting public safety and detecting anomalous behavior in bridge sensor data that may provide early indications of abnormal structural conditions. This paper investigates anomaly detection in real-world bridge sensor data using two different complementary approaches, namely signal processing and the data-driven machine learning model Isolation Forest. The real-time bridge sensor data is collected from an iBridge sensor device installed on a bridge in Norway. The methods are evaluated using anomaly counts, anomaly detection time, processing rate, anomaly rates, visualization, and temporal agreement. Moreover, a controlled anomaly-injection analysis is performed to evaluate the sensitivity of each method. Numerical results demonstrate distinct detection characteristics and computational requirements, highlighting the potential of machine learning, particularly the data-driven Isolation Forest, alongside signal processing for identifying anomalies in bridge sensor measurements.

</details>


### [359] [Light Entropic Optimal Transport on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03085)
*Xavier Aramayo-Carrasco,Petr Mokrov,Alexander Korotin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ManifoldLightOT，一种在常见流形上直接学习核诱导熵最优传输耦合的轻量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵最优传输（EOT）已广泛用于学习复杂分布间的随机耦合，应用包括生成建模与域适应。但多数EOT求解器面向欧氏空间，流形扩展有限，且常依赖昂贵的迭代方法、模拟动力学或通用神经模型，未能充分利用底层几何结构。

Method: 利用EOT解的核形式，为球面、环面、SO(3)和SE(3)构造几何特定的Gibbs核及兼容的势参数化，从而获得闭式归一化和可直接采样的条件分布；该形式可自然扩展到流形乘积；势参数通过蒙特卡洛估计的学习目标直接从样本优化。

Result: 在合成和真实世界实验中，ManifoldLightOT通常优于现有流形OT方法，同时保留直接采样的能力。

Conclusion: ManifoldLightOT为流形上的熵最优传输提供了一种高效、几何感知且可直接采样的学习框架，并可推广至更复杂几何结构。

Abstract: Entropic Optimal Transport (EOT) has become a practical framework for learning stochastic couplings between complex distributions, with applications in generative modeling and domain adaptation. However, most EOT solvers are designed for Euclidean spaces, while manifold extensions remain limited and often rely on costly iterative methods, simulated dynamics, or generic neural models that do not fully exploit the underlying geometry. We introduce ManifoldLightOT, a light approach for learning kernel-induced EOT couplings directly on common manifolds. Using the kernel form of the EOT solution, we construct geometry-specific Gibbs kernels together with compatible potential parameterizations for spheres, tori, $\mathrm{SO}(3)$, and $\mathrm{SE}(3)$. These choices yield closed-form normalization and directly sampleable conditional distributions. Our formulation naturally extends to products of manifolds, making it applicable to more complex geometries. The parameters of the potentials are optimized directly from samples using Monte Carlo estimates of the learning objective. Through synthetic and real-world experiments, we show that ManifoldLightOT often outperforms existing manifold OT methods while retaining direct sampling.

</details>


### [360] [Zephon: Elastic Determinism for Online, Stateful Foundation Model Data Loading Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03087)
*Maximilian Böther,Josh Wills,Ties Robroek,Sonnet Xu,Paul Burstein,Daniel Zayas,Cody Blakeney,Siddharth Joshi,Haoli Yin,Rishabh Adiga,Haakon Mongstad,Luke Merrick,Pratyush Maini,Ari Morcos,Matthew Leavitt,Ana Klimovic,Bogdan Gaza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Zephon 是一个面向基础模型的数据加载器，支持在线有状态流水线，同时提供弹性确定性与高效断点恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型研究者需要确信跨昂贵消融实验观察到的差异源于所改参数而非训练数据序列的非确定性。数据加载器必须提供弹性确定性：在 GPU 拓扑变化、频繁断点恢复、不同数据处理后端下，仍保持全局训练批次序列确定。现有加载器多假设可索引的 1-to-1 流水线，而在线 tokenize、pack、mix 引入有状态 n-to-n 变换，破坏样本索引；离线物化方案昂贵，对视频等模态甚至不可行。

Method: Zephon 将全局流划分为与拓扑无关的 lane，串行化排序决策，同时在可互换后端上并行化无状态工作，并且仅对有限量的在途状态做检查点，使恢复成本不随训练进度增长。

Result: 在文本与视觉-语言工作负载上评估，Zephon 在保持有竞争力吞吐的同时，提供了现有加载器对在线有状态流水线所不具备的保证组合。

Conclusion: Zephon 为在线有状态数据流水线实现了弹性确定性与高效断点恢复，兼顾吞吐，适用于基础模型训练。

Abstract: Deterministic data loading is important for foundation model development: model researchers need confidence that differences they observe across costly ablations are caused by the parameter they changed rather than non-determinism in the training data sequence. The data loader must provide elastic determinism, i.e., a deterministic sequence of global training data batches despite changes to the GPU topology across runs (e.g., due to GPU scarcity), frequent checkpoint-resume cycles, and different data processing execution backends. Achieving this is difficult because modern foundation model data pipelines tokenize, pack, and mix samples online, introducing stateful n-to-m transformations that break sample indexing. Existing data loaders largely assume indexable 1-to-1 pipelines, and the common workaround of offline materialization is expensive and, for some modalities such as video, infeasible.
  We present Zephon, a data loader for foundation models that supports online, stateful pipelines while providing elastic determinism and efficient resumption from checkpoints. It partitions the global stream into topology-independent lanes, serializes ordering decisions while parallelizing stateless work on interchangeable backends, and checkpoints only bounded in-flight state so recovery cost does not grow with training progress. We evaluate Zephon on text and vision-language workloads and show that it achieves competitive throughput while providing a combination of guarantees that no existing loader offers for online, stateful pipelines.

</details>


### [361] [ULTRADISCOVERY: Abductive Exploration in an Interconnected, Epistemically Open Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03092)
*Weihan Li,Tianshi Zheng,Yangqiu Song,Ginny Y. Wong,Simon See*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 ULTRADISCOVERY 交互式基准，含五个领域，通过 2×2 设计分离"表征开放性"与"证据分布性"两个变量，考察智能体在溯因式科学发现中修正理论并预测跨域干预结果的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现常常始于零散线索，需要构建新的世界表征并进行溯因探索。这种探索面临两重要求：一是在认识论开放时构造推出解释所需的表征，二是在结构互联时整合跨情境分散的证据。现有基准很少区分这两种要求，也无法独立控制它们，因此难以定位智能体发现能力的瓶颈。

Method: 构建 ULTRADISCOVERY：一个包含五个领域的交互式世界。智能体需修正一个初始成功的理论，并预测未见过的跨域干预结果。采用 2×2 设计：表征或保持开放或直接披露，证据或分散或对齐，同时固定潜在动力学。测试十一个模型，并考察两个厂商-框架系统在更多领域中的表现。

Result: 表征开放时，十一个模型常撤回被教授的公理，但无一引入未被观测的实体或改写替换所需的变量。披露表征使干预请求增至三倍，并多获得约十八项发现中的一项，证据对齐的增益更小。两个厂商-框架系统将发现拓展到更多领域，其中一个在开放场景中改写了变量。没有任何系统能在 200 次付费动作内做出精确预测；在更大预算下，同时使用两种辅助手段时出现一次精确预测，而所有开放场景仍不精确。

Conclusion: 困难的关键在于从"积累证据"到"将证据组合成可迁移表征"这一步。表征开放性与证据分布性是相互独立的挑战，现有系统在此核心环节上仍存在明显不足。

Abstract: Scientific discovery often begins when scattered clues call for a new way of describing the world. Such abductive exploration can require constructing the representation in which an explanation is stated, when the world is epistemically open, and composing evidence scattered across contexts, when it is structurally interconnected. Existing benchmarks rarely separate these two demands or control them independently. We introduce ULTRADISCOVERY, an interactive world of five domains in which an agent revises an initially successful theory and predicts the outcome of an unseen cross-domain intervention. A $2 \times 2$ design leaves the representation open or discloses it, and leaves the evidence distributed or aligns it, with the latent dynamics fixed. With the representation open, agents across eleven models often retract the axiom they were taught, and none introduces the unobserved entity or rewrites the variables that a replacement requires. Disclosure triples intervention requests and adds about one of the eighteen findings the world affords, and alignment adds less. Two vendor-harness systems carry discovery into more domains, and one of them rewrites the variables in Open episodes. No system makes the exact prediction within 200 paid actions. At larger budgets one exact prediction appears with both aids, while every Open episode remains inexact. The results locate the difficulty in the step from accumulating evidence to composing it into a representation that transfers.

</details>


### [362] [LS-AR: Future-Predictive Latent Steering in Autoregressive LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03093)
*Anubha Gupta,Eduardo Pignatelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双通道架构LS-AR，用FiLM将连续目标引导与离散token解码解耦，在长程检索与规划任务中显著优于单通道基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归模型将高层任务指令、状态历史与瞬时token混在同一共享序列中，缺乏将宏观目标与上下文噪声隔离的架构机制，存在单通道局限。

Method: 设计Latent-Steered Autoregressive（LS-AR）双通道架构，通过FiLM条件化把连续目标引导与离散token解码分离；评估静态目标编码器P_0（长期宏观目标保持）与动态状态追踪器P_t（生成中循环隐状态更新）两种变体。

Result: 长程检索（H=1024, W=500）中LS-AR(Static)达100%目标召回，参数匹配基线为0%；吞吐提升约35%，峰值VRAM降低52.8%。Blocksworld强制扰动规划(k=1)完成率89.0% vs 基线71.0%；但零样本实体扩展(N→N+1)失败(0%)，暴露单向量容量瓶颈。

Conclusion: 双通道权威性分析表明文本目标dropout可形成隐向量主导控制，为文本提示注入提供结构性防御，但同时引入隐向量攻击面；单向量容量仍限制零样本扩展能力。

Abstract: Standard autoregressive (AR) models process high-level task instructions, state history, and transient tokens within a single shared sequence of tokens. Consequently, they lack the architectural mechanisms needed to isolate macro-objectives from context noise. To overcome this single-channel limitation, we introduce Latent-Steered Autoregressive (LS-AR), a dual-channel architecture that decouples continuous goal steering from discrete token decoding via FiLM conditioning. We evaluate a Static Goal Encoder (P_0) for persistent macro-objective retention across long rollouts and a Dynamic State Tracker (P_t) for recurrent latent updates during generation. On long-horizon retrieval past context limits (H=1024, W=500), LS-AR (Static) achieves 100% target recall where parameter-matched baselines collapse (0%), while increasing throughput by ~35% and cutting peak VRAM by 52.8%. In Blocksworld planning under forced perturbations (k=1), LS-AR (Dynamic) sustains an 89.0% completion rate vs. 71.0% for the baseline, though zero-shot entity scaling (N -> N+1) exposes single-vector capacity limits (0%). Finally, dual-channel authority analysis shows that text goal dropout establishes latent-dominant control, offering structural defence against text prompt injection while introducing a latent vector attack surface.

</details>


### [363] [Exploring the Trade-Off Between Structured Pruning and Fault Tolerance in Deep Neural Networks for Space Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03117)
*Toon Vinck,Naïn Jonckers,Jaro De Roose,Jeffrey Prinzie,Peter Karsmakers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了模型宽度与单粒子翻转（SEU）鲁棒性的关系，发现结构化剪枝虽提高单次推理的故障敏感性，但缩短的执行时间降低了遭遇SEU的概率，二者相互抵消，故剪枝可在不损害整体可靠性的前提下节省能耗与时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN因分布式信息表示而对位级故障具有一定鲁棒性，且理论上模型越宽信息越分散、单个位故障影响越小。然而模型宽度与SEU鲁棒性之间的实际关系尚缺乏系统性的实证研究，本文旨在填补这一空白，并为航天场景下鲁棒AI系统设计提供指导。

Method: 对基线模型进行迭代式结构化剪枝以逐级减小宽度，同时尽量保持任务性能；在每个剪枝阶段开展有针对性的故障注入实验，评估模型在模拟位翻转场景下的表现。

Result: 结构化剪枝因减少冗余而提高了单次推理对故障的敏感性，但这一负面效应被更短的执行时间（降低遭遇SEU的概率）有效抵消。

Conclusion: 结构化剪枝可在不牺牲整体可靠性的情况下带来显著的能耗与时延节省，为面向空间应用的鲁棒AI系统设计提供了有价值的指导。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) inherently exhibit a degree of robustness to bit-level faults due to their distributed representation of information. As a model increases in width, this information becomes more dispersed, theoretically reducing the impact of any single bit fault. In this paper, we empirically investigate the relationship between model width and robustness to Single Event Upsets (SEUs). We conduct a comprehensive experiment in which baseline models undergo iterative structured pruning to reduce their width while preserving task performance as much as possible. At each pruning stage, we run a targeted fault-injection campaign to evaluate the model's performance under simulated bit-flip scenarios. Our results show that, although structured pruning increases per-inference sensitivity to faults by reducing redundancy, this effect is effectively counterbalanced by shorter execution time, which lowers the probability of encountering an SEU. These findings suggest that structured pruning can yield significant energy and latency savings without compromising overall reliability, providing useful guidance for designing robust AI systems for space applications.

</details>


### [364] [How to Find and Reuse Policies for Continuous Adaptation in Lifelong Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03119)
*Saptarshi Nath,Inish M. D'Souza,Antonio Carta,Soheil Kolouri,Andrea Soltoggio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应掩码选择与组合（AMSC）方法，通过在线估计任务相似度来选择并加权先前的策略，用于终身强化学习中的知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在终身强化学习中，仅保留先前策略不足以有效迁移到新任务。有用知识可能分散在多个先前策略中，且其相关性会随学习经验变化。作者假设任务相似度可用于持续学习场景，以找到并组合先前学习的策略。

Method: AMSC 通过基于状态-动作-奖励样本的非参数 Wasserstein 任务嵌入，从在线经验中估计相似度；对相似度分数进行 z-score 归一化后的 sparsemax 处理，得到可变大小的支持集，用于周期性地选择并加权策略，形成学习新任务时的先验。

Result: 在 CT-graph 和 MiniGrid 上，AMSC 的平均性能和前向迁移均高于所评估的模块化组合基线，且无遗忘。在 Continual World 上的结果表明，识别相关先验知识及其层级特定组合可能需要额外的层级特定调优。消融实验显示，选择相关来源和确定复用强度是收益的关键。独立测量的成对迁移与任务嵌入相似度呈正相关。

Conclusion: 任务相似度可成为终身强化学习中用于选择和加权特定知识以复用的有效准则。

Abstract: In lifelong reinforcement learning, retaining previously learned policies is not sufficient for effective transfer to a new task. Useful knowledge may be distributed across several prior policies, and its relevance may change as the learner acquires experience. One hypothesis is that task similarity can be effectively used in a continual learning setting to find and combine previously learned policies. To test it, Adaptive Mask Selection and Composition (AMSC) is designed to estimate similarity from online experience via non-parametric Wasserstein task embeddings from state-action-reward samples. The z-score-normalized sparsemax of the similarity scores are used to derive a variable-size support to periodically choose and weight policies to form a prior when learning a new task. On CT-graph and MiniGrid, AMSC achieves higher mean performance and forward transfer than the evaluated modular composition baselines while exhibiting no forgetting. Results on Continual World suggest that identifying relevant prior knowledge and determining its layer-specific composition may require additional layer-specific tuning. Ablations show that selecting relevant sources and determining how strongly to reuse them are central to these gains. Independently measured pairwise transfer is also positively associated with task-embedding similarity. These results indicate that task similarity can be an effective criterion to select and weight specific knowledge for reuse in lifelong reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [365] [Coverage You Can Steer: Online Conformal Calibration for RL-Driven Hardware-Aware NAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03127)
*Pedro Brandimarte,Nerea Aranjuelo,Marcos Nieto,Oihana Otaegui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用在线共形推断替代一次性分位数校准，使硬件感知NAS中RL搜索循环内的候选剪枝覆盖率可控：请求目标覆盖率可单调、可复现地达到（误差约10^-3），剪枝25-50%评估且精度无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件感知神经架构搜索（NAS）的主要开销在于评估成本：每个架构都必须训练后才能获得回报。共形预测过滤器可通过剪枝预测回报上界未达阈值的候选来降低成本，并给出“最多δ比例被错误丢弃”的无分布保证。但该保证依赖校准集与测试候选之间的可交换性，而这被外层强化学习循环破坏：随着搜索进行，策略提出的架构不断改进；在逐层构建时，每个episode内部候选分布也在变化。因此静态一次性校准会失去对覆盖率的控制。

Method: 将一次性分位数估计替换为在线反馈控制，采用自适应共形推断（Adaptive Conformal Inference）及其无需调参、局部自适应、群组条件等变体，从而在任意序列上恢复可操纵的覆盖率。在三种神经网络架构族、单步与顺序搜索（三个随机种子）上验证，并将同一乐观上界用作采集函数在一个带约束测试台上与随机搜索对比。

Result: 所提方法能将任意请求的覆盖率水平追踪到约10^-3的误差内，同时剪枝25-50%的评估且未观察到精度损失；相比之下静态校准失去覆盖率控制，高斯过程基线无论请求如何都过于保守。作为采集函数使用时，同样的乐观上界优于随机搜索，这一增益固定乐观策略本身即可获得，而在线校准进一步将其放大。

Conclusion: 在RL驱动的硬件感知NAS中，在线自适应共形推断能够恢复分布无关的覆盖率保证，使评估预算的剪枝比例可精确调控且不损失精度，是对静态共形过滤的有效替代；代码已开源。

Abstract: Hardware-aware neural architecture search (NAS) is dominated by evaluation cost: every architecture must be trained before its reward is known. Conformal-prediction filters cut this cost by pruning candidates whose predicted-reward upper bound misses a threshold, with a distribution-free guarantee that at most a fraction $δ$ are wrongly discarded. That guarantee assumes exchangeability between calibration and test candidates, which the surrounding reinforcement-learning (RL) loop violates: the policy's proposals improve as search proceeds and, in layer-by-layer construction, shift within every episode. We replace one-shot quantile estimation with online feedback control (Adaptive Conformal Inference, with tuning-free, locally-adaptive, and group-conditional variants), restoring steerable coverage: dialing the target delivers it, monotonically and reproducibly, for arbitrary sequences. Across three neural-network architecture families and both single-step and sequential search (three seeds), it tracks every requested level to within ${\sim}10^{-3}$ while pruning 25-50% of evaluations at no measured accuracy cost, whereas static calibration loses control of its coverage and a Gaussian-process baseline stays conservative regardless of the request. Finally, used as an acquisition function on one constrained testbed, the same optimistic bound beats random search, a gain that fixed optimism already carries and online calibration sharpens. The source code is available at https://github.com/Vicomtech/rl-hw-nas.

</details>


### [366] [Page-EntroKV: Hardware-Aligned, Entropy-Weighted KV-Cache Eviction under Grouped-Query Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03135)
*Inbasekaran S*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Page-EntroKV是一种面向GQA服务的KV缓存淘汰框架，以（层、组、页）硬件粒度执行淘汰，用sink隔离的Renyi-2熵加权池化组内头，避免并集开销并保持检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文自回归模型服务受KV缓存限制。现有动态淘汰方法按每个查询头独立打分选token，不适配GQA：多个查询头共享一个物理KV缓冲区，各自选择的分歧迫使引擎保留并集，缓存膨胀可达组比r；而算术平均池化会稀释承担事实召回的专业检索头。

Method: 提出Page-EntroKV框架。组内头用基于sink隔离碰撞（Renyi-2）熵推导的权重池化，每头仅一次内积，在prefill时计算一次、无需校准，使sink头不能冒充检索头。池化得分投影到PagedAttention页帧，淘汰在硬件元组（层、组、页）上执行。形式化了并集开销比（UOR）和组内分歧，证明二元组下二者的精确恒等式及各组比下的双侧界，证明严格预算保持与有限上下文needle保留界（算术平均池化可证违反），并给出逐层精确页核算。

Result: 在试点架构（Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct，r=6）上，对2,240组测量做头独立回放，2%预算下并集开销高达4.75x，而Page-EntroKV的UOR恰为1.000；sink隔离消除了13x的sink冒充；20%预算下needle召回为100%（平均池化为0%）；各页大小下保留基数精确；20%保留率下QA和代码任务仍可解。

Conclusion: Page-EntroKV在GQA实际分配的粒度上进行KV淘汰，理论上保证预算保持与检索保留，并在实验中显著优于按头独立选择与算术平均池化。

Abstract: Serving long-context autoregressive language models is constrained by the key-value (KV) cache. Most dynamic eviction methods score token importance per query head and choose tokens independently. This fits poorly with grouped-query attention (GQA), where several query heads share one physical KV buffer: divergent per-head selections force the serving engine to retain the union of their choices - inflating the cache by up to the group ratio r - while arithmetic mean pooling dilutes the specialized retrieval heads that carry factual recall. We introduce Page-EntroKV, a formal framework for KV-cache eviction operating at the granularity GQA serving actually allocates. Heads within each physical group are pooled by weights derived from sink-isolated collision (Renyi-2) entropy - one inner product per head, computed once at prefill with no calibration - so sink heads cannot masquerade as retrieval heads. Pooled scores are projected onto PagedAttention page frames, and eviction executes at the hardware tuple (layer, group, page). We formalize the union overhead ratio (UOR) and intra-group disagreement, prove an exact identity linking them for two-head groups alongside two-sided bounds at every group ratio, prove strict budget preservation and a finite-context needle-retention bound that arithmetic mean pooling provably violates, and give exact per-layer page accounting. On a pilot architecture (Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, r=6), head-independent replay over 2,240 group measurements yields union overhead up to 4.75x at a 2% budget, while Page-EntroKV holds UOR exactly 1.000; sink isolation removes a 13x sink masquerade; needle recall is 100% versus 0% for mean pooling at a 20% budget; retained cardinality is exact for every page size; and QA and code tasks remain solvable at 20% retention.

</details>


### [367] [Sample complexity of variance-reduced policy gradient: weaker assumptions and lower bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03165)
*Gabor Paczolay,Matteo Papini,Alberto Maria Metelli,Istvan Harmati,Marcello Restelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Defensive Policy Gradient（防御性策略梯度）算法，基于防御性重要性采样，无需对普通重要性权重方差作任何假设，即可达到 O(ε^{-3}) 的样本复杂度，并从下界角度证明该加速率是本质最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有基于重要性采样的方差缩减 REINFORCE 变体虽能达到 O(ε^{-3}) 样本复杂度，但依赖于关于重要性权重方差的不现实假设。本文旨在去除该假设，同时从理论下界角度说明更快收敛率的来源与最优性。

Method: 提出 Defensive Policy Gradient（防御性策略梯度）算法，采用防御性重要性采样；并构建一个隐藏状态与动作、允许参数依赖奖励的广义黑盒策略优化模型，用于建立下界分析。

Result: 在无重要性权重方差假设下，新算法达到 O(ε^{-3}) 样本复杂度。广义黑盒模型中，有界方差单策略反馈的最优率为 Θ(ε^{-4})，均方光滑耦合双策略反馈的最优率为 Θ(ε^{-3})。在标准策略正则条件下，REINFORCE 与本文算法分别实现 O(ε^{-4}) 与 O(ε^{-3}) 上界，与相应 oracle 条件对应。

Conclusion: 尽管下界并不直接适用于这些算法实际运行的经典 MDP 交互模型，但其对应关系在 oracle 层面提供了证据：防御性策略梯度的更快收敛率是最优的，且与普通策略梯度存在本质分离。

Abstract: Several variance-reduced versions of REINFORCE based on importance sampling achieve an improved $O(ε^{-3})$ sample complexity to find an $ε$-stationary point, under an unrealistic assumption on the variance of the importance weights. In this paper, we propose the \algo (Defensive Policy Gradient) algorithm, based on defensive importance sampling, which achieves the same rate without any assumption on the variance of ordinary importance weights. We also establish lower bounds in a generalized black-box policy-optimization model that hides states and actions and permits parameter-dependent rewards. In this model, the optimal rates are $Θ(ε^{-4})$ with bounded-variance one-policy feedback and $Θ(ε^{-3})$ with mean-square-smooth coupled two-policy feedback. Under standard policy-regularity conditions, REINFORCE and \algo realize the corresponding oracle conditions and attain the $O(ε^{-4})$ and $O(ε^{-3})$ upper bounds, respectively. Although the lower bounds do not apply directly to the classical MDP interaction model in which these algorithms operate, this correspondence provides oracle-level evidence that the faster rate of \algo is optimal and genuinely separated from that of vanilla policy gradient.

</details>


### [368] [Predicting and Repairing Merge Collapse in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03199)
*Jungseob Lee,Seungyoon Lee,Sugyeong Eo,Hyeonseok Moon,Jaehyung Seo,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用专家任务向量间的方差（干扰度）作为预合并评分，可在评估前预测模型合并崩溃，并据此设计PRISM算子修复崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享基座微调的大模型可通过平均任务向量合并，但部分合并在评估前会严重崩溃，而现有合并算子无法提前预警。作者希望找到一个能同时预测并修复崩溃的统计量。

Method: 作者证明平均所移除的功率等于专家间任务向量的方差，将其定义为干扰度。在噪声模型下，合并引入的扰动随合并系数和干扰度增长，形成预合并评分。基于此提出PRISM算子：先平均任务向量，再按每层干扰度水平对每层做软阈值处理。仅在评分超阈值时使用PRISM，否则保留普通平均。

Result: 在四个模型家族的22种合并配置中，仅破坏性合并超过评分阈值。符号冲突统计（现有算子常用目标）具有反预测性。作者在评估前预测14次合并结果，其中12次正确，包括持续预训练使专家对越过阈值导致破坏性结果。PRISM无需数据或调参，使全部5次破坏性合并保持在阈值以上，与基座模型评估噪声相当；而普通平均至少低14.4分或完全崩溃。

Conclusion: 任务向量方差是预测和修复模型合并崩溃的有效统计量，PRISM能仅在必要时修复破坏性合并，同时保留对无害合并的普通平均，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned from a shared base can be merged by averaging their task vectors, but some merges collapse far below the base model, and common merge operators give no warning before evaluation. We show that one statistic of the specialists' task vectors both predicts this collapse and calibrates its repair. The power that averaging removes equals the variance of the task vectors across specialists, our measure of interference. Under a working noise model, the disturbance that a merge injects grows with the merge coefficient and with interference, yielding a pre-merge score. In our experiments on twenty-two merge configurations from four model families, only destructive merges exceed a threshold on this score. We find that statistics of sign conflict between specialists, a common target of existing merge operators, are anti-predictive. We then predicted the outcomes of fourteen merges before evaluating them, and twelve predictions were correct, including the destructive outcome of a specialist pair pushed past the threshold by continued pretraining. To address this collapse, we introduce PRISM, an operator that averages the task vectors first and then soft-thresholds each layer at a level set by the layer's interference. Without data or tuning, PRISM keeps all five destructive merges above the threshold within evaluation noise of the base model, where plain averaging falls at least 14.4 points below it or collapses entirely. We apply PRISM only above the threshold and keep the plain average for merges below it, which include all fifteen harmless ones. Code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/PRISM.

</details>


### [369] [HyperFuse: Fast Self-Supervised Node Embeddings for Attributed Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03211)
*Megha P,Harshit Kumar,Srajan Agarwal,Anirban Banerjee,Olaf Wolkenhauer,Saptarshi Bej*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyperFuse是一种无需标签的快速超图表示学习流水线，通过谱松弛、特征摘要与轻量编码器实现高效嵌入生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督超图表示学习方法通常需要训练数百轮次的深层编码器，即使只有几千个节点的超图嵌入生成成本也很高，限制了需要为大量或演化超图生成嵌入的应用场景。

Method: HyperFuse包含三步：(i) 通过最大化超图模块度的谱松弛计算结构节点坐标，使用Banerjee超图邻接和免矩阵算子，代价与节点-超边关联数线性相关；(ii) 在特征与成员掩码下基于成员稳定性构建多尺度特征摘要并分配有界效用权重给超边；(iii) 使用不变性-去相关目标训练轻量级效用加权超图编码器100轮次。

Result: 在九个公开超图上与TriCL、SE-HSSL、VilLain、HypeBoy比较，使用六种下游分类器和k-means聚类。在全部方法完成的八个数据集上，HyperFuse平均每个数据集只需8.7秒，实现13-179倍几何平均加速。在六种分类器中五种取得最高平均准确率，分类和聚类性能与TriCL、SE-HSSL无显著差异。相比HypeBoy快13倍，准确率高2.1-4.1个百分点。

Conclusion: HyperFuse为快速、重复的超图嵌入生成提供了一种实用方法。

Abstract: Self-supervised hypergraph representation learning can produce informative node embeddings, but existing methods often require deep encoders trained for hundreds of epochs, making embedding generation costly even for hypergraphs with a few thousand nodes. This limits applications requiring embeddings for many or evolving hypergraphs. We present HyperFuse, a label-free pipeline for fast hypergraph representation learning. HyperFuse (i) computes structural node coordinates by maximizing a spectral relaxation of hypergraph modularity using Banerjee's hypergraph adjacency and a matrix-free operator with cost linear in node-hyperedge incidences; (ii) constructs multi-scale feature summaries and assigns bounded utility weights to hyperedges based on member stability under feature and membership masking; and (iii) trains a lightweight utility-weighted hypergraph encoder for 100 epochs using an invariance-decorrelation objective. We compare HyperFuse with TriCL, SE-HSSL, VilLain, and HypeBoy on nine public hypergraphs using six downstream classifiers and k-means clustering. On the eight datasets where all methods completed, HyperFuse required 8.7 s per dataset on average, achieving 13-179x geometric-mean speed-ups over the baselines. It achieved the highest average accuracy with five of six classifiers, while classification and clustering performance was not significantly different from TriCL and SE-HSSL. Compared with HypeBoy, HyperFuse was 13x faster and 2.1-4.1 percentage points more accurate across all classifiers. HyperFuse provides a practical approach for fast, repeated hypergraph embedding generation.

</details>


### [370] [Kernel Singular Value Decomposition with Extension to Multiple Data Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03216)
*Xinjie Zeng,Qinghua Tao,Johan Suykens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将核奇异值分解（KSVD）从双数据源扩展到多数据源，提出eKSVD，实现非对称核上的联合非线性特征学习，并通过拉格朗日对偶与神经网络显式映射两种视角求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KSVD只能处理由两个数据源诱导的非对称核矩阵（如自注意力中的query与key、矩阵的行与列），无法应对多个数据源的联合特征学习需求，因此需要将其扩展到多数据源场景。

Method: 提出eKSVD，在原始形式中联合学习每个数据源对应的投影以捕获最大信息并引入成对耦合；借助拉格朗日函数及KKT条件，将优化转化为对偶问题，得到KSVD中Lanczos分解定理位移特征值问题的推广；进一步推导基于协方差的框架，并用神经网络实现显式特征映射，与基于核的解释和优化互补。

Result: 数值实验表明，eKSVD在处理多数据源时优于基于Mercer核的方法，且引入神经网络为核方法带来了很大的灵活性。

Conclusion: eKSVD成功将KSVD推广到多数据源的非对称核联合非线性特征学习，兼具核方法与神经网络两种实现途径，在多数据源任务上表现有效且灵活。

Abstract: Kernel Singular Value Decomposition (KSVD) learns a pair of singular vectors w.r.t. an asymmetric kernel matrix, which can be induced by two data sources, e.g., the queries and keys in self-attention or the rows and columns of a given matrix. In this work, we extend KSVD to multiple data sources, namely eKSVD, which conducts joint nonlinear feature learning upon asymmetric kernels. In the primal formulation, the projections associated with each data source are jointly learned to capture maximal information, while incorporating pair-wise couplings. With the Lagrangian and its Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions, the optimization in the dual leads to a generalization of the shifted eigenvalue problem in Lanczos decomposition theorem of KSVD. Further, a covariance-based framework is derived together with using neural networks (NNs) for explicit feature mappings, complementary to the kernel-based interpretation and optimization. Numerical experiments verify the effectiveness of our eKSVD compared to methods based on Mercer kernels for tackling multiple data sources, and our innovation of deploying NNs demonstrates great flexibility for kernel methods.

</details>


### [371] [AdaStep: Adaptive Step Credit Weighting for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03223)
*Xin Wang,Wenhao Wu,Menghao Zhang,Zhi Wang,Kun Shao,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaStep：面向长时程LLM智能体的自适应步级信用加权方法，通过推导最优收缩系数在保留有效局部信用与抑制下游随机噪声间取得平衡，仅需轻量标量计算，在三个基准上稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM智能体通常只用稀疏的结果奖励训练，轨迹级目标过于粗糙，难以区分单个决策的贡献；而步级信用分配虽提供更细粒度监督，其估计却不可靠，因为观测回报还受后续动作、环境转移和轨迹长度影响。

Method: 提出AdaStep自适应步级信用加权方法，控制每组派生的局部优势对轨迹级信号的修正强度。将加权建模为潜在步级优势的均方误差估计问题，在显式条件采样假设下推导出最优的逐状态收缩系数；该系数具有信噪方差比解释。方法只需轻量标量计算，无需critic、额外rollout或额外模型推理。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld三个环境中，使用三种模型骨干进行实验，相比基线均取得一致性提升，且计算开销低。

Conclusion: AdaStep以低成本实现了更可靠的步级信用分配，通过最优收缩系数自适应调节局部信用影响，为长时程LLM智能体训练提供了有效的细粒度监督方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents are typically trained with sparse outcome rewards, making trajectory-level objectives too coarse to distinguish the contribution of individual decisions. Step-level credit assignment provides finer-grained supervision, but its estimates can be unreliable because observed returns also depend on subsequent actions, environment transitions, and trajectory length. We propose AdaStep, an Adaptive Step-credit weighting method that controls how strongly each group-derived local advantage modifies the trajectory-level signal. We formulate this weighting as a mean-squared-error estimation problem for the latent step advantage and, under an explicit conditional sampling assumption, derive an optimal per-state shrinkage coefficient. The coefficient admits a signal-to-total-variance interpretation: it preserves local credit when return variation is attributable to the selected action and suppresses it when variation is dominated by downstream randomness. AdaStep requires only lightweight scalar computation, with no critic, additional rollouts, or extra model inference. Experiments with three model backbones on ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld show consistent improvements over baselines at low computational cost.

</details>


### [372] [The Neuro-Physical Inverter: A Modular Framework for Magnetotelluric Inversion Coupling Ensemble Conditioning with Residual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03225)
*Jae Deok Kim,Sai Ravela,Rob. L. Evans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NPI框架：集合条件高斯过程+残差学习网络两阶段反演，具不确定性感知，在1D大地电磁中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球物理反演需要兼具物理可行性与不确定性量化的方法，尤其需要能推广到高维参数化的模块化框架。

Method: 两阶段：EnsCGP将先验电阻率集合条件化于观测响应，得到物理可容许参考集合；残差学习网络预测针对性修正，用合成数据训练、按台站微调并提供物理耦合目标；集合在两阶段中传播以携带离散度。

Result: 合成实验显示NPI系统性降低集合均值误差且不破坏集合稳定性；在Gabbs Valley地热区宽带MT数据上，中周期频段跨台站平均失配下降，集合离散度相当。

Conclusion: 传播的集合给出不确定性因子，可作为模型类内约束程度的操作度量；两阶段公式维度无关，设计原则可扩展至高维参数化。

Abstract: We present the Neuro-Physical Inverter (NPI), a modular, uncertainty-aware framework for geophysical inversion that couples ensemble-based conditioning with constrained residual learning, demonstrated in the 1D magnetotelluric (MT) setting as a controlled testbed. The framework operates in two stages. An Ensemble-Conditional Gaussian Process (EnsCGP) conditions a prior ensemble of resistivity models on the observed response, producing a physically admissible reference ensemble. A residual-learning neural network then predicts targeted corrections to this reference, trained on synthetic data and fine-tuned per station for field application through a physics-coupled objective. Because an ensemble is conditioned, refined, and propagated through both stages, every estimate carries an associated ensemble spread. Synthetic experiments show that NPI systematically reduces ensemble-mean error without destabilizing the ensemble. Applied to broadband MT data from the Gabbs Valley geothermal region (Nevada, USA), NPI reduces the across-station mean misfit over the mid-period band while retaining comparable ensemble spread. The propagated ensemble yields a factor of uncertainty that serves as an operational measure of constraint within the assumed model class. Both stages are dimension-agnostic in formulation, and the design principles established here are intended to scale to higher-dimensional parameterizations.

</details>


### [373] [D2K-Bench: Can LLM Agents Turn Expert Designs into Efficient GPU Kernels?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03226)
*Daifeng Li,Huiqiang Jiang,Chengruidong Zhang,Wei Wu,Xudong Guo,Jianhong Tu,Jianwei Zhang,Binhang Yuan,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了D2K-Bench，一个诊断型基准，用于衡量LLM智能体将专家设计指导转化为高效GPU内核的能力，发现专家指导显著提升正确率和性能分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核仅看运行时间无法揭示效率差距与设计发现、实现之间的关系，因此需要一个能诊断智能体如何将专家设计指导转化为高效内核的基准。

Method: 构建D2K-Bench，包含26个任务、85个工作负载，指导分为L1（高层算法洞见）、L2（数据流设计）、L3（底层优化技巧）及其依赖关系。采用有/无指导的成对运行，共享任务描述、工作负载、工具、硬件和350轮预算，并辅以独立设计提案和生成代码中设计属性的评估。

Result: 在NVIDIA B200 GPU上测试五个模型：指导将有指导与无指导的130个模型-任务对的正确率从93.1%提升至98.5%；全部26个任务的Performance Score从1.46提升至1.95。三个前沿模型（GPT-6-Astra、Claude-Opus-4.8、GPT-5.6-Sol）几何平均加速比从1.69倍提升至2.49倍。五模型平均综合实现分从57提升至70（满分100）。

Conclusion: 结果证明了专家设计指导的价值，同时指出了尚未实现的设计属性。

Abstract: GPU kernels generated by large language model (LLM) agents can remain less efficient than expert implementations, but runtime alone does not reveal how the gap relates to design discovery and implementation. We introduce D2K-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark of 26 tasks and 85 workloads that measures how effectively agents translate expert design guidance into efficient GPU kernels. The guidance covers L1: high-level algorithmic insights, L2: dataflow design, and L3: low-level optimization tricks, including dependencies among these levels. Pairwise runs with and without guidance share task descriptions, workloads, tools, hardware, and a 350-turn budget. Complementary assessments examine independently proposed designs and the design properties implemented in generated code. Across five models on NVIDIA B200 GPUs, guidance raises correctness over 130 model-task pairs from 93.1% to 98.5% and increases the Performance Score over all 26 tasks from 1.46 to 1.95. For the three frontier models with correct submissions on all 26 tasks in both runs (GPT-6-Astra, Claude-Opus-4.8, and GPT-5.6-Sol), geometric mean speedup increases from $1.69\times$ to $2.49\times$. Across all five models, the mean combined implementation score increases from 57 to 70 out of 100. These results show the value of expert design guidance while identifying design properties that remain unimplemented.

</details>


### [374] [Cross-cohort TB classification using clinical data gathered in Uganda and South Africa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03256)
*Joshua M. Jansen van Vüren,Devendra S. Parihar,Daphne Naidoo,Marisa Klopper,Frank Cobelens,Lutz Kolbe,Kimsey Zajac,Willy Ssengooba,Moses Joloba,Grant Theron,Thomas R. Niesler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次评估将机器学习应用于南非和乌干达两国收集的患者临床与人口学数据，用于结核病筛查以识别需要昂贵分子检测的人群。基于CAGE-TB数据集，比较了逻辑回归、多层感知机和卷积神经网络三种架构并结合贪心特征选择，其中CNN提出联合优化特征选择与特征排序的策略。特征选择使开发集AUROC提升2-7%，LR在留出队列上AUROC达0.8和0.84。更深的网络虽在开发集上表现更优，但在留出队列上趋势不一致，LR性能接近最佳结果。LR灵敏度距WHO最低要求仅差4-9%。基于神经网络的结核病筛查分类器具有可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结核病筛查中，昂贵的分子检测资源有限，需要有效方法识别真正受益的人群。本文旨在评估机器学习能否利用多国收集的临床与人口学数据支持筛查决策，并探索特征选择与网络架构的影响。

Method: 基于新构建的CAGE-TB数据集（含南非和乌干达社区医疗中心疑似结核病患者子队列），采用逻辑回归（LR）、多层感知机（MLP）和卷积神经网络（CNN）三种架构，结合贪心特征选择；对CNN提出联合优化特征选择与特征排序的策略。

Result: 特征选择使三种模型开发集AUROC提升2-7%。LR经特征选择后，在留出的乌干达和南非数据上AUROC分别为0.8 [0.75,0.86]（95% CI）和0.84 [0.78,0.9]。更深的网络（MLP、CNN）在开发队列上优于LR，但在留出队列上趋势不一致，LR的AUROC距最佳结果仅差1-2%。LR灵敏度距WHO最低要求仅差4-9%。

Conclusion: 尽管更深网络表现不稳定，逻辑回归通过特征选择即可达到接近最佳的性能。基于神经网络的结核病筛查分类器开发具有可行性，但距WHO灵敏度标准仍有差距。

Abstract: We present a first evaluation of machine learning applied to patient clinical and demographic data gathered in two different countries for the purpose of tuberculosis (TB) screening to identify people who would benefit from expensive molecular testing. Experiments are based on the recently-compiled CAGE-TB dataset, which includes sub-cohorts of people with presumptive TB presenting at community health care centres in South Africa and Uganda. Three neural network architectures (logistic regression (LR), multilayer perceptrons (MLP) and convolutional neural networks (CNN)) are considered in conjunction with greedy feature selection. For the convolutional neural network, a strategy that jointly optimises feature selection and feature ordering is proposed and shown to lead to consistent development and test set improvements. For all three models, development set area under the receiver operating characteristic (AUROC) curve is improved by 2-7% using feature selection. LR after feature selection achieves an AUROC of 0.8 [0.75,0.86] (95% CI) and 0.84 [0.78,0.9] when testing on the held-out Ugandan and South African data respectively. Although outperforming LR on the development cohort, the deeper networks (MLP, CNN) show inconsistent trends on the held-out cohorts, while LR achieves performance within 1-2% of the best achieved in terms of AUROC. LR narrowly misses the WHO minimum requirements by 4-9% in sensitivity even though the network is being evaluated on a completely held-out cohort. The development of neural-network based classifiers for TB screening therefore appears viable.

</details>


### [375] [Mapping and Advancing the Scalability-Accuracy Frontier of Nonlinear Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03258)
*Hendrik Suhr,Sascha Xu,Jilles Vreeken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证比较了四类可扩展非线性因果发现方法，揭示了各自的精度-运行时瓶颈，并提出SPADE——一种基于样条的评分方案，通过一次编译充分统计量并在组合搜索中复用，大幅提升组合搜索的可扩展性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展非线性因果发现需要兼具灵活机制估计器与高效图空间搜索的方法。现有多个算法族虽被提出，但其精度-运行时的权衡关系尚不清晰，亟需系统性比较以识别瓶颈并加以改进。

Method: 对四类主要方法进行实证比较：可微结构学习、摊销结构学习、得分匹配与组合搜索。针对组合搜索因重复冗余局部评分而变慢的瓶颈，提出SPADE：一种基于样条的评分评估方案，一次编译充分统计量并在组合搜索全程复用；在有界入度下，其高斯变体将算法复杂度从O(nd^3)降至O(nd^2+d^3)。

Result: 可微与摊销方法扩展性好但存在精度差距；得分匹配在低维准确但随特征数增加迅速退化；组合方法保持准确却受重复冗余局部评分拖累。SPADE将可扩展性-精度前沿推进数个数量级：秒级求解100变量、16万样本问题，分钟级求解1600变量、2.5千样本问题，并在合成与真实基准上保持高结构精度。

Conclusion: SPADE显著改变了组合搜索的实际可扩展规模，表明沿完整精度-运行时前沿评估可扩展因果发现方法具有重要意义。

Abstract: Scalable nonlinear causal discovery requires methods that combine flexible mechanism estimators with efficient search over large graph spaces. Several algorithmic families have been proposed to address this challenge, yet their accuracy-runtime trade-offs remain poorly understood. We empirically compare the four major approaches: differentiable structure learning, amortized structure learning, score-matching, and combinatorial search. Our results reveal complementary bottlenecks: differentiable and amortized methods scale well but exhibit an accuracy gap, score-matching methods can be accurate in low dimensions but degrade quickly for increasing feature sizes, and combinatorial methods remain accurate but are slowed by repeated and redundant local scoring. Motivated by this bottleneck, we develop SPADE, a spline-based score-evaluation scheme that compiles sufficient statistics once and reuses them throughout combinatorial search. Under bounded indegree, its Gaussian variant reduces algorithmic complexity from O(nd^3) to O(nd^2+d^3). Empirically, SPADE shifts the observed scalability-accuracy frontier by orders of magnitude: it solves 100-variable problems with 160K samples in seconds and 1600-variable problems with 2.5K samples in minutes, while retaining high structural accuracy across synthetic and real-world benchmarks. These results reveal a substantial shift in the practical scale of combinatorial search and highlight the importance of evaluating scalable causal-discovery methods along the full accuracy-runtime frontier.

</details>


### [376] [PaMIR: Open Benchmark of Public Credit-Default Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03259)
*Mikhail Liashkov,Ilyas Varshavskiy,Shuhratjon Khalilbekov,Azizjon Azimi,Bonu Boboeva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了 PaMIR，一个面向信用违约预测的开放基准，针对标签稀缺且延迟到达的场景，整合了 19 个公开数据集，并采用统一的无泄漏重建流程与多种评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信用违约预测基准研究通常只使用八个数据集，且其中仅两到四个是公开的，导致研究难以复现和公平比较。同时，现实场景中标签往往稀缺且延迟到达，亟需一个统一、公开、经过泄漏审计的基准来支撑该条件下的模型评估。

Method: PaMIR 整合了 19 个带二分类违约标签的公开数据集，覆盖 124 万笔贷款、企业和信用卡账户，来自九个国家。数据由固定源快照按同一套泄漏审计流程重建，且不再二次分发。每个模型被实现为单一函数，在重复的 i.i.d. 划分和标签延迟流两种设置下评分：延迟流中每个申请在到达时即被评分，并按标签预算报告 AUC。除非所有数据集均被评分，否则不公布整体均值。此外还提供合成数据测试框架，用于检验生成的训练样本，同时避免生成器看到留出行。

Result: PaMIR 构建了一个包含 19 个公开数据集、124 万条记录的信用违约预测基准，具备统一的无泄漏数据处理流程和两种评估协议，并以按标签预算报告 AUC 的方式衡量模型性能。当前为 0.4.0 版本的持续更新基准。

Conclusion: PaMIR 为标签稀缺且延迟到达条件下的信用违约预测提供了公开、可复现且经过泄漏审计的基准，是目前同类中较为独特的资源，并通过持续版本更新支持后续研究。

Abstract: We release PaMIR (Public Arrival-ordered Measurement for Inference in Risk), an open benchmark for credit-default prediction when labels are scarce and arrive late. The field's reference benchmark studies use eight datasets each, only two or four of them public. PaMIR brings together 19 public datasets with binary default labels -- 1.24M loans, firms and card accounts from nine countries -- rebuilt from pinned source snapshots by one leakage-audited recipe and never redistributed; to our knowledge it is the one of its kind as of today. Every model is a single function, scored under a repeated i.i.d. split and a label-delayed stream in which each application is scored on arrival, with AUC reported by label budget; fleet means are withheld unless every dataset is scored. A synthetic-data harness tests generated training rows without letting a generator see held-out rows. This report describes release 0.4.0 of this living benchmark.

</details>


### [377] [SPEAR: A Spectral-Disentangled MoE Neural Operator with Knowledge-Guided Expert Aggregation for Large-Scale PDE Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03265)
*Dengdi Sun,Xiaoya Zhou,Xiao Wang,Wanli Lyu,Jin Tang,Bin Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPEAR，一种谱解耦的MoE神经算子，通过知识引导的专家聚合缓解异构动力学下的知识干扰与专家冗余，在十二个PDE数据集上实现更优性能并将专家数减少50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PDE基础模型在处理异构动力学时存在共享表示导致的知识干扰问题，而MoE架构虽能缓解该问题，却面临专家冗余日益严重的困境。因此，需要一种既能解耦异构动力学、又能精简专家的预训练框架。

Method: SPEAR将潜在特征解耦为低频与高频分量，以共享建模可迁移动力学并专门学习PDE特有模式；同时设计知识引导的专家聚合策略，从数据集特定的已学知识与路由偏好中度量专家相似性，从而识别并合并相似专家。

Result: 在十二个PDE数据集及多个下游基准上，SPEAR在预训练、微调和迁移学习任务中均取得更优表现；其聚合策略在保持甚至提升预测精度的同时将专家数量减少50%。

Conclusion: SPEAR通过谱解耦与知识引导专家聚合，在模型效率与泛化能力之间取得平衡，为PDE基础模型的大规模预训练提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large-scale pre-training has improved the generalization of neural operators across diverse PDEs. However, existing PDE foundation models still struggle with heterogeneous dynamics, where shared representations may cause knowledge interference, while mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures suffer from increasing expert redundancy. We propose SPEAR, a spectral-disentangled MoE neural operator with knowledge-guided expert aggregation for large-scale PDE pre-training. SPEAR decouples latent features into low- and high-frequency components, enabling shared modeling of transferable dynamics and specialized learning of PDE-specific patterns. To address expert redundancy, we design a knowledge-guided expert aggregation strategy that measures expert similarity from dataset-specific learned knowledge and routing preferences, enabling the identification and consolidation of similar experts. Experiments on twelve PDE datasets and multiple downstream benchmarks demonstrate superior performance in pre-training, fine-tuning, and transfer learning. Furthermore, our aggregation strategy reduces the number of experts by 50\% while maintaining or improving prediction accuracy, achieving a balance between model efficiency and generalization for PDE foundation models.

</details>


### [378] [Architecture-Dependent Fusion Pathways in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03289)
*Hebao Zhu,Dongxia Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对两类多模态大模型（拼接式与原生多模态架构）的跨层融合机制进行了机理分析，揭示了两种不同的融合路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在视觉-语言任务上表现优异，但视觉与文本信息如何在各层间被融合的内部机制仍缺乏理解，需要机理层面的研究。

Method: 选取两类架构范式的代表性MLLM，开展三项递进分析：对齐解耦（识别哪种模态发生变化）、注意力路由与熵（刻画跨模态信息分布）、内在维度（考察融合如何重塑特征空间）；并辅以因果干预实验进行验证，另用visual CKA检验柏拉图表示假说。

Result: 揭示出两条不同的融合路径：拼接式模型遵循"文本优先、视觉在后"的路径，而原生模型表现出更早的视觉-文本协同适应与特征空间重组。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解多模态融合提供了机理视角，并支持面向架构的多模态表示诊断方法。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance across vision-language tasks, yet the internal mechanisms by which visual and textual information are fused across layers remain insufficiently understood. We investigate representative MLLMs from two architectural paradigms: concatenation architectures and native multimodal architectures. We conduct three progressively connected analyses: alignment decoupling identifies which modality changes, attention routing and entropy characterize how cross-modal information is distributed, and intrinsic dimensionality examines how fusion reshapes feature spaces. Separately, we perform causal intervention experiments as a validation of the resulting interpretation. As a supplementary analysis, we use visual CKA to examine the Platonic Representation Hypothesis. Together, these analyses reveal two distinct fusion pathways: concatenation models follow a text-first, vision-later pathway, whereas native models exhibit earlier visual-textual co-adaptation and feature-space reorganization. This work provides a mechanistic perspective for understanding multimodal fusion and supports architecture-aware diagnostics of multimodal representations.

</details>


### [379] [S$^{2}$-PINN: Stochastic Separable Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03303)
*Zhendong Li,Akwum Onwunta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出S²-PINN，用可学习的空间字典、Fourier时间特征和gPC随机基经CP低秩张量耦合来求解随机PDE，兼顾不确定性量化精度与参数效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机PDE的不确定性量化在科学与工程中普遍存在，但经典谱方法面临维数灾难，现有神经求解器又常忽略可利用的随机结构，导致矩计算与校准难以进行。

Method: 将随机PDE解表示为可学习高斯空间字典、Fourier时间特征与广义多项式混沌(gPC)随机基的分离式组合，由低秩CP张量分解核心耦合；采用强形式残差与gPC投影残差混合损失训练。理论证明分离类在L²中稠密、投影残差等价于随机Galerkin约束，并分析小批量投影系数与gPC模态数的对数依赖及正交惩罚对空间字典条件数的控制。

Result: 在四个制造解随机PDE基准上，均值、方差精度与校准性均优于九个基线且参数量显著更少；在非制造Poisson与Darcy问题、随机Navier-Stokes、更高随机维数的扩散缩放研究及两个随机反问题上验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: 随机可分性可作为物理信息神经不确定性量化的有效设计原则，代码已开源。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) for random partial differential equations (PDEs) is ubiquitous in computational science and engineering. However, classical spectral solvers for this class of problems face the curse of dimensionality, and existing neural solvers often ignore the stochastic structure that makes moments and calibration tractable. We introduce a stochastic separable physics-informed neural network, dubbed S$^{2}$-PINN, that represents the solution $u(t,\mathbf{x},\mathbf{Z})$ of a random PDE with a learnable Gaussian spatial dictionary, Fourier temporal features, and a generalized polynomial chaos (gPC) stochastic basis, coupled by a low-rank Canonical Polyadic (CP) tensor decomposition core. The method is trained with a hybrid strong-form and gPC-projected residual loss. Our theoretical analysis establishes that the separable class is dense in $L^2$ under mild conditions, and the projected residual corresponds exactly to a stochastic Galerkin constraint. Furthermore, we show that mini-batch projection coefficients are logarithmically dependent on the number of gPC modes, and that the orthogonality penalty controls the conditioning of the learned spatial dictionary. Using four manufactured random PDE benchmarks, we show that S$^{2}$-PINN outperforms nine baselines in terms of mean and variance accuracy, as well as calibration, while using significantly fewer parameters. Further evaluations on non-manufactured Poisson and Darcy problems, a stochastic Navier--Stokes problem, a diffusion scaling study of higher random dimensions, and two stochastic inverse problems reveal the generalization capabilities of the proposed structure. Together, these results support stochastic separability as an effective design principle for physics-informed neural UQ. The code for the experiments can be found in https://github.com/DMax1314/s2pinn

</details>


### [380] [Training-Loss Guarantees for Muon with Finite-Step Newton--Schulz Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03306)
*Amartya Roy,Souvik Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为Muon优化器在带动量与有限步Newton–Schulz更新的实际设置下建立了有限时间训练保证，证明其能达到任意目标训练损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Muon收敛分析要么假设精确正交化，要么只分析经典Newton–Schulz多项式，且仅保证收敛到驻点，因此无法回答Muon的五个调优Newton–Schulz步骤究竟保持了何种性质、是否足以达到预设的神经网络训练损失。

Method: 针对足够宽的两层ReLU网络（固定随机输出权重、正定极限NTK）进行全批量训练分析，同时考虑正交化前的动量累积与调优的有限步更新，证明Muon以高概率达到任意目标经验平方损失ε。

Result: 对任意动量参数μ∈[0,1)，目标相关常数学习率正比于(1−μ)√ε时，命中时间界为O((1−μ)^{-1}ε^{-1/2})；充分宽度与目标精度和动量均无关。分析表明调优Newton–Schulz映射在限制更新谱范数的同时保持与动量缓冲区的对齐，并通过梯度变化控制将这种对齐传递到当前梯度。数值实验在低于理论阈值的宽度下验证了这些机制：梯度-更新对齐高于分析参考值，30次运行均达到目标损失并保持核正定性。

Conclusion: Muon无需精确正交化即可保证达到预设训练损失，其收敛依赖于调优Newton–Schulz映射保持的对齐性质与谱范数控制，理论宽度阈值可独立于目标精度和动量，且实验支持了该机制在实际宽度下的有效性。

Abstract: Existing convergence analyses of Muon either assume exact orthogonalization or analyze classical Newton--Schulz polynomials, and guarantee only stationarity, so it is unresolved what Muon's five tuned Newton--Schulz steps preserve and whether that suffices to reach a prescribed neural-network training loss. We establish a finite-time training guarantee that accounts for both momentum accumulation before orthogonalization and the tuned finite-step update. For full-batch training of a sufficiently wide two-layer ReLU network with fixed random output weights and a positive-definite limiting neural tangent kernel, we prove that Muon reaches any target empirical squared loss $\varepsilon>0$ with high probability over initialization. For every momentum parameter $μ\in[0,1)$, a target-dependent constant learning rate proportional to $(1-μ)\sqrt{\varepsilon}$ yields a hitting-time bound of $O((1-μ)^{-1}\varepsilon^{-1/2})$, with other problem parameters fixed. The sufficient width is independent of both target accuracy and momentum. The analysis shows that the tuned Newton--Schulz map preserves alignment with the momentum buffer while bounding the update's spectral norm. Control of gradient variation near initialization transfers this alignment to the current gradient, ensuring descent until the target is reached without requiring exact orthogonalization. Numerical experiments support these mechanisms at widths below the sufficient theoretical threshold: gradient-update alignment remains above the analytical reference, and all 30 runs across six widths and five student initializations on a fixed teacher-student dataset reach the target loss while maintaining kernel positivity.

</details>


### [381] [Cordial Learning: Distributed Training with Correlated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03330)
*Sarah Shitrit,Ilai Bistritz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 cordial learning，一种在数据相关场景下通过仅共享低维输出、训练本地模型提取同伴信息的分布式学习方法，并证明线性模型下几乎必然收敛到全局最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化方法（如联邦学习）忽略了问题的相关性结构，在相关数据上表现不佳；而集中式方法因隐私和通信限制不可行。因此需要一种能兼顾隐私约束并利用数据相关性的分布式学习框架。

Method: 提出 cordial（correlated and distributed）learning，agent 之间只共享低维输出，各自训练本地模型以从同伴中提取有用信号。该分布式学习形成一个博弈，每个 agent 的损失函数依赖其他 agent 的模型；在线性模型假设下进行理论分析。

Result: 在线性模型设定下，证明 cordial learning 以概率 1 收敛到全局最优解，尽管全局目标是非凸的。在结构化 multi-digit MNIST 任务上的实验表明，即使在高非线性情形下，cordial learning 仍然非常有效。

Conclusion: cordial learning 填补了忽略相关性的去中心化方法与不可行的集中式方法之间的空白，在隐私与通信受限下能有效处理相关数据，并具有理论收敛保证与良好实验表现。

Abstract: We consider a distributed learning task with agents that have correlated data. Specifically, the label of an agent depends on the input of other agents for the same sample, and these inputs are also correlated. Correlated data is the reality when agents share the same environment. Existing decentralized methods, such as federated learning, ignore the structure of the problem and perform poorly on correlated data. On the other hand, centralized approaches are infeasible due to privacy and communication constraints. We introduce cordial (correlated and distributed) learning to address this gap by sharing only low-dimensional outputs between the agents while training local models to extract informative signals from peers. This distributed learning induces a game in which the loss function of each agent depends on the models of others. Assuming a linear model, we prove that cordial learning converges with probability one to a globally optimal solution, despite the nonconvex global objective. Experiments on structured multi-digit MNIST tasks demonstrate that cordial learning remains highly effective even in highly nonlinear settings.

</details>


### [382] [Follow the Winners: Conservative Policy Improvement with the Cross-Entropy Method for Critic-Free RFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03361)
*Joery Ariën de Vries,Neil David Lawrence,Zhenwen Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FTW，一种无需critic的强化微调算法，用回放缓冲区样本的序数过滤替代群体rollout，适配有状态环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO类方法依赖重复rollout计算群体基线来降低方差，但在有状态环境（如在线服务、安全沙箱）中重复rollout不现实，激进更新会放大长而稀疏验证轨迹中的噪声。

Method: 将交叉熵方法适配到RFT，用带序数过滤的回放缓冲区样本替代群体rollout，使收益序统计量具有多项式集中性；并从"控制即推断"视角推导FTW，同时将GRPO和DPO纳入同一框架。

Result: 理论分析表明GRPO为风险中性，DPO与FTW共享有界风险寻求偏移且FTW可控制该偏移；在Sokoban和Search-R1上，FTW与GRPO、PPO表现相当。

Conclusion: FTW将有状态环境中对价值模型或群体rollout的依赖转移到CPU内存，提供了一种可行且有效的权衡，适用于智能体LLM后训练。

Abstract: Critic-free reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) for agentic large language models is often done through GRPO-style methods, which compute a group baseline over repeated rollouts to reduce target variance. However, this setup is ill-suited to agents acting in stateful environments such as live services or security sandboxes, where repeated rollouts are impractical to obtain and aggressive updates entrench the noise of long, sparsely verified trajectories. We propose \textit{Follow the Winners} (FTW), a critic-free policy-learning algorithm that adapts the cross-entropy method to RFT, replacing group rollouts with an ordinal filter on replay-buffer samples that yields polynomial concentration in the order statistic of returns. We derive FTW through a control-as-inference lens, which also recovers GRPO and DPO as specific modelling choices, identifying GRPO as risk-neutral while DPO and FTW share a bounded risk-seeking offset that FTW controls. We identify this offset as an inherent trade-off of variance reduction through ordinal filters on samples, whereas a critic model induces a different trade-off between bias and variance. Scaled to agentic LLM post-training, FTW matches GRPO and PPO on Sokoban and Search-R1 baselines, showing a viable trade-off from a value model or group rollouts to CPU memory.

</details>


### [383] [SCAD: Structured Credit Assignment and Distillation for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03372)
