*Shangyang Wu,Shuai Zhao,Ziyue Zhu,Jinyang Wu,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCAD方法，通过规划与有界子任务执行的分解、局部蒸馏与跨轨迹前缀树信用分配，提升长时域智能体的训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域智能体训练中，稀疏的终端奖励难以揭示中间步骤的贡献；而在策略蒸馏随着学生生成的轨迹变长，会丢失教师的有效指导信息。

Method: SCAD将交互组织为规划阶段和有界子任务执行阶段，在局部上下文中对执行进行蒸馏，并通过跨轨迹的子任务前缀树来细化规划的信用分配。规划获得完整的终端信用，执行获得正终端信用及教师指导。

Result: 在所有评估基准上，SCAD相比最强训练基线，文本任务宏平均准确率提升4.48个百分点，多模态任务提升4.19个百分点。

Conclusion: SCAD有效结合了基于结果的信用分配与教师引导的蒸馏，改善了长时域智能体的规划与执行能力。

Abstract: Training long-horizon agents to solve complex tasks requires effective supervision over extended interaction sequences. However, sparse terminal rewards obscure intermediate contributions, while on-policy distillation can lose informative teacher guidance as student-generated histories grow. To address this problem, we introduce SCAD, which organizes interactions into planning and bounded subtask execution, distills execution in local contexts, and refines planning credit through cross-rollout subtask prefix trees, with planning receiving full terminal credit and execution receiving positive terminal credit and teacher guidance. Across all evaluated benchmarks, SCAD improves macro-average accuracy over the strongest training baseline by 4.48 percentage points for text tasks and 4.19 points for multimodal tasks. SCAD effectively combines outcome-based credit assignment with teacher-guided distillation to improve planning and execution in long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [384] [Operator-informed initialization for Fourier features physics-informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03378)
*Juan Molina,Paris Perdikaris,Mircea Petrache,Matías Courdurier,Francisco Sahli Costabal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析傅里叶特征PINN在NTK机制下的训练动力学，揭示频域收敛速率由微分算子符号与初始化权重谱密度之积主导，并提出针对性的信息初始化策略以缓解算子引起的谱偏置，提升精度且无额外训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN通常存在谱偏置，即目标函数某些频率的收敛速度明显慢于其他频率，限制了模型的预测精度。作者希望从理论上理解并缓解这一由微分算子引入的谱偏置问题。

Method: 在神经正切核（NTK）机制下分析傅里叶特征PINN的训练动力学，推导出频域残差误差的显式演化方程；基于"收敛速率由微分算子符号与初始化权重谱密度之积主导"的结论，提出一种信息初始化策略，根据具体PDE定制初始权重分布。

Result: 理论推导给出频域残差的显式演化方程；所提初始化策略能够削弱算子引起的谱偏置，平衡各频率的收敛速率；在线性与非线性PDE数值实验中，与标准初始化相比，学习动力学与各频率近似精度均得到提升。

Conclusion: 通过理论与实验表明，依据PDE定制初始权重谱密度可有效缓解谱偏置并提升PINN精度，且不增加额外训练成本，为PINN初始化设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) typically exhibit spectral bias, where some frequencies of the target function converge more slowly than others. In this work, we analyze the training dynamics of Fourier Feature PINNs in the Neural Tangent Kernel regime to address this limitation. We derive an explicit evolution equation to estimate the residual error in the frequency domain, demonstrating that the convergence rate of specific frequencies is primarily governed by the product of the differential operator's symbol and the spectral density of the initialization weights. Leveraging this theoretical insight, we propose an informative initialization strategy that tailors the initial weight distribution to the specific PDE being solved. With this method, we can diminish the operator-induced spectral bias, balancing the convergence rates across the frequency spectrum and achieving better prediction accuracy. Numerical experiments on linear and nonlinear partial differential equations confirm that this initialization strategy improves learning dynamics and approximation accuracy across frequencies compared to standard initialization methods, with no additional training cost.

</details>


### [385] [Bidirectional Voronoi-biased Exploration Curriculum for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03395)
*Juri Pfammatter,Kaixian Qu,Clemens Schwarke,Victor Klemm,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BVER双向课程学习框架，从起点和终点两端同时扩展状态与目标分布以加速稀疏奖励长时程强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程稀疏奖励任务中，策略从初始状态出发难以到达目标，导致缺乏学习信号；参考动作、人工课程和奖励塑形依赖演示或任务定制，而自动化课程通常只从一端扩展，需覆盖全部距离。

Method: 受双向RRT规划启发，BVER从目标端向外生成起点、从初始状态分布向外生成目标，并对两端都偏向未探索任务空间且相互引导，在同一目标条件策略上联合训练。

Result: 在点质量迷宫、四足爬箱和机械臂穿环任务上，BVER比所有对比的无参考课程学习更快；0.4米爬箱约减少65%迭代达95%成功率，唯一学会0.7米爬箱，对起点、目标和偏航变化鲁棒；在0.4米爬箱和穿环任务上接近参考式课程的样本效率；消融显示双向扩展优于单端。

Conclusion: 双向扩展课程能有效缓解稀疏奖励探索瓶颈，无需演示即可接近参考式方法效率，并具备更强泛化与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks with sparse rewards pose an exploration bottleneck for goal-conditioned reinforcement learning: a policy started from the initial state rarely reaches the goal and receives no learning signal. Reference motions, hand-designed curricula, and shaped rewards supply this signal but require demonstrations or task-specific engineering; automatic start-state and goal curricula avoid this but typically expand from one side only, so the full distance to the target must be covered from that side. We propose the Bidirectional Voronoi-biased Exploration curriculum for Reinforcement learning (BVER), which expands from both ends at once. Inspired by bidirectional RRT planning, BVER grows start states outward from the goal and goals outward from the initial state distribution, biases both toward unexplored task space, and steers them toward each other, training one goal-conditioned policy on both. On point-mass mazes, quadrupedal box climbing, and robot-arm ring-on-peg transfer, BVER learns faster than all compared reference-free curricula. On box climbing, it reaches 95% success on a 0.4 m box in roughly 65% fewer iterations than the best of them, is the only one of them to learn to climb a 0.7 m box, and yields a policy robust to start, goal, and yaw variation. Without a demonstration, it approaches the sample efficiency of reference-based curricula on the 0.4 m box and on ring-on-peg transfer. Ablations show that expanding from both ends outperforms either direction alone.

</details>


### [386] [A Unified Framework for Bayesian Data Assimilation with Generative Models and Observation Interpolants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03396)
*Nikolaj T. Mücke,Benjamin Sanderse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出观测插值（observation-interpolant）框架，将预训练的随机插值/流匹配/扩散模型直接转化为后验采样器，无需重新训练，并在高维非线性动力学中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯数据同化需结合模型预报与含噪观测，但高维、非高斯后验采样仍是难点；同时预训练生成模型（扩散、流匹配等）已具备强大先验建模能力，如何低成本地将其复用为后验采样器具有重要价值。

Method: 对插值路径以观测为条件，导出对漂移/速度的共享似然分数修正，统一随机与确定性后验采样；当中间似然分数已知时，所得SDE/ODE可精确采样后验。实际中用闭式高斯代理近似该分数：均值做偏差校正，协方差由模型源协方差膨胀；并采用无雅可比（Jacobian-free）与集成共享（ensemble-shared）近似以保证高维可算性。

Result: 在线性高斯动力学、随机二维Navier-Stokes以及城市气流（自由度达O(10^4)）上进行评估，验证了框架的有效性与高维可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种无需重训练即可将预训练生成模型用作贝叶斯后验采样的统一框架，通过似然分数修正与实用近似，实现了高维非高斯数据同化的可行采样。

Abstract: Bayesian data assimilation combines model forecasts with noisy observations, but sampling high-dimensional, non-Gaussian posteriors remains challenging. We introduce an observation-interpolant framework that turns pretrained stochastic interpolant, flow matching, and diffusion models into posterior samplers without retraining. Conditioning the interpolant path on observations yields a shared likelihood-score correction to the drift or velocity, unifying stochastic and deterministic posterior sampling. The resulting SDEs and ODEs sample the exact posterior when the intermediate likelihood score is known. For practical computation, we approximate this score using a closed-form Gaussian surrogate with a bias-corrected mean and covariance inflated by the model's source covariance. Jacobian-free and ensemble-shared approximations make the method tractable in high dimensions. We evaluate the framework on linear-Gaussian dynamics, stochastic two-dimensional Navier-Stokes, and urban airflow with up to $O(10^4)$ degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [387] [16-bit Precision of Convolutional Neural Networks on Microcontroller Units for 8-bit Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03402)
*Rui Liu,Benjamin Paaßen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 W16A16——一种高精度（16位）、高速、低能耗的量化方法，在 Armv7E-M 微控制器上实现比替代方案更快、更省电的推理，并保持高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在边缘硬件上部署深度神经网络，需要既高效又保持高准确率的推理方案，而现有量化方法（如8位）在精度与效率之间往往难以兼顾。

Method: 提出 W16A16 的16位高精度量化方法；分析 Armv7E-M 架构，阐释16位方法性能优势的原理；在回归与分类任务上评估量化误差，并在 MCU 部署中测量实际时间与能耗。

Result: 相比8位量化方案，量化误差约降低10倍，同时推理时间与能耗相当或更优；在层级与模型级均比替代量化方案速度更快、能耗更低。

Conclusion: 16位量化（W16A16）在 Armv7E-M 微控制器上能同时实现高精度、高速度与低能耗，是边缘部署深度神经网络的有效方案。

Abstract: To deploy deep neural networks on edge hardware, highly efficient inference schemes are necessary that retain high accuracy. This work presents W16A16, a high precision (16-bit), fast speed, low energy quantization method. On a widely applied microcontroller architecture Armv7E-M, our proposed approach achieves faster speed and lower energy consumption on layer- and model-level compared to alternative quantization schemes. We analyze the architecture of Armv7E-M, explain the underlying principles behind the performance advantages of 16-bit approaches, and evaluate the empiric quantization errors for regression and classification tasks, as well as empiric time- and energy consumption in MCU deployment. We observe ca.\ 10 times lower quantization errors compared to 8-bit quantization schemes while achieving similar or better inference times and energy consumption.

</details>


### [388] [AIBL: Augmented Instance-Based Learning with Structured Memory and Neural Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03413)
*Radha Poovendran,Andrea Stocco,Linda Bushnell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AIBL，将实例学习理论扩展到学习向量空间，通过神经嵌入相似度、主动/遗忘/惊奇记忆与毕业机制处理高维时序数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IBLT依赖字典式符号知识表示和人工匹配规则，难以应对文本、图像、交易向量、用户-物品历史等高维输入及其分布随时间变化的问题，需要可学习的相似度表示。

Method: AIBL在学习向量空间中构建基于实例的模型：用神经嵌入相似度替代符号情境匹配，保留实例存储、激活加权检索与效用融合；记忆分为主动、遗忘与惊奇存储，惊奇存储隔离弱匹配、可能分布外或边角案例，减少对最近案例的强制拟合；采用观测驱动的毕业算法，将重复出现的惊奇实例提升至主动记忆以应对概念漂移。

Result: 在五个机器学习任务和三个受控模拟任务上评估同一实现，并与经典IBLT变体及任务特定基线比较，AIBL准确率提升6至17个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明向量空间检索在何处优于符号匹配，以及新增记忆机制如何在测试协议下支持新颖性检测、冷启动处理、漂移适应和奖励学习。

Abstract: Sequential learning systems often make decisions from accumulated experience while receiving high-dimensional inputs whose distribution may change over time. Instance-Based Learning Theory (IBLT) provides a principled case-based framework for such settings through stored situation-decision-utility instances, partial matching, activation, and blending. IBLT relies on symbolic knowledge representation in dictionary-like formats, but text, images, transaction vectors, and user-item histories often require learned similarity rather than hand-specified matching rules. In this paper, we introduce AIBL (Augmented Instance-Based Learning), an instance-learning model formulated in a learned vector space for high- dimensional sequential data. AIBL generalizes symbolic situation matching to neural embedding similarity while retaining instance storage, activation- weighted retrieval, and utility blending. The AIBL model organizes memory into active, forgotten, and surprise stores. Surprise memory separates weakly matched, possible out-of-distribution, or corner-case observations from active memory, reducing forced fitting to the nearest available cases. An observation-driven graduation algorithm promotes recurring surprise instances to active memory, allowing the memory to incorporate repeated novel patterns that may arise under concept drift. We evaluate the same implementation on five machine learning tasks and three controlled simulation tasks, comparing AIBL with classical IBLT variants and task-specific baselines where appropriate. AIBL improves accuracy by 6 to 17 percentage points. The results show where vector-space retrieval improves over symbolic matching and how the added memory mechanisms govern novelty detection, cold-start handling, drift adaptation, and reward learning under the tested protocols.

</details>


### [389] [Rethinking Epistemic Uncertainty in Node Classification through Information Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03418)
*Emma Meneghini,Francesco Ferrini,Bruno Lepri,Andrea Passerini,Veronica Lachi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个研究认知不确定性在信息增长下可约性的统计框架，指出现有图证据深度学习（EDL）方法无法满足一致性，并提出图自助集成作为候选一致认知预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知不确定性应随数据生成过程（DGP）相关信息增多而减少，但现有图EDL方法从图特有属性构建认知不确定性，并在OOD检测等下游任务上评估，无法检验其随DGP信息增长的可约性。

Method: 提出信息增长实验协议与认知预测器的一致性准则，采用投影图DGP确保增长图构成同一过程的相干观测；并设计图自助集成，通过图重采样与随机化训练捕捉数据不确定性与过程不确定性。

Result: EDL方法未显式估计单个有限图观测带来的数据不确定性，而是通过模型超参数调节认知不确定性；在受控信息增长实验中，图自助集成表现出超越标准深度集成的认知不确定性缩减。

Conclusion: 自助集成可作为信息增长下候选的一致认知预测器，为图认知不确定性的可约性研究提供了新框架与方向。

Abstract: Epistemic uncertainty should decrease as additional information about the data-generating process (DGP) becomes available to the predictor. Yet, existing graph evidential deep learning (EDL) methods for node classification typically construct epistemic uncertainty from graph-specific properties and evaluate it on downstream tasks such as out-of-distribution detection, which do not test its reducibility as information about the DGP increases. To make reducibility directly testable, we introduce a statistical framework for studying epistemic uncertainty under information growth. Our framework specifies an information-growth experimental protocol and a consistency criterion for epistemic predictors, while using projective graph DGPs to ensure that growing graphs, which in general need not provide increasing information about the same DGP, constitute coherent observations of the same underlying process. We show that EDL methods do not explicitly estimate data uncertainty arising from a single finite graph observation and instead regulate epistemic uncertainty through model hyperparameters, precluding consistency, as corroborated by controlled information-growth experiments. As an alternative, we propose graph bootstrap ensembles, capturing both data and procedural uncertainty through graph resampling and randomized training. Under the same experimental protocol, these ensembles exhibit epistemic uncertainty reduction beyond standard deep ensembles. These findings support bootstrap ensembles as candidate consistent epistemic predictors under information growth.

</details>


### [390] [Deep Bayesian REFoCUS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03419)
*Simon Penninga,Ruud van Sloun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将超声多静态恢复建模为贝叶斯推断问题，训练深度生成先验，提出Deep Bayesian REFoCUS，在秩亏与噪声条件下优于线性基线，并能表达不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典线性REFoCUS解码器在秩亏（rank-deficient）情形下会失效，需要一种能在欠定条件下仍可稳定恢复多静态超声数据的方法。

Method: 将任意发射序列下的超声多静态恢复表述为贝叶斯推断问题；在多静态数据集上训练深度生成先验，以应对秩亏情形，从而构建Deep Bayesian REFoCUS。

Result: 在所有秩亏程度和噪声水平下均优于线性基线；当反演精确时退化为线性解码；能在采集零空间中表达不确定性，而线性REFoCUS仅给出点估计；在仿真与在体采集之间的分布偏移下无需微调或适配即展现显著泛化能力。

Conclusion: Deep Bayesian REFoCUS为超声多静态恢复提供了兼具性能优势与不确定性量化能力的贝叶斯框架，并对分布偏移具有强泛化性。

Abstract: In this work we formulate ultrasound multistatic recovery from arbitrary transmit sequences as a Bayesian inference problem. To that end, we train a deep generative prior on multistatic data sets to tackle the rank-deficient regime in which classical linear REFoCUS decoders fail. This appproach, which we term Deep Bayesian REFoCUS, outperforms the linear baselines for all regimes of rank-deficiency and noise levels, and regresses to linear decoding when inversion is exact. The model also expresses uncertainty in the null space of the acquisitions, whereas the linear REFoCUS decoders only provide point estimates. Finally, we analyze the impact of distribution shift between simulation and in-vivo acquisitions, showing remarkable generalization ability without any fine-tuning or adaptation.

</details>


### [391] [OptiSelect: How does the Optimizer Shape Data Curriculum?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03432)
*Simin Fan,Alireza Abdollahpoorrostam,Martin Jaggi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OptiSelect，首个系统研究优化器如何影响在线数据选择的理论框架，证明对角自适应优化器可带来更高选择增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线数据选择虽能提升 LLM 预训练效率，但候选样本的价值实际通过优化器对梯度的重塑后生效，现有选择方法未考虑优化器步骤，缺乏原则性设计依据。

Method: 将优化器感知的选择范式形式化为 OptiSelect；建立“选择增益原理”，以优化器诱导效用分数的可判别性衡量在线选择优势；理论分析不同优化器预条件器（符号型、极坐标切向型 vs 对角自适应型）的可判别性；并推导最优候选过采样比例；在 124M、720M 模型上开展预训练实验。

Result: Lion、Muon 的符号/极坐标切向预条件器会出现可判别性崩塌，限制 OptiSelect 增益；AdamW、Sophia 等对角自适应优化器具有更优上界。实验与理论一致，即使使用 Muon 作为优化器，AdamW 的对角自适应评分几何仍最强；OptiSelect 在数据改写下仍保持收益。

Conclusion: 研究为 LLM 预训练中优化器与数据选择的协同设计提供了理论基础与实践指导。

Abstract: Online data selection has demonstrated substantial efficiency gains for LLM pretraining by training on the most valuable candidates within each batch. Since a candidate's value is realized through its effective model update, principled selection should account for the optimizer step, which reshapes the raw gradient before it updates model parameters. We formalize this optimizer-aware selection paradigm as OptiSelect and present the first systematic study of how the optimizer shapes data selection. Our theory establishes a selection gain principle in which the advantage of online selection is governed by the discriminability of the optimizer-induced utility scores. We prove that sign-based and polar-tangential preconditioners of Lion and Muon would suffer from a discriminability collapse which caps attainable gains from OptiSelect, whereas diagonal-adaptive optimizers such as AdamW and Sophia admit strictly better upper bounds. The proposed principle also yields a quantitative derivation of the optimal candidate oversampling ratio. Pretraining experiments on 124M and 720M models are consistent with our theoretical analysis and show that AdamW's diagonal-adaptive scoring geometry remains the strongest scoring geometry even with Muon as optimizer. We further demonstrate that OptiSelect retains its benefits under data rephrasing, a technique used in modern data processing pipelines. Our findings provide theoretical foundations and practical guidance for co-designing optimizers and data selection in LLM pretraining.

</details>


### [392] [Causal Representation Learning with Instantaneous and Lagged Relations via Nonstationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03452)
*Tatsuya Yamada,Hiroshi Morioka,Yoshinobu Kawahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究时间序列数据的因果表示学习，提出了在非平稳条件下同时识别瞬时与滞后因果结构以及潜在状态的充分条件，并据此设计了基于对比学习的框架iCReN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列因果表示学习需同时建模跨观测区间的滞后因果关系和区间内出现的更快的瞬时因果效应，还需处理数据的非平稳性，但现有方法难以联合处理这些因果关系与非平稳性，因此存在研究空白。

Method: 论文建立了潜在状态及其瞬时、滞后因果结构可识别性的充分条件，识别可达组件置换与逐分量可逆变换级别；利用与转移噪声分布变化相关的观测辅助变量（如时间或条件标签）；在此基础上提出iCReN框架，使用结合离散或连续辅助变量的对比学习来学习潜在表示并估计瞬时与滞后因果结构。

Result: 在合成数据上，方法能准确恢复潜在状态以及瞬时和滞后因果结构；在真实数据上，学到的表示在下游预测任务中展现出实用性。

Conclusion: 通过辅助变量利用非平稳性，可以在温和条件下同时识别潜在状态和瞬时、滞后因果结构，iCReN验证了这一理论框架的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Causal representation learning for time-series data aims to identify latent states and their causal relations from observations. In this setting, an important challenge is to model both lagged causal relations across observation intervals and faster causal effects that appear as instantaneous relations within an interval, while accounting for nonstationarity in time-series data. However, methods that jointly handle these causal relations and nonstationarity remain limited. To address this gap, we establish sufficient conditions for identifying latent states up to component permutation and component-wise invertible transformations, and their instantaneous and lagged causal structures up to the same permutation, using an observed auxiliary variable, such as time or a condition label, associated with changes in transition-noise distributions. Based on these results, we propose iCReN, a framework that uses contrastive learning with discrete or continuous auxiliary variables to learn latent representations and estimate their instantaneous and lagged causal structures. Experiments demonstrate accurate recovery of latent states and both instantaneous and lagged causal structures on synthetic data and the utility of the learned representations for downstream forecasting on real-world data.

</details>


### [393] [Measure Less, Know More: Self-Supervised Test-Time Feature Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03454)
*Eeshaan Jain,Linus Bleistein,Bart Deplancke,Charlotte Bunne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ECHO-$k$，一种与任务无关、自监督的模态顺序选择原则，用深度模型内部预训练表示作为跨模态信息的代理目标，并在线性设定下提供理论保证以支撑 RL 策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态高维学习中，测试时获取全部特征或模态成本高昂且常存在冗余；当下游任务或预测目标未知时，顺序选择信息量大的模态尤为困难。

Method: 以深度模型（如基础模型）的内部预训练表示作为代理目标，总结跨模态信息；在风格化线性设定下给出理论保证，据此设计用于顺序模态选择的强化学习策略。

Result: 在与任务无关且无标签的获取基线对比中，ECHO-$k$ 在多种基础模型后端上持续提升预算约束下的下游性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为成本感知的测试时部署提供了有原则的路径，适用于测量昂贵、时间受限且下游任务事先未知的多模态系统。

Abstract: Recent progress in multimodal, high-dimensional learning has enabled foundation models to process heterogeneous, large-scale data. However, at test time, acquiring all features or modalities can be prohibitively costly and often redundant. Sequentially selecting informative modalities is therefore critical, yet challenging when the downstream task or prediction target is unknown. To this end, we introduce ECHO-$k$, a task-agnostic and self-supervised learning principle for modality acquisition: we use a deep model's internal pretrained representations (e.g., from a foundation model) as proxy targets that summarize cross-modal information. We provide theoretical guarantees in a stylized linear setting that motivate a reinforcement learning (RL) policy for sequential modality selection. Across task-agnostic and label-free acquisition baselines, ECHO-$k$ consistently improves budgeted downstream performance across diverse foundation-model backends. Our method provides a principled route to cost-aware test-time deployment, with implications for any multimodal system where measurements are expensive or time-constrained, and downstream tasks unknown a priori.

</details>


### [394] [Beyond Random Splits: Evaluating Drug-Target Affinity Models Under Chemically and Biologically Motivated Distribution Shifts Copy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03456)
*Minjae Chung,Clara Li,Malar Paavai Muthukumaran,Shaunna Wang,Aniket Ramkrishnan Iyer,Shaun Qien Yeau Tan,Harinishree Sathu,Micky C. Nnamdi,J. Ben Tamo,Benoit Louis Marteau,May Dongmei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过构建718,800个药物-蛋白对，比较基线模型与12种受控架构在多种分布偏移下的表现，发现DTA模型的最佳架构选择取决于外推类型，而非平均误差或训练集规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTA预测常用于在昂贵实验筛选前对候选化合物进行优先级排序。但现有DTA模型通常仅在单一数据划分下比较，而实际部署可能需要外推到新的化学系列、新的蛋白靶点或两者兼有。作者希望探究：用于评估的分布偏移形式是否会改变哪种架构表现最佳。

Method: 从ChEMBL和BindingDB数据集中整理出718,800个唯一的药物-蛋白对。将Morgan指纹+蛋白CNN基线，与12种受控架构（4种药物表示×3种ESM-2交互模式）进行比较。评估涵盖scaffold OOD、fingerprint-cluster OOD、protein-cluster OOD和dual OOD等分布偏移设置，并使用留出评估、重复随机种子、分组感知bootstrap分析和规模匹配对照进行验证。

Result: 平均验证RMSE在scaffold和fingerprint-cluster OOD下为0.950和0.945，在protein-cluster和dual OOD下升至1.299和1.321。模型排名在两种化学偏移下相似（tau=0.79），但与scaffold OOD的一致性在protein OOD下下降（tau=0.39），并在dual OOD下发生反转（tau=-0.55）。留出评估、重复种子、分组感知bootstrap分析和规模匹配对照均支持同一结论。

Conclusion: 架构选择取决于外推的形式，而不仅仅取决于平均误差或训练集规模。因此，DTA基准测试应与部署时预期的化学与靶点偏移类型相匹配。

Abstract: Drug-target affinity (DTA) prediction is widely used to prioritize candidate compounds before costly experimental screening. DTA models are often compared under a single data split, even though deployment may require extrapolation to new chemical series, new protein targets, or both. We ask whether the distribution shift used for evaluation changes which architecture appears best. We curate 718,800 unique drug-protein pairs from the ChEMBL and BindingDB datasets. We compare a Morgan-fingerprint + protein-CNN baseline with 12 controlled architectures that combine four drug representations with three ESM-2 interaction modes. Mean validation RMSE increases from 0.950 and 0.945 under scaffold and fingerprint-cluster OOD to 1.299 and 1.321 under protein-cluster and dual OOD. Model rankings are similar across the two chemical shifts (tau = 0.79), but agreement with scaffold OOD falls under protein OOD (tau = 0.39) and reverses under dual OOD (tau = -0.55). Held-out evaluation, repeated seeds, group-aware bootstrap analysis, and a size-matched control support the same conclusion: architecture selection depends on the form of extrapolation, not only on average error or training-set size. DTA benchmarks should therefore match the chemical and target shifts expected at deployment.

</details>


### [395] [Single or Multiple Policies for Phase-Structured Reinforcement Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03475)
*Guilhem Loussouarn,Nancy Nayak,Kin K. Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了非平稳强化学习中单策略与多策略方法的性能差异，提出基于"瞬态动力学持续时间与准平稳期持续时间之比"的阶段分解方法来判断何时多策略更优，并通过实验验证四个假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳RL问题可分解为具有不同转移概率和奖励函数的阶段。当阶段序列已知时，通常做法是用状态增广满足马尔可夫性并应用标准RL。但先前工作发现针对不同阶段的多策略方法可能优于共享的单策略，其原因尚不清楚，因此本文旨在厘清这一现象。

Method: 理论上证明共享策略可达到任意多策略解的性能；指出实际中多策略能否更优取决于函数逼近、学习与优化过程，以及多策略的样本效率和策略间连续性损失。提出基于"瞬态系统动态持续时间相对于准平稳期持续时间"的regime-based阶段分解方法，用于判断哪种策略更优，并在多种非平稳RL问题上进行数值实验。

Result: 实验验证了四个主要假设：(a) 阶段持续时间越长越有利于多策略；(b) 阶段间异质性增加单策略负担；(c) 多策略需要每个阶段有充足数据；(d) 环境特定的阶段间转移动态会影响哪种策略更可取。

Conclusion: 共享单策略在理论上可实现多策略的性能，但实际优劣取决于逼近、学习优化及样本效率等因素。所提出的regime-based阶段分解方法结合四个假设，可为非平稳RL中选择单策略还是多策略提供实用指导。

Abstract: Many reinforcement-learning (RL) problems are non-stationary yet structured and can be decomposed into phases, each with its own transition probabilities and reward functions. When the phase sequence is known, the common solution augments the state with information to satisfy the Markovian property and applies standard RL techniques. However, prior work finds that the multi-policy approach for different phases can outperform a single state-augmented policy shared among the phases, for reasons that remain unclear. In this work, we first show that the shared policy can theoretically achieve performance of any multi-policy solution. However, whether a multi-policy solution can perform better than the corresponding single shared policy in practice depends on function approximation, learning and optimization processes, as well as, for multi-policy solutions, the sample efficiency and loss of continuity from one policy to another. We propose a regime-based phase decomposition method to identify which policy can provide better performance. The method is based on consideration of the duration of transient system dynamics relative to the duration of the quasi-stationary period. Numerical experiments are conducted with different non-stationary RL problems to validate our four major hypotheses: (a) longer phase durations favor multi-policies, (b) the heterogeneity between phases increases the burden on single policy, (c) multi-policies need sufficient data for each phase, and (d) environment-specific transition dynamics between phases can affect which policy is preferable.

</details>


### [396] [Metropolis-Hastings Dominates Importance Resampling for Policy Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03480)
*Alexey Kurennoy,Ramil Yarullin,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了用于解码时策略组合的独立性Metropolis-Hastings（MH）校正方法，证明其在任意rollout预算下都比SIR更接近目标分布，并给出改进下界与两种渐近误差刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对LLM进行后训练时往往需要在多个奖励之间权衡，但为每种权衡重新训练代价高昂。解码时策略组合可在推理阶段通过合并奖励特定策略来调节这些权衡，然而标准实现是合并下一token概率，这会造成采样偏差，因此需要分析并纠正该偏差。

Method: 论文研究了一种已知的基于独立性Metropolis-Hastings（MH）的迭代校正方法，用以逼近策略概率加权乘积所定义的目标分布；分析上采用f-散度等工具，并与采样-重要性重采样（SIR）进行对比，同时推导共识目标下的改进下界，还刻画了奖励特定策略趋于一致和似然比波动增大两种渐近情形下的采样误差，并辅以可枚举与LLM规模实验。

Result: 主要结果表明，在任意rollout预算下，以每一种凸f-散度衡量，MH产生的输出分布至少与同等预算的SIR一样接近目标分布；同时给出了MH相对于未校正解码器在共识目标上的改进下界；此外刻画了校正在两种渐近区域中的采样误差。

Conclusion: MH校正为解码时多奖励策略组合提供了一种比SIR更优的采样方案，并在理论上量化了其改进与误差行为，实验也支持了分析结论。

Abstract: Post-training a large language model (LLM) often requires exploring trade-offs between multiple rewards, but retraining for each trade-off is expensive. Decoding-time policy composition allows these trade-offs to be adjusted by combining reward-specific policies at inference time. This composition targets a weighted product of the policies' probabilities over complete responses, but standard implementations combine their next-token probabilities, generally introducing sampling bias. We analyze a known iterative correction based on independence Metropolis-Hastings (MH). Our main result shows that, for every rollout budget, MH produces an output distribution at least as close to the target as sampling-importance-resampling (SIR) with the same budget, as measured by every convex f-divergence. We also derive a lower bound on MH's improvement over the uncorrected decoder in a consensus objective measuring agreement with the supplied policies. We further characterize the correction's sampling error in two asymptotic regimes: when the reward-specific policies approach agreement, and when the log ratio between target and uncorrected-decoder probabilities fluctuates increasingly widely, as can happen for long responses. We complement our analysis with experiments in enumerable and LLM-scale settings.

</details>


### [397] [Dual-Context Analog Retrieval for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03491)
*Jung Min Choi,Ngoc Son Le,Ibram Abdelmalak,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DuoTS是一种双上下文时间序列预测模型，先由并行patch编码器与线性预测头生成基础预测，再逐patch细化，融合当前上下文与检索到analog的细节上下文，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数长期时间序列预测模型将回看窗口单次直接映射到整个预测区间，虽高效但未显式识别哪些历史状态与不同未来片段最相关，也未利用这些状态之后的走向。Analog forecasting通过检索相似历史状态并利用其后续轨迹，但单最近匹配不可靠、重叠patch易产生冗余候选。

Method: DuoTS先由平行的patch编码器与线性预测头产生基础预测，再逐一未来patch进行渐进细化。每次细化结合两种视角：当前上下文（关注近期token、捕捉最新动态）和细节上下文（提供不同的检索analog及其后续轨迹）。patch级细化使模型能跨预测区间平衡两种视角，并为每个未来片段关联与其时间距离相适应的证据。细化机制为模型无关，只需编码后的回看窗口与未来patch位置。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上，DuoTS达到state-of-the-art性能；消融实验证实了各上下文的贡献；细化机制可集成到现有预测模型中。

Conclusion: DuoTS通过不单纯依赖检索证据、融合当前与细节双上下文并进行patch级渐进细化，提升了长期时间序列预测性能，且细化机制具有模型无关的可插拔性。

Abstract: Most long-term time-series forecasting models map the look-back window directly to the full horizon in a single pass. While efficient, this design does not explicitly identify which historical states are most relevant to different future segments or exploit what followed those states. Analog forecasting addresses this by retrieving past states similar to the present and using their observed continuations, but single nearest matches can be unreliable and overlapping patches may produce redundant candidates. We propose DuoTS, a Dual-Context Time Series forecasting model that uses retrieved evidence without relying on it exclusively. DuoTS first produces a base forecast with a parallel patch encoder and linear prediction head, then progressively refines it one future patch at a time. Each refinement combines two views: a current context that attends to recent tokens and captures the latest dynamics, and a detail context that provides distinct retrieved analogs together with their subsequent trajectories. Patch-wise refinement allows the model to balance these views across the forecast horizon and associate each future segment with evidence appropriate to its temporal distance from the present. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets show that DuoTS achieves state-of-the-art performance, while ablations confirm the contribution of each context. The refinement mechanism is also model-agnostic, requiring only an encoded look-back window and the future-patch position, and can therefore be integrated into existing forecasting models.

</details>


### [398] [Most-Recent Anchoring with Recurrent Ordering for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03494)
*Jung Min Choi,Ngoc Son Le,Ibram Abdelmalak,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MARO模型：以最近patch为锚点、按从新到旧的循环顺序处理回看窗口，用单一共享模块逐patch细化隐状态，实现近期证据中心化的长期时序预测，在多个真实数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期预测模型对回看窗口内所有patch采用相同的固定堆叠处理，使较旧的上下文patch与近期证据获得相同的计算深度。然而靠近预测点的信息与较远上下文贡献并不相等，统一处理使这种区别在架构中没有被表达出来。

Method: MARO（Most-Recent Anchoring with Recurrent Ordering）从最新patch到最旧patch顺序处理回看窗口：最新patch作为锚点，初始化隐状态并条件化后续每一步，使较旧patch被折叠进一个始终以近期证据为中心的表示；每一步复用同一共享模块，向更早历史扩展扫描不增加参数；扫描过程中保留的中间状态使预测头能分别权衡短程与长程历史，通过循环细化的顺序表达近期性。

Result: 在多个真实世界时间序列数据集上的大量实验表明，MARO在长期与短期预测任务上均取得state-of-the-art性能；消融实验进一步考察了主要架构组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过以最近patch为锚点的循环顺序细化，MARO在不增加参数的情况下显式表达了对近期证据的偏好，验证了处理顺序本身可作为编码近期性的有效机制，适用于长期与短期时序预测。

Abstract: Long-term forecasting models commonly process all patches in a look-back window using the same fixed stack. Older contextual patches and recent evidence therefore receive the same computational depth. Yet the information closest to the forecast and the more distant context do not contribute equally. Uniform processing leaves this distinction unexpressed in the architecture. We propose MARO, a Most-Recent Anchoring with Recurrent Ordering model that processes the look-back window from the most recent patch to the oldest. The most recent patch serves as the anchor. It initializes the latent state and conditions each subsequent step, so older patches are folded into a representation that remains centered on recent evidence. A single shared module is reused at every step, so extending the scan further into the past introduces no additional parameters. Intermediate states retained during the scan allow the forecast head to weigh short and long portions of the history separately. This expresses recency through the order of recurrent refinement. Extensive experiments across multiple real-world time series datasets show that MARO achieves state-of-the-art performance on both long-term and short-term forecasting tasks.Ablation studies examine the contribution of the main architectural components.

</details>


### [399] [Below what training size do deep tabular generators stop beating trivial baselines? A preregistered benchmark on a size ladder of clinical and standard datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03500)
*Shivam Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过预注册的"规模阶梯"基准测试（8个公开数据集从200到20,000行子采样，7种生成器，共2,220次运行）检验深度表格生成模型在小数据规模下是否优于简单基线，结果表明在绝大多数情况下深度模型并未超过最佳简单基线，且小规模下排序稳定性高于预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度表格生成模型通常在数万行的数据集上进行基准测试，而临床数据集往往只有数百行。作者希望探究这两个数据规模区间在方法排名上是否存在分歧，即大规模数据上的结论是否能推广到小规模数据。

Method: 预注册并运行规模阶梯基准：8个公开数据集子采样至200到20,000训练行，7种生成器（独立边际、高斯Copula、SMOTE、无条件SMOTE、CTGAN、TVAE、TabDDPM），固定20次试验调参预算与5个评估种子，另加4个原生小规模临床数据集（真实规模），共2,220次承诺运行。主要指标为在合成数据上训练、在真实数据上测试的固定分类器AUROC。

Result: 在24个（数据集，深度模型）组合中有23个，深度模型在任何训练规模下都未超过最佳简单基线超过种子噪声；最佳基线在49个（数据集，规模）单元中赢下40个。预注册的"小规模下深度模型排名不稳定"预测被证伪：5,000行以下相邻层级间平均Kendall tau为0.806（高于0.8阈值），且稳定性在最小规模时最高。81%的单元中前两名方法差距小于种子间变异。原生小规模临床数据集与子采样大规模数据集的排名仅中度一致（平均tau 0.57至0.64）。

Conclusion: 在小数据规模下，深度表格生成模型相对简单基线并无实质优势，且小规模下的方法排名比预期更稳定；子采样的大规模数据集不能可靠替代真实小数据集。所有结果文件、预注册及其哈希、以及重现全部图表和数字的代码均已公开。

Abstract: Deep tabular generative models are benchmarked on datasets with tens of thousands of rows; clinical datasets have hundreds. We preregistered and ran a size-ladder benchmark to find where the two regimes diverge: 8 public datasets subsampled from 200 to 20,000 training rows, seven generators (independent marginals, Gaussian copula, SMOTE, unconditional SMOTE, CTGAN, TVAE, TabDDPM) with a fixed 20-trial tuning budget and 5 evaluation seeds, plus 4 natively small clinical datasets at true size, for 2,220 committed runs in total. The primary metric is the AUROC of fixed classifiers trained on synthetic and tested on real data. In 23 of 24 (dataset, deep model) pairs no deep model ever beats the best trivial baseline by more than seed noise, at any training size we measured. The best baseline wins 40 of 49 (dataset, size) cells. Our preregistered prediction that the deep models' ranking would be unstable at small sizes is falsified: mean Kendall tau between adjacent rungs below 5,000 rows is 0.806, above our 0.8 threshold, and stability is highest at the smallest sizes rather than lowest. One caveat bounds all of this: in 81% of cells the gap between the top two methods is smaller than the variation between seeds. Finally, method rankings on natively small clinical datasets agree only moderately with rankings on subsampled large ones (mean tau 0.57 to 0.64), which questions whether a subsampled large dataset can stand in for a small one. All 2,220 result files, the preregistration and its hash, and the code that regenerates every figure and number from those files are public.

</details>


### [400] [Certified Mechanistic Edits: Behavioral Guarantees for Skill Removal and Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03502)
*Md Sazid Uddin,Md. Khairul Alam Mazumder,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出对神经网络机制编辑（消融、权重编辑、激活引导）的行为效果进行“认证”，而不仅靠测试：证明在连续输入区域内移除某一技能并保持另一技能，实现信息流安全意义上的特征非干扰保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机制编辑方法仅通过测试验证效果，无法覆盖整个连续输入区域；而可解释性-验证方向的工作只认证模型描述（如电路计算内容或解释忠实性），未认证编辑带来的行为效果。

Method: 从玩具ReLU网络到标准softmax+LayerNorm Transformer，对编辑效果进行认证，证明在连续嵌入空间区域上技能移除与保持；采用可靠界传播（sound bound propagation）替代精确求解器，将可处理的输入扰动维度提升约9倍；并证明不存在有限确定性黑盒测试能认证移除。

Result: 在连续嵌入空间区域上证明了移除与保持，处理维度约为精确求解器的9倍；构造出通过穷举测试却可证明在任意小“幸存者口袋”上失败的编辑，说明有限黑盒测试无法认证移除。保证在小规模标准架构网络上成立。

Conclusion: 认证性编辑为机制编辑提供了信息流安全式的保证，但仍有局限：仅适用于小规模标准架构网络，且与任何移除主张一样，预设目标技能具有可判定规范，而现实危害可能不具备该性质。

Abstract: Mechanistic edits (ablations, weight edits, activation steering) are the standard tools for unlearning a harmful capability from a neural network while preserving useful ones. Current approaches validate their effects only by testing, which can never cover an entire continuous region of inputs. Prior work at the interpretability-verification boundary certifies descriptions of a model: what a circuit computes, or whether it faithfully explains the whole. We instead certify the behavioral effect of an edit: that disabling a circuit removes one skill and provably preserves another, for every input in a region; a feature non-interference guarantee in the information-flow-security sense. We demonstrate such certified edits from toy ReLU networks up to a standard softmax + LayerNorm transformer, proving removal and preservation over continuous embedding-space regions and reaching roughly 9x the input-perturbation dimension an exact solver can handle by switching to sound bound propagation. Furthermore, we prove that no finite deterministic black-box test can certify removal, exhibiting an edit that passes exhaustive testing yet provably fails on a survivor pocket that can be made arbitrarily small. Guarantees hold on small, standard-architecture networks and, like any removal claim, presuppose that the target skill admits a decidable specification, a property which real-world harms may not have.

</details>


### [401] [Getting Your Guidance Weights Right in diffusion and flow-matching posterior sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03503)
*Liam Moroy,Jean-François Giovannelli,Yoann Altmann,Steve McLaughlin,Frédéric Champagnat,Guillaume Bourmaud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的离线策略，自动调节扩散/流匹配逆问题求解中的引导权重，将其归结为二维线性最小二乘问题，实现SOTA重建并大幅减少采样步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Plug-and-Play类后验采样方法依赖引导权重来平衡先验信息与测量一致性，但这些权重如何调节缺乏讨论，多依赖启发式设置。

Method: 观察到每步的条件去噪分数匹配（扩散）或条件流匹配（流匹配）目标都是最小二乘目标；当条件预测表示为无条件网络输出与测量引导项的加权和时，对权重的优化即化为二维线性最小二乘问题。由此可针对给定测量算子、噪声水平和采样器，用一个minibatch采样轨迹离线优化出时间相关的引导权重，无需重训练或微调预训练生成模型，并可直接与Tweedie测量一致性项结合。

Result: 在扩散与流匹配方法上均取得SOTA重建性能；优化后的引导权重可将采样步数从1000降至50而重建质量无明显下降。

Conclusion: 提出一种简单且有原则的离线引导权重自动调节方法，提升后验采样效果并显著降低采样成本，代码将公开。

Abstract: Training-free posterior sampling methods, also known as Plug-and-Play methods, leverage pretrained unconditional diffusion or flow-matching models to solve inverse problems. Most existing approaches rely on guidance weights to balance, at each time step, prior information from the unconditional score or velocity network with measurement consistency, yet the tuning of these weights is often not discussed and is largely left to heuristics. We introduce a simple and principled offline strategy for automatically tuning these guidance weights. Our key observation is that, at each time step, the conditional denoising score-matching objective for diffusion models, or the conditional flow-matching objective for flow-matching models, is a least-squares objective. Therefore, when the conditional prediction is expressed as a weighted sum of the unconditional network output and a measurement-guidance term, optimizing over these weights reduces to a two-dimensional linear least-squares problem. The resulting time-dependent guidance weights can be optimized offline for a given measurement operator, noise level and sampler at the cost of a single minibatch of sampling trajectories, without retraining or fine-tuning the pretrained generative model. Instantiated with the standard Tweedie-based measurement-consistency term, our approach improves posterior sampling and achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction performance across diffusion- and flow-matching-based methods. Moreover, the optimized guidance weights enable diffusion samplers to reduce the number of sampling steps from 1000 to 50 with no significant degradation in reconstruction quality. Code will be made available.

</details>


### [402] [An Automated and Reproducible Workflow for Crack Identification and Damage Assessment of Fusion Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03505)
*Rinkle Juneja,Viktor Reshniak,Richard K. Archibald,John W. Duggan,Gregory R. Watson,Cory D. Hauck,Gary M. Staebler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Galaxy科学工作流环境的可复现自动化工作流，用于从SEM图像中识别裂纹并量化损伤，并在钨材料电子束热冲击数据集上进行了演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代聚变材料研究中的曝光后显微分析面临图像数据量大、异质性强、多分辨率等特点，人工分析难以规模化，因此需要自动化、可复现的分析流程。

Method: 在Galaxy工作流环境中实现端到端流程，处理SEM图像与实验元数据，自动识别裂纹、量化损伤，并保留中间产物和处理历史；方法设计为无需针对特定图像调参，可跨钨牌号、显微组织、放大倍数和损伤状态运行。

Result: 在包含418张图像、114个实验、涵盖5种钨牌号和3种显微组织状态的稀疏电子束热冲击数据集上验证了工作流；输出包括裂纹掩膜、骨架化裂纹网络、质量控制可视化和标量损伤描述符，并定义了裂纹密度描述符。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提供了一个端到端、可共享、计算可移植的工作流，将实验表征、自动图像分析、初步损伤预测和模拟引导的数据采集连接起来，服务于聚变材料研究。

Abstract: Post-exposure microscopy is central to qualification of fusion materials. However, manual analysis does not scale to the volume, heterogeneity, and multiresolution character of modern fusion-materials campaigns. To address this challenge, we present a reproducible workflow, implemented in the Galaxy scientific workflow environment, for automated crack identification and quantitative damage assessment from scanning electron microscopy images. The workflow processes SEM images and experimental metadata to identify cracks, quantify damage, and retain the intermediate products and processing history needed for reproducibility. Outputs include crack masks, skeletonized crack networks, quality-control visualizations, and scalar damage descriptors. The method is designed to operate without image-specific parameter tuning across tungsten grades, microstructures, magnifications, and damage states. We demonstrate the workflow on a sparse electron-beam thermal-shock dataset containing 418 images from 114 experiments spanning five tungsten grades and three microstructural states. We define a crack-density descriptor, which provides standardized inputs for downstream machine-learning prediction and physics-based crack simulation. These predictive components are exposed in the same Galaxy environment and are intentionally treated here as extensible workflow modules. The principal contribution is therefore an end-to-end, shareable, and computationally portable workflow that links experimental characterization, automated image analysis, preliminary damage prediction, and simulation-guided data acquisition for fusion-materials research.

</details>


### [403] [Beyond Trained Models: Compiling GNNs for a Sound Explainer Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03526)
*Steve Azzolin,Francesco Paolo Nerini,Stefano Teso,Francesco Bonchi,Bruno Lepri,André Panisson,Andrea Passerini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对图神经网络解释器的合理性评估范式，指出其隐含假设（模型确实依赖预设"基元"）在常用基准上不成立，并提出用"编译"替代"训练"来消除混淆，构建了基于分级模态逻辑编译的 GNN 精确基准 GracrBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GNN 解释器评估普遍采用"合理性（plausibility）"指标，即衡量解释能否恢复数据中预植入的 ground truth（如 motif）。该协议隐含假设：在含植入基元的数据上训练的 GNN 确实依赖该基元。作者指出这一假设此前虽被质疑却仍被广泛采用，因此需要验证该假设是否成立，并在假设失效时提供更严谨的评估方式。

Method: 1) 首先在多个广泛使用的基准上验证上述假设：发现例如仅用度统计量即可完成任务，说明模型未必依赖所植入的基元，存在混淆因素。2) 用"编译"取代"训练"来消除该混淆：提出 Gracr，首个将分级模态逻辑（graded modal logic）公式编译为 GNN 权重的编译器，使所得模型精确复现对应公式的行为。3) 由于模型行为由构造决定、已知，故可形式化定义其 ground truth 解释并精确计算。4) 基于此构建 GracrBench：一个由编译所得 GNN 组成的基准，用于对照精确 ground truth 评估解释器。

Result: 在六个任务、十一种解释器上的实验表明，GracrBench 能进行细粒度诊断评估：尤其发现大多数解释器对"间接影响"以及"同一公式的替代实现"不具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究结果说明，常见基准中的合理性评估存在根本性缺陷，而 GracrBench 通过编译方式提供行为已知、ground truth 可精确计算的模型，为图后置可解释性评估提供了一个新颖且严谨的评估框架。

Abstract: Explainers for Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are commonly evaluated by their plausibility, i.e., how well their explanations recover a predefined ground truth, such as a motif planted in the data. This protocol implicitly assumes that a GNN trained on such data relies on the intended motif. Although prior work has questioned this assumption, plausibility remains widespread. First, we show that the assumption is violated on several widely used benchmarks, where, e.g., degree statistics alone suffice to solve the task. Then, we remove this confounder by replacing training with compilation. We achieve this by introducing $\mathsf{Gracr}$, the first compiler translating graded modal logic formulas into GNN weights, yielding models that replicate the behaviour of the corresponding formulas. Since the behaviour of the model is now known by construction, we can define its ground truth explanation formally and compute it exactly. Building on this, we introduce $\mathsf{Gracr}\mathsf{Bench}$, a benchmark of compiled GNNs for the evaluation of explainers against this exact ground truth. Experiments on eleven explainers across six tasks show its effectiveness for fine-grained diagnostic evaluation: notably, we discover that most explainers are not robust to indirect influences or alternative implementations of the same formula. These results position $\mathsf{Gracr}\mathsf{Bench}$ as a novel, rigorous evaluation setting for graph post-hoc explainability.

</details>


### [404] [Divergence controls entropy in distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03529)
*Nicolas Zucchet,Scott W. Linderman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从熵的视角研究大语言模型蒸馏：前向KL会使学生熵高于教师，反向KL则压低学生熵，散度本身充当隐式熵正则化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏已成为大语言模型训练的核心基础操作，但其性质尚未被充分理解。作者希望从熵的角度揭示蒸馏目标中数据与散度如何决定学生模型的熵行为。

Method: 采用熵的信息论分析视角，理论证明不同散度（前向KL、反向KL及其插值）对学生熵的影响，并在预训练和有监督微调中定量验证；同时对比在线策略蒸馏中token级反向KL与在线采样的作用。

Result: 证明前向KL使学生熵高于教师（交叉熵训练为其特例，并在预训练和SFT中得到定量验证）；反向KL持续压低熵直至师生差距过大；两者插值在训练早期平滑改变熵，但在收敛时出现突变。在线策略蒸馏的低熵来源于token级反向KL而非在线采样。

Conclusion: 散度充当隐式熵正则化器；在自蒸馏中，由于依赖特权信息会压低熵，使效果最佳的散度超参数正是那些能补偿这一效应的取值。

Abstract: Distillation has become a core primitive of large language model training, but its properties are not yet well understood. We take an entropic perspective, studying how the entropy of the student depends on the data and the divergence that define the distillation objective. We prove that forward KL inflates the entropy of the student above that of the teacher. Since cross-entropy training is a special case, this yields an identity that we verify quantitatively in pretraining and supervised finetuning. Other divergences come with no such guarantee: reverse KL deflates entropy until the gap between student and teacher gets too large, and interpolating between the two changes entropy smoothly early in training but abruptly at convergence. The lower entropy of on-policy distillation comes from token-level reverse KL, not from on-policy sampling. The divergence therefore acts as an implicit entropy regularizer, whose role is clearest in self-distillation: as conditioning on privileged information deflates entropy, the divergence hyperparameters that work best are those that compensate for it.

</details>


### [405] [ZeroMAG: Zero-Shot Multimodal Adapter Generation for Plug-and-Play EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03546)
*Yubo Wang,Jingying Ma,Xinliang Zhou,Yangxuan Zhou,Jiquan Wang,Sha Zhao,Yiyuan Yang,Yi Ding,Ziyu Jia,Chenyu Liu,Cuntai Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZeroMAG 是一种零样本多模态适配器生成框架，在冻结 EEG 基础模型的前提下，利用无标签目标数据生成适配器权重，将纯 EEG 模型扩展至多模态场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG 基础模型能从大规模 EEG 数据中获取可复用知识，但许多 EEG 记录还包含可提供互补信息的伴随生理信号。挑战在于：如何在保留预训练知识的同时，通过从无标签目标数据推断的适配，将模型扩展到异构多模态记录。

Method: ZeroMAG 保持 EEG 编码器与预测头冻结，围绕配置无关适配器组织伴随模态，从无标签记录与任务上下文构建"模态-被试-任务"条件，并在由源适配器学习到的函数约束潜空间中生成适配器权重。整个过程不使用目标标签，也不在目标端做优化。

Result: 在六个留出目标数据集和三种 EFM 主干上，ZeroMAG 的平衡准确率比纯 EEG 推理高 7.22 个百分点，比直接权重回归高 4.89 个百分点，平均仅比有监督多模态适配低 0.50 个百分点。消融实验表明，从表示学习或条件生成中移除函数监督都会降低生成适配器的性能。

Conclusion: ZeroMAG 能在无标签、无目标端优化的情况下有效扩展 EEG 基础模型至多模态，性能接近有监督多模态适配，且其表示学习与条件生成中的函数监督均为关键贡献。

Abstract: EEG foundation models (EFMs) capture reusable knowledge from large-scale EEG data, while many EEG recordings also include companion physiological signals that provide complementary information beyond the EEG-only interface. The challenge is to preserve this pretrained knowledge while extending the EFM to heterogeneous multimodal recordings through an adaptation inferred from unlabeled target data. We introduce ZeroMAG, a zero-shot multimodal adapter generation framework that extends a frozen EEG encoder and prediction head using unlabeled target recordings, without target labels or target-side optimization. The target datasets are held out from all model training and selection in the ZeroMAG pipeline. ZeroMAG organizes companion modalities around a configuration-invariant adapter, constructs a modality-subject-task condition from unlabeled recordings and task context, and generates adapter weights in a function-constrained latent space learned from source adapters. Across six held-out target datasets and three EFM backbones, ZeroMAG improves balanced accuracy by 7.22 percentage points over EEG-only inference and 4.89 points over direct weight regression, while coming within 0.50 points of supervised multimodal adaptation on average. Ablations further show that removing functional supervision from either representation learning or conditional generation degrades generated-adapter performance, confirming the contribution of both components.

</details>


### [406] [Objects Without Morphisms: What LLMs for Mathematics Do Not Represent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03551)
*Yanli Wang,Suijin Wang,Xiaopeng Yuan,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM在没有外部评判标准时的翻译能力，发现模型在跨子领域方言翻译中系统性地改变假设的普遍性水平：向一般化框架翻译时60.6%扩大了量化域，向具体框架翻译时28.3%缩小了量化域，且这一不对称性不受模型能力控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 在竞赛数学上的表现主要依赖大量搜索和外部筛选标准，这种方式只改进了最终存活的答案，却没有触及模型内部的表征。论文想探究在没有任何外部评判标准的情况下——即跨数学子领域方言的忠实翻译任务中——模型内部究竟表征了什么。

Method: 作者设计了一套工具，分别用独立的盲队列对真值、内容和范围进行编码，并使用无需评判者的度量来判断改写是否陈述了源文本隐含的假设，同时通过植入阳性对照确立方法的敏感性。在来自四个家族的七个模型上进行测试，并在预注册规则留出的基准子集以及数学家撰写的陈述上验证。

Result: 向一般化框架翻译时，60.6%的改写扩大了量化域，没有一例缩小；向具体框架翻译时，28.3%缩小、0.3%扩大。防止该问题的假设在模型改写中仅21.6%、人类陈述中仅4.2%被明确陈述。这种不对称出现在所有被测模型中，能力最强的模型扩大最少。指令模型陈述所需假设可提高陈述率，但不改变其对方向的敏感性。

Conclusion: 这些系统习得了子领域词汇之间的对象级对应关系，但缺乏理论间翻译所需的约束——即假设应映射到假设。这表明评测所见的数学能力提升并未反映模型内部表征的实质改进。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have reached expert-level performance on competition mathematics largely through the volume of search placed around them: candidate solutions are sampled in quantity and retained only when an external criterion accepts them. Such a procedure improves the outcome that survives it while leaving untouched what the model represents. We examine that question where no external criterion exists: translating statements between the dialects of neighbouring subfields, where fidelity turns on the level of generality at which content is asserted. The source leaves that level implicit in its vocabulary, so a faithful translation must recover it from the relation between the theories. We introduce an instrument that codes truth, content and scope in separate blind queues, with a judge-free measure of whether a rewrite states the hypothesis implicit in its source, and establish its sensitivity with a planted-positive control. Across seven models from four families, translating towards the general framing widens the domain of quantification in 60.6% of rewrites and narrows it in none; translating towards the concrete framing narrows it in 28.3% and widens it in 0.3%. The hypothesis that would prevent it is stated in 21.6% of model rewrites and 4.2% of human statements. Capability does not govern the asymmetry: it appears in every model tested, and the most capable widens least. It replicates on the half of the benchmark held out by a pre-registered rule, and on statements written by mathematicians. Instructing a model to state every hypothesis it requires raises that rate but not its sensitivity to direction. We argue that these systems have acquired an object-level correspondence between subfield vocabularies without the constraint under which a translation between theories carries hypotheses to hypotheses.

</details>


### [407] [Get a GRIP, this will be a long TRIP: A Quantifiable Long-Range Framework for Verifying Over-squashing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03556)
*Ferran Hernandez Caralt,Simon Heilig,Adrián Bazaga,Asja Fischer,Moshe Eliasof,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出长程图基准缺乏严格刻画，提出四条可验证公理并据此构建TRIP/GRIP基准生成框架，可证明地构造长程任务并提供闭式误差下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN中过挤压与长程交互的实证结论依赖基准是否真正需要长程交互，但事实标准LRGB已被证明可被短程模型饱和，合成基准又依赖特定拓扑，缺乏在任意图上证明长程性的原则性证书，根源在于缺少对长程基准的精确刻画。

Method: 提出四条可验证公理：Predictability、Tightness、Strictly k-Range、Topology-Invariance，并证明违反任一公理都会引入削弱结论的失败模式；据此设计TRIP（Truly Ranged Interactions Problem）及泛化GRIP（Generally Ranged Interactions Problem），通过从稳定分布抽取特征，将任意图构造为可证明的长程任务；GRIP还具有闭式、逐range的极大似然oracle，给出首个先验的逐range测试误差下界。

Result: (i) 审计4个常见长程基准并识别其相对公理的失败模式；(ii) 在TRIP实例化拓扑上，发现流行的曲率概念与GNN性能不相关，支持拓扑瓶颈与计算瓶颈的区分；(iii) 展示新基准的过挤压度量还受纯长程性之外的因素影响。

Conclusion: 该工作通过公理化刻画长程基准，提供可证明长程且拓扑无关的TRIP/GRIP框架及逐range误差下界，为长程GNN研究提供了原则性评估工具，代码已开源。

Abstract: Empirical claims about the connection between over-squashing and long-range interactions in GNNs, can only be trusted if the benchmarks used to validate them genuinely require long-range interactions. The de-facto standard, the Long Range Graph Benchmark, has been repeatedly shown to be saturated by tuned short-range models, with existing synthetic alternatives being tied to specific topologies. As such, there is a lack of principled certificate of long-rangedness on arbitrary graphs. This state reflects the absence of a precise characterization of long-ranged benchmarks. We address this fundamental gap by introducing four verifiable axioms: Predictability, Tightness, Strictly $k$-Range, and Topology-Invariance, that any task claiming to test $k$-hop interactions must satisfy. We formally prove that violating any one of them admits failure modes that undermine conclusions drawn from the task. Based on these axioms, we introduce TRIP (Truly Ranged Interactions Problem) and its generalisation GRIP (Generally Ranged Interactions Problem), constructive procedures that turn any graph into a provably long-ranged task by drawing features from stable distributions. Moreover, by construction, GRIP admits a closed-form, per-range Maximum-Likelihood oracle that yields the first a priori per-range lower bound on test error available on any benchmark. Using our framework, we: (i) audit 4 common long-range benchmarks and identify their failures modes with respect to our axioms; (ii) on TRIP-instantiated topologies, we find a popular notion of curvature is uncorrelated with GNN performance, supporting topological-vs-computational bottleneck distinction; and (iii) we show that a novel benchmark's over-squashing measures factors beyond pure long-rangedness. Code to use the framework and reproduce experiments is released https://github.com/ferranhernandezc/graph-grip.

</details>


### [408] [Cephalonauts One: A deep fMRI dataset for decoding naturalistic speech in the human brain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03558)
*Antoine Collas,Louis Jalouzot,Géraud Ilinca,Corentin Caris,Romain Valabrègue,Ahmed Hassayoune,David Goncalves,Madeleine Hueber,Thaddée Delebarre,Julien Savatovsky,Clara Fonteneau,Charles Maussion,Bertrand Thirion,Alexis Thual*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Cephalonauts One 是一个 3T 全脑 fMRI 数据集，记录被试聆听播客时的脑活动，每位被试 30 小时数据，为目前使用自然语音刺激的最深 fMRI 数据集，并提供音频片段检索式脑解码基准及缩放分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语音刺激下的大规模 fMRI 数据稀缺，现有数据集深度不足，难以支撑数据驱动的脑解码研究，也无法评估解码性能随数据量的变化趋势。

Method: 三名健康被试接受多次扫描，每次包含 5 个 15 分钟 run，聆听母语播客；采集 3T 全脑 fMRI，并配套播客音频、转录标注与刺激嵌入。提出以音频片段检索为形式的脑解码基准：给定留出 session 的 fMRI 活动，在候选片段中识别时间对齐的播客音频，提供标准划分、评价指标和基线解码器，并开展数据量缩放分析。

Result: 每位被试 30 小时 fMRI 数据，为使用自然语音刺激的最深公开 fMRI 数据集；基线解码器在检索任务上给出性能参考；缩放分析显示解码性能随训练数据量增加而持续提升。

Conclusion: 该数据集与基准为自然语音脑解码研究提供深层数据资源和可复现的评测框架，并表明增大数据量可继续提升解码性能。

Abstract: Cephalonauts One is a whole-brain 3 Tesla (3T) functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) dataset recorded while subjects listened to audio podcasts. Three healthy subjects underwent multiple scanning sessions, each consisting of five 15-minute runs, while listening to podcasts in their native language. With 30 hours of fMRI data per subject, the current release is the deepest available fMRI dataset using naturalistic speech stimuli. The dataset pairs brain activity with the corresponding podcast audio, transcript annotations, and derived stimulus embeddings. Furthermore, we introduce a brain decoding benchmark formulated as audio segment retrieval: given fMRI activity from a held-out session, the decoder must identify the corresponding time-aligned podcast audio segment among candidate segments. We provide standardized splits, evaluation metrics, and baseline decoders for this task. Finally, a scaling analysis shows that decoding performance improves continuously with the amount of training data per subject.

</details>


### [409] [A Path Integral Surrogate for Multi-Step Gradient Inversion in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03597)
*Agnivo Ghosh,Saumik Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PI-SME，将联邦学习中的累积更新视为梯度场的路径积分，用高斯-勒让德求积在可学习贝塞尔路径的多个节点上逼近，从而更准确地从单次模型更新中重建客户端私有图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习的安全性依赖“模型更新泄露信息远少于原始数据”这一前提，而梯度反转攻击正是对此的直接挑战。在FedAvg下，客户端更新累积了多步本地训练，服务器只能看到隐藏权重轨迹的两个端点。现有梯度反转攻击虽然沿两端点之间拟合代理模型，但仅在单点读取梯度，忽略了整条轨迹的信息，导致重建精度受限。

Method: 提出的路径积分代理模型扩展（PI-SME）把累积更新看作梯度场的路径积分，并用高斯-勒让德求积在一条可学习贝塞尔路径上的多个节点处进行近似，从而在优化中同时利用轨迹上多个位置的梯度信息，而非单一采样点。方法在CIFAR-100与FEMNIST图像上，针对不同轨迹长度和类别受限批次进行了验证。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和FEMNIST图像上，跨越多种轨迹长度与类别受限批次设置，PI-SME在多项反转指标以及匹配损失上均优于最强的代理模型基线，能更忠实地重建客户端私有输入。

Conclusion: 将FedAvg累积更新建模为梯度场的路径积分并配合多点求积，可显著提升梯度反转攻击的重建质量，表明单点代理模型低估了更新所携带的隐私泄露风险，对联邦学习的隐私防护提出了更高要求。

Abstract: Federated learning lets many clients train a shared model together without ever sending their private data to a central server. Each client shares only a model update, and this update should reveal far less about the client than its raw training examples would. This premise is what protects the privacy of the clients. Gradient inversion attacks challenge it directly by trying to reconstruct a client's private input images from the single update it shared. Under FedAvg, a client's update accumulates several local training steps, so the server sees only the two endpoints of a hidden weight trajectory. Recent gradient inversion attacks fit a surrogate model along the path between these two endpoints but they still read its gradient at a single point. We propose the Path-Integral Surrogate Model Extension (PI-SME) which treats the accumulated update as a path integral of the gradient field and approximates it by Gauss--Legendre quadrature over several nodes along a learnable Bézier path. On CIFAR-100 and FEMNIST images across a range of trajectory lengths and class-restricted batches PI-SME reconstructs the private inputs more faithfully than the strongest surrogate baseline on several inversion metrics and the matching loss.

</details>


### [410] [Mastering Atari 2600 Games with Discovered Options](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03604)
*Erik M. Lintunen,Marlos C. Machado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Wayfarer，一种通用的、域无关的在线深度 RL 智能体，通过从高维观测中学习拉普拉斯表征来发现选项（options），并用于控制，从而提升探索、加速信用分配与泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序抽象（如选项）长期被认为能加速信用分配、促进探索和实现泛化，但开发在大规模、高维领域中有效且通用的选项发现方法仍是根本性挑战。现有方法要么局限于简单域，要么依赖手工设计或准符号化表征，要么相比无选项学习提升甚微。

Method: Wayfarer 从高维观测出发，通过拉普拉斯表征学习（Laplacian representation learning）在线发现选项，并将这些选项用于控制，是一个通用的、域无关的在线深度 RL 智能体。

Result: 所得到的选项同时改善探索、加速信用分配，并能有效泛化到未见场景，显著加快复杂策略的学习。Wayfarer 在最困难的 Atari 2600 游戏中达到单流（single-stream）智能体的最优性能，在需要长时程探索和策略行为的游戏（如 Montezuma's Revenge 和 Private Eye）中增益最大。

Conclusion: 通过学习拉普拉斯表征来发现选项，可构建通用且域无关的在线深度 RL 智能体，在高维困难任务上实现探索、信用分配与泛化的协同提升，并取得单流智能体的最先进性能。

Abstract: Temporal abstractions, often instantiated as options, have long been regarded as a mechanism for accelerating credit assignment, facilitating exploration, and enabling generalisation in reinforcement learning (RL). However, developing general option discovery methods that are effective in large-scale, high-dimensional domains remains a fundamental challenge. Existing option discovery methods are either confined to relatively simple domains, depend on handcrafted or quasi-symbolic representations, or offer little improvement over learning without options. We present Wayfarer, a general, domain-agnostic, online deep RL agent that discovers options through Laplacian representation learning from high-dimensional observations and leverages them for control. We show that the resulting options simultaneously improve exploration, accelerate credit assignment, and generalise effectively to unseen settings, enabling substantially faster learning of complex policies. Wayfarer achieves state-of-the-art performance among single-stream agents on the most challenging Atari 2600 games, with the largest gains in games that require long-horizon exploration and strategic behaviour, such as Montezuma's Revenge and Private Eye.

</details>


### [411] [UniIntervene++: An Adaptive Intervention Agent for Efficient Real-World Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03620)
*Yudong Lin,Haoyuan Deng,Zhuoxuan Yuan,Zaijia Yang,Yuanjiang Xue,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniIntervene++是一种自适应干预代理，在在线强化学习中动态分配自主执行与异构辅助行为之间的控制权。它在五个真实操作任务上平均成功率89.67%，人类干预降低至0.77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习使机器人可通过物理交互提升策略，但随着能力演化，其所需辅助也在变化。基于离线估计或固定决策规则的现有干预策略会与当前策略不匹配。

Method: 将演化中的RL策略、轨迹修正与任务结构化的CodePolicy统一建模为半马尔可夫决策过程中的Options并在线学习其相对价值；引入能力自适应干预，通过无辅助执行周期性探测RL策略；采用耦合经验学习，使辅助行为改进RL策略并反作用于未来干预决策。从而联合决定何时干预、如何干预以及何时归还控制权。

Result: 在五个真实操作任务上平均成功率达89.67%，至少超过所有基线6个百分点；人类干预降至0.77%，相较最佳基线至少相对减少94.6%。

Conclusion: UniIntervene++通过能力自适应与耦合经验学习，实现高效的在线强化学习干预，显著提升成功率并大幅减少人类干预。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) enables robot policies to improve through physical interaction, but the assistance they require changes as their competence evolves. Existing intervention strategies based on offline estimates or fixed decision rules can therefore become mismatched to the current policy. To address this, we propose UniIntervene++, an adaptive intervention agent that learns to allocate control between autonomous execution and heterogeneous assisted behaviors during online RL. Specifically, UniIntervene++ first formulates the evolving RL policy, trajectory correction, and a task-structured CodePolicy as Options in a unified semi-Markov decision process and learns their relative values online. Building on this, competence-adaptive intervention periodically probes the RL policy through unassisted execution, keeping control allocation responsive to its evolving capability. Finally, coupled experience learning allows assisted behaviors to improve the RL policy, whose evolving outcomes in turn reshape future intervention decisions. In this way, UniIntervene++ jointly determines when to intervene, how to intervene, and when to return control as the RL policy improves. Across five real-world manipulation tasks, UniIntervene++ achieves an average success rate of 89.67%, outperforming all baselines by at least 6 percentage points, while reducing human intervention to 0.77%, a relative reduction of at least 94.6% from the best baseline. Code is available in our \href{https://github.com/dannyyudong/An-Adaptive-Intervention-Agent-for-Efficient-Real-World-Reinforcement-Learning}{GitHub repository}.

</details>


### [412] [Broken scale symmetries in undercomplete linear autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03640)
*Farhad Pashakhanloo,Jacob A. Zavatone-Veth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究欠完备线性自编码器中SGD对参数尺度对称性的破坏，发现SGD在PCA解流形上偏好大解码器权重的定向尺度漂移，并给出可解析的有效描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络损失景观具有对称性，梯度流保持对称性而有限步长SGD破坏它。已有工作记录了SGD打破尺度对称以平衡梯度噪声或最小化波动的案例，但尚不清楚在欠完备线性自编码器这类具体几何结构下，噪声如何转化为沿功能等价解流形的定向运动。

Method: 以欠完备线性自编码器为模型，分析其在PCA解流形上的尺度漂移行为；通过可解析的有效描述刻画慢时间尺度上的定向尺度漂移动力学，并考察其与有限步长稳定性边界的关系及损失Hessian最大特征值等锐度度量。

Result: 在PCA解流形上，SGD偏好大的解码器权重，产生方向明确的尺度漂移；该漂移发生在慢时间尺度且具有可解析的有效描述；但它不会无限持续，增大的尺度最终将动力学推向有限步长稳定性边界，所得解在Hessian最大特征值意义上比平衡基线更尖锐，而不同锐度度量可能朝相反方向变化。

Conclusion: 欠完备自编码器具体展示了损失几何如何将残余梯度噪声转化为沿功能等价解流形的定向运动，说明SGD的尺度漂移方向由模型几何结构选择，而非单纯的噪声平衡。

Abstract: Neural network loss landscapes have many symmetries, which are preserved by gradient flow but broken by finite-stepsize stochastic gradient descent (SGD). A canonical example of such a symmetry is scale in homogeneous networks: one can scale up the parameters in one layer and down in the next without changing the network output. Previous work has documented cases in which SGD breaks this symmetry in favor of balancing gradient noise or minimizing fluctuations. Here, we show that the solution geometry of undercomplete linear autoencoders instead selects a preferred sign for scale drift: on the PCA solution manifold, SGD favors large decoder weights. This directed scale drift occurs on a slow timescale, and its dynamics admit an analytically-tractable effective description. However, it cannot continue indefinitely: increasing scale eventually drives the dynamics towards a finite-stepsize stability boundary. The resulting solutions are sharper than a balanced baseline in the sense of the maximum eigenvalue of the loss Hessian, but different sharpness measures can move in opposing directions. Thus, undercomplete autoencoders give a concrete illustration of how loss geometry can convert residual gradient noise into directed motion along a manifold of functionally-equivalent solutions.

</details>


### [413] [IDRF: Inverse-Distilled Reward Fine-tuning of Masked Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03641)
*Vladislav Gromadskii,David Li,Samson Gourevitch,Yazid Janati,Eric Moulines,Maxim Panov,Alexander Korotin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IDRF框架，用逆蒸馏正则替代不可解的序列级KL惩罚，实现少步掩码离散扩散生成器的奖励微调，最多减少32倍去噪步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码离散扩散模型是自回归生成的有力替代方案，但其迭代采样成本高昂，且序列似然不可解，导致奖励微调困难。

Method: 从标准反向KL正则目标出发，用逆蒸馏正则替代不可解的序列级KL惩罚；在最优辅助去噪器下证明群体逆蒸馏损失上界序列级KL散度；优化该损失的轨迹代理（无需参考模型rollout），将少步生成视为有限时域MDP，用裁剪策略梯度目标优化奖励。

Result: 在DNA、图像和文本生成任务上，IDRF取得高奖励，去噪步数比参考模型最多减少32倍，同时缓解奖励劫持并保持样本质量。

Conclusion: IDRF为少步掩码离散扩散生成器提供了有效的奖励微调方案，兼顾高效采样、高奖励和样本质量。

Abstract: Masked discrete diffusion models offer a promising alternative to autoregressive generation, but iterative sampling can be costly, and intractable sequence likelihoods complicate reward fine-tuning. We introduce IDRF, a framework for reward fine-tuning of few-step masked discrete diffusion generators. Starting from a standard reverse-KL-regularized objective, IDRF replaces the intractable sequence-level KL penalty with inverse-distillation regularization. With an optimal auxiliary denoiser, we prove that the population inverse-distillation loss upper-bounds the sequence-level KL divergence to the reference distribution. IDRF optimizes a trajectory-based surrogate of this loss without reference-model rollouts, so the student keeps its own few-step sampler. We view few-step generation as a finite-horizon Markov decision process and optimize reward with a clipped policy-gradient objective over the student's trajectories. Across DNA, image, and text generation, IDRF achieves high reward with up to $32\times$ fewer denoising steps than the reference while mitigating reward hacking and preserving sample quality.

</details>


### [414] [On the Convergence of Success Conditioning for Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03642)
*Matthew Brun,Xu Andy Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究成功条件化（success conditioning）策略在随机环境中的收敛性与收敛速率，证明其在一大类MDP上收敛到最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成功条件化广泛用于强化学习，但其极限行为和收敛速率尚不清楚，缺乏理论保证。

Method: 通过理论分析，研究成功条件化在MDP上的收敛性质，并针对折扣MDP和单周期MDP推导收敛速率。

Result: 折扣MDP中以O(1/ε^p)次迭代收敛到ε-最优策略（指数p依赖问题数据）；单周期MDP中以O(log(1/ε))次迭代获得该策略。

Conclusion: 成功条件化在一大类MDP上收敛到最优策略，且在不同设定下具有明确的收敛速率保证。

Abstract: Success conditioning is a strategy for improving decision-making policies in stochastic environments; it updates a policy by increasing the probability of taking actions that yield successful outcomes. Success conditioning is common to many reinforcement learning applications, yet its limiting behavior and convergence rates are not well understood. In this work, we demonstrate that success conditioning converges to an optimal policy on a broad class of Markov decision processes (MDPs). We also derive convergence rates in some common settings. For discounted MDPs, we prove convergence within $\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon^p)$ iterations to an $\varepsilon$-optimal policy, where the exponent $p$ depends on problem data. For single-period MDPs, such a policy is obtained within $\mathcal{O}(\log(1/\varepsilon))$ iterations.

</details>


### [415] [When May a Bandit Leave Its Anchor? E-Process-Authorized Thompson Sampling under Non-stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03646)
*Mayand Gulati,Kerong Wang,WeiChen Au*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 e-ATS 方法，通过 anytime-valid e-process 授权折扣状态的使用，从而在非平稳环境中实现受控的“遗忘”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平稳环境下历史信息有益，但环境变化后旧历史可能产生误导。核心问题是：何时应当允许“遗忘”旧信息。

Method: e-ATS 为每个臂维护全历史和折扣 Beta 状态；先用 anytime-valid e-process 授权折扣状态，再用可逆的相关性分数控制其影响。在授权前，e-ATS 完全遵循乐观 Thompson 采样（OTS）。

Result: 在 Beta-Bernoulli 先验预测平稳模型下，e-ATS 偏离 OTS 的概率不超过选定阈值 α_E，且无需拟合阈值。去掉授权机制后，注册测试集上平均归一化动态伪遗憾增加 38.4%，但文献回放测试集上减少 7.5%。

Conclusion: 证据决定适应何时开始，而非保证适应总是有益。

Abstract: Stationarity rewards memory, but after a change the same history can mislead. We ask when forgetting should be permitted. E-process-authorized Thompson sampling (e-ATS) gives each arm full-history and discounted Beta states. An anytime-valid e-process first authorizes the discounted state, then a reversible relevance score controls its influence. Before authorization, e-ATS exactly follows optimistic Thompson sampling (OTS). Under a Beta-Bernoulli prior-predictive stationary model, e-ATS's probability of ever departing from OTS is at most the chosen $α_E$, without fitted thresholds. Relative to e-ATS, removing authorization increased mean normalized dynamic pseudo-regret by $38.4\%$ on the registered suite but reduced it by $7.5\%$ on the literature-derived replay suite. Therefore, evidence controls when adaptation begins, not whether it always helps.

</details>


### [416] [Forecasting from Counterfactual Simulator Rollouts: A Sim2Real Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03662)
*Angel Wang,Dominique Perrault-Joncas,Alvaro Maggiar,Dean Foster,Carson Eisenach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过仿真生成的"反事实"数据可解决新决策策略下的预测冷启动问题，并可在真实部署后进一步校准优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署新决策策略时，预测模型面临冷启动：历史观测反映的是旧策略，而新策略下的真实观测尚不可得，导致模型难以学习系统对新控制动作的响应。

Method: 利用仿真在各反事实情景中展开目标策略，生成轨迹用于训练预测模型，再将此"仿真到现实（Sim2Real）"模型与真实历史部署观测进行回测。以两个真实库存控制部署为例，从三个角度评估：仿真保真度、零样本迁移到真实行为、以及随真实目标策略观测积累的适应能力。

Result: 仿真训练的预测器MAPE低于同一架构用历史真实数据训练的结果：研究1降低1.2–3.1个百分点，研究2降低12.5–18.7个百分点；部署后利用早期真实观测进行轻量校准，可将误差进一步最多降低2.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 实证表明，仿真生成的反事实数据可支撑新策略下的冷启动预测，且模型可随真实部署数据的积累而持续优化。

Abstract: Deploying a new decision policy creates a cold-start problem for prediction models whose targets depend on the policy's actions: historical observations reflect earlier policies, while real observations under the new policy are not yet available. Simulation offers a way to address this gap by rolling out the target policy across counterfactual scenarios and using the resulting trajectories to learn how the system responds to those controls. The simulation-to-reality (Sim2Real) transfer of this simulator-trained model can then be backtested by evaluating it against real observations from past deployments. Using two real-world inventory-control deployments, we evaluate this process from three angles: simulator fidelity, zero-shot transfer to real behavior, and adaptation as real target-policy observations accumulate. The simulator-trained forecaster achieves lower point-estimate mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) than the same architecture trained on historical real data, reducing MAPE by 1.2-3.1 percentage points in Study 1 and 12.5-18.7 points in Study 2. After deployment, lightweight calibration using early real observations further reduces error by up to 2.5 percentage points. These results provide empirical evidence that simulator-generated counterfactual data can support cold-start forecasting under a new policy, and the resulting model can be further refined as real deployment data become available.

</details>


### [417] [Pivot-SD: Efficient Self-Distillation for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03665)
*Seo Hyun Kim,Sunwoo Hong,Younwoo Choi,Chen-Hao Chao,Se-Young Yun,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Pivot-SD 是一个高效的离线自蒸馏框架，通过信息增益指标筛选出对去噪过程影响最大的"枢纽"token（pivots），仅对这些关键承诺进行监督：成功轨迹的枢纽用交叉熵训练，失败轨迹的枢纽用针对性非似然训练。仅用200个问题和4次采样，即在数学和代码基准上超越全序列SFT和扩散RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型（dLMs）在复杂推理上是自回归模型的并行替代方案，但存在独特的信用分配难题：去噪过程中少数几个承诺会急剧降低剩余掩码位置的不确定性，并塑造大部分响应内容。然而多数dLM后训练方法并未利用这一信号来挑选训练token——它们要么在最终文本上训练，要么把奖励分配给整个去噪步骤，而非选择塑造响应的单个关键承诺。

Method: 提出 Pivot-SD 离线自蒸馏框架，仅监督高影响力的关键承诺（pivots）。采用信息增益指标衡量对剩余掩码位置的不确定性削减程度来筛选枢纽；成功轨迹的枢纽使用交叉熵训练，失败轨迹的枢纽使用针对性非似然（targeted unlikelihood）训练，失败轨迹的其余部分保持不变。

Result: 在仅使用200个问题、每个问题4次采样的条件下，Pivot-SD 在数学和代码基准上均优于全序列SFT以及预算匹配的扩散RL基线，提升了 LLaDA-8B-Instruct 的表现。

Conclusion: 通过将监督信号聚焦于塑造响应的少数高影响力承诺，Pivot-SD 实现了高效且有效的dLM后训练，以极低的训练数据与计算成本取得了超越现有SFT和扩散RL方法的性能。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (dLMs) offer a promising parallel alternative to autoregressive models for complex reasoning. However, they face a distinct credit-assignment challenge, since a few commitments during denoising sharply reduce the uncertainty over the remaining masked positions and shape much of the response. Most post-training recipes for dLMs do not use this signal to decide which tokens to train on: they typically train on the final text or assign rewards to whole denoising steps, rather than selecting the individual commitments that shape the response. We introduce Pivot-SD, an efficient offline self-distillation framework that supervises only these high-impact commitments (pivots). Pivot-SD selects pivots using an information-gain metric measuring uncertainty reduction over the remaining masked positions. Pivots from successful trajectories are trained with cross-entropy, and pivots from failed trajectories with targeted unlikelihood, leaving the rest of the failed trajectory untouched. Using only 200 questions and four rollouts each, Pivot-SD improves LLaDA-8B-Instruct over full-sequence SFT and budget-matched diffusion RL baselines across math and code benchmarks.

</details>


### [418] [Planning to Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03667)
*Ian Osband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将分类视为一个策略梯度问题，发现精确策略梯度因"短视"而劣于交叉熵，提出将交叉熵解释为"耐心的准确率"（无限视界），并据此设计"视界损失"（horizon loss），在 MNIST 与 ImageNet 上以多种网络结构验证其可提升 top-1 准确率，且标签噪声越大增益越明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略梯度方法在强化学习中至关重要，但常受探索、信用分配与动作采样噪声困扰。分类任务不存在这些问题：其期望奖励即期望准确率，标签已知，梯度精确且平滑。然而精确策略梯度仍败给交叉熵（即便在期望准确率指标上）。作者希望解释这一反直觉现象并加以改进。

Method: 作者指出精确策略梯度是"短视"的：它只以当前收益评估更新，而每次更新还决定了下一步的起点，因此更新的价值取决于剩余学习量。据此，将交叉熵解释为"耐心的准确率"——即若某样本的对数几率以单位速度永远上升，它将付出的总误差；而精确策略梯度对应零视界极限。将这一总量在"剩余学习量"处截断，得到"视界损失"，只需一行改动，且在训练接近结束时从交叉熵平滑过渡到精确策略梯度。

Result: 在一个简单的分配模型中，视界损失可证明地避开了两个端点各自会陷入的陷阱。在 MNIST 以及使用 ResNet-50、ResNet-101、ViT-S/16 的 ImageNet 上，在固定学习率下，视界损失相对交叉熵提升了 top-1 准确率，且增益随标签噪声增加而增大。

Conclusion: 分类可被理解为一种无探索、无采样噪声的精确策略梯度问题，其关键在于视界长度。交叉熵与精确策略梯度分别是无限视界与零视界的两个端点，而视界损失通过按剩余学习量截断，兼具二者优势，在理论与实证上均优于交叉熵，为分类训练目标提供了新的视角与实用改进。

Abstract: Policy-gradient methods are central to modern reinforcement learning, including LLM post-training. When they struggle, the usual suspects are exploration, credit assignment and action-sampling noise. Classification has none of them. A classifier is a policy whose expected reward, its \emph{expected accuracy}, is the probability it assigns to the correct label, and because that label is known, the policy gradient is exact and smooth. Yet exact policy gradient loses to cross-entropy, even on expected accuracy. The exact gradient is myopic: it values an update only by what it buys now, but each update also sets where the next one starts, so an update's value depends on how much learning remains. Viewed this way, cross-entropy is patient accuracy, the total error an example would pay if its log-odds rose at unit speed forever, while exact policy gradient is the zero-horizon limit. Truncating this total at the learning that remains yields the horizon loss, a one-line change that moves from cross-entropy toward exact policy gradient as training runs out. In a simple allocation model, it provably escapes the trap that catches each endpoint. On MNIST and on ImageNet with ResNet-50, ResNet-101 and ViT-S/16, the horizon loss improves top-1 accuracy over cross-entropy at a flat learning rate, and the gain grows with label noise.

</details>


### [419] [Simulation-Free Learning of Population Dynamics with Wasserstein Lagrangian Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03679)
*Fedor Sergeev,Markus Heinonen,Daniel Waxman,Tim Cooijmans,Ricardo Baptista,Dmitry Batenkov,Eli Bingham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Double-Stitch：一种无模拟方法，通过学习人口路径上运动方程残差惩罚来学习Wasserstein空间中的拉格朗日力学，训练速度比传统WLM快4-14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞、生物体和流体的动力学常被建模为随时间演化的概率分布。从非配对快照中重建和推断这种演化需要对底层过程做假设。Wasserstein梯度流是常见选择，但无法描述保守或周期性动力学。Wasserstein空间中的拉格朗日力学可覆盖两者，但现有学习方法基于模拟，需在每个训练步骤运行数值求解器，训练代价高昂。

Method: 提出Double-Stitch，一种无模拟方法，通过惩罚沿学习到的人口路径上运动方程的残差来学习力学。该运动方程从Clebsch变分原理推导，无需梯度速度（gradient velocities），并证明当方程成立时残差恰好为零。

Result: 在合成数据集、单细胞数据集和海洋涡旋数据集上测试Double-Stitch，发现其在多数任务上匹配或超越梯度流方法和基于模拟的WLM，同时训练速度比WLM快4-14倍。提供JAX实现。

Conclusion: Double-Stitch通过无模拟的残差惩罚方式学习Wasserstein空间中的拉格朗日力学，兼顾保守与周期动力学建模能力，并在性能与训练效率上优于现有方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: The dynamics of cells, organisms, and fluids are often modeled as probability distributions evolving over time. Reconstructing and extrapolating this evolution from unpaired snapshots requires assumptions about the underlying process. Wasserstein gradient flows are a common choice, but they cannot describe conservative or periodic dynamics. Lagrangian mechanics in Wasserstein space covers both, but existing methods for learning it are simulation-based: they run a numerical solver at every training step, which makes training expensive. We propose Double-Stitch, a simulation-free method that learns these mechanics by penalizing the residual of the equation of motion along a learned population path. We derive this equation from a Clebsch variational principle that does not require gradient velocities, and show that the residual vanishes exactly when the equation holds. We test Double-Stitch on synthetic, single-cell and ocean vortex datasets and find that it matches or outperforms gradient-flow methods and simulation-based WLM on most tasks, while training $4$-$14$ times faster than WLM. We provide a JAX implementation of Double-Stitch at https://github.com/BasisResearch/stitching.

</details>


### [420] [LESSER: Post-Training Data Selection with Output-Layer Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03702)
*Lyuxin David Zhang,Eric Wong,Surbhi Goel,Anton Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LESSER：用输出层梯度替代全参数梯度进行训练数据选择，只需前向传播，大幅降低计算成本且保持下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
