Motivation: 基于梯度的数据选择对下游性能影响显著，但全参数梯度排序需要对每个样本做昂贵的反向传播，面对大规模候选池时计算不可行。因此需要探索能否以更低成本近似全梯度特征。

Method: 发现输出层梯度足以进行有效的数据选择，且其计算只需较便宜的前向传播，无需对每样本反向传播。将该思路实现为 LESSER——一种可直接套用于现有选择方法的封装模块。

Result: 在 SFT 场景下将特征提取的 FLOP 成本降低 9.7 倍，在 RL 基准上降低 3.0 倍，同时在下游任务上保持与全梯度方法相当的性能。实验还发现，即便输出层梯度与全梯度对单样本的排序不同，它们选出的批次梯度仍是对齐的。

Conclusion: 输出层梯度可有效替代全参数梯度用于数据选择，在显著降低计算成本的同时维持选择质量，为大规模数据选择提供了一种高效方案。

Abstract: The choice of post-training data for large language models substantially affects downstream performance. Gradient-based data selection is a popular approach that ranks training data by how well their gradients align with those of a small validation set. However, ranking with full-parameter gradients requires an expensive backward pass on every sample, making computation intractable for large candidate pools. This raises a natural question: can we approximate full-gradient features at a fraction of the cost? Conveniently, we find that output-layer gradients suffice for effective data selection, yet require only the cheaper forward pass. We implement this as LESSER, a drop-in wrapper for selection methods that reduces the feature-extraction FLOP cost by $9.7\times$ for SFT and $3.0\times$ for RL benchmarks, while tracking full-gradient performance on downstream tasks. Empirically, we find that even when output-layer and full gradients rank individual samples differently, they select batches with aligned gradients.

</details>


### [421] [RNADyn: A Benchmark for Generating and Understanding RNA Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03712)
*Yiming Huang,Lennart Bastian,Hanqun Cao,Luis Vollmers,Tolga Birdal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RNADynBench（2585条质控100ns全原子轨迹的标准化RNA分子动力学基准）与RNADynNet（生成轨迹与提取动力学指纹的统一模型），实现RNA动力学的生成与理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNA功能依赖构象变化，静态结构无法充分刻画；大规模标准化RNA动力学数据有限，且现有方法通常将轨迹生成与动力学理解视为相互独立的目标。

Method: 构建RNADynBench，包含2585条质控100ns全原子轨迹及防泄漏数据划分；提出RNADynNet，采用共享骨干网络，从单构象同时实现轨迹生成与动力学指纹提取，融合坐标去噪、单帧到轨迹对齐与物理约束。

Result: 在两个测试集（含高柔性挑战集）上，生成轨迹的RMSF相关性达0.875与0.766；单构象预测与MD动力学一致性相当；物理约束同时提升生成动力学与指纹可恢复的物理信息。

Conclusion: RNADynBench与RNADynNet共同建立了RNA动力学生成与理解的基准及统一建模框架。

Abstract: Ribonucleic acid (RNA) functions through conformational changes that are not fully captured by static structures. However, large-scale standardized RNA dynamics data remain limited, and existing approaches typically treat trajectory generation and dynamics understanding as separate objectives. Here, we introduce RNADynBench, a standardized RNA molecular dynamics (MD) benchmark with 2585 quality-controlled 100-ns all-atom trajectories and leakage-controlled splits. Building on RNADynBench, we develop RNADynNet, a unified model for RNA dynamics learning that uses a shared backbone for both trajectory generation and dynamics fingerprint extraction from a single conformer. It combines coordinate denoising, single-frame-to-trajectory alignment, and physical grounding to connect all-atom trajectory generation with dynamics representation learning. Physical grounding improves both generated dynamics and the physical information recoverable from these fingerprints. Across both test sets, including the high-flexibility challenge set, the generated trajectories achieve RMSF correlations of 0.875 and 0.766, while single-conformer predictions show comparable agreement with MD-derived dynamics. RNADynBench and RNADynNet together establish a benchmark and unified modeling framework for generating and understanding RNA dynamics.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [422] [From Expression to Reaction: Role-aware Visual Transfer and Stimulus-guided Reasoning for Interlocutor Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03016)
*Wei Wang,Zhaowu Li,Jianjie Luo,Fu Lee Wang,Lap-Kei Lee,Zhenguo Yang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出角色感知刺激引导(RASG)框架，仅用听者视频与说话者音频预测听者情绪，在MER-Cross数据集上达76.25%，较基线提升超17%，并在ACM MM 2026 MER Grand Challenge赛道1中获第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话者情绪识别面临两大难题：一是缺少标注的听者数据导致监督不匹配，二是视觉上相似的听者反应需依赖说话者语境才能正确解读，存在歧义。

Method: RASG包含两个模块：角色感知视觉迁移(RVT)选取面部表情与情绪标签一致的说话者样本，过滤听者轨迹并用可靠伪标签训练以听者为中心的视觉专家；刺激引导边界推理(SBR)仅在视觉模型不确定时启用二分类语言推理器，将说话者音频与文本作为语境而非直接情绪证据来区分相似的听者反应。

Result: 在MER-Cross数据集上取得76.25%的性能，较基线提升超过17%，团队在ACM MM 2026 MER Grand Challenge赛道1(MER-Cross)中排名第二。

Conclusion: RASG通过角色感知迁移与刺激引导推理，有效缓解监督不匹配与听者反应歧义问题，验证了以语境辅助情绪识别的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a Role-aware Stimulus-guided (RASG) framework for interlocutor emotion recognition, which predicts listener emotions from listener-only videos and speaker-only audios. RASG consists of Role-aware Visual Transfer (RVT) and Stimulus-guided Boundary Reasoning (SBR) modules, which address supervision mismatch due to the lack of labeled listener data and ambiguity among visually similar listener reactions whose interpretation depends on speaker context, respectively. More specifically, RVT selects speaker samples whose facial expressions support their emotion labels. It then filters listener tracks and uses reliable pseudo-labels to train a listener-centric visual expert. SBR uses a two-class language reasoner only when the visual model is uncertain. It treats speaker audio and text as context rather than direct emotion evidence to distinguish similar listener reactions. Experiments conducted on MER-Cross dataset shows that RASG achieves 76.25\% on MER-Cross and improves the performance of the baseline over 17\%. Our team ranks second in Track 1 (MER-Cross) of the MER Grand Challenge at ACM MM 2026.

</details>
